### [1] [RadarGSOcc: Doppler-Aware 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction Using 4D Radar Tensor with Geometric-Semantic Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18686)
*Christofel Rio Goenawan,Dong-Hee Paek,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RadarGSOcc，一种直接处理4D雷达张量的高效3D语义占用预测框架，并发布首个基于K-Radar的大规模4D雷达张量3D语义占用基准K-Radar 3DSemOcc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机方法在低光照下性能下降，LiDAR方法对恶劣天气敏感；4D雷达更鲁棒，但其稀疏性及雷达张量处理成本高限制了应用。

Method: RadarGSOcc直接处理4D雷达张量，分别提取几何与语义特征（含多普勒信息），核心为基于可变形注意力的几何精炼模块；同时构建K-Radar 3DSemOcc基准，基于K-Radar数据集并精炼LiDAR标注，覆盖51.2m范围、15个类别。

Result: 在K-Radar 3DSemOcc上达到38.2% IoU、7.6% mIoU、25.5%加权mIoU；加权mIoU相对RadarOcc提升17%（25.5 vs. 21.9），且参数量和计算量更少；显著提升卡车预测，但牺牲部分轿车精度；优于相机方法，缩小与LiDAR方法的差距。

Conclusion: 4D雷达是3D语义占用预测中可靠且高效的模态；提出的EBKL代理指标可在恶劣天气无逐帧真值时评估鲁棒性。数据集与代码将公开。

Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction is crucial for autonomous driving, but camera-based methods degrade in low-light conditions and LiDAR-based methods are sensitive to adverse weather. 4D radar is more robust, yet its sparsity and the high cost of radar tensor processing limit its use. We propose Radar Geometric-Semantic Occupancy Prediction (RadarGSOcc), an efficient framework that directly processes 4D radar tensors by separately extracting geometric and semantic features, including Doppler information, with a deformable attention-based geometric refinement module as its key component. We also introduce the K-Radar 3DSemOcc benchmark, the first large-scale benchmark for 3D semantic occupancy prediction using 4D radar tensors, built on the K-Radar dataset with refined LiDAR-based annotations. RadarGSOcc predicts 15 classes within a 51.2m range and achieves 38.2% IoU, 7.6% mIoU, and 25.5% weighted mIoU, outperforming RadarOcc by 17% relative (25.5 vs. 21.9) in weighted mIoU while using fewer parameters and lower computation. It substantially improves truck prediction while trading off car accuracy, outperforms camera-based approaches, and narrows the gap with LiDAR-based methods. To assess robustness without per-frame ground truth in adverse weather, we further propose a novel proxy metric, Environment-Based KL Divergence (EBKL), under which 4D radar proves a reliable and efficient modality for 3D semantic occupancy prediction. The dataset and code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [2] [LLM2R: From Large Language Models to 3D Reassemblers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18604)
*Zhouhan Zhong,Qiuhong Shen,Xingyi Yang,Jinyi Xu,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LLM2R，将3D部件装配建模为序列任务，用预训练解码器LLM、Point-BERT、Q-Former和LoRA，在一次非自回归前向中预测所有部件的SE(3)位姿；在多个数据集上取得领先精度，且随部件数增加优势扩大并支持零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D部件装配需从一组未装配部件重建完整形状，为每个部件预测刚体SE(3)变换。部件放置很少仅由其自身几何决定，因此任务要求对全部部件进行全局推理；现有依赖成对关系或迭代生成与验证的方法难以扩展到部件较多的对象。

Method: LLM2R将部件装配视为序列建模：Point-BERT编码器和Q-Former把每个部件压缩为几何token块，拼接所有部件块后，由LoRA适配的因果LLM一次前向处理。为弥补因果注意力只能观察前序部件的限制，在最后一个部件后附加可学习摘要token块，使其跟随所有部件并以恒定token成本聚合整体装配状态；部件感知位姿头将每个部件的隐藏状态与摘要token融合，预测其旋转和平移。该统一架构可处理语义和几何装配，并以单次非自回归方式预测全部位姿。

Result: 在PartNet三个类别上取得最高部件准确率和成功率；在PartNeXt上取得最佳旋转误差、平移误差和部件准确率；在Breaking Bad上取得最低平移误差和最快推理，仅专用骨折方法更准确。其优势随部件数增加而扩大：100个部件时，在Breaking Bad上部件准确率超GARF 33.5个点，在PartNet上超Assembler 29.1个点；并可零样本迁移到未见类别，部件准确率比三个基线中最强者高16个点以上。

Conclusion: LLM2R验证了以LLM序列建模进行3D部件装配的有效性与可扩展性，能统一处理语义和几何装配、高效预测全部位姿，并在多部件和跨类别场景中表现优越。

Abstract: 3D part assembly aims to reconstruct a complete shape from a set of unassembled parts by predicting a rigid SE(3) transformation for each part. Because the placement of a part is rarely determined by its own geometry, the task demands reasoning over the entire part set, and existing methods, which rely on pairwise relations or iterative generation and verification, scale poorly to objects with many parts. We present LLM2R, which casts part assembly as sequence modeling with a pretrained decoder-only large language model (LLM): a Point-BERT encoder and a Q-Former compress each part into a block of geometric tokens, the blocks of all parts are concatenated, and a causal LLM adapted with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) processes the sequence in one forward pass. Causal attention, however, lets the hidden states of a part observe only the parts that precede it. LLM2R therefore appends a block of learnable summary tokens after the last part: these tokens follow every part, so their states aggregate the whole assembly at a constant token cost, and a part-aware pose head fuses each part's states with them before predicting its rotation and translation. One architecture thus handles semantic and geometric assembly and predicts all poses in a single non-autoregressive pass. LLM2R achieves the highest part accuracy and success rate on all three PartNet categories, the best rotation error, translation error, and part accuracy on PartNeXt, and the lowest translation error and fastest inference on Breaking Bad, where only a fracture-specialized method is more accurate. Its advantage grows with the number of parts: at 100 parts it exceeds GARF by 33.5 points in part accuracy on Breaking Bad and Assembler by 29.1 points on PartNet, and it transfers zero-shot to unseen categories with more than 16 points higher part accuracy than the strongest of three baselines.

</details>


### [3] [DriveWLA: Unifying Future Dynamics and Scene Semantics through World-Language-Action Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17903)
*Shuzheng Wang,Haoxuan Li,Shengzhen Li,Chenxi Wu,Yajing Sun,Lu Hou,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveWLA 是一个基于 Mixture-of-Transformers 的统一 World-Language-Action 模型，联合建模语义理解、未来场景动态与轨迹生成，并通过 Flow-GRPO 强化学习后训练提升轨迹生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型与世界模型在自动驾驶的语义理解和未来场景演化方面表现突出，但如何有效整合二者以生成轨迹仍具挑战；本文旨在统一语义理解、未来动态预测和轨迹生成。

Method: 提出 DriveWLA，基于 Mixture-of-Transformers 构建三个专家：World Expert 负责未来预测，Language Expert 负责语义理解，Action Expert 负责轨迹生成。引入 Cross-Expert Attention，实现跨专家受控信息流并对齐语义与动态表示。采用 Flow-GRPO 强化学习后训练，超越模仿学习以精炼 Action Expert。

Result: 大量实验表明 DriveWLA 在开环与闭环评测中均取得优越性能：NAVSIM v1 上达到 92.3 PDMS，NAVSIM v2 上达到 90.1 EPDMS，在基于 VLM 和世界模型的规划器中表现突出。无需特定微调，DriveWLA 还在 HUGSIM、nuScenes 和 Bench2Drive 上展现出强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结果验证了统一 World-Language-Action 建模的有效性，并将 DriveWLA 确立为面向自动驾驶的可泛化框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and world models have demonstrated strong capabilities in semantic understanding and future scene evolution for autonomous driving. However, effectively integrating both capabilities for trajectory generation remains challenging. In this work, we propose DriveWLA, a unified World-Language-Action model based on Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) that jointly models semantic understanding, future scene dynamics, and trajectory generation. DriveWLA consists of three experts: a World Expert for future prediction, a Language Expert for semantic understanding, and an Action Expert for trajectory generation. We further introduce Cross-Expert Attention, which enables controlled information flow across experts while aligning semantic and dynamic representations. To improve trajectory generation beyond imitation learning, we apply Flow-GRPO reinforcement learning post-training to refine the Action Expert. Extensive experiments show that DriveWLA achieves superior performance in both open-loop and closed-loop evaluation. DriveWLA achieves 92.3 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 90.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, demonstrating strong performance among VLM-based and world-model-based planners. Without specific fine-tuning, DriveWLA further demonstrates strong zero-shot generalization to HUGSIM, nuScenes, and Bench2Drive. These results validate the effectiveness of our unified World-Language-Action modeling, establishing DriveWLA as a generalizable framework for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [4] [Physical-Field-Bridged Material Parameter Inference from Multi-View Deformation Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17591)
*Linrui Shou,Jie Li,Yu Liu,Tao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理场桥接的材料推断框架：用稠密物理响应连接多视角RGB形变视频与材料本构类型分类及连续参数回归，实现单次前向快速推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉观测估计可仿真材料参数对可形变物体操作很重要，但材料属性只能通过物理响应间接体现；现有逆物理方法常需逐场景重建、在线调参或仿真闭环优化，并可能依赖外观或类别捷径。

Method: 方法预测力/接触掩码、投影运动流场和投影应力响应图，并以这些稠密物理场为条件进行材料推断，减少对外观或类别捷径的依赖；同时构建DeformFieldBench，基于3DGS与MPM，包含100种物体形状、5类力学加载模式、5000次仿真及像素对齐物理监督。

Result: 在四个连续参数上取得最佳中位误差；应力热点召回率从0.0762提升至0.5121；未见物体的同仿真器回放达0.8676 SSIM/36.82 dB PSNR；协议匹配GSO评测中推断与未来状态预测约30秒，而逐场景优化需15–120分钟；在5个真实序列上有可测回放一致性。

Conclusion: 结果表明稠密物理场可作为快速材料推断的有效桥梁，但跨仿真器与真实世界测试仍暴露域差距和可辨识性缺口。

Abstract: Estimating simulation-ready material parameters from visual observations is crucial for deformable-object manipulation but challenging because material properties are only indirectly revealed through physical responses. We introduce physical-field-bridged material inference, a single-pass framework that uses dense physical responses to connect multi-view RGB deformation videos with constitutive-type classification and continuous parameter regression. Unlike inverse-physics methods requiring per-scene reconstruction, online tuning, or simulation-in-the-loop optimization, our model predicts force/contact masks, projected motion-flow fields, and projected stress-response maps, then conditions material inference on these fields to reduce reliance on appearance or category shortcuts. We construct DeformFieldBench, a 3DGS MPM learning environment comprising 5,000 simulations across 100 object shapes and five mechanically informative loading modes, with dense pixel-aligned physical supervision. Our method achieves the best median errors on all four continuous parameters, improves stress-hotspot recall from 0.0762 to 0.5121, and attains 0.8676 SSIM/36.82 dB PSNR in same-simulator replay on unseen objects. Under a protocol-matched GSO evaluation, inference and future-state prediction take approximately 30 seconds, compared with 15-120 minutes for per-scene optimization. The method also yields measurable replay agreement on five real-world sequences. These results support dense physical fields as an effective bridge for fast material inference, while cross-simulator and real-world tests reveal remaining domain and identifiability gaps.

</details>


### [5] [Proactive Physical AI through Continuous Observation and Timely Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16967)
*Zhenhua Yang,Jiafeng Liang,Xingyu Zhou,Yi Jiang,Xiayu Cao,Zui Chen,Luqi Wang,Haiyang Sun,Yufei Xiang,Chang Li,Rongchuan Mu,Bowen Chi,Zihe Tong,Mingyu Li,Huijie Ma,Yiqiao Yang,Baoqi Ren,Zhiming Ma,Zhihao Zhu,Jiahao Ji,Zhuoyao Wang,Qianyu Wang,Xiangyu Liu,Wang Zixiang,Shunliang Zhang,Ming Liu,Xiaocheng Feng,Duyu Tang,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出“主动式物理AI”（Proactive Physical AI）问题，并构建端到端系统 ProAgent，使AI持续观察物理世界、识别需要介入的情境，并在合适时机主动执行动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理AI通常围绕明确指定的任务或指令进行感知、推理与行动，但真实世界中许多重要任务会在人明确表达之前，从持续变化的世界状态中自发出现。因此需要研究系统如何主动发现并响应这些新兴需求。

Method: ProAgent 包含两个核心组件：StreamingJev，一种Jev风格主动时机模型，用于判断是否需要介入以及何时介入；Response Model，用于生成面向人的回复或调用可用设备能力进行物理执行。为支持大规模学习，作者还设计了自动化数据流水线，将视频转化为主动时机监督信号。

Result: 实验表明，在明确目标和固定角色设定下，ProAgent均表现出较强主动性能，相比源骨干基线具有更准确的介入时机和更少的误触发。真实机器人评估进一步显示，ProAgent能够识别新兴需求并将其转化为合适的物理动作。

Conclusion: ProAgent验证了主动式物理AI的可行性，表明系统可通过持续观察、时机判断和响应生成，在恰当时机主动介入并执行物理行动。

Abstract: Physical AI is commonly formulated around an explicitly specified task: given a goal or instruction, a system perceives, reasons, and acts. In many real-world settings, however, important tasks emerge from continuously changing world states before a person explicitly formulates them. We study this complementary problem as Proactive Physical AI, where an AI system continuously observes the physical world, identifies situations that warrant intervention, and initiates appropriate actions at the right time. We develop ProAgent, an end-to-end system built around two components: a Jev-style proactive timing model (dubbed StreamingJev) that determines whether and when intervention is needed, and a Response Model that generates responses for people or invokes available device capabilities for physical execution. To support learning at scale, we develop an automated data pipeline that converts videos into supervision for proactive timing. Experiments demonstrate strong proactive performance under both explicit objectives and standing roles, combining more accurate intervention timing with fewer false triggers than the source-backbone baseline. Real-robot evaluations further demonstrate ProAgent's ability to identify emerging needs and translate them into appropriate physical actions.

</details>


### [6] [ConDenseDrive: Knowledge-Dense, Token-Sparse Visual Encoder for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16908)
*Shengzhen Li,Yongkang Li,Hao Gao,Chenxi Wu,Yajing Sun,Lu Hou,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向端到端自动驾驶的视觉编码器 ConDenseDrive，通过知识密集化与 token 稀疏化三阶段设计，在严格时延约束下提升时空表征质量与推理效率，并在 NAVSIM 与 HUGSIM 上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶需要丰富时空表征以支撑场景理解与轨迹规划，但现有系统多从通用图像预训练初始化视觉编码器，并对每个相机视角和输入帧的所有 patch token 进行密集处理，导致表征质量与推理效率受限。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：首先从三个视觉基础模型中蒸馏互补的视觉、语义与几何知识，使编码器知识密集；其次将蒸馏编码器适配到多帧端到端驾驶，并把时空上下文集成到 register tokens 中以服务规划；最后采用 register 引导的 token 压缩实现 token 稀疏化，减少冗余 patch 计算。

Result: 实现 2.0× 加速并提升规划性能；在 NAVSIM v1 上达到 94.5 PDMS，在 NAVSIM v2 上达到 90.8 EPDMS，在 HUGSIM 零样本评估中达到 37.4 HD-Score。

Conclusion: 实验与定性分析表明，视觉编码器设计是通往可靠且高效端到端自动驾驶的有效路径。

Abstract: End-to-end driving requires rich spatiotemporal representations to support scene understanding and trajectory planning under strict latency constraints. However, most systems initialize their visual encoders from generic image pretraining and densely process all patch tokens for each camera view and input frame, limiting both representation quality and inference efficiency. To address these challenges, we present ConDenseDrive, a knowledge-dense and token-sparse visual encoder for end-to-end autonomous driving. Our three-stage framework first makes the encoder knowledge-dense by distilling complementary visual, semantic, and geometric knowledge from three vision foundation models. It then adapts the distilled encoder to multi-frame end-to-end driving and integrates spatiotemporal context into register tokens for planning. Finally, it makes the encoder token-sparse by applying register-guided token compression, reducing redundant patch computation and yielding a 2.0$\times$ speedup while improving planning performance. ConDenseDrive achieves 94.5 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 90.8 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, and reaches 37.4 HD-Score in zero-shot evaluation on HUGSIM. Extensive experiments and qualitative analyses demonstrate that visual encoder design provides an effective route to reliable and efficient end-to-end driving.

</details>


### [7] [VLA-GAUGE: Systematically Evaluating Generalization and Robustness in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16567)
*Jun Xiao,Rui Ye,Dong Cao,Jinyu Gu,Ying Wang,Zhiliang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLA-GAUGE诊断框架显示，12个VLA模型平均零样本得分仅22.3%，组合扰动下平均R-Score仅9.3；诊断驱动的微调可显著提升π0.5的零样本泛化与鲁棒性，表明熟悉任务上的高成功率并不代表强泛化与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA在熟悉操作任务上成功率很高，但这种分数是否反映真正的零样本泛化与扰动鲁棒性尚不明确；已有评测框架缺乏能力级诊断、组合扰动覆盖，且未将诊断与定向模型改进连接起来。

Method: 提出VLA-GAUGE诊断框架，在共享可执行任务表示下统一两类评测：(1)覆盖六个能力套件的成对分布内与分布外任务；(2)单因素敏感性分析与组合扰动筛选及评估；(3)通过定向数据增强和模型微调实现诊断驱动改进。在统一协议下评测12个不同规模与动作建模范式的VLA。

Result: VLA-GAUGE平均零样本得分仅22.3%，SimpleVLA-RL仅8.0%且排名倒数第二；在三种代表性组合扰动配置下平均R-Score为9.3。诊断驱动微调使π0.5在留出场景上的零样本泛化与选定扰动组合鲁棒性分别提升72.4和31.8分，后者超过LIBERO-Plus单因素微调的10.9分增益，尽管成功训练轨迹更少。

Conclusion: 熟悉操作任务上的高报告成功率不能保证对未见任务的强零样本泛化或对组合扰动的鲁棒性；诊断式评测可为定向模型改进提供可操作信号。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have achieved high success rates on familiar manipulation tasks, yet it remains unclear whether such scores reflect genuine zero-shot generalization and perturbation robustness. Existing evaluation frameworks provide limited capability-level diagnosis and combinatorial perturbation coverage, often without connecting diagnosis to targeted model refinement. We introduce VLA-GAUGE, a diagnostic framework that unifies both evaluations under a shared executable task representation. It combines (1) paired in-distribution and out-of-distribution tasks across six capability suites, (2) single-factor sensitivity analysis with combinatorial perturbation screening and evaluation, and (3) diagnosis-driven refinement through targeted data augmentation and model fine-tuning. We evaluate 12 VLAs spanning different model sizes and action-modeling paradigms under a unified protocol. We find that (1) The mean Zero-Shot Score on VLA-GAUGE is only 22.3%, with SimpleVLA-RL scoring 8.0% and ranking second to last among the evaluated models. (2) Under three representative combinatorial perturbation configurations, the mean R-Score is 9.3. (3) Diagnosis-driven fine-tuning improves $\pi_{0.5}$'s zero-shot generalization and robustness under a selected perturbation combination on held-out scenarios by 72.4 and 31.8 scoring points, respectively; the latter exceeds the 10.9-point gain from LIBERO-Plus single-factor fine-tuning despite fewer successful training trajectories. These results show that high reported success rates on familiar manipulation tasks do not guarantee strong zero-shot generalization to unseen tasks or robustness to combinatorial perturbations, while diagnostic evaluation provides actionable signals for targeted model improvement.

</details>


### [8] [Recovering from Pose-Selection Failures in Repeated Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16409)
*Jaehyun Kim,Wonseok Kang,Tae-Wan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出历史引导位姿重选 HPR，利用最多五个历史定位输出的粗位置预测来重选当前查询的位姿候选，从而缓解重复结构导致的错误内点支持问题，在多种视觉定位配置中提升召回且几乎不损失成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复结构可能让错误位姿候选获得比正确候选更多的内点支持，导致视觉定位选出错误位姿；因此需要一种不依赖里程计、也不做位姿精化的候选重选机制。

Method: HPR 使用最多五个过去定位输出生成粗位置预测，并用其区分当前查询中彼此分离的候选。若最佳拟合预测提出一个替代候选，且所有拟合良好的预测都明显更接近该替代候选而非定位器原输出，则接受该替代；否则保留原候选。HPR 不依赖里程计，不改变被选中的候选，仅将预测用于选择而非位姿精化，并使用一个开发集阈值。

Result: 在 HLoc、基于 MASt3R 的位姿生成和基于 JIST 的检索共 12 个主要配置中，HPR 在 5 米和 10 度阈值下均提升召回，成功丢失少于 0.4% 查询。在 NAVER Gangnam B1 上，HPR 纠正 97 个 HLoc 失败并丢失 6 个成功；在三个 HLoc 测试集上，0.25 米和 2 度阈值下仍有增益。HLoc 候选上的 HPR 中位运行时间低于 1 毫秒，不含位姿生成；定位频率降低时恢复效果减弱。

Conclusion: 历史预测可有效辅助位姿候选重选，缓解重复结构导致的误选，且方法轻量、通用、几乎不损失成功；但其收益依赖较频繁的定位输出。

Abstract: Repetitive structures can give an incorrect pose candidate more inlier support than an accurate one, causing visual localization to select it. History-Guided Pose Reselection (HPR) uses coarse position forecasts from up to five past localization outputs to distinguish well-separated current-query candidates. The best-fitting forecast proposes an alternative; HPR accepts it only if every well-fitting forecast is much closer to it than to the localizer's pose. HPR needs no odometry and returns the selected candidate unchanged, using prediction for selection rather than pose refinement. With one development-set threshold, HPR improves recall within 5 meters and 10 degrees in all 12 main configurations of Hierarchical Localization (HLoc), MASt3R-based pose generation, and JIST-based retrieval, losing successes on fewer than 0.4 percent of queries. On NAVER Gangnam B1, HPR corrects 97 HLoc failures and loses six successes; gains persist within 0.25 meters and 2 degrees on all three HLoc test sets. Median HPR runtime on HLoc candidates is below one millisecond, excluding pose generation. Recovery weakens under less frequent localization.

</details>


### [9] [ClothAtlas: Joint Garment Identification and State Estimation under Self-Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16293)
*Jens Lundell,Alberta Longhini,Lei Shu,Hemal Arora,Evan Robert,Carlota Parés-Morlans,Marcel Büsching,Mårten Björkman,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ClothAtlas：在给定规范网格库与部分点云时，用共享图条件流匹配模型为每个候选网格采样完整构型并按观测拟合度排序，从而联合完成衣物身份识别与构型估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 衣物操作需同时推断衣物身份与完整构型，但部分自遮挡观测造成先有鸡还是先有蛋的困境：补全部分观测依赖衣物特定的变形先验，而识别衣物又依赖能解释观测的构型。

Method: 以规范网格库为候选集，共享的图条件流匹配模型为每个候选网格采样构型并依据观测拟合度对候选排序；该模型可处理不同网格形状、顶点数与连接关系，使新衣物仅凭规范网格即可加入而无需重训练。

Result: 在留出的 DexGarmentLab 衣物上，单一平坦观测下对全部 22 件测试衣物均在 221 个候选中将正确网格排第一；单一自遮挡观测下识别 15/22，确定性图估计器为 8/22；持久顶点身份支持真实衣物抓取目标定位，相比稠密描述子匹配，总体平均误差由 15.4 降至 6.4 cm，遮挡目标由 17.0 降至 8.3 cm。

Conclusion: ClothAtlas 通过联合身份识别与构型估计，实现新衣物免重训练的泛化，并借助持久顶点对应提升遮挡条件下的抓取目标定位精度。

Abstract: Garment manipulation requires inferring both garment identity and complete configuration from partial, self-occluded observations. This creates a chicken-and-egg problem: completing a partial observation requires a garment-specific deformation prior, while identifying the garment requires a configuration that explains the observation. We introduce ClothAtlas, which jointly identifies garments and estimates their complete configurations from partial point clouds and a library of canonical meshes. A shared graph-conditioned flow-matching model samples configurations for each candidate mesh and ranks candidates by observation fit. The model accommodates different mesh shapes, vertex counts, and connectivities, allowing new garments to be added from canonical meshes alone without retraining. On held-out DexGarmentLab garments, ClothAtlas ranks the correct mesh first among 221 candidates for all 22 test garments from a single flat observation. From a single self-occluded observation, it identifies 15/22 garments, compared with 8/22 for a deterministic graph estimator. Persistent vertex identities support grasp-target localization on real garments, reducing mean error from 15.4 to 6.4 cm overall and from 17.0 to 8.3 cm on occluded targets compared with dense descriptor matching. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [10] [Graph3R: Unifying View Graph Initialization with Feed-Forward Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16272)
*Jingwen Yu,Hengli Wang,Jianhao Jiao,Zhonghang Liu,Ping Tan,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Graph3R 将视图图初始化与前馈重建通过共享几何表示统一起来，可从包含不相关场景的未整理图像集合中实现多场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈重建通常假设所有视图来自同一场景；实际未整理图像集合常包含不相交场景，联合重建会破坏相机位姿和几何。已有流程一般在最终重建前分阶段解决视图关联，导致视图图初始化与重建推断耦合松散。

Method: Graph3R 利用 π³ 的多视图特征，通过轻量验证头预测成对共视性以构建视图图，并将输入视图划分为场景组。对每个组，在解码相机位姿和稠密几何前掩码共享特征；复用多视图特征使得单次骨干网络前向即可分别重建不相交场景。

Result: 在三个基准上，Graph3R 在 100% 精度下的平均最大召回率较基线最高提升 8.99 个百分点，同时改善混合场景输入的重建质量。在多会话建图任务中，它提升跨会话定位并取得有竞争力的位姿估计精度，重建查询组时相较基线约 35.9 倍运行时加速。

Conclusion: 通过将学习到的视图关联与几何推断集成，该方法向从未整理图像集合进行端到端重建迈出了一步。

Abstract: Feed-forward reconstruction recovers camera poses and dense geometry from image sets whose views are assumed to come from a single scene. In practice, uncurated image collections often contain views from disjoint scenes, and reconstructing these views jointly can corrupt camera poses and geometry. Existing pipelines typically resolve view associations in separate stages before final reconstruction, leaving view graph initialization and reconstruction inference only loosely coupled. We introduce Graph3R, which unifies view graph initialization with feed-forward reconstruction through a shared geometric representation. A lightweight verification head predicts pairwise covisibility from the multi-view features of $\pi^3$ to construct a view graph. This graph classifies the input views into scene groups. For each group, we mask the shared features before decoding camera poses and dense geometry. Reusing the multi-view features allows Graph3R to reconstruct disjoint scenes separately with a single backbone pass. Across three benchmarks, Graph3R improves average maximum recall at 100% precision by upto 8.99 percentage points over the baseline, while improving reconstruction quality on mixed-scene inputs. Regarding the multi-session mapping task, Graph3R improves cross-session localization and achieves competitive pose estimation accuracy, while delivering a runtime speedup of approximately $35.9\times$ over the baseline when reconstructing the query group. By integrating learned view association with geometric inference, our approach takes a step toward end-to-end reconstruction from uncurated image collections.

</details>


### [11] [Harnessing SPAD Noise: Test-Time Gated Attention for Indoor Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15431)
*Yiquan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HSN，一种利用SPAD原生噪声作为信息先验的轻量级测试时门控注意力框架，用于室内单目度量深度估计，在域内达到SOTA并展现更强的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目度量深度估计受尺度模糊、边缘渗色和零样本泛化差等问题困扰；现有多模态方法虽引入1D/3D SPAD数据，但通常将SPAD噪声视为需去噪的有害伪影或直接忽略。本文的核心动机是转变视角：原生SPAD噪声并非无用干扰，而是隐含编码确定性环境特征的信息先验。

Method: 提出HSN（Harnessing SPAD Noise）框架，面向室内场景，结合1D SPAD直方图与Gate Adapters（GAs）。通过三个互补优化阶段，在推理时生成通道级门控注意力向量，动态调整编码器特征图，从而在不引入高计算开销或密集3D模态的情况下缓解尺度模糊和边界问题。

Result: 在具有挑战性的真实室内基准上，HSN在域内设置中取得最先进的度量深度精度，并在向陌生环境的零样本泛化中表现出更大潜力。可解释性分析表明，随着SPAD噪声增强，所提出的门控注意力机制会自适应地将注意力转向更深的语义层。

Conclusion: SPAD噪声可被有效用作有益先验；HSN以轻量测试时门控注意力方式提升单目度量深度估计的精度与泛化性，并为理解噪声驱动的注意力调整提供可解释证据。代码将公开。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation (MDE) has emerged as a cost-effective alternative to hardware-dependent 3D sensing modalities, yet predicting precise metric depth remains a persistent challenge due to intrinsic scale ambiguity, edge bleeding, and limited zero-shot generalization in unseen environments. Recent multi-modal approaches incorporate 1D/3D Single-Photon Avalanche Diode (SPAD) data to resolve these ambiguities, but they mainly treat SPAD noise as a harmful artifact required for denoising or leave the noise ignored. In this paper, we present an essentially different insight: native SPAD noise is an informative prior that implicitly encodes deterministic environmental characteristics. Driven by this paradigm shift, we introduce a novel, lightweight Test-Time Gated Attention framework by Harnessing SPAD Noise, therefore dubbed HSN. Tailored for indoor scenarios, HSN leverages the synergistic combination of 1D SPAD histograms and Gate Adapters (GAs). Through three complementary optimization phases, it generates channel-wise gated attention vectors to dynamically adjust encoder feature maps at inference time, effectively resolving ambiguous scale and boundary challenges without the heavy computational overhead or dense 3D modalities that other TTA methods suffer from. Extensive experiments on challenging real-world indoor benchmarks show that HSN achieves state-of-the-art metric depth accuracy in in-domain settings, while exhibiting even greater potential under zero-shot generalization to unfamiliar environments. Furthermore, interpretability analysis reveals that the proposed gated attention mechanism adaptively shifts attention toward deeper semantic layers under intensifying SPAD noise. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [12] [ASM-6D: Fast Certified Pose-Shape Inference with Complete Correspondence Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15287)
*Ho Jin Choi,Minku Kim,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ASM-6D通过共享对应关系将生成式完整几何预测与类别级位姿-形状推断耦合，在无需实例模型的情况下实现类别级6D位姿与形状估计，并在ROPE和HouseCat6D上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别级6D位姿与形状估计需从部分观测中恢复物体位姿和几何，且不能依赖精确实例模型；现有方法常需独立对应分配，效率与遮挡处理受限。

Method: 给定RGB-D图像，ASM-6D生成多个完整3D对应假设，其点身份与类别级主动形状模型（ASM）共享（含遮挡点）。每个假设直接拟合旋转、平移、度量尺度和实例形状，无需单独对应分配；通过解析消去平移和形状系数，将拟合简化为九变量缩放旋转优化，支持多假设实时批量评估。

Result: 在ROPE和HouseCat6D基准上取得SOTA；消融实验表明生成式对应预测和主动形状变化的重要性；支持形状补全、形状相关位姿歧义、跟踪和机器人抓取，速度6–38 Hz（取决于推理预算）。

Conclusion: ASM-6D表明将生成式完整几何预测与显式类别级位姿-形状推断通过共享对应统一，可高效且鲁棒地处理类别级6D位姿与形状估计，并拓展至多种下游任务。

Abstract: Category-level 6D pose and shape estimation requires recovering an object's pose and geometry from partial observations without an exact instance model. We present ASM-6D, which couples generative complete-geometry prediction with explicit category-level pose-shape inference through shared correspondences. Given an RGB-D image, ASM-6D generates alternative complete 3D correspondence hypotheses whose point identities are shared with a category-level Active Shape Model (ASM), including occluded points. Each hypothesis is fitted directly for rotation, translation, metric scale, and instance shape without a separate correspondence-assignment stage. We analytically eliminate translation and shape coefficients, reducing fitting to a nine-variable scaled-rotation optimization problem that enables efficient batched evaluation of multiple hypotheses in real time. ASM-6D achieves state-of-the-art performance on ROPE and HouseCat6D benchmarks. Ablations demonstrate the importance of generative correspondence prediction and active shape variation. The representation also supports shape completion, shape-dependent pose ambiguity, tracking, and robotic grasping at 6-38 Hz, depending on the inference budget.

</details>


### [13] [YOUDrive: Driving Style as a Composable Direction for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14802)
*Guantian Zheng,Lidong Yu,Tianyu Gao,Jiaxing Chen,Jiashu Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 YOUDrive，一种视觉-语言-动作规划器，通过教师行为蒸馏得到的任务向量实现连续、可组合的驾驶风格控制，并在 NAVSIM navtest 上保持高任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶策略任务表现强，但对驾驶风格（加速、制动、跟车距离）控制有限；缺乏显式偏好条件的策略在推理时无直接机制调整这些行为。

Method: 将每位教师的行为蒸馏为任务向量（学习到的参数更新），在规划器表示中诱导风格方向；推理时可缩放和组合这些方向而无需重训练主干。以这些方向为条件，流匹配解码器建模多模态轨迹分布并生成运动学可行的运动候选。提出 Style Alignment Score（SAS），在安全与任务门控后描述人类参照的运动学行为。

Result: 在 NAVSIM navtest 上，单一系数可单调控制六种教师衍生人格的激进程度；在测试的个体与混合配置中 PDMS 均高于 0.90，最高达 0.954。进一步分析显示，控制场景上下文后存在策略特有的系统性运动学差异，而所评估的离散风格标签额外解释力有限。

Conclusion: YOUDrive 实现了无需重训练主干的可连续组合驾驶风格控制，同时保持高安全与任务性能，并提供 SAS 指标；代码、检查点和指标将公开。

Abstract: End-to-end driving policies can achieve strong task performance while offering limited control over their driving style. Driving style describes how a vehicle accelerates, brakes, and maintains distance. Policies trained without explicit preference conditioning lack a direct mechanism for adjusting these behaviors at inference. We propose YOUDrive, a vision--language--action planner for continuous and composable driving-style control. Each teacher's behavior is distilled into a task vector, a learned parameter update that induces a style direction in the planner's representation. These directions can be scaled and combined at inference without retraining the backbone. Conditioned on these directions, a flow-matching decoder models a multimodal trajectory distribution and generates kinematically feasible motion alternatives. We introduce the Style Alignment Score (SAS) to describe human-referenced kinematic behavior after safety and task gating. On NAVSIM navtest, a single coefficient monotonically controls assertiveness across six teacher-derived personas, while PDMS remains above 0.90 across the tested individual and blended configurations, reaching 0.954. Further analyses reveal systematic policy-specific kinematics after controlling for scene context, whereas the evaluated discrete style labels provide limited additional explanatory power. Code, checkpoints, and the metric will be released.

</details>


### [14] [Can Synthetic UAV Benchmarks Be Trusted? Real-Synthetic Validation for UAV Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14647)
*Yuyi Zhi,Min Du,Bill Shi,Guodong Yuan,Junheng Liang,Peifan Liao,Xin Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究合成UAV影像用于模型选择评估时能否保持真实UAV数据上的模型排序结论；结果表明其可信度是条件性的，依赖任务、运行域与模型集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务专用真实UAV数据稀缺，合成影像可扩展部署前测试证据；但仅凭视觉合理性或语义标签对齐不足以证明其用于模型选择的有效性。

Method: 在固定评估协议下开展两个互补案例：1）需求特定的道路碎片基准，比较四个语言条件感知系统；2）在独立构建的业务语料上做闭集压力测试，使用四个VisDrone兼容检测器，覆盖八个经验支持类别。并辅以五个UAV业务场景审计，分析真实-合成在类别组成、目标密度和目标尺度上的不匹配。

Result: 道路碎片基准中，合成数据绝对F1更低，但点估计排序与最优模型保持不变；bootstrap分析未支持成对反转，尺寸匹配重采样也保持最优模型决策。业务语料压力测试中，合成评估改变了点估计最优检测器，并反转了六组成对偏好方向中的三组。场景审计发现真实-合成在类别组成、目标密度、目标尺度上存在显著不匹配，尤其系统性低估小目标。

Conclusion: 合成UAV基准的可信度是条件性的：语义覆盖不足以保证有效性，必须针对具体任务、运行域和模型面板进行经验验证。

Abstract: Synthetic UAV imagery can expand pre-deployment test evidence when task-specific Real data are scarce, but visual plausibility or semantic label alignment alone does not establish validity for model selection. We study when Synthetic evaluation preserves the model-selection conclusions obtained from Real UAV data through two complementary cases under fixed evaluation protocols. In a requirement-specific road-debris benchmark, four language-conditioned perception systems obtain lower absolute F1 on Synthetic data, yet the point-estimate ordering and top-ranked model are preserved; bootstrap analysis yields no supported pairwise reversal, and size-matched resampling preserves the top-model decision. In contrast, a closed-set stress test on a separately materialized business corpus, using four VisDrone-compatible detectors over eight empirically supported classes, changes the point-estimate top-ranked detector and reverses three of six pairwise point-estimate preference directions. A complementary audit of five UAV business scenarios further reveals substantial Real-Synthetic mismatch in class composition, object density, and target scale, including systematic under-representation of small targets. These results show that Synthetic UAV benchmark trust is conditional: semantic coverage is insufficient, and validity must be established empirically for the task, operating regime, and model panel of interest.

</details>


### [15] [TartanMatch: Towards Universal Dense Correspondence Across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14513)
*Hyeokjoon Kwon,Jiting Cai,Ruogu Li,Kritan Bhandari,Geethika Hemkumar,Parv Maheshwari,Yuheng Qiu,Yuchen Zhang,Sebastian Scherer,Wenshan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TartanMatch，一个统一模型，可在RGB、事件、热成像、深度和投影LiDAR的25个有序模态对之间进行稠密对应，单一共享网络在零样本真实基准上显著超越逐对训练的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人平台使用多模态传感器，但现有稠密对应方法多限于RGB-RGB或少数量模态对；为每个模态对单独训练模型扩展性差，且无法利用跨模态对之间的正迁移。

Method: TartanMatch基于RGB预训练的对应模型，通过轻量模态适配模块和真实与合成数据混合，在同一个共享网络中适配全部25个模态对。

Result: 在零样本真实世界基准上，跨模态匹配的端点误差（EPE）较最强基线降低61.9%，同模态匹配降低49.6%；联合训练所有模态对相比逐对训练平均匹配精度更高，尤其在困难跨模态对上。

Conclusion: 单一统一模型即可实现广泛的多模态覆盖，并达到最先进的稠密对应匹配精度。

Abstract: Robotic platforms combine multi-modal sensors to operate across diverse conditions, but most dense correspondence methods are limited to RGB-RGB or a small set of modality pairs. Training a model for each modality pair scales poorly with the number of sensors and forgoes potential positive transfer across modality pairs. We introduce TartanMatch, a unified model for dense correspondence across all 25 ordered pairs among RGB, event, thermal, depth, and projected LiDAR. TartanMatch adapts an RGB-pretrained correspondence model to all modality pairs within one shared network using lightweight modality adaptation and a mixture of real and synthetic data. On zero-shot real-world benchmarks, TartanMatch reduces Endpoint Error (EPE) over the strongest baselines by 61.9% for cross-modal matching and 49.6% for same-modal matching. We further show that joint training across all modality pairs improves average matching accuracy over pair-specific training, especially on challenging cross-modal pairs. These results demonstrate that a single model achieves broad multi-modal coverage and state-of-the-art matching accuracy.

</details>


### [16] [Learning Transferable World-Action Models from Task-Paired Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14351)
*Jeongeun Park,Taekyung Kim,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WATCH 是一种跨视频预训练方法，通过让模型从一条人类演示预测另一条任务配对演示的动作与未来帧，学习可跨场景和具身迁移的世界-动作表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准视频内下一帧预测允许世界-动作模型依赖上下文与未来帧共享的外观信息，而非学习可跨场景和具身迁移的任务交互；同时人-机器人配对数据获取困难。

Method: WATCH 利用跨多样环境收集的任务配对人类演示进行跨视频预训练。给定一条演示的视频和动作，模型以另一条演示的当前观测为条件，联合预测该演示的动作和未来帧；预测另一条演示促使模型学习任务相关、可跨场景与演示者迁移的交互。该预训练仅需可扩展的人类-人类配对数据，无需匹配的人-机器人演示。

Result: 机器人后训练前分析表明：跨具身运动对齐改善，在未见机器人上的动作与视频预测更好，表征更接近机器人配对学习所得。机器人后训练后，WATCH 将 MimicDroid 的上下文内成功率从 40.9% 提升至 68.3%（Cosmos Policy），从 59.8% 提升至 77.7%（Cosmos3），在未见层级增益最大；跨机器人具身的无上下文后训练下增益仍保持。WATCH 优于视频内预训练和协同训练基线。

Conclusion: 跨视频预测是一种可扩展的学习可迁移世界-动作表征的方法，WATCH 验证了其有效性和优于基线的性能。

Abstract: World-action models inherit broad visual and physical priors from video pre-training. However, standard within-video next-frame prediction allows them to rely on appearance shared between the context and future, rather than learning interactions that transfer across scenes and embodiments. We introduce WATCH, a cross-video pre-training that learns from task-paired human demonstrations collected across diverse environments. Conditioned on the video and actions of one demonstration, the model jointly predicts another demonstration's actions and future frames from its current observation. Predicting one demonstration from another encourages the model to learn task-relevant interactions that transfer across scenes and demonstrators. Our pre-training shows that these capabilities can be learned from readily scalable human-human pairs alone, without requiring matched human-robot demonstrations. Analyses before robot post-training show improved motion alignment across embodiments and better action and video prediction on unseen robots, with representations closer to those learned from robot pairs. After robot post-training, WATCH improves MimicDroid in-context success from 40.9% to 68.3% with Cosmos Policy and from 59.8% to 77.7% with Cosmos3, with the largest gains on the unseen levels. Gains persist under context-free post-training across robot embodiments. WATCH outperforms within-video pre-training and co-training baselines, supporting cross-video prediction as a scalable approach to learning transferable world-action representations.

</details>


### [17] [RoboFake: Are Deepfake Detectors Ready for Humanoid Face Robots?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14269)
*Hao Chen,Junnan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 RoboFake：物理人脸机器人同步唇动并配合生成语音，由相机实拍、无像素编辑，却可构成伪造视频；测试六个在 FaceForensics++ 上训练的公开深度伪造检测器对机器人脸的反应，发现其多因机器人脸不像人而误报，而非识别伪造，且响应脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前 deepfake 检测器面对真实拍摄的机器人人脸伪造时表现如何，以及它们是否真正识别伪造，还是仅对非人/异常人脸产生反应。

Method: 在物理唇形同步人脸机器人视频、同机器人的合成视频、渲染机器人脸上测试六个在 FaceForensics++ 上训练的公开检测器；阈值固定于 FaceForensics++；与经过相同处理的真人脸比较；用匹配真人对照和掩蔽排除处理或背景影响；仅人脸可见时比较物理与合成；并施加轻量 JPEG 压缩。

Result: 在将 5% 真人脸判为假的阈值下，三个检测器几乎标记所有物理机器人帧，两个检测器标记不足 7%；对照与掩蔽表明响应来自人脸而非处理或背景；仅人脸可见时，六个检测器中三个给物理机器人的假分高于其合成视频；轻量 JPEG 压缩可使部分检测器的多数机器人帧低于阈值。

Conclusion: 当前检测器标记机器人更多是因为其脸不像真人，而非识别伪造；作者发布评估协议与代码，并提出三个关于逼真、交互式 RoboFake 的可测试假设。

Abstract: Humanoid face robots can now move their lips in sync with speech and show detailed facial expressions. Give such a robot the face of a real person and drive it with generated speech, and you get a face forgery that happens in front of the camera: the camera records a real object, and no pixel is edited. We call this a RoboFake. In this paper we ask what current deepfake detectors do when they see a robot face. We test six public detectors trained on FaceForensics++ on video of a physical lip-syncing face robot, on a synthesized video of the same robot, and on rendered robot faces. All thresholds are fixed on FaceForensics++, and every result is compared with real human faces that went through the same processing as the robot videos. At a threshold that flags 5% of real faces, three detectors flag almost every physical robot frame, while two flag fewer than 7%. A matched human control and masking show that the response comes from the face, not from the processing or the background. However, it does not track synthesis: with only the face visible, three of the six detectors give the physical robot a higher fake score than its synthesized video. The response is also fragile, and light JPEG compression is enough to push most robot frames below the threshold for some detectors. Our results suggest that current detectors flag today's robots because their faces do not look human, not because they recognize a forgery. We release our evaluation protocol and code, and state three testable hypotheses about realistic, interactive RoboFakes.

</details>

### [18] [Known-Voice Cloning and the Limits of Personality Transfer for Character Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18669)
*Johanna Magdalena Kuch,Maud van Lier,Daksitha Senel Withanage Don,Patrick Gebhard,Tanja Schneeberger,Elisabeth André*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 两项研究考察当虚拟角色使用熟悉人物的克隆语音时，人格印象是否会迁移。结果显示迁移证据有限：效应具有选择性，未跨特质与情境泛化；在线研究中可识别声音身份线索仅激活对说话者的预期，未重建整体人物印象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本语音克隆可从短录音复现可识别声音并赋予任意虚拟角色。既有研究提示熟悉声音身份可能将相关人格印象迁移到机器人或虚拟角色，但多关注熟悉度与个性化，未直接检验克隆熟悉声音是否系统性地向具身虚拟角色迁移人格印象。这引发担忧：克隆声音可能操纵人们将虚拟角色当作有能力的社交行动者。

Method: 开展两项研究，检验虚拟角色以熟悉人物的克隆声音说话时人格印象是否迁移。研究1在真实大学场景中，使用参与学生熟悉的讲师的克隆声音。研究2在线进行，使用社交媒体内容创作者及其社群成员的语音。分析基于身份激活、特质迁移与人物重建等概念，并辅以质性回答。

Result: 两项研究中人格迁移证据均有限。观察到的效应具有选择性，未能跨人格特质或研究情境泛化。在线研究中，可识别的声音身份线索激活了对说话者的预期，但未重建更广泛的人物印象。质性回答表明，判断常受感知到的人工性以及声音与外观是否匹配影响。

Conclusion: 研究结果缓和了常见担忧，即克隆声音会直接操纵人们将人工角色感知为有能力的社交行动者；熟悉声音克隆对人格印象的迁移并非普遍、系统性发生。

Abstract: Recent advances in zero-shot voice cloning systems make it possible to reproduce a recognizable voice from a short recording and attach it to arbitrary virtual characters. Prior work suggests that familiar vocal identity can carry over associated personality impressions to new targets such as robots or virtual characters. However, these works have mainly examined familiarity and personalization effects rather than directly testing whether a cloned familiar voice systematically transfers personality impressions to an embodied virtual character. Such transference raises the concern that cloned voices could be used to manipulate people into engaging with virtual characters as capable social actors. Across two studies, we investigated whether personality impressions transfer when a virtual character speaks with the cloned voice of a familiar person. Study 1 examined this question in a real-world university setting using the cloned voice of a lecturer familiar to the participating students. Study 2 examined the same question online using voices of social media content creators and members of their communities. Drawing on the concepts of identity activation, trait transfer, and person reconstruction, we show that evidence of personality transfer was limited in both studies. Observed effects were selective and did not generalize across traits or study contexts. In the online study in particular, recognizable voice identity cues activated expectations about the speaker but did not reconstruct the broader person impression. Qualitative responses further indicated that judgments were frequently shaped by perceived artificiality and by whether voice and appearance fit together. Our findings moderate common concerns that cloned voices straightforwardly manipulate people into perceiving artificial characters as capable social actors.

</details>

### [19] [Sensing via whole-body interactions with complex terrains: physical insights from robotic models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15334)
*Chen Li*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出以生物启发的机器人物理模型为桥梁，识别全身交互中产生的候选机械信息，检验其物理机制与行为收益，并生成生物学假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物在复杂地形中交互会产生力、形变和振动等机械信息，可能引导行为；但其产生与利用难以解析，因为运动不断快速重构交互。感觉神经生理学与生物力学揭示了子系统内的信息转换，复杂地形研究揭示了全身交互与行为结果，但二者之间仍难以衔接。

Method: 作者以聚焦视角提出：利用生物启发的机器人物理模型，识别全身交互产生的候选机械信息，检验其物理机制与行为收益，并据此提出生物学假设；通过三个案例研究展示该方法。

Result: 三个案例包括：多足动物穿越密集障碍时的运动转换、无肢动物通过不平地形时的垂直身体弯曲推进、蜘蛛快速屈腿辅助对网上猎物振动的感知；机器人与动物的对应关系支持这些候选物理机制的生物学合理性。

Conclusion: 该视角表明机器人物理模型有助于连接生物力学、感觉神经生理学与行为生态学，并推动更精细的测量与模型来判定相关机械信息如何被感知并用于调节行为。

Abstract: In nature, animals interact with complex terrains and often generate forces, deformations, and vibrations across the body and environment. Such interaction-induced mechanical information may guide behavior, yet its generation and use are difficult to resolve because movement rapidly and continually reconfigures the interactions that produce it. Sensory neurophysiology and biomechanics have revealed how mechanical information is transformed within sensory, neural, and musculoskeletal subsystems, whereas studies in complex terrains reveal rich whole-body interactions and behavioral outcomes. Connecting these levels remains a challenge. Here, I present a focused perspective on how biologically guided robotic physical models can help identify candidate mechanical information generated through whole-body interactions, test its physical mechanisms and behavioral benefits, and generate biological hypotheses. Three case studies illustrate this approach, multi-legged locomotor transitions to traverse dense clutter, limbless vertical body bending to propel through uneven terrain, and rapid spider leg crouching to help vibration sensing of prey on a web. Across these systems, correspondence between robots and animals supports the biological plausibility of these candidate physical mechanisms and motivates better measurements and models to determine how the relevant mechanical information is sensed and used to modulate behavior.

</details>
