### [20] [Assessing the Quality of Concrete Scenarios Generated from Scenario Classes for Automated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18169)
*Lina Putze,Thies de Graaff,Jan Steffen Becker,Christian Neurohr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该工作将场景距离度量聚合为质量函数，用于在自动驾驶场景化开发中量化具体场景集合的多样性，并通过实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景化开发已成为自动驾驶系统先进方法；从覆盖ODD的场景类生成具体场景用于危害分析、风险评估、验证确认时，集合多样性至关重要；但场景类常以约束系统形式化且具体场景空间未知，如何评估具体场景集合质量尚无明确方法。

Method: 将场景距离度量聚合为质量函数；针对以约束系统形式化场景类、具体场景空间不显式已知的情形，基于一组期望属性构造候选质量函数并证明其满足性质；在抽象场景化虚拟验证工具链中开展实验，用质量函数比较采样变异方法、考察不同场景距离度量选择，并研究时间方面对集合质量的影响。

Result: 实验表明质量函数可用于比较采样/变异方法的有效性，评估不同场景距离度量的选择，并揭示时间因素对具体场景集合质量的影响；候选质量函数在期望属性下得到理论保证。

Conclusion: 质量函数为自动驾驶场景化验证中具体场景集合的多样性评估提供了可证明、可实用的量化工具，有助于指导场景生成、采样与验证流程。

Abstract: A scenario-based development approach has become state of the art for automated driving systems. Once a set of scenario classes covering the operational design domain is available, concrete scenarios are generated from these scenario classes, e.g., to be used for hazard analysis and risk assessment or for the system's verification and validation. For such applications, a high diversity among the generated concrete scenarios is paramount. The question arises how the quality of collections of concrete scenarios can be assessed in such a setting. In this work, we aggregate scenario distance measures into so-called quality functions that quantify the diversity of concrete scenario collections in the context of automated driving. In particular, we consider the case where scenario classes are formalized as constraint systems and the associated concrete scenario space is not explicitly known. Based on a set of desired properties we construct candidates for such quality functions and give proofs for their property fulfillment. To show the practical relevance of quality functions, we perform a variety of experiments in the context of an abstract scenario-based virtual verification toolchain for automated driving systems. More specifically, we use quality functions to compare the effectiveness of variation methods for sampling, examine the choice of different scenario distance measures, and investigate the effects of temporal aspects on concrete scenario collection quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [21] [RoboHarness: A Simple Harness Outperforms VLA and World Actions Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14018)
*Bince Qu,Wei Chen,Bo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboHarness：一种机器人执行框架，让 LLM 编程智能体通过视觉-几何控制面板直接理解并调用具身任务；配合 Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 在 BEHAVIOR Challenge 2025 上超过 SOTA VLA 模型，并计划开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 LLM 如何在物理世界中行动。主流路线是训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，但作者认为编程智能体更有潜力实现通用具身操作，只需一个简单有效的机器人框架。

Method: 设计 RoboHarness，为 LLM 智能体提供视觉-几何控制面板，使其无需训练动作头或大规模 VLA，即可直接理解并调用具身操作任务。

Result: 搭载 RoboHarness 的 Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 在 BEHAVIOR Challenge 2025 任务上优于当前最先进的 VLA 模型。

Conclusion: 编程智能体结合机器人框架是实现通用具身操作的可行路径；RoboHarness 的实现将作为开源发布。

Abstract: The rise of large language models (LLMs) has opened a path toward generalist embodied AI, raising the question of how LLMs can act in the physical world. One straightforward approach is training the model to generate actions: collect robot-action data and video demonstration, refine LLM architecture with an action head, and train a vision-language-action (VLA) model at scale. However, we argue that coding agents have the potential to realize generalist embodied manipulation. All they need is a simple yet effective robot harness. We present RoboHarness: a robotic harness that gives LLM agents a visual-geometric control panel so that they can directly understand and invoke embodied tasks. With RoboHarness, Qwen3.8-Flash-Next outperforms state-of-the-art VLA models on BEHAVIOR Challenge 2025 tasks. The implementation will be released as open source.

</details>


### [22] [GNFormer: Gaussian Neural Field Transformer for Efficient Camera-based Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15522)
*Guangcheng Chen,Li He,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出高斯神经场GNF及GNFormer，用于基于相机的占据预测，在提升精度与推理速度的同时大幅减少高斯原语数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯的占据预测方法原语利用率低：无效训练目标使部分高斯留在空白区域，且高斯函数表达能力有限，难以建模复杂形状。

Method: 提出GNF，将体素位置映射为语义占据，由高斯与神经网络联合参数化：高斯定义空间支撑并提供特征供神经解码，神经网络增强占据建模表达能力；进一步提出GNFormer预测GNF。

Result: 在Occ-ScanNet、SSCBench-KITTI-360和nuScenes-SurroundOcc上，GNFormer在单帧方法中取得最高IoU和mIoU及最快推理；仅用256和1024个高斯即可表示室内和室外场景，原语数量显著少于先前高斯方法。

Conclusion: GNFormer验证了GNF在相机占据预测中的有效性，能以更少原语实现更强表达与高效推理，代码将开源。

Abstract: Camera-based occupancy prediction provides geometric and semantic scene understanding for robot perception and has been adopted in autonomous vehicle and UAV perception systems. Gaussian primitives offer a promising alternative by potentially representing only occupied regions, thereby concentrating computation and representational capacity where needed and reducing wasted computation and memory in empty space. However, existing Gaussian-based methods suffer from poor primitive utilization, as ineffective training objectives leave some Gaussians in empty space, while the limited expressiveness of Gaussian functions restricts their ability to model complex shapes. To address these limitations, we propose a novel 3D representation, Gaussian Neural Field (GNF), which can accurately map voxel positions to semantic occupancies. In GNF, Gaussians and a neural network jointly parameterize the field, where the Gaussians define its spatial support and provide features for neural decoding, while the neural network enables more expressive occupancy modeling than Gaussian functions alone. We further develop the Gaussian Neural Field Transformer (GNFormer), a streamlined and flexible method that predicts GNFs for camera-based occupancy prediction. Across Occ-ScanNet, SSCBench-KITTI-360, and nuScenes-SurroundOcc benchmarks, GNFormer achieves the highest IoU and mIoU and the fastest inference among the compared single-frame methods. In the experiments, GNFormer can represent indoor and outdoor scenes with only 256 and 1024 Gaussians, respectively, substantially fewer primitives than prior Gaussian-based methods. The code will be released.

</details>


### [23] [Phase-Gated Constrained Reinforcement Learning for End-to-End Capture and Detumbling of Non-Cooperative Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15491)
*Álvaro Belmonte-Baeza,Celia Redondo-Verdú,José L. Ramón,Miguel Cazorla,Leonard Felicetti,Jorge Pomares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种单一约束强化学习策略，使双臂自由飞行服务航天器在目标质量与惯量未知、状态仅含噪估计下，完成从自由空间接近、基于摩擦的无固定装置捕获到完全消旋的主动碎片清除全流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动碎片清除按阶段划分控制器，依赖目标惯量、专用抓持接口或在接触密集阶段脆弱的手工切换，难以应对非合作翻滚目标。

Method: 将任务建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，并在奖励、动作空间和约束集三处引入平滑接近门：奖励中隐式诱导阶段结构，动作空间中硬性排除“抓取-拖拽”退化利用，约束集中缓解跨阶段约束因负载不均导致的归一化失效。

Result: 在1000次随机试验中成功率达99.5%，全程满足接触力、执行器、基座反作用与碰撞约束；可零样本迁移至未见几何与质心偏移，并在目标质量十倍范围内外推。

Conclusion: 表明单一约束强化学习策略可替代阶段专用管线完成接触丰富的多阶段在轨操作，且所揭示的约束归一化失败与平滑接近门补救对同类多阶段问题具有通用性。

Abstract: Active debris removal requires a servicer spacecraft to approach a non-cooperative tumbling object, capture it, and arrest its rotation. This sequence is conventionally divided among phase-specific controllers, each depending on knowledge of the target's inertia, a dedicated grappling fixture, or a hand-designed switch at the contact-rich seam where such pipelines are most fragile. We show that a single constrained reinforcement learning policy can execute the entire mission, driving a dual-arm free-flying servicer from free-space closing through friction-based fixture-free capture to full detumbling of a target whose mass and inertia are unknown and whose state is observed only through noisy estimates. The enabling mechanism is a smooth proximity gate applied at three loci of a constrained Markov decision process. In the reward it induces the phase structure rather than imposing it; in the action space it forecloses a degenerate grab-and-pull exploit as a hard guarantee; and in the constraint set it resolves a normalization failure of constraint-based learning, in which a constraint spanning phases that load it unequally is rendered nearly inert in the phase where precision matters most. Both the failure mode and the remedy apply to any multi-phase problem of this kind. Over 1000 randomized trials the policy succeeds in 99.5% while holding its contact-force, actuator, base-reaction and collision limits throughout, transfers zero-shot to unseen geometries and centre-of-mass offsets, and extrapolates across a tenfold range of target mass.

</details>


### [24] [Learning Safety-Constrained Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation for Lunar Surface Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15486)
*Alvaro Belmonte-Baeza,Miguel Cazorla,Gabriel J. García,Carlos J. Pérez-Del-Pulgar,Jorge Pomares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向月面四足移动机械臂的约束强化学习框架，学习单一全身策略跟踪任务空间6D末端位姿，在月球模拟中实现厘米级/度级精度并显著减少软约束违反且无硬约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月面机器人操作需自主并保持自身完整性，因为失败不可恢复且无法持续人工监督。

Method: 采用约束强化学习训练四足移动机械臂的单一全身策略，跟踪任务空间6D末端位姿并在学习过程中施加操作限制；通过奖励几何而非显式门控实现移动与操作切换；引入基于引力-惯性倾角裕度（GIIM）的稳定性项以适配月球低重力动力学；给出基于终止型目标乘法结构的奖励正则化设计规则；在月球模拟环境中评估，覆盖三种地形形态、五个难度等级、月球重力，并使用JSC-1a模拟月壤校准的月壤接触特性，密度从松散到密实变化。

Result: 名义条件下平均位置误差3.22 cm、姿态误差6.83°；在不同月壤密度和具有操作代表性的地形难度范围内保持精度；相比无约束基线，软约束违反时间最多减少一个数量级，且无硬约束违反。

Conclusion: 该框架能在月面环境下实现精确、鲁棒且满足约束的四足移动操作，验证了约束强化学习与月面动力学奖励设计在不可恢复、无持续监督场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic systems for lunar surface operations must act autonomously and preserve their own integrity in an environment where failures are unrecoverable and continuous supervision is impossible. This paper presents a Constrained Reinforcement Learning framework for quadrupedal mobile manipulators that learns a single whole-body policy for tracking a 6D end-effector pose specified in task space, with operational limits enforced during learning through a constrained learning mechanism. We introduce a reward formulation in which the transition between locomotion and manipulation emerges from the reward geometry rather than from an explicit gating mechanism, a stability term derived from the Gravito-Inertial Inclination Margin and motivated by the reduced-gravity dynamics of the lunar surface, and a design rule for reward regularization that follows from the multiplicative structure of the termination-based objective. The framework is evaluated in a lunar-analog simulation spanning three terrain morphologies at five difficulty levels under lunar gravity, with regolith contact properties calibrated to the JSC-1a simulant and varied from loose to dense. The learned policy attains mean position and orientation errors of 3.22 cm and 6.83 deg. under nominal conditions, retains this precision across regolith densities and over the operationally representative range of terrain difficulty, and reduces soft-constraint violation time by up to an order of magnitude relative to an unconstrained baseline while incurring no hard-constraint violations.

</details>


### [25] [Exploring the Limits of Combinatorial Generalization in Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15425)
*Xupeng Zhang,Yantai Yang,Chang Guo,Zhaokai Yin,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究长时程具身操作中的技能重组问题，提出HSE诊断框架、RoboCombine基准与PRTI初始化策略，表明组合泛化依赖对交接状态鲁棒的原子技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型可执行原子技能，但长时程操作需要重新组合技能，且无法为每种技能组合提供演示。全指令训练在有限技能组合上表现尚可，但在未见重组上显著下降，说明模型可能记忆序列模板而非稳健重组技能。

Method: 提出HSE（Hierarchical Subtask Execution）诊断框架，通过规划器、触发器和策略共享的模块接口暴露技能重组，并将长时程失败分解为可分离模式。提出RoboCombine基准，在无组合技能训练轨迹下评估技能重组，使用有序成功率和子任务边界转移转化率等指标。识别失败集中在从先前子任务交接状态启动下游子任务时的起始状态差距。进一步提出PRTI（Pose Robust Task Initialization），一种轻量级原子任务后训练策略，扩展起始状态覆盖至规范起始状态之外。

Result: 受控研究中，全指令训练在覆盖组合上有序成功率为40.0%，但在重组上仅7.7%。RoboCombine表明原子能力本身不足以保证有序执行，失败集中于子任务边界交接。在RoboCombine上采用oracle timed HSE时，PRTI达到18.16%有序成功率，随机扰动为4.21%，机器人重置oracle为9.47%，且无需组合技能训练轨迹。

Conclusion: 组合泛化要求原子技能对交接状态鲁棒，并能将第一阶段进展转化为可靠的下游执行，而不仅是将长时程任务分解为已知子任务。

Abstract: In embodied manipulation, Vision Language Action (VLA) models can execute atomic skills, but long-horizon manipulation requires recombining these skills without demonstrations for every skill combination. The full instruction route treats skill chaining as long-horizon imitation. In a controlled study, full instruction training on limited skill combination sequences reaches 40.0% ordered success on covered combinations but only 7.7% on recombination, suggesting memorization of sequence templates rather than robust skill recombination. We introduce Hierarchical Subtask Execution (HSE), a diagnostic framework that exposes skill recombination through a shared modular interface over the planner, trigger, and policy, decomposing long-horizon failures into separable modes. We introduce RoboCombine, a benchmark for skill recombination without combined skill training trajectories, with metrics for ordered success and transition conversion across subtask boundaries. RoboCombine reveals that atomic competence alone does not ensure ordered execution. Failures concentrate at transitions where downstream subtasks launch from handoff states from preceding subtasks rather than canonical atomic starts. We identify this mismatch as the start state gap. To address it, we introduce Pose Robust Task Initialization (PRTI), a lightweight atomic task post-training strategy that broadens start state coverage beyond canonical starts. Under oracle timed HSE on RoboCombine, PRTI reaches 18.16% ordered success, compared with 4.21% under random perturbation and 9.47% under a robot-reset oracle, without combined skill training trajectories. These results show that combinatorial generalization requires handoff robust atomic skills that convert first stage progress into reliable downstream execution, not merely decomposition into known subtasks. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [26] [A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Safety-Aware Haptic Shared Control in Surgical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15422)
*Zihao Zhang,Karl Handwerker,Balint Varga*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将手术机器人触觉共享控制建模为双智能体协作马尔可夫博弈，在集中训练、分散执行下比较多智能体强化学习与固定阻抗/约束方法，评估几何安全选择对任务执行与力协调的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉共享控制在共享末端执行器上融合人与机器人作用力并保留操作者参与，但几何安全选择如何影响任务执行和力协调仍不清晰。

Method: 采用无模型多智能体强化学习，将交互建模为双智能体协作马尔可夫博弈，集中训练、分散执行；比较学习人类控制与任务调优固定阻抗基线；在常见几何任务定义下对比MADDPG、rMAPPO及epigraph约束方法，无需完整解析交互模型即可实现联合策略自适应。

Result: 仿真中rMAPPO总体回报与跟踪性能最高，epigraph方法动作更保守、几何暴露更低，几何迁移后未观察到碰撞或危险区域暴露。真实操作者输入下，固定阻抗训练策略更快且所有记录试验成功；与学习伙伴训练的策略命令对抗更大，固定epigraph条件命令对齐最好。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究触觉共享控制中人类控制表征与约束处理权衡提供了基准。

Abstract: Haptic shared control in surgical robotics combines human-side and robot-side forces at a shared end effector while preserving operator involvement. However, the effects of geometric safety choices on task execution and force coordination remain unclear. We model the interaction as a two-agent cooperative Markov game under centralized training and decentralized execution, comparing learned human control with a task-tuned fixed-impedance baseline. This model-free multi-agent reinforcement learning framework supports joint policy adaptation without requiring a complete analytical interaction model. It compares MADDPG and rMAPPO with an epigraph-constrained approach under common geometric task definitions. In simulation, rMAPPO generally achieved the highest return and tracking performance, whereas the epigraph approach produced more conservative motions and lower geometric exposure, including no observed collision or dangerous-region exposure after geometry transfer. With real operator input, fixed-impedance-trained policies were faster and successful in all recorded trials. Policies trained with a learned partner exhibited greater command opposition, while the fixed epigraph condition produced the most aligned commands. This work provides a benchmark for studying human-control representation and constraint-handling trade-offs in haptic shared control.

</details>


### [27] [Have You Seen It Clearly? Uncertainty-Guided Belief Refinement for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15375)
*Jingzhi Huang,Junkai Huang,Yuhang Peng,Keyu Mu,Jiaru Zhong,Yi Wang,Lujia Wang,Lap-Pui Chau,Siyang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出不确定性引导的建图框架RefBeliefMap与规划器RefBeliefNav，用于零样本目标导航：区分目标位置信念与空间/语义证据不确定性，联合评估前沿与历史路点，在HM3D、MP3D上取得零样本基线中最优性能，并在Unitree Go2四足机器人上验证了仿真到现实的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本目标导航（ZSON）要求智能体在未训练过的类别与环境里定位目标；现有方法虽借助大语言模型与视觉语言模型构建空间表示并引导探索，但两个问题仍未被充分解决：现有证据是否足以支撑导航决策，以及新证据如何改变已访问位置的搜索价值。

Method: 提出不确定性引导的建图框架RefBeliefMap，将目标位置信念与空间、语义证据不确定性区分开；在滑动记忆窗口上联合建模观测内不确定性与观测间分歧，评估两类证据通道的可靠性。当竞争前沿尚未被充分区分时，用不确定性估计引导主动观测与语义推理，在做出导航承诺前精炼支撑目标位置信念的证据。在精炼地图上提出RefBeliefNav，依据目标发现潜力、期望信息增益与导航代价联合评估未探索前沿与历史路点，使历史位置可因新证据而重新被考虑，协调探索与重访。

Result: 在HM3D与MP3D上，所提方法在评估的零样本基线中达到最优性能；消融实验考察了各模块贡献；在Unitree Go2四足机器人上的真实世界实验进一步验证了方法的仿真到现实迁移性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模证据不确定性并统一评估前沿与历史路点，RefBeliefMap与RefBeliefNav有效解决了证据充分性与重访价值更新问题，在零样本目标导航中取得领先结果并具备真实机器人可迁移性。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Object Navigation (ZSON) requires an agent to locate target objects in unfamiliar environments without additional training for the test categories or environments. Recent approaches leverage large language models and vision-language models to construct spatial representations and guide exploration. However, whether the available evidence sufficiently supports a navigation choice, and how new evidence changes the search value of previously visited locations, remain insufficiently addressed. We propose RefBeliefMap, an uncertainty-guided mapping framework that distinguishes target-location belief from spatial and semantic evidence uncertainty. By jointly modeling intra-observation uncertainty and inter-observation disagreement over a sliding memory window, RefBeliefMap assesses the reliability of both evidence channels. When competing frontiers remain insufficiently resolved, these uncertainty estimates guide active observation and semantic reasoning to refine the evidence supporting target-location beliefs before navigation commitment. Building on the refined map, we introduce RefBeliefNav, which jointly evaluates unexplored frontiers and historical waypoints according to their target-finding potential, expected information gain, and navigation cost. This unified objective allows historical locations to be reconsidered as new evidence changes their search value, coordinating exploration and revisitation. Experiments on HM3D and MP3D demonstrate state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated zero-shot baselines, with ablation studies examining the contributions of modules. Real-world experiments on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further demonstrate the sim-to-real transferability of our method.

</details>


### [28] [LERR-Nav: Learning to Coordinate Exploration, Recovery, and Reminiscing for Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15362)
*Jingzhi Huang,Junkai Huang,Yuhang Peng,Keyu Mu,Hailong Huang,Haoyang Yang,Renbo Luo,Lujia Wang,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LERR-Nav 是一个面向 unseen object navigation 的框架，通过学习到的高层策略协调探索、恢复与回忆等导航策略，并在 HM3D 和 MP3D 上取得 state-of-the-art 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 unseen environments 中进行 object navigation 仍具挑战，要求 agent 对新颖布局泛化视觉与空间推理。尽管 LLM/VLM 提升了 semantic mapping、spatial memory 和 waypoint selecting，但成功导航还需识别停滞进度、摆脱无效行为并重访被忽略的目标线索。这需要专门导航策略以及协调其执行的高层策略；作者受失败 navigation episodes 分析启发提出该框架。

Method: LERR-Nav 包含四种互补导航策略：value-function-guided exploration、LLM-guided exploration、针对停滞或重复行为的 recovery、以及 memory-guided reminiscing。为支持 history-aware coordination，维护 execution memory，保存所有策略执行的文本摘要及地图序列滑动窗口。高层 vision-language agent 由 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 初始化，并采用两阶段 curriculum 训练：第一阶段用 map-centric supervised fine-tuning 建立空间 grounding；第二阶段在 variable-length navigation segments 上进行 SFT，基于当前地图和 hybrid execution memory 学习下一步调用哪个导航策略。

Result: 在 HM3D 和 MP3D 上的实验显示 state-of-the-art navigation performance，ablation studies 验证了各单独组件的贡献。

Conclusion: LERR-Nav 通过历史感知的高层协调策略整合 exploration、recovery 与 reminiscing，有效应对 unseen object navigation，并表明策略协调与执行记忆对成功导航具有重要作用。

Abstract: Object Navigation in unseen environments remains a fundamental challenge in embodied AI, as it requires agents to generalize visual and spatial reasoning to novel layouts. While recent advances in large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) have enhanced semantic mapping, spatial memory, and waypoint selecting for navigation, successful navigation requires more than selecting the next promising waypoint. A robot must also recognize stalled progress, escape unproductive behaviors, and revisit overlooked target cues. Addressing these challenges requires both specialized navigation strategies and a high-level policy that coordinates their execution. Motivated by the analysis of failed navigation episodes, we propose LERR-Nav, an object navigation framework that coordinates exploration, recovery, and reminiscing through a learned high-level policy. The framework comprises four complementary navigation strategies: value-function-guided exploration, LLM-guided exploration, recovery from stalled or repetitive behavior, and memory-guided reminiscing. To support history-aware coordination, we maintain the execution memory that preserves textual summaries of all strategy executions alongside a sliding window of map sequences. To learn the coordination policy, we initialize a high-level vision-language agent from Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct and train it through a two-stage curriculum. The first stage uses map-centric supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to establish spatial grounding. The second stage performs SFT on variable-length navigation segments to learn which navigation strategy to invoke next based on current maps and the hybrid execution memory. Experiments on HM3D and MP3D demonstrate state-of-the-art navigation performance, while ablation studies validate the contributions of individual components.

</details>


### [29] [CoLA: Multi-VLA Coordination via Learned Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15337)
*Ahaskar Kashyap,Jiawen Zong,Boyu Ma,Chuhao Zhou,Jingliang Li,Stefano V. Albrecht,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CoLA：在冻结各智能体 VLA 主干的前提下，训练轻量消息通道和每臂扩散动作头，实现完全去中心化的多智能体协调，并系统验证显式通信何时必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型通常作为孤立单智能体策略部署；当多个此类智能体共享工作空间时，可通过观察彼此隐式协调或通过消息交换显式协调，但显式通信何时成为必要仍不明确。

Method: CoLA 保持每个智能体的 VLA 主干冻结，仅在其上训练轻量消息通道和每臂扩散动作头，并以完全去中心化方式执行。

Result: 在遮挡托盘交接任务中，盒子颜色标记仅一个智能体可见，CoLA 成功率为 76.0%，而训练全程将消息置零时为 1.3%。在双臂和双臂交接任务中，使用共享俯拍摄像头时移除消息无明显影响：双臂 90.6% vs. 88.3%，三臂 63.5% vs. 60.0%；但在双臂腕部仅可见变体中，移除消息使成功率从 74.8% 降至 64.3%。该架构可泛化到不同 VLA 主干：在相同遮挡托盘任务中，冻结 π0.5 主干的 CoLA 总体成功率与 LoRA 微调基线相当（37.3% vs. 34.7%），但可训练参数仅为其一小部分。CoLA 放置盒子时选择正确托盘的概率为 71.8%，而独立微调基线因放置臂看不到标记，仅达随机水平（34.7% vs. 33.3%）。

Conclusion: 显式通信仅在智能体拥有私有信息时才带来显著收益；若存在共享观测，隐式协调已足够。CoLA 能跨 VLA 主干泛化，以极少可训练参数实现有效协调，并在需要私有信息传递的放置决策中显著优于独立微调基线。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically deployed as isolated single-agent policies. When several such agents share a workspace, they can coordinate implicitly, by observing one another, or explicitly, by exchanging messages, but it remains unclear when explicit communication becomes necessary. We introduce CoLA (Coordination via Learned Adapters), which keeps each agent's VLA backbone frozen and trains a lightweight message channel and a per-arm diffusion action head on top, with fully decentralised execution. Across three tasks that differ in how much each agent can observe, the channel matters only when information is private. On an occluded tray handover, where the box's colour marker is visible to one agent only, CoLA reaches 76.0% success versus 1.3% with messages zeroed throughout training. On two-arm and three-arm handover tasks, removing messages has no measurable effect with a shared overhead camera (90.6% vs. 88.3% at two arms; 63.5% vs. 60.0% at three), but lowers success on the two-arm task's wrist-only variant (74.8% vs. 64.3%). This architecture generalises across different VLA backbones as well. With a frozen $\pi_{0.5}$ backbone on the same occluded tray task, CoLA matches the overall success rate of a LoRA-fine-tuned baseline (37.3% vs. 34.7%) using a fraction of the trainable parameters. When CoLA places the box, it selects the correct tray 71.8% of the time, whereas the independently fine-tuned baseline, whose placing arm never sees the marker, selects it at chance level (34.7% against 33.3%).

</details>


### [30] [An Open MuJoCo Simulation Framework for a Quadrotor Carrying a Tendon-Driven Soft Continuum Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15308)
*Haitham El-Hussieny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于 MuJoCo 的开源仿真框架，用于四旋翼下挂腱驱动软连续体机械臂，并验证其建模、控制与动力学耦合特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人与软连续体机械臂结合可实现安全、柔顺的环境交互，但自由飞行基座与高度变形、欠驱动机械臂之间的耦合动力学难以在没有合适仿真器时研究。

Method: 基于 MuJoCo 构建开源仿真框架；软臂建模为12段伪刚体链，由正交柔顺铰链连接；3根腱间隔120°布置并穿过每段，实现任意方向弯曲；采用 SE(3) 几何跟踪控制器控制飞行器，旋翼分配矩阵由模型描述直接生成；使用闭式腱张力分配，保证张力限幅且各方向弯矩一致；报告完整建模选择与参数。

Result: 在四个仿真场景中评估：36 s 八字轨迹与腱驱动末端扫掠、姿态增益研究、弯曲工作空间准静态表征、臂端脉冲扰动。标称增益下位置 RMS 误差为3.1 cm，臂端摆幅距体轴达37 cm；低带宽姿态环会让机械臂激发持续振荡；在笔记本 CPU 上约达15倍实时速度。

Conclusion: 该框架能够复现并研究空中软连续体机械臂的耦合动力学，且所有模型、控制器和用于复现图表的脚本均已开源发布。

Abstract: Combining aerial robots with soft continuum manipulators promises safe, compliant interaction with the environment, but the coupled dynamics of a free-flying base and a highly deformable, underactuated arm are difficult to study without a suitable simulator. This paper describes an open-source simulation framework, built on the MuJoCo physics engine, for a quadrotor that carries a tendon-driven soft continuum arm underneath its body. The arm is modelled as a pseudo-rigid-body chain of twelve segments joined by pairs of orthogonal compliant hinges. Three tendons, spaced \SI{120}{\degree} apart and routed through every segment, bend it in any direction. A geometric tracking controller on $SE(3)$ flies the vehicle; its rotor allocation matrix is generated directly from the model description. A closed-form tendon-tension allocation keeps every tension within its limits and gives the same bending moment in every direction. We report the full set of modelling choices and parameters, and we evaluate the framework in four simulated scenarios: a \SI{36}{\second} mission with a figure-eight and a tendon-driven tip sweep, an attitude-gain study, a quasi-static characterization of the bending workspace, and an impulsive disturbance applied to the arm tip. With the nominal gains the vehicle tracks the mission with \SI{3.1}{\centi\meter} RMS position error while the arm tip swings up to \SI{37}{\centi\meter} from the body axis. The gain study shows that a low-bandwidth attitude loop lets the arm excite sustained oscillations. The simulation runs about $15\times$ faster than real time on a laptop CPU. All models, controllers and scripts that reproduce the figures are released.

</details>


### [31] [RM-WAM: Reliable Memory-Augmented World Action Models for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15305)
*Cui Miao,Junhao Yu,Ping Liu,Ning Yang,Xuhua Chen,He Zheng,Xiaodong Wang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RM-WAM：一种可靠记忆增强的世界动作模型，通过选择性地利用历史记忆来提升长时程机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型主要基于当前观测做决策，在长时程任务中，视觉相似状态可能对应不同交互历史与任务阶段，导致可靠性不足；而直接引入历史观测又会带来过时、阶段不匹配或错误污染的信息，造成世界预测不准和闭环误差累积。

Method: RM-WAM将记忆视为上下文相关的预测资源，而非普遍有用的历史上下文。它用短期视觉状态记忆和潜在事件记忆分别捕捉近期交互动态与长期状态转移；采用反事实记忆学习，在固定当前输入和预测目标的同时改变可访问历史，学习历史记忆的上下文相关效用估计；再通过效用感知门控机制自适应控制记忆注入世界-动作主干。

Result: 实验表明，RM-WAM在长时程任务中凸显了可靠且选择性记忆利用的重要性，并验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: RM-WAM说明记忆不应被无差别地使用，而应作为上下文相关的预测资源进行选择性注入，从而提升长时程机器人操作的可靠性与闭环稳定性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robotic manipulation by jointly predicting future visual states and actions, yet most existing methods make decisions primarily from the current observation. This limits their reliability in long-horizon tasks, where visually similar states may correspond to different interaction histories and task stages. Moreover, directly incorporating historical observations can introduce stale, stage-mismatched, or error-contaminated information, leading to inaccurate world prediction and accumulated closed-loop errors. We propose RM-WAM, a reliable memory-augmented World Action Model that treats memory as a context-dependent predictive resource rather than universally useful historical context. RM-WAM represents history using complementary short-term visual state memory and latent event memory, which respectively capture recent interaction dynamics and longer-term state transitions. A counterfactual memory learning scheme varies the accessible history while keeping the current inputs and prediction targets fixed, enabling the model to learn a context-dependent utility estimate for historical memory. Based on this estimate, a utility-aware gating mechanism adaptively controls memory injection into the world-action backbone. Experiments demonstrate that RM-WAM highlights the importance of reliable and selective memory utilization in long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [32] [ProWorld: Multi-View World Model with Proprioceptive Feedback for Closed-Loop Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15294)
*Zijun Wang,Tianyi Yan,Haokun Lin,Panwen Hu,Bohang He,Siyu Ren,Xiaoyu Guo,Weijia Liufu,Shuoshuo Xue,Fengjiao Chen,Zipeng Ye,Xuezhi Cao,Zutao Jiang,Hanhui Li,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ProWorld 动作条件世界模型，联合预测多视角视频与机器人状态并闭环回馈冻结策略，用于评估操作策略，其生成成功率与真实机器人高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过真实机器人反复物理试验评估操作策略成本高昂；世界模型作为闭环测试环境时，不仅要生成逼真视频，还需返回策略下一步动作所需的相机视角和本体状态，并保留失败动作结果，以避免高估策略能力。

Method: ProWorld 联合预测多视角视频和机器人状态，并将二者反馈给冻结策略进行重复交互；模型包含共享 transformer 用于联合视频-状态流预测，以及 Shared-Time View Encoding 用于区分不同相机并保持时间同步。为减少交互幻觉（如漏抓被误判为成功提升），训练中引入高保真仿真收集的失败交互进行增强，以降低生成环境中的虚高成功率。

Result: ProWorld 在 TriWorldBench 上取得对比方法中最高的总体得分；在三种策略和四个局部操作任务上，生成成功率与真实成功率之间的 Pearson 相关系数为 0.95，Spearman 相关系数为 0.94。

Conclusion: 结果表明，联合视觉-本体反馈和失败感知训练可作为基于世界模型的策略评估基础。

Abstract: Evaluating robot manipulation policies through repeated physical trials is costly. We study whether an action-conditioned world model can serve as a closed-loop test environment whose policy success rates track those on real robots. Beyond realistic video, the model must return the camera views and proprioceptive state needed for the policy's next action and preserve failed-action outcomes to avoid overestimating policy capability. We introduce ProWorld, a world model that jointly predicts multi-view video and robot state, then feeds both back to a frozen policy for repeated interaction. ProWorld comprises a shared transformer for joint video-state flow prediction and Shared-Time View Encoding to distinguish cameras while preserving temporal synchronization. To reduce interaction hallucinations that turn missed grasps into successful lifts, we augment training with failed interactions collected in high-fidelity simulation, reducing inflated success rates in the generated environment. ProWorld achieves the highest overall TriWorldBench score among the compared methods. Across three policies and four local manipulation tasks, it attains a Pearson correlation of 0.95 and a Spearman correlation of 0.94 between generated and real success rates. These results support joint visual-proprioceptive feedback and failure-aware training as a basis for world-model-based policy evaluation.

</details>


### [33] [Embodiment-Aware Inference-Time Search for Human Reaction Selection in Humanoid Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15274)
*Yongpeng Cao,Masahiro Hirano,Yuji Yamakawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种推理时反应搜索框架：从冻结的生成式反应模型随机采样多个候选，重定向到目标人形机器人，并联合评估交互质量与本体可行性，从而在不重训练的情况下适配不同本体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人学习人类行为需要昂贵的数据采集，人-机器人交互数据更难获取；为每个本体训练专用反应模型也耗时耗资源。人-人形动作重定向可利用人-人反应数据作为通用运动表示，但人机形态与运动学差异会使人类运动空间中的优选反应未必适合特定机器人本体。

Method: 给定观测到的交互，利用生成式反应模型进行随机采样以生成多个合理反应候选；将每个候选重定向到目标人形机器人；使用互补的交互质量与本体特定可行性准则进行评估，并在目标本体物理约束下选择候选。由于反应生成器保持冻结，框架可在推理时适配不同人形本体而无需重训练。

Result: 实验表明，该框架在保持整体人类反应质量的同时，提升了跨不同本体的人形重定向反应可行性；重定向可行性提升1.61%。此外，人类空间与G1的最佳候选选择在78.98%的样本中不一致，表明存在显著的本体依赖性。

Conclusion: 推理时反应搜索可有效缓解人-人形重定向中的本体差异问题，在冻结生成器的条件下实现跨本体适配，并说明反应选择中本体特定可行性的重要性。代码将开源。

Abstract: Training humanoid robots to learn human behaviors requires expensive data collection, and human-robot interaction data can be even more difficult and costly to collect. Training a specialized reaction model for each humanoid embodiment can also be time-consuming and resource-intensive. Human-to-humanoid motion retargeting provides a more scalable alternative, allowing human-human reaction data to serve as a common motion representation for different humanoid embodiments. However, due to morphological and kinematic differences between humans and humanoid robots, a reaction preferred in human motion space may not be the most suitable one for execution on a particular robot embodiment. In this work, we propose an inference-time reaction search framework that leverages stochastic sampling from a generative reaction model and jointly considers reaction quality and humanoid embodiment feasibility. Given the observed interaction, our framework generates multiple plausible reaction candidates. Each candidate is retargeted to the target humanoid and evaluated using complementary interaction and embodiment-specific feasibility criteria, allowing candidate selection under the physical constraints of the target embodiment. Since the reaction generator remains frozen, the framework can adapt to different humanoid embodiments at inference time without retraining. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework maintains overall human reaction quality while improving the feasibility of retargeted humanoid reactions across different embodiments. The proposed pipeline improves humanoid retargeting feasibility by 1.61%. Moreover, human-space and G1 best-candidate selections disagree in 78.98% of samples, demonstrating substantial embodiment dependence. Code will be released.

</details>


### [34] [Reinforcement Learning Based Heterogeneous Task Allocation Under Spatio-Temporal Environmental Uncertainty for Multi-Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15237)
*Xi Lin,Yaxuan Li,Paul Szenher,Brendan Englot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作面向海上扰动下异构自主水面艇（ASV）的多机器人任务分配问题，提出一种去中心化强化学习策略网络，并在仿真中取得最低 makespan 且可扩展至 100 台机器人、400 个任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来 ASV 被广泛用于海上任务，但异构任务、不同出现时间以及海上扰动带来的未知随机时空不确定性，使可靠且可适应的任务分配策略成为必要。

Method: 将问题建模为异构能力 ASV 团队在不同类型与动态出现任务下的多机器人任务分配问题；提出去中心化 RL 策略网络，训练其对不确定性导致的观测偏差具有鲁棒性，并支持个体决策以实现面向异构任务需求的高效执行行为。

Result: 在改变任务类型比例、任务数量、机器人种类及每类机器人数量的仿真实验中，与四种基线方法相比，所提策略获得最低 makespan，并可部署于 100 个机器人和 400 个任务的场景。

Conclusion: 去中心化鲁棒 RL 策略能够有效应对海上扰动与异构任务需求，在多机器人任务分配中优于基线方法，并展现出大规模场景的可扩展性。

Abstract: Autonomous Surface Vehicles (ASVs) have been increasingly deployed to perform different kinds of maritime tasks in recent years, which motivates the need for a reliable task allocation policy that can adapt to heterogeneous tasks and maritime disturbances. In this work, we formulate a multi-robot task allocation problem in which a team of ASVs with heterogeneous capabilities needs to complete tasks of different types and appearance times under unknown stochastic spatio-temporal uncertainties from maritime disturbances. We propose a decentralized Reinforcement Learning (RL) based policy network that is trained to be robust towards uncertainty-induced observation deviations, and is able to perform individual decision making that leads to efficient execution behaviors toward heterogeneous task requirements. We evaluate the proposed policy and four baseline methods in simulated experiments with varied proportions of different tasks types, numbers of tasks, robot species and robots per species. Experiment results show that our proposed policy achieves the lowest makespan and can be deployed to address scenarios with 100 robots and 400 tasks.

</details>


### [35] [nanoflann: A Header-Only KD-Tree Library for Exact Nearest-Neighbor Search on Compile-Time Product Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15215)
*José Luis Blanco-Claraco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个仅头文件、无依赖的 C++ KD-tree 库，支持在编译期声明的乘积流形上进行精确最近邻搜索，并提供面向高动态数据的增量自平衡单树索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人中大量状态空间并非欧氏空间：方向位于 SO(2)/SO(3)，位姿位于 SE(2)/SE(3)。若直接对原始坐标使用欧氏 KD-tree，会因环绕和双覆盖等问题返回错误邻居。

Method: 通过单一类型别名在编译期组合基础流形（R^n、SO(2)、SO(3)、S^n，以及 SE(2)、SE(3)、环面等乘积），自动合成可证明可采纳的度量；对圆形坐标进行环绕感知处理，对单位四元数进行双覆盖感知处理。还提出增量、自平衡的单树索引，适用于滑动窗口 LiDAR 地图或 SE(3) 轨迹等高变动场景，具有有界内存、更快更新，并可选异步尾延迟限制模式。实现开源。

Result: 实验验证了精确性：在同一乘积度量下召回率为 1.0，与穷举扫描一致；而欧氏原始坐标捷径在 m 个旋转块时召回率约降至 0.5^m。相比暴力搜索，速度提升最多三个数量级。增量索引在更新速度上优于先前工作，且内存有界。

Conclusion: 该工作为机器人非欧氏状态空间提供了精确、高效、易集成的最近邻搜索与动态索引方案，并已开源。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor (NN) search underlies countless robotics algorithms, but many state spaces are not Euclidean: orientations live on $SO(2)$ or $SO(3)$ and poses on $SE(2)$ or $SE(3)$, and a Euclidean KD-tree on their raw coordinates returns wrong neighbors. We present a header-only, dependency-free C++ KD-tree library for exact NN search on user-defined product manifolds declared at compile time: the space is composed from base manifolds ($\mathbb{R}^n$, $SO(2)$, $SO(3)$, $S^n$; products $SE(2)$, $SE(3)$, tori) with a single type alias, and the library synthesizes a metric whose pruning is provably admissible, wrap-aware for circular coordinates and double-cover-aware for unit quaternions. Experiments confirm exactness (recall $1.0$ vs an exhaustive scan under the same product metric), where the Euclidean-on-raw shortcut collapses to recall $\approx 0.5^{m}$ for $m$ rotation blocks, with up to three orders of magnitude speedup over brute force. We further contribute an incremental, self-balancing single-tree index for points under heavy churn (e.g. sliding-window LiDAR maps) or $SE(3)$ trajectories: bounded memory and faster updates than prior work, with an optional asynchronous tail-latency-bounding mode. The implementation is open source: this https URL

</details>


### [36] [RootSLAM: Square Root Bundle Adjustment for Visual-Inertial SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15157)
*Tomás Santucci,Mateo de Mayo,Daniel Cremers,Taihú Pire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RootSLAM 是一个基于 Basalt 的多相机视觉惯性 SLAM 系统，通过平方根束调整的新重表述实现最高 20 倍加速，同时保持与当前最优方法相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人、混合现实、自动驾驶和无人机等小型低功耗设备需要高效感知，并依赖 SLAM 估计自身在环境中的运动。学习型 SLAM 精度高但计算需求大，而经典几何方法效率优势明显且精度损失很小，因此需要兼顾精度与效率的多相机视觉惯性 SLAM。

Method: 基于 Basalt 构建全功能多相机视觉惯性 SLAM 系统；将 SfM 中的平方根 bundle adjustment 适配到多相机视觉惯性 SLAM 场景，利用系统位姿图提出新重表述，使全局优化器可在单精度浮点下运行，从而将性能翻倍。系统包含地图管理、回环检测、位姿图优化器和全局建图组件。

Result: 精度与当前最优方法相当，运行速度最高快 20 倍；论文提供了与当前最优方法的全面基准测试以及对各项贡献的消融研究。

Conclusion: RootSLAM 有助于在资源受限环境中部署感知系统，其新实现将开源。

Abstract: Efficient perception is a prerequisite for the small, low-power devices targeted by robotics, mixed reality, autonomous driving, and drone applications, all of which rely on Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) to estimate their motion within their surroundings. Learning-based SLAM systems achieve state-of-the-art accuracy but are demanding in compute, whereas classical geometry-based methods retain a strong efficiency advantage with little to no loss in accuracy. In this work we present RootSLAM, a fully-featured visual-inertial SLAM system for multi-camera setups built on Basalt. RootSLAM achieves accuracy competitive with the state of the art while running up to 20 times faster, facilitating the deployment of perception systems in resource constrained environments. We adapt the square-root bundle adjustment formulation from structure from motion into the multi-camera visual-inertial SLAM setting through a novel reformulation that leverages the system's pose-graph, letting the global optimizer run in single-precision floating point, thus doubling its performance. We provide a thorough benchmark against the state of the art together with an ablation study of our contributions. Our new implementation, including its map management and loop detection module, pose-graph optimizer, and global mapping components, will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [37] [ChunkAct: Action Chunking for Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18874)
*Bingxin Xu,Wenrui Bao,Xinxin Liu,Jiawei Zhou,Yuzhang Shang,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ChunkAct 将动作分块引入智能体机器人，把记忆离线编译为可自主执行的基元链，仅在失败时交回 LLM 规划，从而将 LLM 计算从随基元数增长变为随决策数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在机器人控制环中组合感知、运动与 VLA 工具，虽能免训练泛化到新任务，但每个基元都需基于完整交互历史进行一轮 LLM 推理，导致开销随基元数增长；而已选子目标后的基元序列往往由记忆预设，推理大量冗余。

Method: ChunkAct 引入动作分块：在线时一次 LLM 调用启动一串链式基元，分块自主执行，仅在失败时返回控制权；离线时将记忆编译为这些分块，使测试时执行无需记忆，并把 LLM 规划器保留给真正需要推理的决策。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Pro 上，ChunkAct 相较智能体机器人 SOTA 将任务成功率提升 5.0 个百分点，同时减少 2.9 倍 LLM 调用、4.4 倍输入 token 和 2.3 倍输出 token。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过动作分块与记忆编译可显著降低智能体机器人的 LLM 推理开销并提升任务成功率，使 LLM 计算随决策数而非基元数扩展，为高效通用机器人控制提供可行路径。

Abstract: Agentic robotics places a large language model (LLM) agent in the robot control loop, where it composes perception, motion, and vision-language-action (VLA) tools under the guidance of a memory and thereby generalizes to new tasks without retraining. This generality comes at a steep price in efficiency. Every primitive requires a round of LLM reasoning over the full interaction history, so the cost of an episode grows with the number of primitives. We find this reasoning largely redundant: once a sub-goal is chosen, its primitives follow a routine already prescribed by memory. We present ChunkAct, which introduces action chunking to agentic robotics. Online, a single call launches a chunk of chained primitives, which executes autonomously and returns control only upon failure. Offline, ChunkAct compiles the memory into these chunks, so execution is memory-free at test time and the planner is reserved for decisions that require reasoning. LLM computation therefore scales with the number of decisions rather than the number of primitives. On LIBERO-Pro, ChunkAct improves task success by 5.0 points over the state of the art in agentic robotics while requiring 2.9x fewer LLM calls, 4.4x fewer input tokens, and 2.3x fewer output tokens.

</details>


### [38] [LoopWAM: Closed-Loop Generalist Policy Improvement from Deployment Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18873)
*Wenxuan Guo,Yingping Liang,Zhaoguo Wang,Ziyuan Li,Angyuan Ma,Zhenyu Wu,Chengkun Wang,Bingyao Yu,Ze Chen,Borui Zhang,Xiuwei Xu,Meng Zhang,Erjin Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LoopWAM 是一种闭环世界-动作模型，可直接利用部署中产生的成功、次优与失败混合质量经验，通过质量引导与并行去噪实现机器人策略的持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅扩展专家示范无法让通用机器人策略从部署中持续提升；真实 rollout 含成功、次优和失败，模仿全部动作会强化不良行为，过滤失败又丢失物理交互及其后果的证据。

Method: 提出 LoopWAM 闭环 world-action model，使用自身及其他部署策略的 rollout。采用来源相关噪声采样统一行为学习与动作条件世界建模；质量引导与质量加权监督在保留全质量视频监督的同时调节动作模仿；视频与动作 token 在并行去噪中交互，使学到的后果直接指导动作生成。

Result: 在 RoboChallenge Table30-V2 上，3轮部署-训练将成功率从23.67%提升至43.33%，提升19.7个百分点，最终得分56.80；在 RoboTwin 2.0 平均成功率94.5%，RoboCasa365 总体成功率49.5%，无需额外具身预训练，在三个基准上总体结果最佳。在衬衫折叠任务上，单任务后训练后经2轮部署驱动改进，成功率从13.20%升至55.56%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，LoopWAM 能在多任务学习与单任务后训练场景下，从混合质量经验中实现闭环持续改进。

Abstract: Scaling expert demonstrations alone does not enable generalist robot policies to improve from deployment. Real-world rollouts contain successes, suboptimal behavior, and failures: imitating every recorded action can reinforce poor behavior, while filtering out failures discards evidence of physical interactions and their consequences. We introduce \textbf{LoopWAM}, a closed-loop world-action model that learns directly from this full quality spectrum, using rollouts from both itself and other deployed policies. Source-dependent noise sampling unifies behavior learning and action-conditioned world modeling, while quality steering and quality-weighted supervision retain full video supervision across qualities and regulate action imitation. Video and action tokens interact throughout parallel denoising, allowing learned consequences to inform action generation directly. On RoboChallenge Table30-V2, three deployment-and-training rounds raise success from 23.67\% to 43.33\%, a 19.7-percentage-point gain, with a final score of 56.80. LoopWAM also achieves 94.5\% average success on RoboTwin~2.0 and 49.5\% overall success on RoboCasa365 without additional embodied pretraining, obtaining the best overall results among the compared methods across all three benchmarks. On challenging shirt folding task, two rounds of deployment-driven improvement after single-task post-training raise success from 13.20\% to 55.56\%. These results demonstrate closed-loop improvement from mixed-quality experience under both multi-task learning and single-task post-training. \href{ this https URL }{\textcolor{orange}{Project Page}}

</details>


### [39] [EgoExplorer: A Vision-Motion-Touch Observatory for Human Loco-Manipulation In Situ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18872)
*Yunsong Zhou,Xuekun Jiang,Haiqi Shen,Hangxu Liu,Chaojian Shi,Yujie Liu,Jingyuan Li,Haoran Yang,Jiafei Cao,Dongzhan Zhou,Dong Wang,Yao Mu,Chunhua Shen,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EgoExplorer：一个基于可穿戴系统的 100 小时视觉-运动-触觉观测数据集，聚焦专业活动中的人类移动操作，并对齐自我中心视频、全身运动、手指关节与触觉，配以步骤级描述；同时提出 Calibrate-Complete-Constrain 流水线，以稀疏身体传感实现高精度运动重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类演示学习人形移动操作，需要理解运动、姿态与物体交互如何随任务共同演化；跨多样环境扩展采集又需要轻量便携系统。现有数据集只能提供这种协调性的部分视图，缺乏同时覆盖全身运动、精细手部与触觉的连续记录。

Method: 构建可穿戴采集系统并发布 EgoExplorer 数据集，包含 100 小时连续记录，对齐自我中心视频、重建全身运动、细粒度手指关节、触觉观测与步骤级描述。为在稀疏身体传感下实现覆盖，提出 Calibrate-Complete-Constrain 流水线：先通过可复用校准固定参与者几何与传感器安装，以解耦安装偏移与关节运动；再用时间先验补全未观测关节运动；最后以分层约束促进测量一致性、时间连续性与几何一致性。

Result: 以 tracker 拟合参考为基准，该方法相对最强基线将身体点误差降低 71.9%；匹配评估还显示手部稳定性与接触估计有所提升。初步研究探索了多模态生成与条件控制，以及在仿真中结合全身控制的人形重定向。

Conclusion: EgoExplorer 为人类移动操作提供了更完整的视觉-运动-触觉观测，其采集与重建流水线可在稀疏传感条件下获得更准确、稳定的全身与手部运动及接触信息，并为多模态生成、条件建模和仿真中人形重定向与全身控制提供数据与方法基础。

Abstract: Learning humanoid loco-manipulation from human demonstrations rests on observing how locomotion, posture, and object interaction evolve together as tasks unfold. Scaling collection across diverse environments, in turn, favors lightweight, portable capture systems. Yet, existing datasets provide only partial views of this coordination. Thus, we introduce EgoExplorer, a 100-hour vision-motion-touch observatory of human loco-manipulation built with a wearable system. With an emphasis on professional activities, its continuous recordings align egocentric video with reconstructed whole-body motion, fine-grained finger articulation, and tactile observations, accompanied by step-level descriptions. To achieve this coverage with sparse body sensing, we develop the Calibrate-Complete-Constrain pipeline. Concretely, reusable calibration fixes participant geometry and sensor attachments before motion inference to disentangle mounting offsets from articulation. A temporal prior then completes unobserved joint motion, while layered constraints promote measurement agreement, temporal continuity, and geometric consistency, properties that motion plausibility alone does not guarantee. Against tracker-fitted references, the method reduces body-point error by 71.9% relative to the strongest baseline; matched evaluations also show gains in hand stability and contact estimation. Preliminary studies explore multimodal generation and conditioning alongside humanoid retargeting with whole-body control in simulation.

</details>


### [40] [What Matters in Quality-Aware VLA Pretraining: A Benchmark and Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18869)
*Mingxuan Yan,Litian Gong,Dihong Huang,Zhixu Li,Tianyu Zhang,Wenqian Zhang,Hefeifei Jiang,Peihao Li,Jian Zhang,Mengfei Zhao,Yikai Tang,Hai Zhai,Zehao Wang,Ruijian Liang,Yanjia Huang,Lin Shao,Changliu Liu,Kaiyu Hang,Zhiwen Fan,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jianfei Yang,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究探讨VLA预训练中如何利用演示质量（加权、过滤、条件化）以提升目标任务微调后的性能，并提出AXIS-Bench进行可控比较；发现approach-aware episode conditioning优于普通行为克隆，标签粒度与质量标签用法存在模型依赖，仿真预训练可将真实任务成功率从9.6%提升至31.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示天然次优，因此需要明确在VLA预训练中应如何利用演示质量信息，并在匹配演示数量与训练预算的条件下公平比较不同质量使用策略。

Method: 使用5,000个自然次优人类演示和24个目标任务，在三个VLA上比较加权、过滤与条件化，覆盖不同奖励定义和标签粒度；进一步考察预训练数据规模及质量标签在微调与推理阶段的作用。提出AXIS-Bench：基于众包仿真演示，记录物体与接触状态，可在共享轨迹上重算质量标签；轨迹可回放，支持工作空间对齐渲染和用户自定义真实目标任务。

Result: approach-aware episode conditioning在平均成功率上优于普通行为克隆；条件化与过滤平均分别偏好episode标签和chunk标签，但存在模型依赖的反转；条件化预训练后，常数标签微调可提升平均成功率，且推理时无论有标签或无标签均有效。真实实验中，仿真预训练将成功率从9.6%提升到31.7%。

Conclusion: 演示质量应通过合适粒度的条件化、过滤或微调标签策略加以利用；episode级approach-aware条件化较稳健，但最优策略依赖模型。AXIS-Bench支持受控比较，仿真预训练能显著迁移到真实目标任务。

Abstract: How should demonstration quality be used during vision-language-action (VLA) pretraining to improve performance after target-task fine-tuning? We compare weighting, filtering, and conditioning across reward definitions and label granularities using 5,000 naturally suboptimal human demonstrations and 24 target tasks. Within each VLA, we match demonstrations and training budgets. Additional comparisons examine pretraining data size and the roles of quality tags during fine-tuning and inference. Across three VLAs, we find that approach-aware episode conditioning improves mean success over plain behavioral cloning; conditioning and filtering favor episode and chunk labels, respectively, on average, with model-dependent reversals; and constant-tag fine-tuning improves mean success after conditioned pretraining with either tagged or untagged inference. We introduce AXIS-Bench to support these controlled comparisons. Built on crowdsourced simulation demonstrations, it provides recorded object and contact states for recomputing quality labels on shared trajectories. Replayable trajectories support workspace-aligned rendering and user-defined real-world target tasks. In our real-world experiment, simulation pretraining raises success from 9.6\% to 31.7\%. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [41] [EmbodiedEvo: Self-improving Coding Agents for Physical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18867)
*Wenyi Li,Yizhe Chi,Youjie Zheng,Chunyu Liu,Xiaoqiu Wang,Mingju Gao,Zheng Jiang,Deyao Hong,Calvin Xiao,Kaisen Yang,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EmbodiedEvo：以代码作为具身智能的可执行策略表示与任务工作空间，让编码智能体在单一 Playground 场景中通过反馈演化可复用 Skills 和 Lessons，再冻结用于新场景；在 RoboDojo-Sim 上超过官方验证结果，但精细接触控制仍困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将通用智能体引入物理世界需要可执行的物理智能表示。相比 VLA 模型直接将观测和语言指令映射为动作，代码能显式表达且可复用控制逻辑，并可作为智能体求解任务的工作空间。SWE 式自主检查状态、修改执行代码并依据反馈决定下一步的循环在数字环境中成功，促使其扩展到物理任务；但物理执行易反复失败，因此需要保留和精炼经验以迁移到后续场景。

Method: EmbodiedEvo 让编码智能体在单个 Playground 场景中利用反馈演化可复用 Skills 与 Lessons，随后冻结所得 harness，用于多样化新场景。它不遵循固定的规划、执行、验证流程，而是自主检查状态、修改并执行代码，并利用反馈选择下一步。

Result: 在 RoboDojo-Sim 上，EmbodiedEvo 取得平均分 44.48、成功率 37.82%，超过所有官方验证结果。配对测试显示，记忆依赖任务增益最大，协调流程可成功复用，但精细接触控制仍困难。更强模型经验可提升弱执行器：Astra 的经验使 Sol 和 Luna 的成功率分别比无经验提高 8.86 和 5.50 个百分点。与原始交互历史相比，Skills 和 Lessons 在任务性能相当的情况下将执行成本降低 53.0%。

Conclusion: 代码化、可演化的 Skills 与 Lessons 能有效提升具身智能体在物理任务中的可复用性和性能，并具有跨场景与跨模型迁移价值；但精细接触控制仍是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Bringing general-purpose agents into the physical world calls for an executable representation of physical intelligence. Compared with vision-language-action (VLA) models, which map observations and language instructions directly to actions, code makes control logic explicit and reusable. Code can serve not only as a policy representation but also as the workspace in which the agent solves a task. Rather than following a prescribed workflow of planning, execution, and verification, the agent can autonomously inspect the state, revise and execute code, and use feedback to choose the next step. The success of this SWE-style loop in digital environments motivates its extension to physical tasks. Yet physical execution remains prone to recurring failures. To retain and refine experience for later scenes, we introduce EmbodiedEvo, where a coding agent evolves reusable Skills and Lessons through feedback on a single Playground scene, then freezes the resulting harness for diverse new scenes. On RoboDojo-Sim, EmbodiedEvo achieves an average score of 44.48 and a success rate of 37.82\%, surpassing all officially verified results. Paired tests show the largest gains on memory-dependent tasks and successful reuse of coordinated procedures, while fine contact control remains difficult. Experience from stronger models also improves weaker executors: Astra's experience raises Sol and Luna's success rates by 8.86 and 5.50 percentage points over no experience. Compared with raw interaction history, Skills and Lessons achieve comparable task performance at 53.0\% lower execution cost.

</details>


### [42] [AutoAgent0: An Agentic Runtime for Safe Closed-Loop Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18866)
*Jiageng Liu,Seth Z. Zhao,Zewei Zhou,Alejandro Huerta,Aidan Bayer-Calvert,Yuxin Bao,Haotian Lin,Robert Spataru,Zijun Cui,Yining Hong,Bolei Zhou,Jiaqi Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoAgent0 将闭环驾驶安全视为测试时执行问题，通过智能体运行时框架为现有驾驶专家提供独立感知、规则验证与反馈恢复，在不重训练底层策略的情况下提升长尾安全关键场景的闭环鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单帧规划精度不足以保证动态环境中的可靠闭环执行；端到端策略会累积闭环误差，尤其在新场景中进入难以恢复的状态，因此需要运行时层面的监督与恢复机制。

Method: 提出 AutoAgent0，一种智能体运行时与测试框架。独立检测模块用互补感知技能构建场景上下文，规则验证器在执行前检查候选轨迹；当名义轨迹不可靠时，编排器触发 Recovery Planning，智能体循环基于动作原语生成替代动作提案，并利用验证反馈迭代优化。

Result: 在 Fail2Drive 和 NavSafe-∞ 上评估，相较单专家执行，AutoAgent0 在长尾与安全关键场景中提升了闭环鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，运行时监督可在不重新训练底层策略的前提下，跨多个驾驶专家提升闭环鲁棒性，为闭环自动驾驶安全提供测试时执行方案。

Abstract: Safe autonomous driving requires more than accurate planning at a single frame: it demands reliable closed-loop execution in dynamic environments. Although end-to-end driving policies have advanced rapidly, the accumulated closed-loop errors, especially in unseen scenarios, can push vehicles into states from which the same policy struggles to recover. We address the challenge by treating closed-loop driving safety as a test-time execution problem. We introduce AutoAgent0, an agentic runtime and harness framework that augments existing driving experts with complementary detection skills, rule-based action verification, and feedback-driven recovery. To address inaccurate scene perception, an independent detection module constructs scene context using complementary perception skills, and a rule-based verifier checks proposed trajectories against this context before execution. When the nominal trajectories are unreliable, the orchestrator of the runtime activates Recovery Planning, where an agentic loop constructs alternative action proposals from action primitives and iteratively refines them using verification feedback. This design combines independent scene assessment with feasibility checks and online recovery, addressing both perception errors and the limited recovery capabilities of individual experts. Evaluations on Fail2Drive and NavSafe-$\infty$ demonstrate improved closed-loop robustness over single-expert execution in long-tail and safety-critical scenarios. These results demonstrate that runtime supervision can improve closed-loop robustness across multiple driving experts without retraining the underlying policies.

</details>


### [43] [Interaction Latents as Actions for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18855)
*Yuhang Zheng,Yujie Zang,Yuchen Liu,Yupeng Zheng,Zhennan Jiang,Zebin Xing,Shuai Tian,Songen Gu,Jingxiang Guo,Chen Gao,Zhongpu Xia,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ILA提出将可解码的交互潜变量作为动作单元，联合编码运动、接触与力的演化，并统一用于动作生成、评估和离线改进；在四个真实任务上，相较触觉条件直接动作基线，平均成功率提升21.0-36.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富接触操作要求机器人不仅决定移动位置，还需在交互过程中协调接触与力的演化。然而，多数机器人策略主要将动作表示为运动轨迹，触觉信号仅作为输入或辅助预测目标，未在动作表示中显式编码接触动力学。

Method: 提出ILA（Interaction Latents as Actions）：用可解码交互潜变量联合捕捉运动、接触和力施加的演化，作为策略动作单元，提供动作生成、评估和离线改进的共享表示。通过视觉条件联合动作-触觉重建与时间条件隐式解码器学习潜变量；将其作为流匹配生成目标，支持预训练VLA/WAM的后训练及从零训练流匹配策略，共享解码器将生成潜变量映射为可执行动作。离线改进中，从专家演示编码交互潜变量，学习触觉条件专家参考模型，以专家一致性奖励引导成功轨迹加权学习，并用观测兼容专家参考纠正失败轨迹的低奖励预测。

Result: 在三个策略家族、四个真实世界任务上，ILA平均成功率较触觉条件直接动作基线提升21.0-36.5个百分点。消融实验表明，在动作目标中编码接触演化优于仅使用触觉条件；同一潜空间还可支持专家引导的离线改进。

Conclusion: ILA表明，将接触动力学显式纳入动作表示可提升富接触操作策略性能，并提供统一潜空间用于动作生成、评估与离线改进。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to coordinate not only where to move, but also how contact and force should evolve throughout an interaction. Yet most robot policies represent actions primarily as motion trajectories, using tactile signals as inputs or auxiliary prediction targets without explicitly encoding contact dynamics in the action representation. We propose ILA (Interaction Latents as Actions), which uses decodable interaction latents that jointly capture the evolution of motion, contact, and force application as policy action units, providing a shared representation for action generation, evaluation, and offline improvement. We learn these latents through visually conditioned joint action-tactile reconstruction with a time-conditioned implicit decoder. Using them as flow-matching generation targets supports post-training of pretrained vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world action models (WAMs), as well as training flow-matching policies from scratch. A shared decoder maps generated latents to executable actions. For offline improvement, we learn a tactile-conditioned expert reference model from interaction latents encoded from expert demonstrations. The resulting expert-consistency rewards guide weighted learning from successful rollouts, while observation-compatible expert references guide correction of low-reward predictions from failed rollouts. Across three policy families, ILA improves success rates averaged over four real-world tasks by 21.0-36.5 percentage points relative to tactile-conditioned direct-action baselines. Ablations highlight the benefit of encoding contact evolution in action targets beyond tactile conditioning, while the same latent space supports expert-guided offline improvement. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [44] [ForceWAM: Decoupling Future and Response Learning from Demo and Play Data for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18852)
*Zijun Xu,Wendi Chen,Xinkai Wang,Jiachen Zou,Hongjin Chen,Hairuo Liu,Bokai Lin,Shihao Ma,Ke Ma,Zhiwei Zhang,Zhuohao Yan,Ke Wu,Haoming Song,Xueqi Li,Chuan Wen,Jieru Zhao,Wenchao Ding,Cewu Lu,Lixin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceWAM 将任务兼容的交互结果知识与局部动作如何改变接触的知识解耦，结合专家示范与任务无关 play 数据，实现接触丰富任务的力调节执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家示范能展示如何完成接触丰富任务，但对不同动作下交互力如何响应覆盖有限；任务无关 play 数据提供更广泛的接触动力学经验，却不指定任务目标。核心动机是同时利用这两类数据。

Method: 提出 ForceWAM（力世界动作模型）。世界模型从成功示范中学习，联合预测未来视频与六轴力/力矩轨迹，共同描述任务中运动与接触的预期演化；独立的逆动力学模型（IDM）从任务与 play 数据中学习，将当前观测和记录的视觉-力旋量（visual-wrench）未来映射为机器人动作。部署时，IDM 反应式地将生成的未来转化为动作，并在世界模型更新之间复用新鲜力反馈。

Result: 在真实机器人力调节滑动、有无扰动的盒子翻转、以及套环放置任务上，ForceWAM 达到 85–95% 平均成功率，优于世界模型和反应式基线。加入任务无关 play 数据使成功率提升 15–45 个百分点。

Conclusion: 任务无关 play 数据对学习接触丰富执行具有重要价值；将任务结果预测与局部接触力响应知识解耦，可有效融合示范与 play 数据。

Abstract: Expert demonstrations show how to complete contact-rich tasks, but provide limited coverage of how interaction forces respond to different actions. Task-agnostic play provides broader experience of contact dynamics without specifying task goals. To fully leverage these two types of data, we introduce ForceWAM, a force world action model that decouples knowledge of task-compatible interaction outcomes from knowledge of how local actions change contact. A world model learns from successful demonstrations to jointly predict future video and six-axis force/torque trajectories. Together, these futures describe the expected evolution of motion and contact during the task. A separate inverse dynamics model (IDM) learns from both task and play, mapping current observations and recorded visual-wrench futures to their associated robot actions. Play data thus provide knowledge of force responses for goal-directed execution. At deployment, the IDM reactively translates generated futures into actions, reusing them with fresh wrench feedback between world-model updates. Across real-robot force-regulated sliding, box flipping with and without disturbances, and ring placement, ForceWAM achieves $85$-$95\%$ mean success, surpassing world-modeling and reactive baselines. Our experiments further show that adding task-agnostic play data improves success by $15$-$45$ percentage points, demonstrating its value for learning contact-rich execution. Project page and code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [45] [Collaborative Planning for UAV-UGV Teams under Operational Constraints via Masked Error-bounded Neuro-Symbolic Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18850)
*Zihao Deng,Qianhuang Li,Maggie Wigness,John Rogers,Donghyun Kim,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MEND，将UAV-UGV协同规划建模为条件掩码生成过程，通过神经符号扩散与误差有界推理调度，在执行中满足运行约束并生成协同动作序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人团队在大规模任务中需要满足运行约束并协调并行行动；UAV覆盖快但机载能量有限，UGV可作移动充电站但受路网可通行性限制。现有掩码扩散模型存在熵引导调度假设分布对齐、依赖可微约束进行扩散时优化等局限。

Method: MEND将协同规划形式化为条件掩码生成过程，生成上下文感知动作序列；引入神经符号扩散范式，在执行时用逻辑诱导的可容许性指标重加权学习到的动作分布以强制满足运行约束；提出误差有界推理调度，基于约束感知动作分布选择最小预测熵位置构建解掩码调度。

Result: 在仿真、真实地图和实体机器人团队上的大量实验表明，MEND提升了运行约束下的UAV-UGV协同规划性能。

Conclusion: MEND能够结合神经生成与符号约束，在执行阶段满足运行约束并增强协同规划最优性，适用于实际异构机器人团队任务。

Abstract: Collaborative planning under operational constraints is essential for heterogeneous robot teams in large-scale missions. Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) provide rapid area coverage but are limited by onboard energy, while Unmanned Ground Vehicles (UGVs) can serve as mobile recharging stations yet are constrained by road-network traversability. Effective collaboration must enforce operational constraints during execution while enabling parallel, coordinated actions. Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) have shown promise through token unmasking but have two key limitations: entropy-guided scheduling assumes distributional alignment and reliance on differentiable constraints for diffusion-time optimization. We propose Masked Error-bounded Neuro-Symbolic Diffusion (MEND), which formulates collaborative planning as a conditional masked generative process to generate context-aware action sequences for UAV-UGV teams. MEND introduces a neuro-symbolic diffusion paradigm that constrains conditional masked diffusion policies at execution by reweighting the learned action distribution with a logic-induced admissibility indicator, thereby enforcing compliance with operational constraints. MEND introduces an error-bounded inference scheduling at execution that constructs the unmasking schedule by selecting positions with minimal predictive entropy over the constraint-aware action distribution, enhancing collaborative planning optimality under operational constraints. Extensive experimental results in simulation, on real-world maps, and with physical robot teams demonstrate that MEND advances UAV-UGV collaborative planning under operational constraints.

</details>


### [46] [Contact Trajectory Prompting: In-Context Transfer of Contact-Rich Behaviors from a Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18848)
*Xian Nie,Yujie Zang,Yuhang Zheng,Yupeng Zheng,Songen Gu,Wendi Chen,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu,Wenchao Ding,Junchi Yan,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出接触轨迹提示（CTP）框架，实现接触丰富操作行为的一次性迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文机器人学习无需测试时参数更新即可从演示中获取新行为，但仅凭视觉与运动学规范难以区分运动相似、交互强度或模式却不同的接触丰富行为。

Method: CTP将单次演示中的运动与接触信息编码为有序的接触轨迹提示，捕捉其时间演化并指定期望的物理交互行为；执行时策略利用在线感官反馈从提示中检索交互相关信息，以演示的接触行为为条件生成闭环动作。

Result: 在训练中未包含的物体上，真实世界擦拭、旋转与插入任务总体成功率达78.7%，比最强基线高13.0个百分点；仅更换参考演示即可遵循接触强度与方向规格，包括中等擦拭力及顺时针/逆时针旋盖。

Conclusion: 接触轨迹可作为有效的行为规格，将上下文机器人学习从运动模仿扩展到物理交互迁移。

Abstract: In-context robot learning enables robots to acquire new behaviors from demonstrations without test-time parameter updates. However, visual and kinematic specifications alone may not distinguish contact-rich behaviors that share similar motions but require different interaction intensities or patterns. We propose Contact Trajectory Prompting (CTP), a framework for one-shot transfer of contact-rich manipulation behaviors. CTP encodes motion and contact information from a single demonstration into an ordered contact trajectory prompt that captures their temporal evolution and specifies the desired physical interaction behavior. During execution, the policy uses online sensory feedback to retrieve interaction-relevant information from the prompt, conditioning closed-loop action generation on the demonstrated contact behavior. Across real-world wiping, rotation, and insertion tasks on objects excluded from training, CTP achieves 78.7% overall success, outperforming the strongest evaluated baseline by 13.0 percentage points. Changing only the reference demonstration enables CTP to follow contact specifications for both intensity and direction, including an intermediate wiping force level and clockwise or counterclockwise cap rotation. These results demonstrate that contact trajectories serve as effective behavioral specifications, extending in-context robot learning beyond motion imitation to physical interaction transfer. Project website: this https URL .

Abstract: Robot policies are expected to generalize across deployment environments, including variations in the robot itself: cameras are bumped and remounted, calibrations drift, controllers run at different rates, and end-effector pads wear down. We present LIBERO-RIG, a benchmark that perturbs only the robot's hardware and setup while holding every LIBERO task fixed. The benchmark spans eleven perturbation axes, including camera mounting and optics, camera-to-arm calibration, actuation gain, control rate, proprioceptive noise, base pose, table height, and gripper pads. Each axis has five severity levels, yielding 210 conditions across the four LIBERO suites. We evaluate nine recent vision-language-action models using 500 episodes per condition and substantiate each finding with rigorous statistical tests. Pooled success falls from 0.932 to 0.767 at the middle severity and 0.509 at the highest severity. Performance degradation varies substantially across perturbation axes and action interfaces, and training on visual perturbations does not mitigate faults that leave camera images unchanged. We release the benchmark together with manifests specifying the fixed perturbation parameters for each episode.

Abstract: It is difficult to accurately model the physics of the interaction between a robot and the objects it manipulates, including contact mechanics, coupled contact dynamics, deformations, stiffness and friction. We present RoboSimStudio (RSS), an interactive toolkit that makes choices of solver, contact model, and physical parameters explicit and editable, allowing both humans and coding agents to iteratively refine simulations based on observed physical behavior. We implement RSS with the open Newton physics simulation system and describe experiments with human-guided real2sim using (1) visual evaluation, (2) force-feedback and (3) agentic Real2Sim2Real. Code, video and additional information can be found on the project website.

### [49] [WorldOracle: Benchmarking Action-Conditioned World Models against Real-World Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18826)
*Haoyi Duan,Liliang Chen,Nan Wang,Yang Zhang,Yuhang Lu,Xiaotong Chen,Wei-Cheng Tseng,Shenyuan Gao,Zhaoshuo Li,Jingyi Jin,Pooya Jannaty,Dhruv Shah,Yuke Zhu,Ming-Yu Liu,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WorldOracle基准，用于在配对真实执行下评估动作条件世界模型（ACWMs），并从四个维度衡量预测 rollout 是否复现真实物理后果与任务状态转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型缺乏与真实世界执行配对的系统评估，难以判断预测 rollout 是否真实反映物理后果和任务状态变化，尤其是在操作任务中。

Method: 构建WorldOracle基准：给定相同初始观测和低级动作序列，评估预测 rollout 与真实执行的匹配；设计四个互补维度——物理状态正确性、交互空间对齐、关键交互区域保真度和多视角动作一致性；整理覆盖不同交互动态与 embodiments 的真实训练/评估套件，评估集包含长多段 episode，既有成功也有失败执行。

Result: 在七种代表性ACWMs上进行实验，揭示了不同模型的优势与失效模式。

Conclusion: WorldOracle为衡量和推进面向操作的动作条件世界建模提供了系统化基础，并将发布数据集、评估代码和排行榜。

Abstract: We introduce WorldOracle, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) against paired real-world executions. Given the same initial observation and low-level action sequence, WorldOracle asks whether a predicted rollout reproduces what happens in the real world, including physical consequences and task-state transitions. We evaluate models along four complementary dimensions: physical state correctness, interaction spatial alignment, key interaction area fidelity, and multi-view action coherence. To support this evaluation, we curate real-world training and evaluation suites that cover different interaction dynamics and embodiments. The evaluation suite contains long, multi-segment episodes with both successful and failed executions, enabling evaluation across varied action outcomes and behavior distributions. Experiments on seven representative ACWMs reveal distinct model strengths and failure modes. WorldOracle provides a systematic foundation for measuring and advancing action-conditioned world modeling for manipulation. We will release the datasets, evaluation code, and leaderboard.

</details>


### [50] [EgoMatrix: Physically Faithful Dexterous Robot Demonstrations from Egocentric Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18822)
*Ziheng Wang,Yanming Shao,Ting Mao,Kaihan Chen,Yiqun Wang,Xuanye Wu,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoMatrix 是一个端到端数据引擎，可将单目第一视角人类视频转换为可直接在真实机器人上重放的双臂灵巧操作轨迹及配对机器人观测视频，用于规模化生成机器人训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人遥操作采集灵巧操作数据成本高、难以扩展，第一视角人类视频是潜在替代来源，但存在两个关键瓶颈：单目 RGB 下手-物交互（HOI）重建不准确，以及难以生成高质量的机器人演示，尤其是视觉观测。

Method: EgoMatrix 不追求精确的度量场景重建，而是强调相对几何与接触关系来重建 HOI；随后通过重定向和强化学习（RL）在仿真中复现演示任务。系统联合渲染机器人手与物体，从仿真轨迹生成与状态、控制记录同步的物理可信观测。同一轨迹可在多样且任务兼容的背景中渲染，从而在保持机器人-物体运动与接触一致的前提下扩展配对训练数据。

Result: 在 TACO、OakInk2 和自采数据上，EgoMatrix 在仿真中端到端成功率达到 54.84%，最强基线为 14.52%。其在两个公开基准的大多数重建指标上达到最先进水平。定性结果显示其手-物交互比先前方法更真实，特征空间分析表明其观测比 GPT-6 更接近真实机器人观测。

Conclusion: EgoMatrix 表明，以相对几何和接触为中心的重建、仿真复现与联合渲染，可有效将第一视角人类视频转化为高质量、可重放的机器人灵巧操作数据，并提升仿真任务成功率与视觉观测对齐质量。

Abstract: Egocentric human videos offer a scalable alternative to robot teleoperation for collecting dexterous manipulation data. However, two challenges limit their use as reliable training data: accurate hand-object interaction (HOI) reconstruction from monocular RGB and high-quality robot demonstrations, especially visual observations. We present EgoMatrix, an end-to-end data engine that converts monocular egocentric human videos into bimanual dexterous trajectories and paired robot observation videos, with the trajectories directly replayable on real robots. It reconstructs HOI with emphasis on relative geometry and contact rather than exact metric scene recovery, then uses retargeting and reinforcement learning (RL) to reproduce the demonstrated tasks in simulation. We jointly render robot hands and objects from these simulated trajectories to produce physically faithful observations synchronized with state and control records. Rendering the same trajectories in diverse, task-compatible backgrounds expands paired training data while preserving robot-object motion and contact. Across TACO, OakInk2, and self-collected data, EgoMatrix achieves 54.84% end-to-end success in simulation versus 14.52% for the strongest baseline. Reconstruction reaches state-of-the-art performance on most metrics of the two public benchmarks. Qualitative results show more faithful hand-object interactions than prior methods, while feature-space analysis shows closer alignment with real-robot observations than GPT-6 this http URL project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [51] [SEAR: Self-Evolving Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18792)
*Xinyi Wang,Bangzheng Li,Hanyu Wang,Lin Zhang,Andrea Yaoyun Cui,Zhenggang Tang,Shyamal Buch,Dejia Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SEAR 自进化机器人智能体框架与基准，检验语言模型智能体是否真正学习并能泛化到新任务、新仿真器和真实机器人，并在 7 个前沿模型、330 个仿真任务和 5 个物理任务上总结学习曲线、迁移与模型差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型智能体已能自主改进机器人：编写控制代码、测试并保留有效技能和记忆。但关键疑问是：智能体是在真正学习，还是只是更频繁地尝试？所学内容能否迁移到新任务、新仿真器和真实机器人？

Method: SEAR 框架支持三种运行模式：code-as-policy（编写代码作为策略）、agent-as-policy（在控制回路中实时行动）和 auto-research（作为研究者微调视觉-语言-动作模型）。基准加入控制变量，以区分保留经验与重复尝试、熟悉任务与新颖任务、特权仿真状态、更大预算与运气。实验覆盖 7 个前沿语言模型、330 个仿真任务、5 个物理任务和超过四年的智能体运行时间。

Result: 发现三点：其一，学习曲线同时取决于总时间预算和相对于模型能力的任务难度；更难任务值得更长会话，简单任务上多次短尝试即可。其二，控制代码和共享例程可跨任务与仿真器迁移，但任务特定方案不行；真实机器人上，智能体设计的练习与新颖人类设计场景之间存在性能差距。其三，不同模型进化方式不同：部分擅长实时行动，部分适合先写代码；纯文本模型可媲美同等多模态模型，感知有助于错误评估，但智能体推理与工具使用可能更关键。

Conclusion: SEAR 表明语言模型驱动的机器人智能体可产生可保留、可迁移的能力，但泛化受任务难度、模型能力和场景新颖性影响；不同模型需匹配不同进化策略。作者发布基准和代码库以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Frontier language-model agents can now improve robots on their own: they write control code, test it, and keep what works as skills and memories for the next task. We introduce SEAR (Self-Evolving Agentic Robotics), a framework and benchmark built around two questions. Is the agent really learning, or just trying more often? And does what it learns still help on new tasks, in a new simulator, and on a real robot? The framework runs an agent in three modes: it writes code that becomes the policy (code-as-policy), acts live in the control loop (agent-as-policy), or works as a researcher that fine-tunes a vision-language-action model (auto-research). The benchmark adds controls that separate retained experience from repeated attempts, familiar tasks, privileged simulator state, larger budgets, and luck. Across seven frontier language models, 330 simulated and five physical tasks, and over four years of agent runtime, we highlight three findings. First, the learning curve of a robotic agent depends both on the overall time budget and the task difficulty relative to model capability. Harder tasks make longer sessions worthwhile; on easier tasks, re-rolling short attempts does just as well. Second, control code and shared routines transfer across tasks and simulators, while task-specific solutions do not. On a real robot, we observe a performance gap between agent-designed practice and novel human-designed scenes. Third, different models evolve more effectively in different ways. Some do best acting live, others writing code first. And a text-only model keeps pace with a comparable multimodal one: perception helps with error assessment, but agentic reasoning and tool use can matter more. We release the benchmark and codebase to support future research.

</details>


### [52] [EqC: Equilibrium Energy Control for Introspective Lifelong Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18790)
*Haodong Yang,Runhan Huang,Bowen Zhang,Iofai Lei,Zunzhe Zhang,Xiangcheng Zhang,Shaoting Zhu,Linzhan Mou,Yicheng Liu,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Equilibrium Energy Control (EqC)，一种基于显式条件能量景观的内省式终身机器人学习框架，通过检测能量一致性来识别策略能力边界并触发专家干预，实现生成式控制器的持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期机器人自主性不仅取决于有效动作生成，还要求策略能识别自身行为何时不再被学习到的模型充分支持，并利用辅助干预来提升能力。

Method: EqC 将生成式动作精炼建模为平衡态搜索优化，利用精炼过程的几何性质判断生成行为是否与学习到的条件能量景观一致；该一致性信号驱动 Energy-Gated DAgger：持续出现能量一致性违规时触发专家接管，并将恢复轨迹聚合用于后续策略更新。EqC 可自然集成到 Diffusion Transformer 策略和基于流匹配的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界双臂操作任务中，EqC 达到或提升了控制性能与鲁棒性，并能在策略能力边界附近及时干预；真实世界研究进一步表明，这些干预逐步扩展了机器人可自主处理的情境范围。

Conclusion: EqC 为从静态生成式控制器迈向可自我改进的机器人系统提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Long-term robot autonomy depends on more than effective action generation: a policy must recognize when its behavior is no longer sufficiently supported and use assistance to improve. We introduce Equilibrium Energy Control (EqC), a framework for introspective lifelong robot learning built around an explicit conditional energy landscape. EqC casts generative action refinement as equilibrium-seeking optimization, while the geometry of the refinement process reveals whether the generated behavior remains consistent with the learned landscape. This competence signal enables Energy-Gated DAgger, where persistent energy-consistency violations trigger expert takeover and the resulting recovery trajectories are aggregated for subsequent policy updates. EqC integrates naturally into both Diffusion Transformer policies and flow-matching-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies. Across simulation and real-world bimanual manipulation, EqC matches or improves control performance and robustness while enabling timely intervention near the policy's competence boundary. A real-world study further shows how these interventions progressively expand the range of situations handled autonomously, providing a practical path from static generative controllers toward self-improving robot systems.

</details>


### [53] [GreenWAM: A World Foundation Model for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18774)
*Gennady Sidorov,Valerii Kornilov,Denis Grankin,Polina Fedotova,Aleksey Postnikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GreenWAM 是一个面向机器人的世界基础模型，可跨20种机器人本体同时充当世界模型与策略；其关键配方为统一解剖动作空间、多模式联合训练，以及从生成器拆分动作专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人既需要世界模型预测动作如何改变场景，也需要策略选择动作，但现有模型通常只针对其中一种角色训练和评估，且各机器人以自有格式记录动作。作者追问：单一机器人世界模型能否跨不同身体同时服务两种角色，以及什么使这成为可能。

Method: 提出 GreenWAM 及训练配方：(1) 共享解剖动作空间，用统一关节坐标描述从双臂机械臂到灵巧人形等20个机器人族；(2) 在这些机器人族上进行视觉、动作条件和世界-动作模式的联合训练；(3) 通过从生成器拆分动作专家来构建策略。

Result: 作为世界模型，GreenWAM 在 WorldArena 1.0 上取得顶级轨迹、深度和3D精度，优于比较中的所有世界-动作模型和视频生成器。作为策略，它进入 RoboTwin 2.0 排行榜前列，在 LIBERO-plus 零样本成功率上为比较方法中最高，并在 CALVIN 和 LIBERO 上与专用视觉-语言-动作及世界-动作策略具有竞争力。在真实人形机器人上，其成功率高于从 π_{0.5} 和 Cosmos 3 Edge 适配的策略，并可蒸馏为单步策略用于实时控制。控制实验有三点发现：机器人专业化同时提升世界预测与控制；动作专家在适配开始时拆分更有效；策略适配期间保留世界模型模式可保持对场景变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 一个机器人世界模型可跨本体同时承担世界预测与策略控制，且共享动作空间、联合训练、动作专家拆分和保留世界模型模式是实现该目标的关键。该工作为通用机器人世界基础模型与跨本体策略学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A robot needs a world model that predicts how its actions will change the scene and a policy that chooses those actions. Models are usually built and evaluated for only one of these roles, and every robot records its actions in its own format. We ask whether one robot world model can serve both roles across different bodies, and what makes this work. We present GreenWAM, a world foundation model for robotics, together with the recipe that produces it: (1) a shared anatomical action space that describes 20 robot families, from bimanual arms to dexterous humanoids, in common joint coordinates; (2) joint training on these families in visual, action-conditioned and world-action modes; and (3) policies that split an action expert off the generator. As a world model, GreenWAM attains top-tier trajectory, depth and 3D accuracy on WorldArena 1.0, ahead of every world-action model and video generator in our comparison. As a policy, it scores among the top entries of the RoboTwin 2.0 leaderboard, reaches the highest zero-shot LIBERO-plus success among the compared methods and is competitive with dedicated vision-language-action and world-action policies on CALVIN and LIBERO. We further adapt GreenWAM to a real humanoid, where it succeeds more often than policies adapted from $\pi_{0.5}$ and from Cosmos 3 Edge, and distill it into a single-step policy for real-time control. Controlled experiments yield three findings: robot specialization improves world prediction and control together; an action expert helps when split off at the start of adaptation; and keeping world-model modes during policy adaptation preserves robustness to scene changes.

</details>


### [54] [Steertuning Policies from a Few Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18711)
*Billy Yan,Isaac Tu,Kelly Lee,Blake Chang,Kenny Ma,Kanad Patel,Dhawal Kabra,Neer Patel,Kevin Wu,Lerrel Pinto,Irmak Guzey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Steertuning 通过100小时自我中心人类交互预训练400M参数扩散策略，再用5个演示微调2分钟并借助最近观测演示引导反向采样，实现新环境新任务5分钟内学习部署，在12个新环境12个任务上达71%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人学习通常依赖大量机器人演示才能获得鲁棒且自适应的策略；本文希望仅用5个人类演示，在新任务和新环境中快速学会并部署机器人策略。

Method: 方法分两部分：(1) 在100小时密集标注的随机自我中心人类交互数据集上预训练400M参数扩散策略，提升对背景差异的鲁棒性以及空间和物体泛化能力；(2) 给定新任务5个演示，仅微调2分钟（2000次训练更新），并在反向采样时使用与部署环境观测最接近的演示来引导策略动作；同时设计数据处理流程以完成具身迁移。

Result: 在12个新环境、12个任务上取得71%成功率，环境从实验室到新家厨房；从新任务数据收集到机器人策略部署全过程少于5分钟。
