Conclusion: Steertuning 表明，大规模人类交互预训练结合极少演示微调与采样引导，可实现快速、可泛化的机器人新任务学习；作者将公开完整数据集、模型权重、代码库和机器人视频。

Abstract: Current recipes for robot learning require massive amounts of robot demonstrations to obtain robust and adaptive policies. In this work, we introduce Steertuning, a system that enables robots to learn new tasks in new environments from 5 human demonstrations in under 5 Steertuning consists of two parts. (1) We pretrain a 400M-parameter diffusion policy on our egocentric human dataset, which consists of 100 hours of densely annotated random interactions. This pretraining makes Steertuning robust to background differences and enables spatial and object generalization when deployed on new tasks. (2) Given 5 demonstrations of a new task, we finetune our model for only 2 minutes (2000 training updates) and steer the policy's actions during reverse sampling using the demonstration whose observation is closest to that of the deployment environment. Together with a carefully designed data processing pipeline for embodiment transfer, this enables Steertuning to achieve a 71% success rate across 12 tasks in 12 new environments, ranging from lab settings to kitchens in new homes. The entire process, from data collection for a new task to robot policy deployment, takes less than 5 minutes. Our full dataset, model weights, codebase, and robot videos will be made available at our website: this https URL .

</details>


### [55] [RoboFQE: Rollout-Free Evaluation of Visual Robot Policies from Offline Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18699)
*Yunfu Deng,Sangwoo Shin,Brahma S. Pavse,Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboFQE 是一种基于离线轨迹的离策略评估器，通过拟合Q评估、预训练视觉基础模型和学习到的终止成功标注，在不估计行为策略密度的情况下准确估计目标策略的折扣期望回报，并在长时程操作任务中保持策略排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人策略部署前评估依赖真实执行、仿真器或学习世界模型生成轨迹，需要额外基础设施，且仿真器/世界模型评估存在预测误差随评估时域累积的问题。本文研究离策略评估作为替代方案。

Method: 提出 RoboFQE：从离线机器人轨迹中评估目标策略的折扣期望回报，不估计行为策略密度；结合 fitted Q-evaluation、预训练视觉基础模型以及学习到的终止成功标注，以连接标准 OPE 基准与连续控制视觉操作任务。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务的受控实验中，RoboFQE 能产生准确的折扣回报估计并保持策略排序；在长时程操作任务中，视频世界模型评估器因自回归预测误差而性能下降，RoboFQE 仍表现良好。消融实验表明，预训练视觉编码器显著优于从头训练方案，尤其在长时程任务上；学习到的终止成功标注可在无真值奖励标签时实现评估，仅带来轻微精度下降。

Conclusion: 离策略评估可作为基于 rollout 的机器人策略评估的有效替代方案；RoboFQE 在视觉操作任务中具有准确性和鲁棒性，并降低对基础设施与真值奖励标签的依赖。

Abstract: Evaluating robot policies before deployment typically requires producing new trajectories through real-world execution, simulators, or learned world models. These rollout-based approaches impose infrastructure requirements; simulator and world-model evaluators additionally face prediction errors that can compound over the evaluation horizon. In this paper, we study off-policy evaluation as an alternative to rollout-based robot-policy evaluation methods. Specifically, we introduce RoboFQE, an evaluator that estimates the discounted expected return of a target policy from offline robot trajectories without estimating a behavior-policy density, combining fitted Q-evaluation with a pretrained vision foundation model and learned terminal success annotation to bridge the gap between standard OPE benchmarks and continuous-control visual manipulation. Controlled experiments across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks show that RoboFQE produces accurate discounted-return estimates and preserves policy rankings even on long-horizon manipulation tasks where video world-model evaluators are degraded by autoregressive prediction errors. Ablation experiments show that pretrained vision encoders substantially outperform from-scratch alternatives, particularly on long-horizon tasks, and that learned terminal success annotation enables evaluation without ground-truth reward labels with only slight reduction of accuracy.

</details>


### [56] [UniW(A)M: A Unified Mixture-of-Experts Framework for World and World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18697)
*Jingbo Xu,Jianjie Fang,Zhuohang Li,Zihao Song,Zhaolu Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Xin Zhang,Haisheng Su,Yinzhou Tang,Chen Gao,Wei Wu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 UniW(A)M：基于扩散 Transformer 的混合专家世界-(动作)模型，统一动作条件世界建模与多种世界-动作模型变体，并用即插即用自适应接口路由器 PAIR 按执行块选择合适 WAM 接口，在多个仿真与真实操作任务上取得领先效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型与世界-动作模型分别侧重未来世界预测和可执行动作生成，虽共享具身轨迹中的物理动力学与交互语义，但通常独立训练，限制了预测与控制之间的知识复用。核心挑战是在统一学习框架中共享动力学，同时保留各范式所需的独特建模能力。

Method: 提出 UniW(A)M，一种基于扩散 Transformer 的混合专家世界-(动作)模型。共享专家捕获通用动力学，原子能力专家跨接口复用，同时每个接口保留自身条件通路。部署时使用即插即用自适应接口路由器 PAIR，从少量目标任务块场景中学习，为每个执行块选择合适的 WAM 接口，在冻结主干的情况下平衡任务成功与执行效率。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0 和三个真实世界操作任务上评估：真实世界平均成功率 88.3%，最强基线为 76.7%；在 WorldArena 上三项任务的动作条件世界建模均领先。AC-WM 协同训练对所有动作接口的动作生成均有贡献；PAIR 将 OOD 成功率从 63.3% 提升至 76.7%。

Conclusion: UniW(A)M 验证了统一框架可在共享物理动力学的同时保留各接口的专门化控制能力，并通过 PAIR 提升任务成功与执行效率，尤其在真实世界与 OOD 场景中显著优于基线。

Abstract: World models (WMs) and world-action models (WAMs) focus on future world prediction and executable action generation, respectively. Although these models learn shared physical dynamics and interaction semantics from embodied trajectories, they are typically trained in isolation, which restricts knowledge reuse between prediction and control. The central challenge is therefore to develop a unified learning framework that shares these dynamics while preserving the distinct modeling capabilities required by each paradigm. We propose UniW(A)M, a mixture-of-experts world-(action) model built upon diffusion Transformers that unifies action-conditioned world modeling and diverse WAM variants. Shared experts capture common dynamics, while atomic capability experts are reused across interfaces so that each interface keeps its own conditional pathway. At deployment, a plug-in adaptive interface router (PAIR) learns from a small subset of target-task chunk scenarios to select a suitable WAM interface for each execution chunk, balancing task success and execution efficiency while keeping the backbone frozen. We evaluate UniW(A)M on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and three real-world manipulation tasks, where it achieves 88.3% average real-world success versus 76.7% for the strongest baseline. On WorldArena, it leads action-conditioned world modeling on all three tasks. AC-WM co-training contributes to action generation on every action interface and PAIR raises OOD success from 63.3% to 76.7%.

</details>


### [57] [Perception Beyond the Visible: Incompleteness-Aware Scene Understanding for Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18670)
*Rohit Mohan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“不完整性感知的场景理解”研究愿景，围绕证据不完整、知识不完整和传感器可靠性不完整三大挑战，通过非模态感知、语义占据预测、开放世界理解、标签高效学习与不确定性引导的多模态融合逐步解决，并展望以信念为中心的4D场景表示与主动感知、安全适应，以支持动态开放世界中的可靠自主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有许多感知系统仅建模可见场景区域，依赖固定语义类别，并假设传感器测量可靠；这些假设在遮挡、新物体出现和传感器退化时会失效。可靠机器人自主需要在部分且含噪的观测下进行世界推理，因此必须显式处理不完整性。

Method: 这是一篇研究愿景/立场论文，而非单一方法论文。作者以三类不完整性组织研究：不完整证据、不完整知识、不完整传感器可靠性；对应研究贡献包括非模态感知、语义占据预测、开放世界场景理解、标签高效学习和不确定性引导的多模态融合。未来方向是构建以信念为中心的4D场景表示，联合建模几何、语义、运动和不确定性，并结合主动感知与安全适应策略，让机器人通过交互降低不确定性。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验结果或定量指标；其主要“结果”是提出并组织了一套研究议程，阐明已有贡献如何应对不完整性挑战，并给出面向信念中心4D表示的未来路线。

Conclusion: 需要从仅处理可见、固定类别和可靠传感器的感知范式，转向不完整性感知的场景理解；目标是形成经过标定且可操作的场景表示，支撑机器人在动态、开放世界环境中的可靠自主。

Abstract: Reliable robot autonomy requires reasoning about the world from partial and noisy observations. However, many perception systems model only visible scene regions, rely on fixed semantic categories, and assume reliable sensor measurements. These assumptions break under occlusion, novel object encounters, and sensor degradation. In this paper, I present a research vision for incompleteness-aware scene understanding. I organize this vision around three fundamental challenges: incomplete evidence, incomplete knowledge, and incomplete sensor reliability. I discuss my research contributions toward addressing these challenges through amodal perception, semantic occupancy forecasting, open-world scene understanding, label-efficient learning, and uncertainty-guided multi-modal fusion. Building on these contributions, I outline future research directions toward belief-centric 4D scene representations. These representations aim to jointly model geometry, semantics, motion, and uncertainty. I also discuss active sensing and safe adaptation strategies that allow robots to reduce uncertainty through interaction. The goal is to develop calibrated and actionable scene representations that support reliable autonomy in dynamic, open-world environments.

</details>


### [58] [ForkCast: Predictive Recovery for Vision-Language-Action Policies via Counterfactual Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18650)
*Chenru Wen,Huajian Zeng,Haokai Ding,Jian Kang,Anqing Duan,Dawei Zhou,Dezhen Song,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForkCast 是一种面向冻结 VLA 策略的预测式恢复框架：在每个决策点比较“继续执行、重观测、重规划”三种反事实未来，并依据任务进度得分同时决定是否干预与如何干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略依赖图像和语言指令执行操作任务，但物体滑动或视觉遮挡会打断执行。现有恢复方式通常需在策略训练中学习，或依赖失败检测器触发预设修复脚本，存在失败检测、干预阈值和骨干微调等限制。

Method: ForkCast 在每个决策点评估三个候选：Commit 执行策略原始动作块，Reobserve 调整相机视角，Replan 重规划机械臂轨迹。候选条件世界模型在共同未来时域预测各候选的潜结果，共享任务进度头对预测结果打分。通过将策略自身延续与修复候选共同比较，基于任务进度的单次比较即可决定是否以及如何干预，无需失败检测器、干预阈值或更新 VLA 骨干。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 五个模拟任务并注入遮挡和物体滑动时，ForkCast 将冻结 π_{0.5} 策略的汇总成功率从 13.3% 提升至 30.0%，且无需更新 ForkCast 即可迁移到两个额外 VLA 骨干。在真实机器人候选 rollout 上适应后，ForkCast 在干净和可见性受限条件下均优于基础策略。

Conclusion: ForkCast 通过比较反事实未来与任务进度，在冻结 VLA 策略上实现无需失败检测、阈值设定或骨干微调的通用恢复干预，并展现出模拟与真实机器人上的有效性。代码将开源以促进研究。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies perform manipulation tasks using camera images and language instructions, but object slip or visual occlusion can disrupt execution. Recovery is typically learned during policy training or delegated to a monitor that triggers a scripted repair after detecting a failure. We introduce ForkCast, a predictive recovery framework that compares counterfactual futures to determine whether and how to intervene in a frozen VLA policy. At each decision point, ForkCast evaluates three candidates: executing the policy's nominal action chunk (Commit), adjusting the camera viewpoint (Reobserve), and replanning the arm trajectory (Replan). A candidate-conditioned world model forecasts the latent outcome of each candidate at a common future horizon, and a shared task-progress head scores the predicted outcomes. By evaluating the policy's own continuation alongside the repair candidates, a single comparison based on task progress determines both whether and how to intervene, without a failure detector, an intervention threshold, or updates to the VLA backbone. On five simulated RoboTwin 2.0 tasks with injected occlusion and object slip, ForkCast increases pooled success from 13.3% for the frozen $\pi_{0.5}$ policy to 30.0% and transfers to two additional VLA backbones without updating ForkCast. After adaptation on real-robot candidate rollouts, ForkCast also improves success over the base policy under both clean and visibility-limited conditions. Code will be released to benefit the research community.

</details>


### [59] [Grid or Nodes? Probing Object Memory in World Models Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18637)
*Justin McMillen,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在FloWM方块世界中比较特征网格世界模型与节点记忆（Kalman跟踪器）对离屏物体的保持能力；节点记忆在内存效率和未见速度泛化上更优，但需学习化以替代oracle。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究机器人世界模型如何记住离开视野的物体，以及基于特征网格的FloWM与显式节点记忆在离屏表示、读出方式、运动泛化与内存效率上的差异。

Method: 在FloWM自身的方块世界中进行对照：节点记忆用oracle-fed Kalman tracker保存每个物体的位置与速度数值；比较不同网格读出探针、视野内外得分、内存占用，并测试五种内置速度之外的斜向运动及更快运动，必要时微调FloWM或增加允许速度。

Result: 单格探针找回66%离屏方块，FloWM自带探针89%；FloWM在视野内虽无帧输入仍高8分，归因于其预测写回。离屏时tracker以14KB达到FloWM 5.6MB的范围且无需探针。斜向运动下FloWM微调后仅11%，tracker保持93%；更快运动两者均失败，增加一个允许速度后tracker达94%，而FloWM需25份网格副本。

Conclusion: 显式节点记忆在内存效率和速度泛化上优于FloWM的固定速度特征网格；但需用学习到的节点记忆替代oracle，并处理噪声、长间隔和反弹等挑战。

Abstract: A robot's world model must remember objects that leave the camera's view. FloWM, a learned world model, keeps them in a spatial grid of features that it moves with five built-in velocities. A node memory instead keeps each object's position and velocity as numbers. We compare them in FloWM's own block world, with an oracle-fed Kalman tracker as the node memory. How the grid is read changes its score: a single-cell probe finds 66% of out-of-view blocks, while FloWM's own probe 89%. FloWM also scores 8 points higher in view although no frame is shown, which we attribute to its write-back of its own predictions. Out of view, the tracker reaches FloWM's range in 14KB against 5.6MB, with no probe. On diagonal motion, outside its five velocities, FloWM finds 11% of blocks even after fine-tuning, while the unchanged tracker keeps 93%. Faster blocks defeat both until one more allowed speed takes the tracker to 94%, where FloWM's built-in shifts would need 25 copies of its grid. A learned node memory must still replace the oracles and handle velocity under noise, long gaps, and the bounce.

</details>


### [60] [ACCRUE: Continual Off-Policy Reinforcement Learning for Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18596)
*Changyeon Kim,Kimin Lee,Yashraj Narang,Yijie Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACCRUE，一种面向机器人基础模型的持续异策略强化学习框架，通过跨任务继承轻量actor-critic并保留旧行为，实现高效学习新任务且几乎不遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练可提升机器人基础模型，但实际部署要求高效学习新任务并保留已有行为。现有基于VLA的持续RL方法多依赖同策略更新，缓解遗忘但前向迁移有限；异策略方法虽提升单任务样本效率，却未解决跨任务学习者的持续性问题。

Method: ACCRUE在任务间携带轻量actor和critic网络，使各阶段复用先前RL获得的行为与价值知识。继承的critic仅在当前任务转移上适配并指导当前actor学习；通过将actor蒸馏到少量保留回合的缓存目标来保持旧行为。固定视觉特征使继承的学习器及其回放与基础策略更新兼容。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上的接触丰富装配序列中，ACCRUE加速后续任务学习，遗忘接近零，并将真实机器人训练时间减少31.0%至68.5%。

Conclusion: ACCRUE验证了持续异策略RL在机器人基础模型上兼顾前向迁移、样本效率和近零遗忘的可行性，适用于仿真与真实机器人任务。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves robot foundation models (RFMs) beyond their demonstrations, but real-world deployment demands more: learning new tasks efficiently while retaining previously acquired behaviors. Existing continual RL methods on top of VLAs mitigate forgetting but rely on on-policy updates with limited forward transfer, whereas off-policy methods improve single-task sample efficiency without addressing learner persistence across tasks. We propose ACCRUE, a continual off-policy RL framework that carries lightweight actor and critic networks across tasks, allowing each stage to reuse behavioral and value knowledge acquired through prior RL. The inherited critic adapts only on current-task transitions and guides current-task actor learning, while prior behavior is preserved by distilling the actor toward cached targets on a small number of retained episodes. Fixed visual features keep the inherited learner and its replay compatible with base-policy updates. Across contact-rich assembly sequences in simulation and on real-robot hardware, ACCRUE accelerates subsequent-task learning with near-zero forgetting and significantly reduces real-robot training time by 31.0\% to 68.5\%.

</details>


### [61] [WAVE-0: Bridging VLA Policies and WAMs with Lightweight Latent Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18568)
*Haomin Wang,Anqiong Ji,Ke Wang,Zhongshan Liu,Ru Ying,Ran Zheng,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WAVE-0 通过轻量潜在世界模型连接预训练 VLA 与未来预测，使预测的动作后果直接指导动作生成，从而提升机器人操作性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略擅长通用操作但不显式建模动作的环境后果；世界动作模型虽引入未来预测，却多依赖昂贵的视频生成，潜在替代方案也仅将预测未来作为辅助目标，或需大规模视频预训练从次优视觉过渡中推断潜在动作。

Method: WAVE-0 基于预训练 VLA，引入 257M 潜在世界模型，将 VLM 生成的潜在动作计划转换为预测未来表征，并以此条件化动作专家，统一潜在规划、未来预测与动作生成；世界模型直接使用真实机器人动作和反事实排序目标训练，无需像素重建或大规模视频预训练。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上成功率为 91.47%，LIBERO 上为 98.7%；在 LIBERO-Plus、LIBERO-Pro、LIBERO-Para 上分别为 88.0%、61.4%、75.0%；真实世界任务中将 π0.5 成功率从 38.3% 提升至 73.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，WAVE-0 能有效利用显式未来预测指导 VLA 动作生成，在多种分布偏移下实现强操作性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies excel at generalist robotic manipulation but generate actions without explicitly modeling their environmental consequences. World action models (WAMs) incorporate future prediction, yet most rely on costly video generation, while latent alternatives either use predicted futures only as auxiliary targets or infer latent actions from suboptimal visual transitions through costly large-scale video pretraining. We introduce WAVE-0 (World-model-Augmented VLA Execution), a framework that bridges VLAs and WAMs through a lightweight latent world model. By connecting the semantic understanding of pretrained VLAs with explicit future prediction, WAVE-0 allows anticipated action consequences to directly guide action generation. Built on a pretrained VLA, its 257M world model translates VLM-generated latent action plans into predicted future representations that condition the action expert, unifying latent planning, future prediction, and action generation. The world model is trained directly on ground-truth robot actions with a counterfactual ranking objective, requiring neither pixel reconstruction nor large-scale video pretraining. WAVE-0 achieves success rates of 91.47% on RoboTwin 2.0 and 98.7% on LIBERO, along with 88.0%, 61.4%, and 75.0% on LIBERO-Plus, LIBERO-Pro, and LIBERO-Para, respectively. On real-world tasks, it improves the success rate of $\pi_{0.5}$ from 38.3% to 73.3%. These results demonstrate strong manipulation performance and generalization across diverse distribution shifts.

</details>


### [62] [Behavioural self-monitoring for autonomous earth observation agents: The attribute reliability monitor (ARM)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18548)
*Alejandro D. Mousist*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出属性可靠性监控框架ARM，通过随时间累积可疑证据来评估单个语义属性的可靠性，并以卫星热红外图像的视觉语言模型进行黑盒监测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言与视觉语言模型可支撑自主系统，但运行条件变化可能损害其语义输出的可靠性，因此需要外部行为监控来判断模型输出是否可继续使用。

Method: ARM对每个语义属性累积怀疑证据，兼容不同检测器和时序监控方法，并将干预策略留给使用系统；初始实现采用黑盒监测，仅利用可观察响应，不访问模型内部状态、置信度分数或外部参考答案。

Result: 在受控输入退化条件下，模型响应之间的不一致为选择性暂停某些属性提供了证据。

Conclusion: 外部行为监控可支持对模型输出的持续使用，并有助于区分运行层面的怀疑与语义正确性。

Abstract: Language and vision language models can support autonomous systems, but changing operating conditions may compromise the reliability of their semantic outputs. This work introduces the Attribute Reliability Monitor (ARM), a framework that accumulates evidence of suspicion over time to assess individual semantic attributes. ARM accommodates different detectors and temporal monitoring methods, while leaving intervention policies to the consuming system. An initial implementation was evaluated using a specialized vision language model developed for satellite thermal infrared imagery. Monitoring followed a black box approach, using only observable responses without access to internal model states, confidence scores, or external reference answers. Under controlled input degradation, disagreement between responses provided evidence for selective suspension of attributes. The results illustrate how external behavioral monitoring can inform continued use of model outputs, while distinguishing operational suspicion from semantic correctness.

</details>


### [63] [APTree: Extended Behavior Trees for Adaptive and Scalable Automated Robotic Task Planning in Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18523)
*Shermin Sherkat,Thomas Wortmann,Andreas Wortmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: APTree 是一个面向离场建筑预制机器人任务规划的模型驱动框架，将自动任务规划与基于行为树的模块化执行结合，以支持规模化、模块化自动规划及运行时局部重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑预制机器人任务规划主要依赖人工，导致规划效率低且阻碍机器人方案采用；现有方法难以支持可扩展、模块化的自动任务规划以及局部运行时重规划。

Method: 提出 APTree：以行为树作为执行模型，在运行时反复应用并协调异构符号规划器，自动生成与组合模块化任务计划；开发用于任务计划形式化规范与验证的领域特定语言（DSL）及兼容执行引擎。

Result: 在真实 livMatS Biomimetic Shell 展亭上评估：建模建筑施工机器人制造领域，可在设计期与运行时检测注入的规范与规划错误，并通过协调三个符号规划器跨多个规划模块自动生成大规模任务计划；机器校验的形式化分析表明，在明确假设（规划器可靠性与生成计划非干扰性）下，组合执行与局部重规划是安全的。

Conclusion: APTree 使建筑从业者无需深入了解符号规划技术或框架内部实现，即可利用自动任务规划，并为可靠计划组合提供明确条件。

Abstract: Task planning for robots in off-site construction prefabrication is primarily manual, leading to planning inefficiencies and reluctance to adopt robotic solutions. Existing approaches provide limited support for scalable, modular automated task planning and localized runtime replanning. This paper proposes APTree, a model-driven framework that integrates automated task planning with modular task execution using behavior trees as the execution model. APTree repeatedly applies and coordinates heterogeneous symbolic planners to automatically generate and compose modular task plans at runtime. To support this approach, we develop a domain-specific language for the formal specification and validation of task plans, together with a compatible execution engine. We evaluate APTree on the real-world livMatS Biomimetic Shell pavilion, where it models the construction robotic fabrication domain, detects injected specification and planning errors at design time and runtime, and automatically generates large-scale task plans by coordinating three symbolic planners across numerous planning modules. A machine-checked formal analysis further shows that composed execution and local replanning are safe under explicitly stated assumptions, including planner soundness and non-interference of generated plans, providing practitioners with explicit conditions for dependable plan composition. APTree thus enables construction practitioners to leverage automated task planning without requiring detailed knowledge of symbolic planning techniques or APTree's internal implementation.

</details>


### [64] [RTISSLAM: In-air Imaging Sonar SLAM using a Robust Pose Graph and Occupancy Grid Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18479)
*Jan Steckel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RTISSLAM：一种面向嵌入式 3D 成像声呐的纯声呐 SLAM 系统，结合 BatSLAM 2.0 位姿图后端与占据栅格地图，在镜面反射环境中实现鲁棒定位与建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声呐 SLAM 依赖几何特征或稀疏距离读数构建占据栅格，难以应对室内声呐中占主导的镜面反射；而相机与激光雷达在黑暗、烟雾、灰尘及玻璃旁容易失效，声呐仍可工作。

Method: 利用延迟-相乘-求和波束形成生成的 energyscapes，并结合距离维 CFAR 作为地点描述子，进行序列验证的闭环检测；用声学里程计在线校准轮式里程计；对识别出的重访进行度量配准；占据栅格按关键帧存储并锚定到位姿图节点，从而随每次闭环更新。

Result: 在具有镜面墙壁的仿真世界中，RTISSLAM 将办公室场景下轮式里程计的轨迹误差从 8.2 m 降至 0.31 m；而在同一声呐数据上，激光雷达式栅格 SLAM 失败。

Conclusion: RTISSLAM 表明纯声呐 SLAM 可通过稳健后端、声学里程计校准、序列验证闭环与关键帧锚定栅格有效克服镜面反射，实现低漂移的建图与定位。

Abstract: Sonar was one of the first sensors with which mobile robots built maps, and in-air sonar keeps working where cameras and lidar fail, in darkness, smoke, dust and next to glass. Classical sonar SLAM, however, relied on geometric features or occupancy grids built from sparse range readings, and both struggle with the specular reflections that dominate indoor sonar. In this letter, we introduce RTISSLAM, a novel sonar-only SLAM system for an embedded 3D imaging sonar, which combines the robust pose graph of BatSLAM 2.0 as back-end with an occupancy grid as map. Energyscapes from delay-multiply-and-sum beamforming with a range-wise CFAR serve as place descriptors for sequence-verified loop closures, acoustic odometry calibrates the wheel odometry online, recognized revisits are registered metrically, and the grid is stored per keyframe and anchored to the pose-graph nodes, so that it follows every loop closure. In simulated worlds with specular walls, RTISSLAM reduces the trajectory error of the wheel odometry from \SI{8.2}{\meter} to \SI{0.31}{\meter} in an office, while lidar-style grid SLAM on the same sonar data fails.

</details>


### [65] [MemoryVLN: From Ego-Aware Memory Representation to Cross-Context Memory Reuse in Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18478)
*Xueyao Wei,Yifei Wang,Delun Li,Shaoan Wang,Junzhi Yu,Wenjie Song,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MemoryVLN：一种以记忆构建与管理为核心、面向长时程视觉语言导航（VLN）的统一记忆驱动导航系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在具身 VLN 中潜力显著，但在长时程轨迹上因上下文长度限制、历史观测冗余、跨时间空间对齐不稳定而性能下降；因此需要压缩长导航历史并可靠复用过去空间经验。

Method: MemoryVLN 包含记忆构建与记忆管理。构建阶段：Long Sequence Compression 采用遗忘曲线启发的时序采样，在有限 token 预算下保留密集近期证据与稀疏但具代表性的远期观测；Ego-Aware Memory Representation 融合 2D 语义 token、3D 几何 token 与 6D 智能体位姿，使记忆同时编码“看到了什么”和“在哪里看到”。管理阶段：Hierarchical Memory Bank 通过跨上下文热启动与置信度控制回写维护回合内与上下文级记忆；Memory Enhancement 根据当前导航状态检索 Top-K 相关记忆并与当前视觉 token 融合。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE 上取得一致提升，成功率分别较 SOTA 基线提升约 2% 与 6.5%。

Conclusion: 这些机制将原始导航历史转化为紧凑、空间 grounding、可选择性检索且可复用的导航经验，有效缓解长时程 VLN 中的上下文与空间对齐问题。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have shown strong potential for embodied vision-language navigation (VLN), yet their performance degrades on long-horizon trajectories due to context-length limits, redundant historical observations, and unstable spatial alignment across time. These issues make it difficult to both compress long navigation histories and reliably reuse past spatial experience. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{MemoryVLN}, a unified memory-driven navigation system built around memory construction and management. During memory construction, \emph{Long Sequence Compression} employs forgetting-curve-inspired temporal sampling to preserve dense recent evidence and sparse yet representative distant observations under a limited token budget. \emph{Ego-Aware Memory Representation} further integrates 2D semantic tokens, 3D geometric tokens, and 6D agent poses, allowing each memory to encode both what was observed and where it was observed. During memory management, \emph{Hierarchical Memory Bank} maintains in-episode and context-level memories through cross-context warm-start and confidence-controlled write-back. \emph{Memory Enhancement} then retrieves the Top-$K$ relevant memories according to the current navigation state and fuses them with the current visual tokens. Together, these mechanisms transform raw navigation history into compact, spatially grounded, selectively retrievable, and reusable navigation experience. Extensive experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE demonstrate consistent improvements over the state-of-the-art baselines by a substantial margin (i.e., 2\% and 6.5\% in successful rate).

</details>


### [66] [PACT: Posture-Aligned Co-Design Technique for Hand Configuration and Retargeting in Multi-Finger Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18443)
*Koyo Sasazaki,Satoshi Funabashi,Pranav Ponnivalavan,Alexander Schmitz,Tetsuya Ogata,Shigeki Sugano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACT 通过联合优化 17 自由度灵巧手的机械参数与非线性姿态重定向器，提升遥操作示教保真度、抓取性能与模仿学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作示教用于模仿学习时，其保真度取决于机器人手能否忠实复现操作者姿态；但姿态重定向律通常在硬件定型后才选择，且传统手部设计常忽视类人抓取姿态。

Method: 提出 PACT：以人类抓取姿态为基础，联合优化 17 舵机机器人手的机械参数（连杆长度、关节偏置、安装角、基座布局）与非线性姿态重定向器（线性人-机器人关节映射、逐关节姿态 IK 权重、阻尼）。优化以 DexYCB 中人体姿态为目标，强制全手指对应（每指 MCP、PIP、DIP 与指尖），并受舵机干涉、装配间隙、平手突出约束，保证可物理装配。随后制造 PACT Hand，并通过遥操作实验与模仿学习演示验证。

Result: PACT Hand 复现所有人类手关键点的运动学 RMSE 为 3.9 mm，优于 LEAP Hand 基线的 12.8 mm；每个抓取姿态下所有指尖误差均在 10 mm 内，而基线仅 21.6% 姿态达到该标准。在匹配的逐关节力矩限制下，其负载为基线的两倍，达 2 kg 对 1 kg；手持纸杯变形量小 7 倍，接近人手水平；在 YCB 日常物体上抓取成功率为 86%，基线为 64%。最后，使用 PACT Hand 采集示教训练的模仿学习策略，在罐体拾放任务的留出放置位置达到 75% 成功率。

Conclusion: PACT 表明，将机器人手形态与姿态重定向器联合优化，可显著提高遥操作姿态复现精度、抓取成功率、负载与物体保持能力，并改善下游模仿学习表现，且所得设计可实际制造与部署。

Abstract: The fidelity of teleoperated demonstrations used for imitation learning is determined by how faithfully a robot hand can replicate the operator's posture. The posture retargeting law that the robot will execute is typically selected only once the hardware has been finalized, and conventional hand designs often neglect human-like grasping postures. We introduce PACT, an optimization based on human grasping postures that jointly optimizes the mechanical parameters of a 17-servo robot hand (link lengths, joint offsets, mounting angles, and base placements) and its nonlinear posture retargeter (a linear human-to-robot joint map, per-joint posture-IK weights, and damping). The optimization enforces full-finger correspondence (MCP, PIP, DIP, and tip of each finger) to human poses from the DexYCB dataset, and is constrained by servo interference, assembly clearances, and flat-hand protrusion, so the resulting design can be physically assembled. We fabricate the PACT Hand and validate it through teleoperation experiments and an imitation-learning demonstration. The hand reproduces all human hand keypoints with a kinematic RMSE of 3.9 mm, compared to 12.8 mm for the LEAP Hand baseline, and keeps all fingertips within 10 mm on every grasping posture, while the baseline achieves this for only 21.6% of the poses. Under matched per-joint torque limits, it lifts twice the baseline payload, 2 kg against 1 kg, and deforms a held paper cup seven times less, reaching a deformation level comparable to a human hand. It also achieves an 86% grasping success rate on YCB daily objects, compared to 64% for the baseline. Finally, as a demonstration, an imitation-learning policy trained on demonstrations collected with the PACT Hand achieved a 75% success rate at held-out placements in a can pick-and-place task. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [67] [CALM: Decentralized Control of Cooperative Aerial Manipulation with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18425)
*Jiaxi Huo,Petre Ricioppo,Fausto Francesco Lizzio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CALM是一种去中心化多无人机空中操作控制器，用于携篮跟踪移动地面机器人；它结合MAPPO训练的参数共享MARL策略、目标估计、每机ESO、篮筐质量估计和前瞻队形偏航前馈，在风扰、负载运动与噪声目标广播下降低位置与姿态误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机通过共享负载耦合，合作空中操作在风扰、负载运动与噪声目标广播下控制困难，且需要在分散执行条件下实现鲁棒跟踪。

Method: 提出CALM：参数共享MARL策略，使用MAPPO训练；融合目标估计、每架无人机扩展状态观测器、篮筐质量估计和前瞻队形偏航前馈。前瞻队形偏航前馈利用估计的目标运动提前旋转队形，以减少篮筐偏航滞后且不增加净指令力；扰动条件critic支持集中训练、分散执行。

Result: 在留出轨迹上，CALM的平均位置与姿态误差低于所有评估基线，包括匹配机械臂基线（7.68 cm对10.95 cm，3.3°对4.7°）、缆索悬挂实现和两个模型基控制器；在未见的全信息路径退化情况下，CALM与机械臂基线统计相当或更优。

Conclusion: CALM表明，将学习策略、干扰观测与前瞻队形控制结合，可在分散执行下提升多无人机携篮跟踪的鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: Cooperative aerial manipulation is difficult under wind, payload motion, and noisy goal broadcasts because multiple Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are coupled through a shared payload. We present CALM (Cooperative Aerial Learned Manipulation), a decentralized controller for aerial manipulators carrying a basket while tracking a moving ground robot. CALM combines a parameter-shared Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) policy, trained using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO), with goal estimation, per-UAV Extended State Observers (ESOs), basket-mass estimation, and anticipatory formation-yaw feedforward. The anticipatory formation-yaw feedforward uses estimated goal motion to rotate the formation before position error develops, reducing basket-yaw lag without adding net commanded force. A disturbance-conditioned critic supports centralized training while execution remains decentralized. Across held-out trajectories, CALM achieves lower mean position and orientation errors than all evaluated baselines, including the matched arm baseline (7.68 versus 10.95 cm and 3.3 versus 4.7$^\circ$), the cable-suspended realization, and two model-based controllers. Under unseen full-information-path degradation, CALM remains statistically comparable or superior to the arm baseline. Videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [68] [Intent-guided Adaptive Stiffening for Visuomotor Policies under Unknown Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18386)
*Francesco Cufino,Carmela Mariniello,Mario Selvaggio,Andreas Sochopoulos,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需力传感的残差增刚层，通过策略指令运动与实际运动的不匹配识别阻力，并沿策略动作块指示的单一方向提高刚度，从而提升高摩擦操作任务的成功率并降低离轴力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视觉运动策略能预测运动，但无法提供克服高摩擦阻力所需的力权限；底层阻抗控制器在有限跟踪误差下可施加的力有限，且所需力取决于观测未揭示的接触状态，因此当阻力超过柔顺控制器能力时，任务即使预测准确也无法完成。

Method: 在未修改的策略下方加入残差增刚层。无需力传感，通过策略指令运动与实际实现运动之间的不匹配识别阻力；四模式监督器从策略自身动作块中读取单一方向，提高该方向刚度，在任务推进期间保持，并在任务完成后释放。该方法无需柔顺性标签、无需重新训练、无需重新采集演示。

Result: 在两个真实任务（拧开瓶盖、沿T型槽抵抗非均匀摩擦推动T型螺母）上，于六种阻力水平评估，其中两种阻力水平未出现在演示中；该层将成功率从52%提升至90%，且在相同阻力下，峰值离轴力比统一增刚控制器减少61%。

Conclusion: 该残差增刚层可在不改变原策略、无需力传感和重新训练的情况下，为视觉运动策略提供克服高摩擦阻力所需的力权限，提高任务成功率并减少非期望方向的力。

Abstract: Manipulation tasks such as unscrewing a cap or sliding a part in contact with a surface require imposing force along a high-friction direction: the robot must overcome a resistance rather than yield to it. Generative visuomotor policies predict the motion for such tasks but not the authority needed to realize it, and that authority is bounded by the impedance controller beneath them: when the resistance exceeds what a compliant controller can exert under a bounded tracking error, the task is unattainable however accurate the prediction is. The required amount cannot be anticipated either, since it depends on a contact state the observation does not reveal. In this paper, we propose a residual stiffening layer placed underneath an unmodified policy. Resistance is recognized, without force sensing, from the mismatch between the motion the policy commands and the motion the robot realizes, and a four-mode supervisor raises the stiffness along a single direction read off the policy's own action chunk, retains it while the task progresses and releases it afterwards. The layer needs no compliance labels, no retraining and no re-collected demonstrations. On two real tasks, unscrewing a bottle cap and pushing a T-nut along a T-slot groove against non-homogeneous friction, evaluated at six resistance levels of which two were withheld from the demonstrations, the layer raises the success rate from 52% to 90% while producing 61% less peak off-axis force than a uniformly stiff controller at the same resistance.

</details>


### [69] [Benchmarking Interaction-Oriented Aerial Mobility across Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18381)
*Zhibo Duan,Hanlin Kong,Tianran Ma,Yuze Wu,Yuxuan Xu,Kenghou Hoi,Changheng Xie,Xin Zhou,Jianzhe Gao,Fei Gao,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CrossFly，一个面向跨异构环境、长时程且以交互为导向的空中移动操作基准，包含 15K 轨迹、20+ 重建场景与 Interaction Readiness Rate（IRR）指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中基准多局限于单一环境，难以评估智能体在连续环境转换中的表现；且交互评估常从目标附近初始化，基本未考察从导航到交互的过渡过程。

Method: 在统一仿真与评估框架下构建 CrossFly，涵盖 Indoor、Outdoor 及 Indoor↔Outdoor 轨迹；提出 IRR 以评估终端导航状态是否为下游交互提供合适条件，且不假设特定操作策略；测试 5 个代表性空中 VLA 智能体及 CrossFly-Agent。

Result: 实验表明 Indoor↔Outdoor 设置比单一环境更具挑战性，在未见场景中最高成功率为 9.7%；下游操作案例研究进一步验证了交互导向到达对后续物理交互的相关性。

Conclusion: CrossFly 有望推动空中具身智能在异构环境中实现统一移动与物理交互。

Abstract: Aerial mobile manipulation requires unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to traverse heterogeneous environments such as indoor and outdoor domains, and connect navigation with subsequent physical interaction within the same trajectories. Existing aerial benchmarks are typically confined to single-environment settings, limiting evaluation of agents across continuous environmental transitions. When interaction is evaluated, agents are often initialized near the target, leaving the transition from navigation to interaction largely unassessed. This work introduces CrossFly, a benchmark for long-horizon interaction-oriented aerial mobility across heterogeneous environments. CrossFly contains 15K trajectories across more than 20 reconstructed scenes under a unified simulation and evaluation framework, covering Indoor, Outdoor, and Indoor$\leftrightarrow$Outdoor trajectories. It further introduces Interaction Readiness Rate (IRR) to assess whether terminal navigation states provide suitable conditions for downstream interaction without assuming a specific manipulation policy. Experiments with five representative aerial vision-language-action (VLA) agents and the proposed CrossFly-Agent show that Indoor$\leftrightarrow$Outdoor settings are more challenging than single-environment settings, with the highest success rate reaching 9.7% on unseen scenes. A downstream manipulation case study further validates the relevance of interaction-oriented arrival to subsequent physical interaction. We hope CrossFly helps advance aerial embodied intelligence toward unified mobility and physical interaction in heterogeneous environments.

</details>


### [70] [DR-VLA: Learning Human-Like Quadruped Navigation from Egocentric Web Videos via Domain-Invariant Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18373)
*Jun Tian,Yingjie Niu,Ruo Chen,Hongyi Lin,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DR-VLA：一种基于Mixture-of-Transformers的框架，通过领域不变场景表示DISR将人类第一视角导航经验迁移到四足机器人，并融合视觉语言理解、潜在世界建模与流匹配动作专家，在EgoLynx数据集上取得优于竞争方法的导航表现，并在NAVSIM v1上达到92.1 PDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人户外配送需要理解复杂场景、预判动态变化并以社交合规方式导航；机器人演示数据采集成本高、规模难以支撑泛化。第一视角网络视频蕴含丰富人类导航经验，但视角与运动差异导致其难以直接迁移到机器人。

Method: 提出DR-VLA框架，采用Mixture-of-Transformers，并通过领域不变场景表示DISR迁移人类导航经验；框架结合视觉语言理解、潜在世界建模与流匹配动作专家。构建EgoLynx数据集，包括10,000小时精选第一视角网络视频EgoLynx-Web和2,000段真实配送遥操作机器人导航EgoLynx-Robot。先在无动作标签的EgoLynx-Web上预训练理解与世界专家，再适配机器人观测，并在EgoLynx-Robot上训练动作专家以预测可执行轨迹。同时提出伪闭环指标QNS，综合安全、指令遵循、舒适性与进度。

Result: 在留出的EgoLynx-Robot测试集上，DR-VLA较该指标最佳竞争结果将QNS提升约5%，并将开环最终朝向误差FHE降低14.6%。消融表明网络视频预训练提升泛化能力，关键组件均有贡献。作为未来预测目标，DISR优于五种通用视觉表示，并在无需专用几何标签或点云构建目标的情况下接近任务专用3D感知。另在NAVSIM v1上不进行网络预训练单独训练，达到92.1 PDMS。

Conclusion: 结果表明，DR-VLA能够有效利用第一视角网络视频中的人类导航经验，提升四足机器人户外配送导航的性能与泛化能力；其框架还具有推广到自动驾驶等领域的潜力。

Abstract: Quadruped robots performing outdoor deliveries must interpret complex scenes, anticipate changes, and navigate in socially compliant ways. Robot demonstrations are costly to collect at the scale needed for generalization. Egocentric web videos offer abundant human navigation experience, but differences in viewpoint and motion make this experience difficult to transfer to robots. We introduce DR-VLA, a Mixture-of-Transformers framework that transfers human navigation experience through a domain-invariant scene representation (DISR). The framework combines vision-language understanding and latent world modeling with a flow-matching action expert. We introduce EgoLynx, comprising 10,000 hours of curated egocentric web video (EgoLynx-Web) and 2,000 clips of teleoperated robot navigation collected during real-world deliveries (EgoLynx-Robot). We pretrain the understanding and world experts on EgoLynx-Web without action labels, then adapt the model to robot observations and train the action expert on EgoLynx-Robot to predict executable trajectories. We also propose the Quadruped Navigation Score (QNS), a pseudo-closed-loop metric combining safety, instruction compliance, comfort, and progress. On the held-out EgoLynx-Robot test split, DR-VLA improves QNS by approximately 5% and reduces open-loop final heading error (FHE) by 14.6%, each relative to the best competing result for that metric. Ablations show that web-video pretraining improves generalization and that the model's key components contribute to performance. As a future-prediction target, DISR outperforms five generic visual representations and yields performance close to task-specific 3D perception without dedicated geometric labels or point clouds for target construction. Trained separately on NAVSIM v1 without web pretraining, DR-VLA achieves 92.1 PDMS, suggesting that the framework also applies to autonomous driving.

</details>


### [71] [Visual Sevoing from Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18363)
*Nicolas Andreff,Bernard Espiau,Radu Horaud*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双归一化Plücker坐标的直线视觉伺服新方法，并设计具有部分解耦、解析逆和全局渐近稳定性的控制律，且在正交三面体情形下完成仿真与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直线视觉伺服中，直线表示方式难以定义合适的图像直线对齐概念，因此需要研究新的直线表示以设计性能良好的控制律。

Method: 对主要直线表示进行理论与几何研究，提出双归一化Plücker坐标；基于该表示定义图像直线对齐概念，并推导实现该对齐的控制律，分析其解耦性、解析逆和稳定性。

Result: 所提控制律具有旋转与平移部分解耦、运动方程可解析求逆以及全局渐近稳定条件；并在正交三面体这一特定情形下通过仿真和实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 双归一化Plücker坐标适合用于直线视觉伺服，所提控制律有效且具备良好理论性质，实验验证了其可行性。

Abstract: In this work, we present a fundamentally new approach to visual servoing using lines. It is based on a theoretical and geometrical study of the main line representations which allowed us to define a new representation, the so-called binormalized Plucker coordinates. They are particularly well suited to visual servoing. Indeed, they allow the definition of a proper image line alignment concept. The control law which realizes such an alignment has moreover several properties: partial decoupling between rotation and translation, analytical inversion of the motion equations and global asymptotic stability conditions. This control law was validated both in simulation and experimentally in the specific case of an orthogonal trihedron.

</details>


### [72] [Let the Mind Conceive, Let the Hand Achieve: Following Multimodal Instructions for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18352)
*Youpeng Wen,Gang Wang,Tianyu Huang,Weiwei Zhang,Zhijian Huang,Wei Chen,Xieyuanli Chen,Yunhui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Orchestra 是一种将任务规划与执行管理同动作生成解耦的机器人智能体框架，用于多步指令下的长序列子任务执行，并通过进度评估器决定何时推进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人执行多步指令时，需要在场景变化中按正确顺序操作正确对象，并持续跟踪任务进度、判断何时进入下一子任务；而仅在原子指令和短轨迹上训练的机器人难以直接完成更长子任务序列。

Method: Orchestra 将完整多模态指令分解为有序子任务，跟踪已完成步骤，并在执行中更新相关对象与目的地位置；它维护包含当前子任务、空间 grounding 和近期观测的共享上下文，动作策略据此生成动作，单独训练的进度评估器则估计何时推进至下一子任务。为评估泛化能力，作者提出 RMBench-Compose 基准，用于测试原子指令和短轨迹训练的机器人执行更长子任务序列的能力。

Result: Orchestra 在九个 RMBench 任务上平均成功率为 68.22%，比 Mem-0 高 26.22 个百分点；在 RMBench-Compose 上平均成功长度（ASL）为 7.35 个子任务，是 HarnessVLA 的 2.46 倍；在四个真实世界多步任务中，相比使用基础 π0.5 策略的 Orchestra，平均 ASL 提升 25.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，Orchestra 能帮助机器人执行更长的子任务序列，同时保持所要求的子任务顺序并操作指定对象。代码与项目页面已公开。

Abstract: A robot following a multi-step instruction needs to act on the right objects in the right order, even as the scene changes. It must also maintain awareness of task progress and determine when to advance. To address these challenges, we introduce Orchestra, an agent harness that separates task planning and execution management from action generation. Orchestra decomposes a complete multimodal instruction into ordered subtasks, tracks completed steps, and updates the locations of relevant objects and destinations during execution. It maintains a shared context containing the active subtask, spatial grounding, and recent observations. An action policy uses this context to generate actions, while a separately trained progress evaluator estimates when to advance to the next subtask. To evaluate whether robots trained on atomic instructions and short trajectories can execute longer subtask sequences, we introduce a benchmark named RMBench-Compose. Orchestra achieves a mean success rate of $68.22\%$ across nine RMBench tasks, outperforming Mem-0 by $26.22$ percentage points. On RMBench-Compose, it achieves an Average Success Length (ASL) of $7.35$ subtasks, $2.46$ times that of HarnessVLA. Across four real-world multi-step tasks, it improves mean ASL by $25.0\%$ over Orchestra using the base $\pi_{0.5}$ policy. These results show that Orchestra helps robots carry out longer sequences of subtasks while preserving the requested subtask order and acting on the specified objects. Our code and project page are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [73] [CoSMO: Conformal Semantic Mapping for Open-World Safe Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18321)
*Shuqi Wang,Yiwei Zheng,Pingrui Zhang,Yue Gao,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoSMO 是一个面向开放世界安全机器人导航的共形语义建图框架，可将 VLM 嵌入转化为动态扩展风险词表上的校准覆盖集与安全感知障碍膨胀，在分布偏移下提供覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全自主导航不仅需要几何避障，还需语义感知：不同物体类别需要不同的安全间距，且相关类别会随机器人探索新环境而扩展。VLM 虽能提供丰富语义表示，但其置信度校准较差，原始预测难以用于安全关键规划；现有共形预测方法假设固定标签集与平稳数据，限制其在开放世界中的应用。

Method: 提出 CoSMO：将 VLM 嵌入转换为校准的覆盖集，并在动态扩展的风险词表上生成安全感知的障碍膨胀，即使在分布偏移下也适用；方法基于共形预测提供覆盖保证，并处理延迟专家反馈等实际约束。

Result: 在 Matterport3D 数据集上，CoSMO 相比仅几何基线将语义碰撞减少 61.6%，并在延迟专家反馈下仍保持形式化覆盖保证。四足机器人真实世界实验中，长期经验覆盖率达到 90.73%，超过 90% 目标，展示了泛化能力。项目网站：this https URL。

Conclusion: CoSMO 通过将 VLM 语义嵌入与共形预测结合，实现了开放世界中动态风险类别下的安全导航，在仿真与真实机器人上均验证了碰撞减少、覆盖保证与泛化性能。

Abstract: Safe autonomous navigation requires not only geometric obstacle avoidance but also semantic awareness. Different object categories demand different safety clearances, and the set of relevant categories may expand as the robot explores novel environments. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer rich semantic representations for building maps of unknown scenes, yet their confidence scores are poorly calibrated, making raw predictions unreliable for safety-critical planning. Conformal prediction (CP) can provide coverage guarantees, but existing methods assume a fixed label set and stationary data, limiting their use in open-world settings. We propose Conformal Semantic Mapping for Open-World Safe Robot Navigation (CoSMO), a semantic safety mapping framework that transforms VLM embeddings into calibrated coverage sets and safety-aware obstacle dilations over a dynamically expanding hazard vocabulary, even under distribution shift. Experiments on the Matterport3D (MP3D) dataset show that CoSMO reduces semantic collisions by 61.6% relative to a geometry-only baseline, while maintaining formal coverage guarantees even under delayed expert feedback. Real-world experiments on a quadruped robot further demonstrate generalization by achieving long-run empirical coverage of 90.73% against the 90% target. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [74] [I2O2I-Nav: A Benchmark with a Modular Agent for Long-Horizon Indoor-to-Outdoor-to-Indoor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18307)
*Zhengjie Shu,Wanyu Li,Di Xiao,Lingxin Jiang,Lai Jiang,Kaidi Hu,Xincheng Tang,Wenkang Hu,Haoran Wang,Jinchuan Zhang,Chang Chen,Wei Li,Jia Pan,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出物理仿真基准 I2O2I-Nav 与 MODULAR-Agent，用于评估室内-室外-室内（I2O2I）连续长程导航中异构能力的组合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航基准多评估孤立阶段或部分跨域行程，难以衡量连续执行下不同导航能力如何组合；而服务与配送机器人常需完成跨室内外的长程任务。

Method: 构建 20 个真实复合场景、覆盖超 115,000 平方米、多建筑多楼层，并对齐 3DGS 渲染与碰撞几何。评估分三级：独立原语、两阶段组合、完整四阶段 I2O2I；每阶段从前一阶段实际终点开始，并用阶段条件指标跟踪完成与失败定位。提出 MODULAR-Agent，通过完成验证、阶段交接和有界恢复协调冻结导航策略。

Result: 在 1,374 个 episode 中，MODULAR-Agent 在单个原语上成功率为 40.5%–76%，但完整 I2O2I 成功率仍低。相比使用相同底层策略的固定策略链，它在 I2O 和 O2I 上成功率翻倍以上，并将全任务成功率从 1.15% 提升至 4.02%。阶段分析表明更多任务能到达最终物体导航阶段，但该阶段条件成功率仍低。

Conclusion: 连续组合仍是主要难点，长程阶段执行与完成决策存在挑战；模块化协调可显著改善跨阶段组合，但完整 I2O2I 任务尚未解决。

Abstract: Service and delivery robots often need to complete long-horizon navigation missions spanning indoor and outdoor environments. Existing embodied navigation benchmarks primarily evaluate isolated stages or partial cross-domain journeys, providing limited support for assessing how heterogeneous navigation capabilities compose under continuous execution. We introduce I2O2I-Nav, a physics-enabled benchmark of 20 real-world composite scenes spanning over 115,000 square meters across multiple buildings and floors, with aligned 3DGS rendering and collision geometry. Its three-level evaluation progresses from independent primitives to two-stage compositions and complete four-stage I2O2I missions. Each stage starts from its predecessor's actual endpoint, while stage-conditional metrics track completion and localize failures along the mission. We further propose the MODULAR-Agent that coordinates frozen navigation policies through completion verification, stage handoffs, and bounded recovery. Evaluation across 1,374 episodes highlights the difficulty of continuous composition. The MODULAR-Agent achieves 40.5-76% success on individual primitives, but full I2O2I success remains low. Compared with a fixed policy chain using the same underlying policies, it more than doubles success on both Indoor-to-Outdoor (I2O) and Outdoor-to-Indoor (O2I), while improving full-mission success from 1.15% to 4.02%. Stage-wise analysis shows that the MODULAR-Agent enables more missions to reach the final object-navigation stage, where conditional success remains low, revealing challenges in both long-horizon stage execution and completion decisions.

</details>


### [75] [Cross-Embodiment Affordance Transfer for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18194)
*Sawera Yaseen,Serge Thill,Rekha Raja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将人类可供性标注转化为机器人可执行可供性预测的框架，依据机器人几何与约束判断物体表面区域是否可被特定机器人操作，并在UR5e与平行夹爪上验证其优于不感知机器人的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类标注的可供性反映人手交互可能性，而非特定机器人的物理约束；被标注为可抓取的区域对机器人可能几何不可达或机械不可行，这一不匹配亟需显式建模。

Method: 给定物体3D点云和编码机器人几何与约束的描述子，训练网络预测机器人物理可执行的表面区域；训练数据由人类可供性标注与仿真中多次采样接近试验的机器人执行结果共同构成，执行结果用于确定可执行区域。

Result: 在家庭物品数据集上，使用UR5e机械臂与两种平行夹爪配置评估；所提框架优于不接收机器人信息的模型。

Conclusion: 可供性预测必须以机器人为条件，仅依赖人类标注不足以学习机器人可执行的可供性表示。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation systems commonly learn object affordances from human-annotated datasets, which reflect interaction possibilities for a human hand rather than the physical constraints of a specific robot. For example, a surface region of an object identified as graspable by a human annotator may be geometrically unreachable or mechanically infeasible for a robot, yet this mismatch is rarely addressed explicitly. To tackle this challenge, we proposed a framework that translates human affordance annotations into robot-executable affordance predictions conditioned on the physical properties of the robot. The network predicts which surface regions of an object are physically executable by the robot, given a 3D point cloud of an object and a descriptor encoding the robot's geometry and constraints. The model was trained using human-annotated affordances together with robotic execution outcomes from multiple sampled approach trials in simulation, where execution outcomes determined which regions are executable for the robot. The evaluation was performed on a household objects dataset using two parallel-jaw gripper configurations on a UR5e robot arm. The framework outperformed a model that does not receive information about the robot. The experimental results demonstrated that affordance prediction must be conditioned on the robot and that human annotations alone are insufficient for learning robot-executable affordance representations.

</details>


### [76] [OneDemo: Unified Policy Learning and Data Generation from a Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18188)
*Ying Qian,Jianshu Hu,Han Fang,Shuguang Li,Yutong Ban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OneDemo 是一个强化学习框架，通过编辑单条成功演示来适应新任务配置，既能学习任务策略，也能生成可复用演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人从演示学习策略常受限于采集多样高质量专家数据的成本；现有单演示增强方法多依赖手工变换规则或复杂轨迹规划，需要大量任务特定工程和人工投入。

Method: 将演示编辑形式化为结构化强化学习问题：编辑器从稀疏任务成功奖励中学习何时在物体中心轨迹段之间切换，以及如何对每个轨迹段进行几何适配；学习到的编辑器既可作为任务策略，也可作为可复用的演示生成器。

Result: 在 15 个 Meta-World 任务上，OneDemo 平均仅用 53.7k 次环境交互达到 92.7% 成功率；在 9 个 DexMimicGen 任务上，生成演示平均成功率为 98.4%，在匹配数据规模下训练的 BC-RNN 策略平均成功率达 99.6%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学习编辑单条成功演示为策略学习和可扩展演示生成提供了结构化且有效的基础。

Abstract: Learning robot policies from demonstrations is often limited by the cost of collecting diverse, high-quality expert data. While an appealing alternative is to expand a single successful demonstration into a diverse set of successful trajectories, existing augmentation methods typically rely on manually designed transformation rules or complex trajectory planning, requiring substantial task-specific engineering and human effort. We introduce OneDemo, a reinforcement learning framework that learns to edit a single successful demonstration for new task configurations. We formulate demonstration editing as a structured reinforcement learning problem, in which an editor learns, from sparse task-success rewards, both when to transition between object-centric trajectory segments and how to geometrically adapt each segment. The learned editor serves both as a task policy and as a reusable demonstration generator. Across 15 Meta-World tasks, OneDemo reaches a 92.7% success rate within 53.7k environment interactions on average. Across 9 DexMimicGen tasks, it generates successful demonstrations with a 98.4% average success rate, and BC-RNN policies trained on the generated data achieve 99.6% average success at matched dataset scale. These results show that learning to edit a single successful demonstration provides a structured and effective basis for both policy learning and scalable demonstration generation.

</details>


### [77] [Beyond Planning: Differentiable Control-in-the-loop End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18167)
*Zhidong Wang,Zirui Li,Zitong Shan,Zhiqi Mao,Qihang Huang,Zhan Chen,Jingsong Liang,Han Yu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DIVA 将辨识得到的执行器级车辆模型嵌入端到端自动驾驶训练回路，用可学习控制解码器替代手调跟踪控制器，解决规划到控制的不可微、监督混叠、频率不匹配与模型失配问题；在 Bench2Drive 上显著提升性能、平顺性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶虽可直接从原始传感器规划轨迹，但规划到控制仍依赖手调跟踪控制器，阻断从车辆运动到网络的梯度；核心障碍包括车辆动力学不可微、粗采样专家标签导致控制监督混叠、高频率控制与低频率重规划速率不匹配、训练与部署车辆模型存在差异。

Method: 提出 DIVA：在端到端驾驶网络训练循环中嵌入辨识的执行器级车辆模型，构建从感知到车辆状态的可微链路；以学习型控制解码器替代外部控制器，并基于滚动展开的车辆状态监督，绕过混叠的控制输入标签；采用速率一致的控制输入参数化统一异构采样率，支持异步执行，控制周期仅 3.8 ms；通过在线策略重辨识缓解训练时分布偏移，并用在线扰动观测器应对部署时模型失配。

Result: 在闭环 Bench2Drive 基准上，DIVA 取得 86.7 驾驶得分，较广泛采用的最先进方法高 3.3 分；平均 jerk 接近减半，制动切换减少 64%，并在部署时显著车辆模型失配下仍保持更优路径跟踪精度。

Conclusion: DIVA 通过可微且可辨识的车辆动力学闭环打通端到端规划与控制，提升驾驶性能、乘坐舒适性与控制稳定性，并增强对训练—部署模型差异的鲁棒性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has achieved remarkable progress by planning trajectories directly from raw sensor data. Yet the step from planning to control still relies on hand-tuned tracking controllers that block the gradient path from vehicle motion back to the network. This gap stems from non-differentiable vehicle dynamics, aliased supervision from coarsely sampled expert labels, rate mismatch between high-rate control and low-rate re-planning, and vehicle model discrepancies between training and deployment. This paper proposes DIVA (Differentiable Identified Vehicle dynamics for end-to-end Autonomous driving) to close this gap. An identified actuator-level vehicle model is embedded in the training loop of the end-to-end driving network, forming a differentiable chain from perception to vehicle states. A learning-based control decoder replaces the external controller and is supervised on the rolled-out vehicle states, bypassing the aliased control-input labels. A rate-consistent control-input parameterization reconciles the heterogeneous sampling rates and enables asynchronous execution with a 3.8 ms control-only cycle. On-policy re-identification and an online disturbance observer address training-time distribution shift and deployment-time model mismatch respectively. On the closed-loop Bench2Drive benchmark, DIVA achieves a driving score of 86.7, surpassing the widely adopted state-of-the-art approach by 3.3 points while nearly halving mean jerk and reducing brake switching by 64%, and maintains superior path tracking accuracy under significant vehicle model mismatch at deployment.

</details>


### [78] [RESTA: Time-Scaling Augmentation for Data-Efficient Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18163)
*Yunpeng Jiang,Jianshu Hu,Han Fang,Zhiyuan Guan,Paul Weng,Yutong Ban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RESTA 通过轨迹反转与时间步长重采样进行时间尺度数据增强，提升机器人模仿学习的数据效率；在16个双臂操作任务中，25和50条演示即达到78.4%和85.2%平均成功率，匹配或超过双倍数据基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略的模仿学习通常依赖昂贵演示，且演示多局限于单一任务方向和单一时间分辨率，导致数据效率低。

Method: 提出 Reversal and Stride-based Trajectory Augmentation (RESTA)，利用时间尺度数据增强。轨迹反转增强将成对相反目标任务间的演示反转并重新标注，复用已有 rollout，无需额外环境交互；因接触丰富操作并非精确可逆，反转被视作近似成对任务先验而非物理对称。时间步长增强改变观测与动作采样步长，并以相应偏移为策略条件，支持固定或随机时间调度。

Result: 在16个成对双臂操作任务上，轨迹反转在低数据场景尤其有效，时间步长增强在随机时间调度下提升成功率。每任务25和50条独立演示时，RESTA 平均成功率为78.4%和85.2%，匹配或超过使用两倍演示的基线（77.3%和85.4%）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，RESTA 能有效提升机器人模仿学习中的数据效率。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies trained through imitation learning commonly rely on costly demonstrations recorded in one task direction and at one temporal resolution. We propose Reversal and Stride-based Trajectory Augmentation (RESTA), which exploits time-scaling data augmentation to improve data efficiency through trajectory reversal and temporal resampling. Trajectory-reversal augmentation reverses and relabels demonstrations between paired tasks with opposite objectives, reusing recorded rollouts without additional environment interaction. Because contact-rich manipulation is not exactly reversible, we treat reversal as an approximate paired-task prior rather than a physical symmetry. Temporal-stride augmentation varies observation and action sampling strides and conditions the policy on the resulting offsets, enabling fixed or randomized temporal schedules. Experiments across 16 paired bimanual manipulation tasks show that trajectory reversal is particularly effective in the low-data regime and that temporal-stride augmentation improves success under randomized temporal schedules. With 25 and 50 independently collected demonstrations per task, RESTA achieves 78.4% and 85.2% average success, respectively, matching or exceeding baselines trained with twice as many demonstrations (77.3% and 85.4%). These results show that RESTA can improve data efficiency in robot imitation learning.

</details>


### [79] [Force Sensor-Based Picking Pattern Analysis and Two-Jaw Gripper Design for Cherry Tomato Robotic Harvesting in Greenhouse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18160)
*Rajmeet Singh,Manveen Kaur,Yusuf Adamu,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种力数据驱动的自主樱桃番茄采摘框架，集成六轴力/扭矩传感、仿生柔性夹爪和移动机器人平台，在温室中实现86.0%采摘成功率、2.3%损伤率和8.4 s/果周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温室樱桃番茄机器人采摘面临果簇密集、果皮娇嫩以及需轻柔且有效脱果等挑战，现有末端执行器在成功率与损伤控制方面仍有不足。

Method: 通过佩戴式ATI六轴力/扭矩传感器采集150次人工试验中抓取、扭转和侧向拉拔三种基本采摘动作的力觉特征；据此设计指尖局部Finray夹爪，接触区采用TPU 95A柔性材料、本体采用PLA刚性材料，模拟人手双刚度指尖；使用Dynamixel XM430-W350电机进行电流扭矩控制，施加测得抓取力及45°腕部扭转；有限元分析显示3.92 N载荷下最大von Mises应力0.24 MPa，低于番茄表皮损伤阈值；系统集成于UR5机械臂与Clearpath Husky A200底盘，并在NVIDIA Isaac Sim和SILAL商业温室50次自主试验中验证。

Result: 混合抓-扭-拉策略取得86.0%采摘成功率、2.3%果实损伤率和8.4 s单果周期；优于真空式66.3%、旋转式70.1%、夹持式76.0%和吸切式52.4%末端执行器。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在测试设施和品种条件下，该力数据驱动采摘框架具有初步可行性。

Abstract: Robotic harvesting of cherry tomatoes in greenhouses remains challenging due to dense fruit clusters, delicate skin, and the need for gentle yet effective detachment. This study presents a force-data-driven framework for autonomous cherry tomato harvesting that integrates six-axis force/torque sensing, a bio-inspired compliant gripper, and deployment on a mobile robotic platform. A wearable ATI six-axis force/torque sensor mounted on the index finger captured kinetic signatures of three elemental picking patterns (gripping, twisting, and lateral pulling) across 150 manual trials (50 per pattern) in laboratory and greenhouse settings. Gripping required a peak normal force of 3.92 and 0.45 N, twisting produced a dominant torque of 19.20 2.10 Nmm about the pedicel axis with minimal truss disturbance, and pure lateral pulling alone was insufficient for reliable detachment. These measurements informed a novel fingertip-localised Finray gripper, with compliant TPU 95A Finray geometry restricted to the fruit-contact region and a rigid PLA body, emulating the dual-stiffness human fingertip. A Dynamixel XM430-W350 servomotor under current-based torque control applies the measured gripping force and a 45° wrist twist. Finite element analysis showed a maximum von Mises stress of 0.24 MPa under the 3.92 N load, below the bruising threshold of cherry tomato skin. Integrated on a UR5 arm and Clearpath Husky A200 base, the system was validated in NVIDIA Isaac Sim and in 50 autonomous trials at the SILAL commercial greenhouse, Abu Dhabi, UAE. The hybrid grip-twist-pull strategy achieved an 86.0% picking success rate, 2.3% fruit damage, and an 8.4 s cycle time per fruit, outperforming vacuum (66.3%), rotating (70.1%), clamping (76.0%), and suction-cutting (52.4%) end-effectors. These results demonstrate preliminary feasibility under the tested facility and cultivar conditions

</details>


### [80] [Walk Like This: Stylized Locomotion for Physics-Based Characters through Example-Driven Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18147)
*Joshua Aurand,Agon Serifi,Sammy Christen,Ruben Grandia,Espen Knoop,Marco Hutter,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种实时表达性腿部运动框架，可独立控制速度与风格；利用大规模无结构运动数据训练风格编码器，并以其输出条件化自回归扩散模型，生成运动学参考供强化学习控制器跟踪，在仿真与实体人形机器人上实现鲁棒实时控制与连续风格调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动控制器通常需要显式风格标签进行训练，难以利用大规模无标注运动数据，且难以泛化到未见风格；同时，速度与风格往往难以独立控制。

Method: 通过定制采样策略将风格与功能性运动解耦，训练风格编码器；编码器输出条件化自回归扩散模型。推理时用户提供风格参考片段与摇杆速度指令，扩散模型生成运动学参考，再由强化学习控制器跟踪。为提升可控性与风格化强度，引入注意力偏置机制和速度指令校正策略。

Result: 在基于物理的人形角色仿真和实体机器人上验证了方法，展示出鲁棒的实时控制与连续风格调制能力；补充视频提供最佳效果展示。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效利用无结构运动数据、泛化到未见风格，并实现速度与风格的独立实时控制，适用于仿真与真实人形机器人平台。

Abstract: We present a real-time framework for expressive legged locomotion that enables independent control over velocity and style. Unlike existing data-driven controllers, which typically require explicit style labels for training, our method efficiently leverages large-scale unstructured motion data and generalizes to unseen styles. We disentangle style from functional locomotion through a tailored sampling strategy used to train a style encoder whose output conditions an autoregressive diffusion model. During inference, the user provides a style reference clip and a joystick velocity command. The diffusion model then generates a kinematic reference tracked by a reinforcement learning controller. To improve user controllability and stylization strength, we propose an attention biasing mechanism and a velocity command correction strategy. We demonstrate our method on a physics-based humanoid character in simulation and on a physical robot, showcasing robust real-time control and continuous style modulation. The results can be best seen in our supplemental video ( this https URL ).

</details>


### [81] [SkipWorld: Latent Skip Planning with Distilled World Models for Efficient Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18135)
*Yifei Yang,Zehua Fan,Zike Yan,Zhongyu Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SkipWorld，一种用于高效视觉导航的潜在跳跃规划框架，通过潜空间自回归展开、跳跃世界模型蒸馏与由粗到精规划，在保持轨迹评估精度的同时显著降低规划计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于世界模型的视觉导航通过预测候选轨迹下的未来状态进行动作规划，但长时程 rollout 面临训练与闭环推理不匹配、以及逐步评估大量候选轨迹计算代价高的问题。

Method: SkipWorld 包含三部分：(i) Latent AutoRoll，在潜空间中递归展开未来状态，减少训练与闭环推理之间的不匹配；(ii) SkipDistill，将潜自回归模型蒸馏为跳跃世界模型，直接预测长步未来潜状态；(iii) Skip-then-Refine Planning，采用双模型由粗到精规划，先用跳跃模型筛选候选轨迹，再用更强的自回归模型对选中候选进行重排。

Result: 在 RECON、HuRoN、SCAND 和 TartanDrive 上，相比 NWM，SkipWorld 将平均 ATE 降低 4.2%、RPE 降低 2.7%，同时将规划时间减少 62.4%，实现 2.7 倍加速。

Conclusion: SkipWorld 在保持准确轨迹评估的同时减少冗余 rollout 计算，为高效视觉导航提供了一种有效的潜在跳跃规划方案。

Abstract: World-model-based visual navigation enables action planning by predicting future states under candidate trajectories. However, long-horizon rollout remains challenging: models are often trained with ground-truth visual histories but deployed in closed-loop prediction, and evaluating many candidates with step-by-step rollout is computationally expensive. We propose SkipWorld, a latent skip planning framework for efficient visual navigation. SkipWorld introduces three components: (i) Latent AutoRoll, which trains a world model to recursively roll out future states in latent space, reducing the mismatch between training and closed-loop inference; (ii) SkipDistill, which distills the latent autoregressive model into a skip world model that directly predicts long-stride future latent states; and (iii) Skip-then-Refine Planning, a dual-model coarse-to-fine planner that first filters candidate trajectories with the skip model and then reranks selected candidates with the stronger autoregressive model. This design preserves accurate trajectory evaluation while reducing redundant rollout computation. Experiments on RECON, HuRoN, SCAND, and TartanDrive show that, compared with NWM, SkipWorld reduces the average ATE by 4.2% and RPE by 2.7%, while decreasing planning time by 62.4% with a 2.7-fold speedup.

</details>


### [82] [MARTS: A Modular Agricultural Robot for Targeted Spraying of Diseased Tomato Plants in Greenhouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18123)
*Rajmeet Singh,Manveen Kaur,Yusuf Adamu,Lochan Kshetrimayum,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MARTS 是一种低成本模块化农业机器人，在商业温室中实现从植株病害检测到叶片局部精准喷洒的闭环，显著减少农药用量并保持高靶向成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温室番茄病害防治仍以广播式喷洒为主，无法区分健康与病叶，导致农药浪费、工人暴露于有害气溶胶，且效果依赖操作者技能。

Method: MARTS 集成 Husky A200 移动底盘、UR5 机械臂、基于单个 12 V 微型隔膜泵的模块化喷洒末端执行器以及腕装 Intel RealSense D435 RGB-D 相机。病害检测复用已发表的 TLDVLM（Grounding DINO、SAM-2、LoRA 微调 BLIP-2，10 类准确率 97.27%），本文聚焦执行、控制与系统集成。ROS 流水线将检测结果提升为 3D 目标，利用拟合叶片法线在 120 mm 间距生成避障喷嘴位姿，并按病斑面积调节泵脉冲时长。

Result: 水敏纸标定显示脉冲-体积呈线性关系，R²=0.994。MuJoCo 仿真中，自适应底盘重定位策略对 18 个路点实现 100% 覆盖，最大姿态误差 1.1°。在阿联酋 Al Ain 的 SILAL 商业温室对自然感染的樱桃番茄进行 12 次自主试验，共处理 312 片病叶，靶向成功率 92.4%，平均喷洒中心误差 18.7 mm，邻近叶片过喷率 6.1%，农药用量较广播式喷洒减少 71%。平均单叶周期 4.8 s，喷洒子系统成本 412 美元，比同类系统低 6–10 倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在商品化硬件上即可实现准确的叶片级靶向喷洒，MARTS 以低成本完成从检测到局部施药的闭环，为温室病害精准防控和减少农药暴露提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Broadcast spraying remains the dominant method of disease control in greenhouse tomato production, yet it treats healthy and diseased tissue alike, exposes workers to hazardous aerosols, and depends on operator skill. This paper presents MARTS (Modular Agricultural Robot for Targeted Spraying), a low-cost robotic platform that closes the loop from plant-level disease detection to leaf-localised spray delivery in commercial greenhouses. MARTS integrates a Clearpath Husky A200 mobile base, a UR5 manipulator, a novel modular spraying end-effector built around a single 12 V micro-diaphragm pump, and a wrist-mounted Intel RealSense D435 RGB-D camera. Disease detection reuses our previously published Tomato Leaf Disease Vision Language Model (TLDVLM; Grounding DINO, SAM-2 and LoRA-tuned BLIP-2, 97.27% accuracy over 10 classes); this work focuses on actuation, control and system integration. A ROS pipeline lifts detections into 3D targets, generates collision-aware nozzle poses at a 120 mm stand-off using fitted leaf normals, and scales pump pulse duration to lesion area. Water-sensitive paper calibration showed a linear pulse-volume relationship (R^2 = 0.994). In MuJoCo simulation, an adaptive base-repositioning strategy achieved 100% coverage of 18 waypoints with a maximum orientation error of 1.1 deg. Deployed at the SILAL commercial greenhouse (Al Ain, UAE) on cherry tomatoes under natural infection, MARTS treated 312 diseased leaves in 12 autonomous trials with 92.4% targeting success, 18.7 mm mean spray-centre error, 6.1% adjacent-leaf overspray, and a 71% reduction in pesticide volume versus broadcast spraying. Mean cycle time was 4.8 s per leaf, and the spraying subsystem costs USD 412, 6-10x lower than comparable systems, showing that accurate leaf-level targeting is achievable on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [83] [ForeTac: Predictive Contact Guidance for Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18118)
*Zhiyuan Chen,Shuai Chen,Haoran Zhou,Yizhe Zhao,Kaiyuan Yang,Zhixiang Duan,Shaoqing Xu,Ran Cao,Diyun Xiang,Yangwei You*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ForeTac预测性触觉引导框架，通过“表示-预测-评估-引导”闭环，在执行前预判并优化候选动作的接触结果，显著提升接触丰富操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉相似动作可能导致接触丢失、卡阻、滑移或不可逆损坏，且触觉反馈常在关键转变后才到达，因此生成式策略需要提前预判并评估候选动作的接触后果。

Method: ForeTac编码当前触觉状态，预测候选动作序列的触觉后果，并用从成功与失败物理交互中学到的接触质量目标评估预测轨迹；部署时梯度经触觉预测器回传以引导动作生成，同时冻结预训练模型参数，并在平面/曲面擦拭、卡片滑动和易碎物抓取四类真实机器人任务上结合扩散与流匹配策略验证。

Result: 相比仅视觉Diffusion Policy，ForeTac将平均任务成功率从36.3%提升至71.3%，平均接触成功率从20.0%提升至66.3%；组件分析支持触觉表示与动作条件前瞻，并表明预测触觉可保持接触质量排序。

Conclusion: ForeTac表明，在动作执行前利用触觉预测和接触质量评估进行引导，可显著改善接触丰富操作，且无需微调预训练策略参数即可生效。

Abstract: Visually similar actions can produce contact loss, jamming, slippage, or irreversible damage, while tactile feedback may arrive after a critical transition. Contact-rich manipulation therefore requires generative policies to anticipate and evaluate whether candidate actions will produce desirable contact outcomes, then adjust action generation before execution. We present ForeTac, a predictive contact-guidance framework organized as a represent-predict-evaluate-guide loop. ForeTac encodes the current tactile state, predicts the tactile consequences of a candidate action sequence, and evaluates the predicted trajectory using a contact-quality objective learned from successful and failed physical interactions. At deployment, its gradient propagates through the tactile predictor to guide action generation while the pretrained model parameters remain frozen. We evaluate ForeTac on four real-robot tasks (planar and curved-surface wiping, card swiping, and fragile-object grasping) with diffusion and flow-matching policies. Across these tasks, ForeTac raises the average task success rate from 36.3% to 71.3% and the average contact success rate (CSR) from 20.0% to 66.3% compared with vision-only Diffusion Policy. Component analyses support the tactile representation and action-conditioned foresight, and show that predicted touch preserves contact-quality ordering. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [84] [LynxScenes: A Large-Scale Real-World Benchmark for Outdoor Wheel-Legged Robot Perception, Navigation, Imagination and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18093)
*Jun Tian,Hao Wu,Ruo Chen,Yili Liao,Hongyi Lin,Jincheng Yu,Chao Li,Yingjie Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LynxScenes：基于 LYNX M20 轮腿机器人采集的大规模真实世界数据集与基准，面向户外人机共享空间导航，涵盖感知、轨迹预测、未来场景预测与场景重建，并提供伪闭环评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集通常只支持单一能力，且很少在真实轮腿机器人平台上提供密集的空间与行为级监督；户外轮腿机器人需要精确感知、社会合规导航、未来场景推理和场景重建。

Method: 使用 LYNX M20 轮腿机器人采集约 10 万个 30 秒导航片段，总计 833.6 小时、3294.7 公里、300 万标注帧；同步记录四路鱼眼相机、双 LiDAR、IMU、里程计和机器人状态。标注包括 27 类 2082 万个有向 3D 框、1389 万道路结构标注，以及 20 条三层导航指令并与连续未来轨迹对齐。支持四类任务：指令条件轨迹预测、BEV 3D 目标与道路结构感知、未来场景预测、多视角场景重建，并提出基于日志回放的伪闭环评估器。

Result: 在代表性导航、感知、世界模型和重建方法上的广泛评估表明，近距离户外机器人导航仍存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: LynxScenes 为真实轮腿机器人户外导航提供大规模多任务基准与评估工具，数据集和评估工具包计划公开发布。

Abstract: Wheel-legged robots operating in outdoor spaces shared with humans require accurate perception, socially compliant navigation, future-scene reasoning, and scene reconstruction. However, existing datasets typically support these capabilities in isolation and rarely provide dense spatial and behavior-level supervision on real wheel-legged platforms. We introduce LynxScenes, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark collected with a LYNX M20 wheel-legged robot. It contains approximately 100K 30-s navigation clips spanning 833.6 h and 3,294.7 km, with 3.0M annotated frames. The synchronized recordings include four fisheye cameras, dual LiDARs, IMU, odometry, and robot states. Annotations comprise 20.82M oriented 3D boxes across 27 categories, 13.89M road-structure annotations, and 20 navigation instructions organized into three layers and aligned with continuous future trajectories. LynxScenes supports four complementary tasks: instruction-conditioned trajectory prediction, BEV-based perception of 3D objects and road structures, future scene prediction, and multi-view scene reconstruction. For trajectory prediction, we further introduce a log-replay-based pseudo-closed-loop evaluator that measures safety, progress, and instruction compliance beyond trajectory imitation. Extensive evaluations across representative navigation, perception, world-model, and reconstruction methods reveal substantial remaining challenges in close-range outdoor robot navigation. The dataset and evaluation toolkit are planned for public release.

</details>


### [85] [LADDER: A Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18053)
*Petko Petkov,Giuliano Albanese,Nikolay Nikolov,Luc Van Gool,Jonathan Tremblay,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LADDER 是一个长时程机器人操作基准，在同一环境中将任务从1个子目标扩展到10个子目标，覆盖3个套件、145个任务、8个类别、2种本体，每系统2,024个模拟回合。实验表明：底层策略在单子目标任务上达85.4%，但在长时程任务上接近0；orchestration可缓解但仅约25%，且瓶颈在底层物理执行而非高层规划；GPT-6 Astra上的GPT-Policy工具选择orchestration达约62%，但时间与成本开销大；真实世界实验验证了底层策略崩溃和orchestration增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作策略能有效学习短时程技能，但在长时程任务上性能迅速退化。现有基准无法隔离失败原因，因为任务时程要么过短，要么与场景和物体变化纠缠在一起，难以单独评估长时程带来的影响。

Method: 提出LADDER长时程机器人操作基准，在同一环境中将子目标数从1扩展到10，包含SimplerEnv-Long、RobotArena-∞-Long和RoboLab-Long三个套件，共145个任务、8个类别、2种本体，每个系统2,024个模拟回合。评估两类orchestration范式：面向学习型底层策略的文本子目标方法VoLo，以及工具选择orchestration方法CaP-X、GPT-Policy、PSP-Agent和TiPToP，并与直接给定完整指令的底层策略对比。同时在两个本体上进行真实世界实验。

Result: 直接给定完整指令时，底层策略在单子目标任务上成功率为85.4%，但在扩展长时程任务上降至接近零。Orchestration可缓解性能下降，但成功率仅约25%，且受限于底层策略的物理执行误差，而非高层规划能力。基于GPT-6 Astra的GPT-Policy工具选择orchestration显著优于学习型策略方法，但时间与成本预算开销显著，成功率仅约62%。真实世界双本体实验确认了底层策略在长时程任务上的崩溃以及orchestration带来的增益。

Conclusion: 长时程机器人操作的核心瓶颈在于底层策略的物理执行可靠性，而非高层规划；现有学习型策略和orchestration方法在长时程任务上仍表现不足。LADDER能够隔离并量化任务时程扩展导致的失败，工具选择orchestration虽有潜力但代价高昂，未来需提升底层策略的可靠性与效率。

Abstract: General-purpose robot manipulation policies learn short-horizon skills effectively, but degrade rapidly on long-horizon tasks. Existing benchmarks cannot isolate failures because task horizon is either too short or entangled with scene and object variations. We propose LADDER, a long-horizon robot manipulation benchmark scaling from 1 to 10 subgoals within the same environment over three suites (SimplerEnv-Long, RobotArena-$\infty$-Long, and RoboLab-Long), 145 tasks, eight categories, two embodiments, and 2,024 simulated episodes per system. We evaluate two orchestration paradigms: text subgoals for learned low-level policies (VoLo) and tool selection orchestration (CaP-X, GPT-Policy, PSP-Agent, TiPToP). Given the full instruction directly, low-level policies achieve 85.4% on single-subgoal tasks, but drop to near zero on extended tasks. While orchestration remedies this, it remains bounded by physical execution errors of the low-level policy rather than high-level planning and achieves only ~25%. Tool selection orchestration with GPT-Policy on GPT-6 Astra significantly outperforms learned-policy methods, but incurs significant time and cost budget and succeeds only ~62%. Real-world experiments across two embodiments confirm the collapse of the low-level policy and the gain from orchestration. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [86] [Contractor Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18040)
*Simon Rohou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Contractor Filters（CFs），一种面向非线性连续时间状态估计的集合隶属架构，用于处理有界不确定性和偶发异常值；将可行状态用 contractor 组合符号化表示，避免每次更新后重新物化几何包围，并给出精确标称递推与保证鲁棒包围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集合论滤波在每次更新后需重新构造几何包围，隐含依赖特定状态几何，难以统一表达预测、校正、模糊性与异常值处理；同时需要在异常值、模型失配等条件下保持鲁棒性。

Method: CFs 以 contractor 组合象征性表示可行状态，在集合论滤波方程与数值实现之间引入抽象层，使预测、校正、模糊和异常值处理在同一代数框架内完成而不预设状态几何；推导精确标称递推和保证鲁棒包围，采用验证流包含保持可靠性，并可选子铺砌压缩获得有限外表示。

Result: 在仅距离和地形定位任务中，针对异常值、概率模型失配、关联模糊和多模态场景，与鲁棒粒子滤波对比，CFs 在所有测试配置中均保留仿真真值；粒子滤波在持续偏差和空间混叠下可靠性下降。Contractor 强度和压缩预算控制铺砌紧密度与计算成本之间的权衡，同时保持包围保证。

Conclusion: CFs 提供了一种统一的、可保证可靠性的非线性连续时间集合隶属状态估计框架，其抽象层允许灵活数值实现，并通过可调压缩在精度与成本间取得平衡。

Abstract: We introduce Contractor Filters (CFs), a set-membership architecture for nonlinear continuous-time state estimation with bounded uncertainties and sporadic outliers. CFs represent feasible states symbolically by compositions of contractors rather than rematerializing a geometric enclosure after each update. This provides an abstraction layer between the set-theoretic filtering equations and their numerical realization, so prediction, correction, ambiguity, and outlier handling can be expressed within one algebra without prescribing a state geometry. We derive an exact nominal recursion and a guaranteed robust enclosure, with validated flow inclusions preserving soundness. Optional sub-paving compression then provides finite outer representations. CFs are evaluated against a robust particle filter on range-only and terrain-based localization under outliers, probabilistic model mismatch, association ambiguity, and multimodality. CFs retain the simulated truth in all tested configurations, while particle-filter reliability degrades under persistent bias and spatial aliasing. Contractor strength and compression budget control the trade-off between paving tightness and computational cost, while preserving the enclosure guarantee.

</details>


### [87] [Data-Free Continual Learning for World Action Model with Rectified Generative Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18038)
*Kaipeng Fang,Hangxu Liu,Chaojian Shi,Yunsong Zhou,Hongzi Zhu,Minyi Guo,Jingkuan Song,Lianli Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: R²-WAM 让世界动作模型自回归重生成并回放旧任务 rollout，通过任务完成截断与动作-观测一致性过滤，在无旧数据下将顺序微调遗忘降低 90% 以上并接近经验回放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于预训练视频生成模型的 WAM 能快速适应新机器人任务，但顺序微调会灾难性遗忘旧任务；而机器人场景中旧任务训练数据通常不可得，仅部署权重，因此需要不依赖真实旧数据的持续学习方案。本文探究 WAM 能否通过“重生成自己的过去”实现无数据持续学习。

Method: 作者观察到 WAM 可自回归生成先前任务的完整 rollout 并回放以缓解遗忘，但原始 rollout 回放远逊于真实数据，原因有二：WAM 无法识别任务完成，完成后继续生成无意义动作；开环生成中动作逐渐偏离生成观测。为此提出 R²-WAM：用智能体评估器在任务完成处截断生成 rollout，并过滤动作与观测物理不一致的 rollout，以修正生成回放。

Result: 在四个 LIBERO 套件和五个真实世界任务上，R²-WAM 相比顺序微调减少遗忘超过 90%，且在不访问任何旧任务数据的情况下几乎匹配经验回放。分析进一步表明，WAM 的遗忘集中于逆动力学建模：遗忘后的 WAM 仍能生成旧任务视觉上合理的观测，但其生成动作失败。

Conclusion: WAM 可通过自生成旧任务 rollout 实现有效的无数据持续学习；关键在于修正生成回放的质量与物理一致性，且遗忘主要体现为逆动力学能力退化，而非观测生成能力完全丧失。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) built on pretrained video generation models adapt quickly to new robotic tasks, but they forget previously learned tasks under sequential fine-tuning. Experience replay (ER) mitigates such forgetting by rehearsing data of previous tasks, yet this data is often unavailable in robotics, where a policy is deployed as weights only and its training data is not shared along with it. In this work, we ask whether a WAM can achieve data-free continual learning by regenerating its own past. Our key observation is that a WAM can indeed autoregressively regenerate complete rollouts of previously learned tasks and replay them to mitigate forgetting. However, replaying such raw rollouts still lags far behind replaying real data, which we attribute to two issues. First, the WAM is unable to recognize task completion and keeps generating purposeless motion after the task is done. Second, under open-loop generation, the generated actions gradually drift away from the generated observations. To address both issues, we present rectified generative replay (R$^2$-WAM), which truncates generated rollouts at task completion with an agentic evaluator and filters out rollouts whose actions are physically inconsistent with the observations. On the four LIBERO suites and five real-world tasks, R$^2$-WAM reduces forgetting by over 90% relative to sequential fine-tuning and nearly matches ER without accessing any data from previous tasks. Our analysis further reveals that forgetting in WAMs concentrates in inverse dynamics modeling: a forgotten WAM still produces visually plausible observations of previous tasks while its generated actions fail.

</details>


### [88] [TRACE: Efficient Transfer Learning for Neural Whole-Body Control Across Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17999)
*Qingyu Lan,Zhihao Tian,Zifan Wang,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACE通过注入运动学先验、对齐关节动力学、编码器蒸馏与低秩强化学习，实现预训练人形控制器跨本体高效迁移，并以极低计算成本将SONIC从Unitree G1迁移至Unitree H2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形行为基础模型可实现多样化全身控制，但预训练控制器难以适应新本体，主要受运动学与动力学不匹配制约。

Method: TRACE在保持预训练运动表示的同时，将反馈控制特化到目标本体：先向输入/输出投影层注入运动学先验并对齐标称关节动力学，以减小本体差异并支持稳定微调；再在紧凑且经质量筛选的动作集上，结合编码器蒸馏与低秩强化学习，采用两阶段课程先建立稳定跟踪，再精调困难动作。

Result: 以1000万帧动作数据、单张RTX PRO 6000 GPU约8小时训练，将SONIC从Unitree G1迁移至Unitree H2，GPU机时成本不足从头训练的0.1%；在高度缩放的跟踪准则下，对67,953条评估动作达到96.08%成功率。真机演示涵盖动态动作跟踪与全身遥操作，采集的演示数据支持下游视觉-语言-动作策略后训练。

Conclusion: 结果表明，预训练人形控制器可在不同身体尺度与动力学的机器人之间实现实用化复用。

Abstract: Humanoid behavior foundation models (BFMs) enable diverse whole-body control, but adapting a pretrained controller to a new embodiment remains challenging because of kinematic and dynamic mismatch. We present TRACE, a framework for efficient cross-embodiment adaptation that preserves pretrained motion representations while specializing feedback control to the target this http URL first injects kinematic priors into input and output projection layers and aligns nominal joint dynamics to reduce embodiment mismatch and support stable fine-tuning. It then combines encoder distillation with low-rank reinforcement learning on a compact, quality-screened motion set, using a two-phase curriculum to establish stable tracking before refining difficult motions. We apply TRACE to transfer SONIC from Unitree G1 to Unitree H2 using 10 million motion frames and approximately eight hours of training on a single RTX PRO 6000 GPU, at less than 0.1% of the GPU-hour cost of training SONIC from scratch.
