The adapted controller achieves 96.08\% success on 67,953 evaluation motions under a height-scaled tracking criterion. Real-robot demonstrations span dynamic motion tracking and whole-body teleoperation, with collected demonstrations supporting downstream vision-language-action policy post-training. These results demonstrate practical reuse of a pretrained humanoid controller across robots with different body scales and dynamics.

</details>


### [89] [Breaking the Average: Overcoming Convergent Defaults in Robotic Manipulation via Diagnostic Chain-of-Thought and Structured Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17983)
*Mohammed Daba,Waleed Abdulrahman Saleh AlAkwa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型因模式平均导致分布收敛、退化为安全但次优的Convergent Defaults，提出无需参数更新的三阶段框架，用Lens Prompts重定向采样分布，并在ManiSkill3中以π0.5验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA（如π0.5）通过大规模行为克隆获得强泛化，但训练数据中的模式平均使策略收敛到“安全”但次优的均值行为，表现为通用抓取策略，在受限或长尾场景中失效。

Method: 提出无需参数更新的识别与纠正框架，含三阶段：(1) Probing for Defaults，识别任务特定的模式坍缩；(2) Diagnostic Root Cause Analysis，利用VLA的潜在推理能力用语言解释次优动作逻辑；(3) Structured Guidance Injection，将具体且不同的替代方案编码为可复用的Lens Prompts，以偏移采样分布。

Result: 在ManiSkill3仿真环境中使用π0.5进行验证；作者假设该方法在复杂操作任务中优于原始π0.5和标准Chain-of-Thought提示，能有效打破收敛默认行为。摘要中结果为验证设置与假设，未给出定量结果。

Conclusion: 通过诊断并注入结构化引导，可在不更新参数的情况下纠正VLA的收敛默认行为，提升复杂长尾操作任务表现。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, such as $\pi_{0.5}$, have demonstrated impressive generalization capabilities by scaling behavior cloning across massive datasets. However, these models frequently suffer from distributional convergence, a phenomenon where the policy regresses to "safe" but suboptimal mean behaviors due to mode averaging in the training data. This often results in generic grasp strategies, Convergent Defaults, that fail in constrained or long-tail scenarios. In this work, we propose a novel framework to identify and correct these behaviors without parameter updates. We introduce a three-stage methodology: (1) Probing for Defaults, identifying task-specific mode collapse; (2) Diagnostic Root Cause Analysis, utilizing the VLA's latent reasoning capabilities to verbalize the logic behind suboptimal actions; and (3) Structured Guidance Injection, where concrete, distinct alternatives are encoded into reusable "Lens Prompts" that shift the sampling distribution. We validate this approach using $\pi_{0.5}$ in ManiSkill3 simulation environments. We hypothesize that our method outperforms vanilla $\pi_{0.5}$ and standard Chain-of-Thought prompting by effectively breaking convergent defaults in complex manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [90] [ReEnvision: Future-Guided Selective Action Repair for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17977)
*Xiyin Zeng,Xuewei Huang,Yi Lu,Weihao Yuan,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReEnvision，一种未来引导的选择性动作修复框架：利用执行反馈更新联合未来-动作信念，并学习该信念修正对每个未执行动作的影响，从而在缓存动作块中仅选择性修复受偏差影响的动作，保留有效动作，仅在严重偏差时全量重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作需在多步相互依赖的动作序列上鲁棒执行；分布偏移引起的局部偏差通常只影响少数动作，但现有方法要么重规划整个剩余动作块（从陌生状态重新生成，可能破坏原本有效的动作），要么逐步校正（反应过晚，未显式预测偏差对后续动作的影响）。

Method: ReEnvision用执行反馈更新一个联合的未来-动作信念，并学习信念修正如何影响每个未执行动作；基于该信念修正，在缓存的动作块中仅选择性修复受影响动作，保留有效动作，并将完整重规划保留给严重偏差情形。

Result: 在CALVIN上达到86.6%的5步成功率；在L-CALVIN上将10步成功率从56%提升至63%；在RLBench上平均成功率为88.8%；在五个真实世界操作任务上平均成功率为82.8%；受控修复实验进一步验证了局部定位受影响动作并保留有效动作的收益。

Conclusion: 通过未来引导的选择性动作修复，可在执行偏差下精准定位并修复受影响动作，同时保留有效动作，显著提升长时程机器人操作的鲁棒性与成功率。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation requires robust execution over sequences of interdependent actions. Existing methods typically handle execution deviations by replanning the remaining action chunk or correcting actions step by step as new observations arrive. However, a local deviation may affect only a few actions under distribution shift, yet replanning regenerates the entire chunk from an unfamiliar state, potentially introducing errors into otherwise valid actions. Step-wise correction may react too late, as it does not explicitly anticipate how the deviation affects subsequent actions. We propose ReEnvision, a future-guided selective action repair framework that updates a joint future-action belief using execution feedback. It then learns how the resulting belief revision affects each unexecuted action. The resulting belief revision enables selective repair of only the affected actions within a cached action chunk, preserving valid actions and reserving full replanning for severe deviations. On CALVIN, L-CALVIN, and RLBench, ReEnvision achieves 86.6% 5-step success, improves 10-step success from 56% to 63%, and reaches an average success rate of 88.8%, respectively. On five real-world manipulation tasks, it achieves an average success rate of 82.8%. Controlled repair experiments further demonstrate the benefits of localizing affected actions and preserving valid ones during execution.

</details>


### [91] [Locomotion with Online Awareness of Dynamics for Precision Footholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17930)
*Kunzhao Ren,Zhishuo Zhu,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LOAD感知运动框架，在未知负载导致机器人动力学变化、窄梁落脚裕度小时，结合显式惯性估计与隐式动力学特征，实现窄梁精确穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知负载会改变机器人动力学，在落脚裕度很小的窄梁上难以精确执行接触，因此需要提升负载变化下的精密运动能力。

Method: LOAD通过独立循环模块从本体感受推断躯干惯性变化和任务相关历史；将高度图教师策略蒸馏为基于深度的学生策略；在仿真和硬件上广泛评估及其变体。

Result: 隐式动力学特征加速训练，显式惯性估计进一步提高穿越成功率；二者结合使LOAD接近使用真实惯性参数的 privileged policy。硬件实现单侧6 kg负载通过10 cm窄梁、单侧9 kg通过15 cm窄梁，以及均匀分布11 kg通过15 cm窄梁。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合物理估计与历史特征有助于在未知负载下实现精密运动。

Abstract: Unknown payloads alter robot dynamics and make precise contact execution difficult when foothold margins are small. We present LOAD, a perceptive locomotion framework that combines explicit inertial estimates with implicit dynamics features for narrow-beam traversal. Separate recurrent modules infer torso inertial changes and task-relevant history from proprioception, while a height-map teacher is distilled into a depth-based student. We extensively evaluate LOAD and its variants in simulation and on hardware across beam widths and payload configurations. The results show that implicit dynamics features accelerate training, while explicit inertial estimation further improves traversal success; combining both enables LOAD to approach the performance of a privileged policy supplied with ground-truth inertial parameters. Hardware experiments demonstrate traversal with unilateral payloads of 6 kg on a 10 cm beam and 9 kg on a 15 cm beam, and an evenly distributed 11 kg payload on a 15 cm beam. These results support combining physical estimates and history features for precision locomotion under unknown loads.

</details>


### [92] [GLST: Global-Local Spatio-Temporal Memory Decomposition Framework for Online Chained-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17927)
*Yousef Liang,Ryan Ardalan,Yarden Goraly,Chenhao Zhu,Yue Yin,Jonas Frey,Claire Tomlin,Marco Pavone,Shankar Sastry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GLST记忆分解框架，通过全局-局部空间与长-短时序信念分离及目标条件奖励，实现部分可观测下的无地图在线链式目标导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无地图导航多假设回合内目标固定，难以适用于目标在线演化（已有目标重定向或新目标出现且无显式转换信号）的实际场景；该设定下导航需在持续探索与策略性重返间切换，对记忆形成冲突性需求。

Method: GLST将潜在信念分解为全局-局部空间表示与长-短时序表示，以支持长时程推理；并引入目标条件奖励，在目标演化下平衡探索与利用。

Result: 多地形仿真实验表明链式目标导航性能优于基线；基于Spot四足机器人的真实机器人实验进一步验证了记忆分解框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 面向在线链式目标导航，记忆分解与目标条件奖励能有效缓解冲突性记忆需求，并在仿真与真实机器人上具备可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in real-world environments often requires long-horizon operation under partial observability and evolving goals. Existing mapless navigation approaches predominantly assume fixed goals throughout an episode, limiting applicability to practical scenarios where goals change online during execution. We study online chained-goal navigation, where goals evolve through existing-goal redirection or new-goal emergence without explicit transition signals. Under this setting, we find navigation may require either continued exploration or strategic revisitation, creating conflicting memory requirements. We propose Global-Local Spatio-Temporal Belief Learning (GLST), a memory-decomposition framework that separates latent beliefs into global-local spatial and long-short temporal representations for long-horizon reasoning. We further introduce goal-conditioned rewards to balance exploration and exploitation under evolving goals. Extensive experiments in simulation across diverse terrains demonstrate improved chained-goal navigation performance over baselines. Real-robot experiments using the Spot quadruped further validate our memory decomposition framework.

</details>


### [93] [WoVR+: World Model as Reliable Simulator for Versatile VLA Policy Optimization with RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17922)
*Shangqing Zhou,Zhennan Jiang,Ning Ma,Xin Fu,Yupeng Zheng,Shuai Tian,Huangrui Li,Jiahao Liu,Chao Yu,Zhongpu Xia,Haoran Li,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WoVR+ 在 WoVR 的幻觉感知世界模型强化学习框架上，重新设计学习环境与想象交互协议，以提升 VLA-RL 在长时程任务中的性能并缓解世界模型幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型可减少 VLA-RL 中昂贵的真机交互，但其幻觉会误导策略优化；扩展到更丰富的 VLA 接口和长时程任务时，还面临时空与跨模态一致性、探索-可靠性平衡以及 rollout 误差累积等挑战。

Method: WoVR+ 重新设计学习环境和想象交互协议：采用与 VLA 接口对齐的世界模型进行多视角预测和本体感觉更新，并缓解幻觉；使用 chunk-wise 噪声在保持基于流策略探索的同时限制会降低预测质量的 action jitter；针对长时程任务提出 stage-local imagined training，结合阶段入口重置、阶段特定奖励、局部时域和可控采样，以缩短生成前缀并平衡各阶段训练。

Result: WoVR+ 在 RoboTwin 和 6 个真机任务上相较 SFT 分别提升成功率 21.09% 和 16.7%；长时程手机包装任务成功率从 30% 提升至 75%。

Conclusion: WoVR+ 通过幻觉感知的世界模型与面向长时程的想象训练协议，有效提升 VLA-RL 的成功率、长时程任务表现和训练可靠性。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models can reduce costly real-robot interaction in reinforcement learning for Vision-Language-Action models (VLA-RL), but hallucinations can mislead policy optimization. Extending them to richer VLA interfaces and long-horizon tasks raises challenges in spatiotemporal and cross-modal consistency, exploration-reliability balance in RL, and compounding rollout errors. We present WoVR+, extending WoVR's hallucination-aware WM-RL framework by redesigning the learned environment and imagined interaction protocol: A VLA-interface-aligned world model provides multi-view prediction and proprioceptive updates while mitigating hallucinations. Chunk-wise noise preserves flow-based policy exploration while limiting prediction-degrading action jitter. For long-horizon tasks, stage-local imagined training combines stage-entry resets, stage-specific rewards, local horizons, and controllable sampling to shorten generated prefixes and balance training across stages. WoVR+ improves success over SFT by 21.09% and 16.7% on RoboTwin and 6 real-robot tasks. Long-horizon phone-packaging success rises from 30% to 75%. Videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [94] [Agentic Robot Creation through Generation, Verification, and Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17881)
*Xuebing Li,Yibo He,Sitong Liu,Ran Ji,Jiayuan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究AI智能体能否将高层用户意图转化为可运行机器人并教会其行为，提出具身创建评估指标和Roboforge行为创建框架，并完成仿真与实物验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI智能体从高层意图到机器人实体与行为全流程自动化的可行性；现有研究在生成设计的机械可行性评估与行为策略的闭环修正方面仍不足。

Method: 从具身创建和行为创建两方面展开：用多模态模型生成设计，并引入计算指标在制造前评估机械要求；提出Roboforge，将任务规格转为可执行策略，流程包括运动生成、策略学习和基于执行的修正。给定具身与任务，智能体生成参考运动、配置学习目标并训练策略；将运动保持在闭环中，利用rollout反馈修正参考运动、奖励或两者。评估多个MLLM生成的具身，并在四类机器人上与奖励修正和一次性运动基线比较行为创建。

Result: 计算指标揭示生成设计中反复出现的机械故障；行为实验表明基于执行的修正提升任务级行为和执行质量；最终制造出智能体生成的Gorilla机器人，并将学习到的行为直接部署到实体机器人上。

Conclusion: AI智能体可在一定程度上完成从高层意图到机器人具身与行为的学习和部署，但生成设计的机械可靠性仍需计算指标筛查与闭环修正；执行反馈对行为质量至关重要，实物部署验证了该流程的可行性。

Abstract: Can an AI agent turn high-level user intent into a functioning robot and teach it how to behave? We study this question from two complementary aspects: embodiment and behavior creation. For embodiment creation, we investigate how well current multimodal models capture mechanical requirements, and introduce computational metrics for evaluating generated designs before fabrication. For behavior creation, we present Roboforge, an agentic framework that turns task specifications into executable policies through motion generation, policy learning, and execution-grounded revision. Given a robot embodiment and task, the agent generates a reference motion, configures the learning objective, and trains a policy. Crucially, motion remains in the loop: rollout feedback can revise the reference motion, reward, or both. We evaluate a generated embodiment across multiple MLLMs and behavior creation across four diverse robots against reward-refinement and one-shot motion baselines. Our computational metrics expose recurring mechanical failures in generated designs, while behavior experiments show that execution-grounded refinement improves task-level behavior and execution quality. Finally, we fabricate an agent-generated Gorilla and directly deploy learned behaviors on the physical robot.

</details>


### [95] [Information-based Unified Design Framework for NMR Magnetic Tracking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17873)
*Seçkin Eren Yetim,Mehmet Efe Tiryaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于信息/局部协方差分析的NMR磁跟踪统一框架，用于评估机载/离板NMR跟踪中的接收器几何、标记间距与外源配置，并给出阵列定位精度、双标记轴向跟踪精度及点偶极源-接收器对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NMR磁跟踪有望替代MRI定位用于医疗机器人导航，但标量场测量和场对准标记矩限制了位置与姿态跟踪，因此需要精心设计跟踪配置。

Method: 构建统一的信息论框架，利用局部协方差分析评估接收器几何、标记分离和外部源配置；对机载/离板NMR跟踪进行数值评估；建立点偶极源-接收器对偶以统一分析两类跟踪。

Result: 平面阵列精度由接收器数量和几何共同决定；在9–20个接收器的评估布局中，三角晶格阵列取得最低最佳位置不确定度。两个空间分离的场对准标记可实现器件轴向跟踪，在评估工作区中心、合适标记间距下预测角度不确定度低于2°。点偶极源-接收器对偶统一了机载/离板NMR跟踪分析。

Conclusion: 该框架可为NMR磁跟踪的配置设计与性能预测提供依据，并通过源-接收器对偶统一机载与离板跟踪分析。原文摘要末尾“These”后内容缺失，完整结论未给出。

Abstract: Nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) magnetic tracking offers a promising alternative to magnetic resonance imaging (MRI)-based localization for medical robotic guidance. However, scalar field measurements and field-aligned marker moments constrain position and orientation tracking, requiring careful configuration design. We present a unified information-based framework that uses local covariance analysis to evaluate receiver geometry, marker separation, and external source configuration in on/off-board NMR tracking. Numerical evaluation shows that planar-array precision depends jointly on receiver count and geometry, with triangular lattice arrays achieving the lowest best-case position uncertainty among the evaluated layouts for 9--20 receivers. Two spatially separated, field-aligned markers enable device-axis tracking, with predicted angular uncertainty below $2^\circ$ at the evaluated workspace center for suitable marker separations. We further establish point-dipole source--receiver duality, unifying the analysis of on/off-board NMR tracking. These

</details>


### [96] [LAG-IO: Multi-IMU State Estimation for Legged Robots Using Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17870)
*Yicheng Zhou,Longwen Chen,Qinghua Yu,Junhao Xiao,Huimin Lu,Xieyuanli Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LAG-IO：一种基于因子图的多IMU腿式机器人状态估计框架，紧耦合主体IMU、腿部多IMU与关节编码器，并结合KDE连续概率接触检测与ZUPT协方差自适应，提升复杂地形下的本体感知状态估计鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在外感受传感器不可靠的环境中，鲁棒本体感知状态估计至关重要；传统腿式里程计多依赖单主体IMU与腿运动学经EKF融合，易受线性化误差和硬接触假设影响，在打滑或动态接触时产生严重漂移。

Method: 在滑动窗口优化中，基于因子图紧耦合主体IMU、多个腿载IMU和关节编码器测量；引入基于核密度估计（KDE）的连续概率接触检测模块，自适应调整零速度更新（ZUPT）因子的协方差；构造运动学相对位置因子，将独立腿部惯性状态结构性锚定到机器人主体。

Result: 在四足机器人多种非结构化地形实验中，与现有基线相比，LAG-IO取得更高的平面轨迹精度，降低不可观测的垂直发散，在接触不稳定时提供更平滑的轨迹过渡，同时保持有竞争力的姿态可观测性。

Conclusion: LAG-IO通过多IMU因子图优化、概率接触检测和运动学锚定，有效提升腿式机器人在复杂接触条件下的本体感知状态估计精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robust proprioceptive state estimation is crucial for legged robots, particularly in challenging environments where exteroceptive sensors are unreliable. While traditional leg odometry typically relies on a single body-mounted inertial measurement unit (IMU) fused with leg kinematics via an extended Kalman filter (EKF), such methods are vulnerable to linearization errors and hard-contact assumptions, leading to severe drift during slippage or dynamic this http URL this paper, we propose LAG-IO, a factor graph-based multi-IMU state estimation framework that tightly couples measurements from a main body IMU, multiple leg-mounted IMUs, and joint encoders within a sliding-window optimization. To handle non-stationary foot-ground interactions, we introduce a continuous probabilistic contact detection module based on kernel density estimation (KDE), which adaptively scales the covariance of zero-velocity update (ZUPT) factors. Furthermore, kinematic relative position factors are formulated to structurally anchor the independent leg inertial states to the robot's main body. Experimental results on a quadruped robot across various unstructured terrains demonstrate that, compared to existing baselines, LAG-IO achieves higher planar trajectory accuracy, reduces unobservable vertical divergence, and provides smoother trajectory transitions during unstable contacts, while maintaining competitive attitude observability.

</details>


### [97] [Physics-Aware Object-Centric SE(3) Forecasting for Real-Time Proactive Dynamic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17843)
*Yirui Zhou,Hongli Xu,Ying Zheng,Yi Wang,Lap-Pui Chau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以接触相关的SE(3)刚体状态作为紧凑且任务充分的未来抽象，采用“先预测后选择”的物体中心框架，实现实时主动动态抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动动态抓取要求机器人预测未来发生物理接触时的状态，而非仅对当前观测到的物体位姿作出反应；但机器人应预测何种未来、在何种抽象层级上预测仍不明确。

Method: 作者主张，接触相关的SE(3)刚体状态是紧凑且任务充分的抽象，可直接决定接触发生的位置与时间，无需视觉上完整的未来场景。为此提出轻量级物体中心forecast-then-select框架：从短位姿历史预测未来刚体轨迹，并使用可达性感知的interception selector，在运动学与时间约束下选择可行接触状态。模型在随机物理仿真的基元形状上训练，在恢复真实物体的度量几何与SE(3)状态历史后迁移到真实物体。

Result: 在仿真与真实动态抓取任务中，框架将仿真平均成功率从24.5%提升至62.0%，在真实接球、传送带与转盘拦截任务中从15.3%提升至67.3%。SE(3)预测器运行时间为15 ms，比稠密视觉未来预测快数个数量级，支持实时接触决策。

Conclusion: 有效的主动抓取不需要想象整个未来场景；预测决定接触的紧凑物理未来，为实时动态抓取提供了一种有效且实用的抽象。

Abstract: Proactive dynamic grasping requires robots to predict the future state at which physical contact will occur, rather than reacting to the currently observed object pose. But what future must the robot predict, and at what level of abstraction? We argue that contact-relevant rigid-body state in SE(3) is a compact task-sufficient abstraction for this problem: it directly determines where and when the robot should establish contact, without requiring visually complete future scenes. This insight leads to a lightweight object-centric forecast-then-select framework that predicts future rigid-body trajectories from short pose histories and uses a reachability-aware interception selector to choose feasible contact states under kinematic and timing constraints. Trained with randomized physics simulation over primitive shapes, the forecaster transfers to real objects once their metric geometry and SE(3) state histories are recovered. Experiments across simulation and real-world dynamic grasping tasks show that our framework improves the average success rate from \(24.5\%\) to \(62.0\%\) in simulation, and from \(15.3\%\) to \(67.3\%\) in real-world catching, conveyor, and turntable interception. The SE(3) forecaster runs in \(15\,\mathrm{ms}\), orders of magnitude faster than dense visual future prediction, enabling real-time contact decision making. Our results suggest that effective proactive grasping does not require imagining the entire future scene. Instead, predicting the compact physical future that determines contact provides an effective and practical abstraction for real-time dynamic grasping.

</details>


### [98] [Which Trajectory Is Safer? Combining Rule and Learning for Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17806)
*Xuewen Luo,Linlian Jiang,Bo Yu,Fan Ding,Xinxin Zuo,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出连续安全指标FutureRisk，通过上下文自适应风险模型CARM量化轨迹未来风险，在多个数据集上优于现有替代安全指标并能区分无碰撞轨迹间的安全差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞率虽是自动驾驶轨迹安全评估的主导指标，但其二值性掩盖了无碰撞轨迹之间巨大的安全差异；现有替代安全指标虽更细粒度，却常在物理可解释性与交互上下文自适应性之间权衡。

Method: FutureRisk以轨迹实际交互接近不安全状态的程度与持续性定义连续风险；核心CARM结合上下文相关的安全时间分布与独立监督的关键事件时间分布，其安全准则以闭式物理先验为中心并保持有界，同时通过有界残差实现额外自适应而不放弃物理锚点。

Result: 在DeepAccident和Bench2Drive上，FutureRisk与原生碰撞标签的一致性优于现有替代安全指标，能区分无碰撞轨迹的风险，并实现零样本跨数据集迁移；对四个规划器的比较进一步表明，FutureRisk可揭示二值碰撞结果所隐藏的安全差异。

Conclusion: FutureRisk是一种细粒度、可解释且上下文自适应的轨迹安全评估指标，可作为碰撞率之外更有效的安全评价工具。

Abstract: Collision rate remains a dominant metric for evaluating autonomous driving trajectory safety, but its binary nature obscures substantial safety differences among collision-free trajectories. Existing surrogate-safety metrics provide finer-grained assessments, yet often trade off physical interpretability against adaptivity to interaction context. We introduce FutureRisk, a continuous metric that quantifies the latent future risk of a trajectory by how closely and persistently its realized interactions approach unsafe states. At its core, our Context-Adaptive Risk Model (CARM) combines a context-dependent safety-time distribution with an independently supervised critical-event-time distribution. Its safety criterion is bounded around a closed-form physical prior, while a bounded residual provides additional adaptation without abandoning this physical anchor. Experiments on DeepAccident and Bench2Drive show that FutureRisk achieves stronger agreement with native collision labels than existing surrogate-safety measures, discriminates risk among collision-free trajectories, and transfers zero-shot across datasets. Comparisons across four planners further show that FutureRisk reveals safety differences hidden by binary collision outcomes. Together, these results establish FutureRisk as a fine-grained, interpretable, and context-adaptive metric for trajectory safety evaluation. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [99] [Beyond the End-Effector: Learning Whole-Arm Contact Manipulation from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17802)
*Bo-Ruei Huang,Daniel Seita,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出整臂接触学习框架WACL，用机器人无关表示与准静态表面接触优化器，使7自由度机械臂能完成整臂非抓取接触任务，并仅需每任务10次裸手人类演示即可学习，且无需重训练即可适应新的末端执行器约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人操作多依赖末端执行器中心交互，而人类常用整臂接触提高效率、鲁棒性与适应性，尤其在手被占用或无需精确抓取时。机器人实现该能力需解决接触部位选择、非抓取交互生成以及末端位姿或安全约束满足等问题。

Method: WACL是分层框架：用三个机器人无关表示描述整臂接触任务，包括场景语义关键点、指定手臂如何按压物体的参考接触轨迹、由视觉语言模型根据任务描述生成的安全感知路径约束；再交给准静态表面接触优化器。机器人运动链仅进入优化器，联合搜索接触连杆、连杆上的接触位置及实现该接触的关节构型。接触轨迹从每任务10次裸手人类演示中学习，无需遥操作；新的末端执行器约束只改变优化器输入，不改变任何已训练组件。

Result: 在7自由度机械臂上针对四个整臂任务进行真实世界实验。在末端执行器约束下，WACL成功率为78%，显著优于强连杆位姿基线33%，并能泛化到新约束而无需重训练。

Conclusion: 结果表明，整臂接触学习可借助少量人类演示实现受末端约束的非抓取操作，并具备对新任务约束的泛化能力，为机器人整臂接触操作提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Robot manipulation has traditionally relied on end-effector-centric interactions. In contrast, humans routinely exploit whole-arm contacts to improve efficiency, robustness, and adaptability, especially when the hands are occupied or when precise grasping is unnecessary. Enabling similar capabilities in robots remains challenging: the robot must select where on its arm to make contact, generate effective non-prehensile interactions, and satisfy task-specific pose or safety constraints on the end-effector. We propose Whole-Arm Contact Learning (WACL), a hierarchical framework that describes each whole-arm contact task with three robot-agnostic representations and hands them to a quasi-static surface-contact optimizer: semantic keypoints that summarize the scene, a reference contact trajectory that specifies where and how the arm presses on the object, and safety-aware path constraints that a vision-language model generates from the task description to protect the end-effector's task. The robot's kinematic chain enters only in this optimizer, which grounds these representations onto the arm by jointly searching over which link makes contact, where on that link, and the joint configuration that realizes it. This decomposition lets WACL learn the contact trajectory from 10 bare-hand human demonstrations per task collected without teleoperation, while new end-effector constraints change only the optimizer's input rather than any trained component. Real-world experiments on a 7-DoF arm across four whole-arm tasks show that WACL sustains contact under end-effector constraints where a strong link-pose baseline fails (78% vs. 33% success) and generalizes to new constraints without retraining. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Safe Execution: Safe Online Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving with Value Calibration and Adaptive Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17799)
*Zeqiao Li,Yijing Wang,Haoyu Wang,Zheng Li,Zhiqiang Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOAR-Flow 是一种面向离线初始化 Flow 驾驶策略的安全稳定在线微调框架，通过 CBF-MPC 安全过滤、Cal-QL 高斯回报感知价值校准、成对纠正学习与干预到未来预测器，在保证执行约束的同时提升驾驶性能并减少对安全纠正的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习可使驾驶策略适应部署条件变化，但训练全程需要安全探索。仅靠安全过滤不足以确保持续稳定的策略改进，也不能降低对纠正/干预的依赖。

Method: 提出 SOAR-Flow：1) 用控制屏障函数（CBF）与模型预测控制（MPC）过滤器在执行前纠正潜在不安全动作；2) 基于 Cal-QL 引入高斯回报感知价值校准，利用记录的行为回报和预测回报波动平滑保守 critic 更新，缓解策略退化；3) 采用成对纠正学习教授可行动作调整；4) 训练干预到未来（intervention-to-go）预测器，鼓励产生更少未来纠正的决策，并据其预测调节引导强度，使恢复监督随预测依赖降低而减弱；5) 逐步降低校准正则化，以在保持硬执行约束下持续提升任务表现。文中还分析了控制步安全执行条件及辅助学习影响递减的条件。

Result: 在仿真和无人地面车辆（UGV）上评估 SOAR-Flow。结果表明，在受保护的在线微调过程中，驾驶性能得到改善，且对安全纠正的依赖减少。

Conclusion: SOAR-Flow 能实现安全、稳定的离线初始化 Flow 驾驶策略在线微调，在不违反硬执行约束的前提下持续改进任务性能并降低安全纠正需求；项目视频与概览见论文给出的链接。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) enables driving policies to adapt to changing deployment conditions, but requires safe exploration throughout training. Safety filtering alone does not ensure stable policy improvement or reduced dependence on corrections. We propose Safe Online Adaptive Refinement (SOAR-Flow), a framework for safe and stable online fine-tuning of offline-initialized Flow-based driving policies. A control barrier function (CBF)--model predictive control (MPC) filter corrects potentially unsafe actions before execution. To support stable policy updates, we introduce Gaussian-return-aware value calibration based on calibrated Q-learning (Cal-QL), using recorded behavior returns and predicted return variability to smooth conservative critic updates and mitigate policy degradation. Paired corrective learning teaches feasible action adjustments, while an intervention-to-go predictor encourages decisions requiring fewer future corrections. Its predictions also regulate guidance strength, allowing recovery supervision to weaken as predicted dependence decreases. Together with gradually reduced calibration regularization, this supports continued task improvement while preserving hard execution constraints. We analyze conditions for safe execution at control steps and diminishing auxiliary learning influences. We evaluate SOAR-Flow in simulation and on an uncrewed ground vehicle (UGV). The results demonstrate improved driving performance and reduced reliance on safety corrections during protected online fine-tuning. The demonstration video and project overview are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [101] [DexStitch: Learning and Composing Dexterous Skills from Human Videos via Contact-Aware Robotic Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17789)
*Byeonggyeol Choi,Seongmin Jung,Unghui Nam,Youngbo Song,Sunhong Min,MinHyung Lee,Byonghyo Shim,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexStitch 将长时程人类视频切分为技能与过渡，转换为对齐的机器人观测-动作-技能数据，并将学习到的技能拼接为指定序列，实现从人类视频到机器人灵巧操作的可扩展迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作任务由基本技能及其过渡组成，学习可顺序执行的技能需要同时监督技能与过渡，而在机器人上收集此类监督成本高昂。人类视频提供了可扩展的示范来源，但迁移到机器人需同时提取机器人兼容的观测、动作及技能结构。

Method: 提出 DexStitch 框架：首先用技能分类器将长时程人类视频分割为操作技能与过渡；随后将人类示范转换为机器人观测与动作，并保留对应技能标签。转换过程中，利用逐指人-物接触指导灵巧手重定向与机器人修复/补全，以保持接触几何与手指-物体遮挡顺序。为将技能拼接为指定序列，在合成数据上预训练共享的技能条件策略，并在合成数据与少量单技能遥操作回合的混合数据上微调；通过以当前技能和下一技能为条件引导过渡，并利用预测的技能进展实现闭环切换。

Result: 在留出会话上，技能分类器与人工标注在 92.4% 的帧上一致；在四个家庭技能上，DexStitch 达到 80.0% 的单技能成功率和 77.5% 的序列成功率。

Conclusion: DexStitch 通过从人类视频中提取技能-过渡结构并生成机器人兼容数据，结合技能条件策略与闭环切换，有效实现了长时程灵巧操作技能的拼接与执行，展示了从人类视频可扩展迁移到机器人操作的可行性。

Abstract: A long-horizon manipulation task consists of primitive skills and the transitions connecting them. Learning skills that can be executed in sequence requires supervision for both, which is costly to collect on a robot. Human videos offer a scalable source of demonstrations, but transferring them to robots requires extracting robot-compatible observations and actions together with their skill structure. We present DexStitch, a framework that cuts long-horizon human videos into skills and transitions, converts them into aligned robot observation-action-skill data, and stitches the learned skills into a requested sequence. A skill classifier first segments the videos into manipulation skills and transitions. DexStitch then converts the human demonstrations into robot observations and actions while retaining their corresponding skill labels. During conversion, per-finger human-object contacts guide dexterous hand retargeting and robotic inpainting, preserving contact geometry and finger-object occlusion ordering. To stitch the learned skills into a sequence, a shared skill-conditioned policy is pretrained on the resulting synthetic data and finetuned on a mixture of synthetic data and a small set of single-skill teleoperation episodes. Conditioning on the current and next skills guides each transition toward the following skill, while predicted skill progression enables closed-loop switching. On a held-out session, the classifier agrees with manual annotations on 92.4% of frames. Across four household skills, DexStitch achieves 80.0% single-skill success and 77.5% sequence success.

</details>


### [102] [Learning Robot Policies for Whole-Body Mobility Assistance via Dynamic Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17750)
*Shikun Ban,Shohei Nagai,Qingyang Song,Hanxi Wan,Eric Zhao,Thomas Wang,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于仿真的全身辅助机器人策略学习框架，利用对抗运动先验训练人类策略并由人类自适应脚本控制器收集示范，再训练3D扩散策略，实现坐立（STS）与转移（L&T）辅助，并初步验证STS的sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助人类移动可帮助受护者并减轻护理者体力负担，但STS和L&T等全身辅助任务的人机交互数据采集成本高且安全敏感，仿真成为有吸引力的替代方案。既有工作常将人建模为静态或低响应，无法体现移动辅助中的协作本质。

Method: 构建仿真框架：核心是带对抗运动先验训练的人类策略，可针对机器人动作生成真实受护者运动；人类自适应脚本控制器与该策略交互，大规模采集多样化机器人示范；基于示范训练3D扩散策略，将分割后的人体点云和机器人关节位置映射为移动机械臂的全身动作，部署时无需特权人类状态。

Result: 所得机器人策略在仿真中成功完成STS和L&T两类辅助任务；初步实验表明，STS任务在少量真实数据微调后可实现sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过响应式人类策略、规模化示范采集和3D扩散策略，可学习协作式全身辅助机器人策略，并为真实世界迁移提供可行路径。

Abstract: Robotic assistance for human mobility could benefit care recipients and reduce caregiver burden during physically demanding tasks such as Sit-to-Stand (STS) and Lift-and-Transfer (L&T). Learning robot policies for these whole-body assistive tasks is challenging: real-world data collection for human-robot interaction is costly and safety-critical, making simulation an attractive alternative. Prior work, however, typically treats the human as static or minimally responsive and thus fails to capture the cooperative nature of mobility assistance. We present a simulation-based framework for learning whole-body assistive robot policies. At its core is a human policy trained with an adversarial motion prior that produces realistic care-recipient motion in response to the robot's actions. A human-adaptive scripted controller interacts with this policy to collect diverse robot demonstrations at scale. From these demonstrations, we train 3D diffusion policies that map segmented human point clouds and robot joint positions to whole-body actions for a mobile manipulator, without relying on privileged human state at deployment. The resulting robot policies successfully perform both STS and L&T assistance in simulation. Preliminary experiments further demonstrate sim-to-real transfer for STS after fine-tuning on a small amount of real-world data. Videos are available on our project page: this https URL

</details>


### [103] [I3L: Iterative Interactive Imitation Learning for Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17748)
*Wensi Ai,Christopher Luey,Karthik Dharmarajan,Evans Han,Yunfan Jiang,Stef Ren,Jianwen Xie,Jiajun Wu,Li Fei-Fei,Ruohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: I3L 通过 JoyLo+ 全身纠错接口与迭代策略精炼，缓解长时程全身移动操作中离线策略的“最后一公里”可靠性问题；相比基础策略平均提升自主成功率 68.3 个百分点，并在所有任务上达到至少 90% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线从遥操作示范训练的策略在分布外会退化，长时程全身移动操作中长时程会放大误差累积，且手臂、躯干与基座的协调运动使穷举式示范覆盖不切实际，因此需要解决部署可靠性的最后一公里问题。

Method: 提出 I3L，一个结合全身纠正与迭代策略精炼的集成硬件与学习系统。其低成本、开源的 JoyLo+ 接口使用策略跟踪 leader 臂与触摸触发接管，实现跨身体部位的协调纠正；通过迭代部署与再训练，让操作者针对更新后策略的失败进行纠正。评估涵盖 3 种机器人实体、8 个仿真与真实任务，包括 4 个长时程全身移动操作任务；仿真对照实验采用四轮 HG-DAgger。

Result: 四轮 HG-DAgger 在两个任务上取得最高最终成功率。该协议下，I3L 相比基础策略平均提升自主成功率 68.3 个百分点，并在每个任务上达到至少 90% 成功率；在相同训练轨迹数下，比仅示范训练平均高 35.6 个百分点。四轮后，每回合平均接管时长下降 72%。

Conclusion: I3L 有助于弥合离线训练与可靠部署之间的最后一公里差距，并为长时程全身移动操作中纠正数据的收集与使用提供了实践见解。

Abstract: Policies trained offline from teleoperated demonstrations can degrade outside their training distribution. This gap is particularly acute in long-horizon whole-body mobile manipulation, where long horizons amplify compounding errors and coordinated arm, torso, and base motion makes exhaustive demonstration coverage impractical. We introduce I3L, an integrated hardware and learning system that combines whole-body correction with iterative policy refinement to address this last-mile reliability gap. Its low-cost, open-source JoyLo+ interface uses policy-tracking leader arms and touch-triggered takeover to enable coordinated corrections across the body parts, while iterative deployment and retraining allow operators to target failures of the updated policy. We evaluate I3L across three robot embodiments and eight simulated and real-world tasks, including four long-horizon whole-body mobile manipulation tasks. Controlled simulation experiments show that four-round HG-DAgger achieves the highest final success on both tasks. Under this protocol, I3L improves autonomous success over the base policies by an average of 68.3 percentage points and achieves at least 90% success on every task, outperforming demonstration-only training by 35.6 percentage points on average with the same number of training trajectories. Averaged across tasks, takeover duration per episode decreases by 72% over four rounds. Together, these results show that I3L helps close the last-mile gap between offline training and reliable deployment and provides practical insights into collecting and using corrections for long-horizon whole-body mobile manipulation. Videos, visualizations, and appendix are at this https URL .

</details>


### [104] [The Human--VLA Communication Gap: What Humans Leave Implicit and What Policies Cannot Recover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17746)
*Yiqing Xu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将人–VLA通信差距分解为信息差距与残余执行差距，发现人类随任务复杂化而压缩指令、依赖语境与隐式执行结构，而现有策略在重建任务语义/事件结构及应对命令粒度与调用状态上仍较脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人可能掌握任务执行能力，却在人类自然提出请求时失败。论文旨在研究人类自然语言与视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型之间的通信差距，区分信息差距（任务语义、指代 grounding、事件结构未明确）与残余执行差距（任务已完整指定仍受组织与调用方式影响）。

Method: 通过刻画人类如何自然表达已演示目标，并对同一物理任务的控制变体进行评测；以 π0.5 为重点分析，并在 π0-FAST 与 Cosmos3-Edge 上验证一致趋势。

Result: 人类随任务增长而压缩指令，更依赖上下文 grounding 与隐式执行结构。当前策略与这种表达方式不匹配：对 grounding 依赖容忍较好，但在需重建任务语义或事件结构时表现困难；即使信息差距被解决，执行仍对命令粒度与调用状态敏感。

Conclusion: 研究识别出面向人类的策略学习与交互界面的具体改进目标，以更好连接自然人类目标与机器人行为。

Abstract: A robot may know how to perform a task yet fail when a human asks for it naturally. We study this human--VLA communication gap by separating an \emph{information gap}, where task semantics, grounding, or event structure remain unresolved, from a residual \emph{execution gap}, where even a fully specified task depends on how it is organized and invoked. To do this, we characterize how people naturally express demonstrated objectives and evaluate controlled variants of the same physical tasks, with detailed analysis on $\pi_{0.5}$ and consistent trends on $\pi_0$-FAST and Cosmos3-Edge. Our study reveals that humans increasingly compress instructions as tasks grow, relying more on contextual grounding and implicit execution structure. Current policies are misaligned with this behavior: they tolerate grounding dependence relatively well, but struggle when task semantics or event structure must be reconstructed. Even after the information gap is resolved, execution remains sensitive to command granularity and invocation state. These findings identify concrete targets for human-centered policy learning and interfaces that better bridge natural human objectives to robot behavior. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [105] [HelpMeOut: A Neuropsychology-Grounded Benchmark for Error-Aware Monitoring of Household Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17694)
*Haoran Wang,Jingwei Huang,Anqing Duan,Ho Seok Ahn,Bruce Macdonald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HelpMeOut 是一个用于家庭任务执行中错误感知监控的具身 AI 基准，包含 20000 对活动片段和九类神经心理学错误，旨在评估智能体能否实时发现并尽早诊断结构化执行错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身家庭任务基准通常假设人类能正确执行任务，但认知障碍者或老年人的日常活动常产生结构化执行错误，因此需要新的基准来支持错误感知的辅助智能体研究。

Method: HelpMeOut 将日常动作错误研究操作化为 20000 对活动片段，覆盖九类神经心理学动机的错误类别；智能体观察流式人类活动轨迹，需监控执行是否正确，并尽早诊断结构化错误。研究评估了学习型监控器、符号方法、变化点检测和前沿 LLM 提示等基线。

Result: 这些基线方法作为错误监控器表现不佳，常常在執行出错时未能检出错误。

Conclusion: 结果表明，标准目标推断和任务完成基准留下了空白，HelpMeOut 可作为错误感知辅助智能体的测试平台。

Abstract: We introduce HelpMeOut, an embodied AI benchmark for error-aware monitoring of household task execution. Existing embodied household benchmarks typically assume that humans execute tasks correctly, but this assumption does not hold for cognitively impaired persons or older adults whose everyday activities produce structured execution errors. To address this gap, HelpMeOut operationalizes research on everyday action errors into 20000 paired activity episodes, covering nine neuropsychologically motivated error categories. In each episode, agents observe a streaming human activity trajectory and must monitor whether the execution is unfolding correctly, diagnosing any structured errors as early as possible. We evaluate learned-monitor, symbolic, change-point, and frontier LLM prompting baselines. Results show that these methods perform poorly as error monitors, often missing when execution goes wrong. These results suggest a gap left by standard goal-inference and task-completion benchmarks, making HelpMeOut a testbed for error-aware assistive agents.

</details>


### [106] [OccWAM: Occupancy World-Action Models with Joint Scene-to-Ego Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17688)
*Qihang Huang,Wenhui Huang,Collister Chua,Shiyu Fang,Zhan Chen,Songyan Zhang,Zhidong Wang,Daiying Tian,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 OccWAM：一种占用原生世界-动作模型，通过流匹配联合生成稠密未来占用与连续自车轨迹，并在 Bench2Drive、nuScenes 上取得闭环驾驶、开环规划与未来占用预测的 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占用感知规划器通常以层级耦合或并行多任务方式耦合占用预测与动作生成，前者保留显式生成顺序，后者存在角色不对称，均限制了世界预测与动作生成在轨迹形成过程中的双向交互。

Method: OccWAM 以场景表示为条件，用流匹配联合生成稠密未来占用和连续自车轨迹；进一步提出 scene-to-ego world-action flow matching 两阶段框架：第一阶段预测场景中心占用与绝对自车轨迹，提供稳定的未来场景演化表示；随后将预测世界重索引到初始轨迹诱导的未来自车坐标系，以相邻位姿增量表示自车运动，并通过第二次联合流细化自车中心占用与运动，实现动作对齐规划。与基于视频的世界-动作模型不同，OccWAM 保留显式语义几何以直接支持规划推理，无需像素合成。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 和 nuScenes 上的大量实验表明，OccWAM 在闭环驾驶、开环规划和未来占用预测任务上均达到 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: OccWAM 通过占用原生的联合世界-动作建模范式，增强了未来世界预测与动作生成之间的相互交互，为端到端自动驾驶提供了无需像素生成、可直接用于规划推理的有效方案。

Abstract: Occupancy world models provide a structured, metric representation of future driving scenes, making them a promising basis for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing occupancy-aware planners typically couple occupancy prediction and action generation through either hierarchical coupling or parallel multi-task learning. Hierarchical methods use occupancy either as a fully predicted future preceding action generation or as an action-conditioned rollout for trajectory evaluation, whereas parallel multi-task methods predict occupancy alongside planning through a separate branch or auxiliary objective. These interfaces retain either an explicit generation order or a role asymmetry, limiting reciprocal interaction between world prediction and action generation during trajectory formation. We propose OccWAM, an occupancy-native world-action model that jointly generates dense future occupancy and continuous ego trajectories via flow matching conditioned on scene representations. Building on this joint formulation, we introduce scene-to-ego world-action flow matching, a two-stage formulation. The first stage predicts scene-centric occupancy together with an absolute ego trajectory, providing a stable representation of future scene evolution. The same predicted world is then re-indexed into the future ego frames induced by the initial trajectory, where ego motion is represented through adjacent pose increments and a second joint flow refines ego-centric occupancy and motion for action-aligned planning. Unlike video-based world-action models, OccWAM preserves explicit semantic geometry for direct planning reasoning without pixel synthesis. Extensive experiments on Bench2Drive and nuScenes demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in closed-loop driving, open-loop planning, and future occupancy prediction.

</details>


### [107] [PrT-Flow: Probability-Tractable Flow Matching for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17667)
*Congzhong Sun,Ning Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PrT-Flow，一种概率可处理的 Flow Matching 方法，在保留多模态、多步动作生成的同时计算动作概率密度，并成功用于机器人策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和 Flow Matching 能表示灵活的机器人动作分布，但许多强化学习算法需要动作概率，因此需要同时具备多模态生成和概率密度计算能力的方法。

Method: PrT-Flow 采用 Jacobian-Tractable Transformer (JTT) 使每个采样步骤可逆并高效计算雅可比行列式；使用 Conditional Euler-step Training (CET) 监督一步位移而非瞬时速度，并证明在特定假设下 CET 与拟合 Flow Matching 的 Euler-solver 更新具有相同最小化器。

Result: 在 Robomimic Lift 上经 300 epochs 模仿学习达到 96% 成功率，匹配 diffusion policy 参考；在 OGBench Cube-Triple 上经 600k 在线步达到 96% 成功率。

Conclusion: 具有可处理概率密度的可逆网络能学习有效机器人策略，并支持 SAC 风格的 actor-critic 训练。

Abstract: Diffusion models and Flow Matching can represent flexible distributions over robot actions, but many reinforcement learning algorithms also require the probability of each action. We introduce PrT-Flow (Probability-Tractable Flow Matching), which computes probability density while retaining multimodal, multi-step action generation. Its Jacobian-Tractable Transformer (JTT) makes each sampling step invertible and enables efficient computation of its Jacobian determinant. We train these finite steps with Conditional Euler-step Training (CET), which supervises their displacement over one step during sampling rather than their instantaneous velocity. We prove that, under certain assumptions, CET has the same minimizers as fitting the Euler-solver updates of Flow Matching. PrT-Flow achieves 96% success on Robomimic Lift after 300 epochs of imitation learning, matching the diffusion policy reference, and 96% success on OGBench Cube-Triple after 600k online steps. These results demonstrate that invertible networks with tractable probability densities can learn effective robot policies and support SAC-style actor-critic training. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [108] [Towards a Systematic Study of Embodied System 2 over Full Task Execution Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17657)
*Yuxi Wei,Ye Fang,Shuai Yang,Tong Wu,Tai Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出SCOPER套件，用于研究基于观察的规划、执行与恢复，包含基准SCOPER-bench和无需训练的SCOPER-harness，在仿真与真实机器人数据上提升具身任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体（System 2）需要将抽象目标转化为动作，发现相关场景条件，并检查已执行技能是否达到预期效果。现有开环评估无法测试由智能体决策塑造的观察；部分闭环评估在执行中提供的证据有限，或假设理想化的技能结果，因而难以诊断完整执行周期中的规划、执行与恢复能力。

Method: SCOPER-bench结合高层目标、持续视觉观察、共享规则式System 1低层动作与受控执行失败；其构建与验证流程在BEHAVIOR和ProcTHOR中生成870个任务，并配有约3500个与评估智能体执行相关的情境问题。SCOPER-harness将Evidence Memory与Progress Verification同动态任务议程和有界探索相结合，且无需额外模型训练。

Result: 基准结果显示，智能体在保留场景证据、监控进度和处理意外结果方面存在困难。在两种开源骨干模型和两个模拟器上，harness将任务成功率提升11.1–28.6%；经harness增强的9B模型在两个模拟器上均优于直接执行的27B模型。在基于200个真实机器人演示观察的离线下一动作评估中，harness将完整调用准确率分别提升14.7%和15.3%。

Conclusion: SCOPER为诊断和改进完整任务执行周期中的具身推理提供了实验环境，并表明无需训练即可通过外部记忆与验证机制显著增强具身智能体的规划、执行与恢复能力。

Abstract: An embodied brain, or System 2, must turn an abstract goal into actions while discovering relevant scene conditions and checking whether executed skills achieved their intended effects. Open-loop evaluations cannot test observations shaped by an agent's decisions, while some closed-loop evaluations provide limited evidence during execution or assume idealized skill outcomes. We introduce SCOPER, a suite for studying observation-grounded planning, execution, and recovery. SCOPER-bench combines high-level goals, ongoing visual observations, a shared rule-based System 1 for low-level actions, and controlled execution failures. Its construction and validation pipeline yields 870 tasks across BEHAVIOR and ProcTHOR, complemented by about 3,500 situated questions tied to the evaluated agent's execution. Benchmark results reveal difficulties in retaining scene evidence, monitoring progress, and recovering from unexpected outcomes. To address these difficulties, SCOPER-harness combines Evidence Memory and Progress Verification with a dynamic task agenda and bounded exploration, without additional model training. Across two evaluated open-source backbones and two simulators, the harness improves task success by 11.1-28.6%; a harnessed 9B model outperforms a directly executed 27B model on both simulators. In an offline next-action evaluation using observations from 200 real-robot demonstrations, the harness also improves full-call accuracy by 14.7% and 15.3%. SCOPER provides a setting for diagnosing and improving embodied reasoning throughout a complete task execution cycle.

</details>


### [109] [Code2Worlds-2: Physically Plausible Agent Harness for Embodied World Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17651)
*Haoyu Zhang,Siming Li,Zeyu Zhang,Yi Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Code2Worlds-2将代码大模型生成的4D视觉世界扩展为可由物理引擎加载与步进的具身仿真世界，新增SimReady场景编译与仿真器在环细化，并用SimCode4D基准验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码LLM虽能从自然语言合成动态4D世界，但结果只是视觉产物，缺少碰撞几何、质量、摩擦、惯性、关节结构等物理语义；其动力学仅为视觉上看似合理，而非可执行。因此需要可由物理引擎加载、步进并物理求解接触的可执行世界。

Method: 在保持原Code2Worlds双流生成流程不变的基础上追加两模块：1）SimReady Scene Compiler，通过凸分解分配碰撞网格，用视觉语言模型估计物理属性，推断轻量支撑图，实例化接触、摩擦与动力学约束，执行重力沉降，并导出MuJoCo MJCF；2）Simulator-in-the-Loop 4D Refiner，构建compile-simulate-critique-refine自动代理闭环，将视觉动力学转为可执行刚体与关节动力学，并优化物理设置而非仅代码，同时以仿真器评论器检查穿透深度、碰撞对、稳定性、接触力、关节限制和机器人可达性。还提出SimCode4D状态基准，含五类任务模板。

Result: 在SimCode4D及自然、室内、工业、城市、服务等场景上的实验表明，Code2Worlds-2能生成物理上合理、与仿真器兼容且可供机器人交互的场景。

Conclusion: 该工作将4D世界生成推进到具身仿真与可执行物理层面，为语言驱动的可交互机器人仿真环境生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent coding large language models (LLMs) can synthesize dynamic 4D worlds from natural language, but the resulting content remains a visual artifact. It lacks physical semantics such as the collision geometry, mass, friction, inertia, and joint structure, required to enable it to simulate the physical world, and its dynamics are scripted to look plausible rather than designed to execute. What is needed is an executable world: one that a physics engine can load and step, resolving contact physically. We present Code2Worlds-2, an extension of Code2Worlds that turns 4D world generation into an embodied world simulation. We keep the original dual-stream generation pipeline intact and append two modules. First, a SimReady Scene Compiler that turns a generated visual scene into a simulator-compatible representation: it assigns collision meshes via convex decomposition, estimates physical attributes with a vision-language model, infers a lightweight support graph, instantiates the engine's contact, friction, and dynamics constraints, and applies a gravity settling pass, before exporting the scene as MuJoCo MJCF. Second, a Simulator-in-the-Loop 4D Refiner that implements an automated agent harness-a compile, simulate, critique and refine loop-that converts visual dynamics into executable rigid and articulated dynamics and evolves the physical settings rather than only the code, replacing the purely visual motion critic with a simulator critic that inspects penetration depth, collision pairs, stability, contact forces, joint limits, and robot reachability. We further propose SimCode4D, a state-based benchmark with five task templates for evaluating embodied simulation. Experiments on SimCode4D and across natural, indoor, industrial, urban, and service scenes show that Code2Worlds-2 produces physically plausible, simulator-compatible, and robot-interactable scenes.

</details>


### [110] [Acting Through Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17638)
*Yiwei Zhang,Yantai Yang,Jin Gao,Jin Lou,Andong Chen,Yufei Liu,Boyang Xing,Yuchen Zhu,Weiming Hu,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RegisterWAM，通过非空间寄存器与观测流匹配监督重组织世界动作模型中的高范数空间离群点，从而显著提升机器人操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）使用生成式视觉表征来条件化机器人动作，但这些表征如何组织动作相关信息仍不清楚。作者发现WAM表征中持续存在高范数空间离群点，且即使经过归一化和K/V投影削弱其极端幅度，这些离群点仍与动作高度相关，因此需要对其动作相关信息进行重组织。

Method: 提出RegisterWAM：在世界模型中添加可学习的非空间寄存器，并直接用于动作条件化。单独使用寄存器不能完全消除空间离群点，因此结合观测流匹配（FM）损失监督，抑制空间离群点并将高范数响应集中到寄存器中。此外，观测FM监督在无寄存器时也能改善动作学习，因而引入额外的噪声观测FM目标，使模型从损坏输入中学习当前场景恢复。

Result: 无需额外具身预训练数据或基础模型，在LIBERO-Plus上达到81.64%成功率，在RoboTwin 2.0上达到93.0%平均成功率，并提升真实世界操作性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过非空间寄存器与观测流匹配监督重组织WAM中的高范数空间离群点，可有效提升动作学习与机器人操作性能；噪声观测恢复目标进一步带来额外收益。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) use generative visual representations to condition robot actions, yet how these representations organize action-relevant information remains poorly understood. We identify persistent high-norm spatial outliers in WAM representations and find that their action relevance persists even after normalization and K/V projection attenuate their extreme magnitude. Motivated by this observation, we introduce RegisterWAM, which adds learnable non-spatial registers to the world model and uses them directly for action conditioning. Registers alone do not fully eliminate spatial outliers; pairing them with observation flow-matching (FM) loss supervision suppresses spatial outliers while concentrating high-norm responses in registers. Beyond this reorganization, observation FM loss supervision also improves action learning without registers, motivating an additional noised-observation FM objective that learns current-scene recovery from corrupted inputs. The resulting model achieves 81.64\% success on LIBERO-Plus without additional embodied pretraining data or foundation models, reaches 93.0\% average success on RoboTwin 2.0, and improves real-world manipulation performance.

</details>


### [111] [From Pixels to Executable Physics: Behavior-Grounded World Models for Embodied Action Consequence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17630)
*Ruo Chen,Jiahua Pang,Yingjie Niu,Hongyi Lin,Jun Tian,Yang Zhu,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DR-WM：一种融合机器人-地形接触动力学的物理一致世界模型，用于预测四足机器人动作后果并辅助路径选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外地形与接触条件变化会使相同动作产生不同结果，现有视频世界模型虽能生成视觉上合理的未来，但难以捕捉机器人动力学与地形交互，常高估动作效果，不利于可靠导航。

Method: 从初始 RGB 图像重建场景几何，并引入经真实动作-响应数据校准的、密集且无量纲的地形接触属性先验；构建支持摩擦等空间变化接触属性的地形模型。给定机器人初始状态与候选动作序列，模拟未来运动，并用相机轨迹与渲染几何指导视频世界模型训练；生成未来观测再更新地形重建，实现无需额外真实图像的迭代 rollout。

Result: 大量实验表明，DR-WM 在刻画动作结果的关键物理指标上显著优于仅几何基线和均匀物理参数基线，并在所评估基线中取得最佳性能。在 90 个挑战场景中，8B VLM 结合 DR-WM 在六项指标中的五项上超过无辅助的 32B VLM。

Conclusion: DR-WM 通过物理接地预测提升四足机器人动作后果建模与动作选择，表明接触动力学感知的世界模型可增强户外导航决策，并可能以更小模型取得优于更大基线模型的表现。

Abstract: In outdoor environments, varying terrain and contact conditions can cause identical action commands to produce different motion outcomes for quadruped robots. Predicting these outcomes is essential for reliable navigation and informed path selection. Existing video world models can generate visually plausible futures, yet often struggle to capture robot dynamics and terrain interactions, leading to overly optimistic predictions of action outcomes. We present DR-WM, a physically consistent world model that incorporates robot-terrain contact dynamics to predict the consequences of quadruped actions. Starting from an initial RGB image, our method reconstructs scene geometry and integrates dense, dimensionless priors over terrain contact properties, calibrated using real-world action-response data. The resulting terrain model supports the simulation of physics with spatially varying contact properties such as friction. Given the initial state of the robot and candidate action sequences, we simulate its future motion and use the resulting camera trajectories and rendered geometry to guide the training of a video world model. The generated future observations are then used to update the terrain reconstruction, enabling iterative model rollouts without additional real-world images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DR-WM substantially outperforms geometry-only baselines and baselines with uniform physical parameters on key physical metrics characterizing action outcomes, while achieving the best performance among the evaluated baselines. These physically grounded predictions support more informed action selection: across 90 challenging scenarios, an 8B vision-language model augmented with DR-WM outperforms an unassisted 32B vision-language model on five of six evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [112] [Training Generalist Navigation Policies via Action Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17593)
*Bowen Feng,Keqin Wang,Yulong Yang,Amogh Joshi,Christine Allen-Blanchette,Dhruv Shah,Felix Heide*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Action Self-Distillation (ASD)，无需人工标注语言或推理标签，利用开源 VLM 自蒸馏学习语言条件机器人导航推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言是自然的目标指定与推理接口，但训练语言条件机器人策略与推理通常依赖昂贵的人工标注。

Method: ASD 将开源 VLM 同时作为教师网络和学生策略；训练时自教师接收特权轨迹反馈，推断潜在导航目标及目标导向推理；学生仅基于机载观测，学习以推断目标为条件，预测未来 ego-frame 轨迹，并生成场景语义、规划与推理描述。

Result: 在 13 个具有挑战性的室内外真实导航任务中，成功率较最强基线在室内设置提升 30%，多阶段任务提升 30%，室外任务达到 95% 成功率。

Conclusion: 验证了特权轨迹反馈可支持自蒸馏，从而在无人工标注下实现可扩展的通用导航策略。

Abstract: Language provides a natural interface for humans to specify and reason about goals, but training language-conditioned robotic policies and reasoning typically requires expensive manual annotations. We propose Action Self-Distillation (ASD), a method for learning language-conditioned reasoning for robotic navigation without human-annotated language or reasoning labels. ASD uses an open-source VLM, which contains broad world knowledge and reasoning capabilities, as both a teacher network and a student policy. During training, the self-teacher receives privileged trajectory feedback and infers the underlying navigation goal together with goal-oriented reasoning. The student is then trained to condition on the inferred navigation goal, predict future ego-frame trajectory, and provide a description of the scene semantics, plan, and reasoning, from onboard observations alone. Across 13 challenging indoor and outdoor real-world navigation tasks, our method improves success rate over the strongest baseline by $30\%$ in indoor settings, $30\%$ on multi-stage tasks, and achieves $95\%$ success rate on outdoor tasks, validating that privileged trajectory feedback can enable self-distillation for scalable generalist navigation policies without human annotations.

</details>


### [113] [TacSpin: Tactile Tracking of In-Gripper Object Rotation on SO(3) via Axis-Magnitude Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17574)
*Yuhao Wu,Linhao Song,Huan Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacSpin仅用两个视触觉传感器，通过解耦估计旋转轴与角度并融合双指信息，实现夹持器内物体在SO(3)上的旋转跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夹持器内物体运动跟踪对机器人操作至关重要，但接触覆盖有限且变形不均，导致触觉估计困难。

Method: 提出TacSpin：分别估计旋转响应的轴与幅值，而非最小二乘拟合刚体模型；利用接触边界线索细化各手指响应；依据双指一致性进行融合。

Result: 在标准物体上平均检查点误差更低；在真实物体上，通过标准球迁移标定，展示了不同接触几何下的跟踪效果。

Conclusion: TacSpin能有效实现夹持器内物体旋转跟踪，并具备向真实物体与不同接触条件泛化的潜力。

Abstract: Tracking object motion within a gripper provides essential feedback for robotic manipulation. However, limited contact coverage and uneven deformation make tactile estimation challenging. In this study, we present TacSpin, a method that tracks in-gripper object rotation on SO(3) using only two vision-based tactile sensors. The key idea is to estimate the axis and magnitude of the rotation response separately, rather than fitting a rigid-body model by least squares. Each finger's response is further refined with contact-boundary cues. The two fingers are then fused according to their agreement. On standard objects, TacSpin achieves lower mean checkpoint errors. Qualitative demonstrations on real-world objects, using calibration transferred from a standard sphere, illustrate tracking under different contact geometries.

</details>


### [114] [EgoCPT: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from a Single Ego-Centric Video with Contact-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17572)
*Zhixin Sun,Kairui Shi,Tianao Ren,Ming Yang,Mark Cutkosky,James Jingxi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EgoCPT，从第一人称RGB视频和参考手部姿态轨迹直接学习可部署到真实机器人的灵巧手重定向轨迹，并通过接触感知优化提升对噪声手部姿态的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人专家演示难以收集，而互联网上人类第一人称视频丰富易得；但由于人与机器人具身差异大，重建具有物理合理性的机器人轨迹仍很困难，阻碍了从人类视频学习灵巧操作策略。

Method: 仅输入第一人称RGB视频和参考手部姿态轨迹（可由现成软件或专用相机获得）；提出接触感知优化框架，迭代优化机器手与物体之间的接触点，从而直接从视频生成高质量重定向灵巧手轨迹，并可部署到真实机器人。

Result: 在五个操作任务上，对含噪EgoDex手部姿态重建取得67.3%的总体成功率，对运动捕捉硬件采集的高质量TACO重建取得92.0%的总体成功率，并在两个基准上优于多个基线。

Conclusion: EgoCPT为从人类第一人称视频学习可部署灵巧操作轨迹提供了有效且鲁棒的范式，表明接触感知重定向可缓解具身差距问题，并在噪声与高质量输入下均有优异表现。

Abstract: While expert demonstrations on real robots are difficult to collect, human ego-centric videos are easy to collect and abundant on the internet. However, learning dexterous manipulation policies from human videos remains challenging---one of the most important challenges being the difficulty of reconstructing physically grounded robot trajectories due to the large embodiment gap. In this paper, we propose EgoCPT, a novel paradigm for learning high-quality retargeted dexterous hand trajectories directly from an ego-centric video, which can then be deployed directly on real robots. The only inputs to our method are an ego-centric RGB video and a reference hand pose trajectory, which can be easily obtained through off-the-shelf software or specialized cameras during recording. Our method is particularly robust to noisy hand poses attributed to our novel contact-aware optimization framework, which iteratively optimizes the contact points between the robot hands and objects. Across five manipulation tasks, our method achieves overall success rates of 67.3% on noisy EgoDex hand-pose reconstructions and 92.0% on high-quality TACO reconstructions collected using motion-capture hardware, outperforming multiple baselines on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [115] [Mobility-Budget-Constrained Submodular Maximization for Distributed Semantic Multi-Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17562)
*Changxuan Yao,Sandilya Sai Garimella,Lu Gan,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分布式语义探索框架，结合Dirichlet地图、预算约束轨迹选择与RAG协调，在通信和移动约束下提升多机器人探索覆盖与语义精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人探索在通信与移动约束下需平衡信息增益与可达性；最短距离前沿选择难以同时满足移动预算和语义不确定性降低需求。

Method: 框架包含Dirichlet建图、每个机器人聚类前沿、用单次预算受限Dijkstra搜索计算地形与朝向感知的最小代价路径，并按边际信息增益（Dirichlet熵期望减少）选择可行路径；采用Resource-Aware distributed Greedy (RAG)协调。每轮单机选择精确，团队每轮联合信息增益至少为中心化最优的一半，通信受限时该界优雅退化。

Result: 十次网格世界消融表明，相比最短距离前沿选择，覆盖、语义mIoU和地图确定性更高，熵与单位距离遍历代价更低。预算敏感性实验显示，中等预算最能平衡路径可达范围与滚动时域执行。在真实感全系统仿真中对比五种原生协调规则，本文方法取得最高平均覆盖和语义精度，且是唯一遵守每条轨迹移动预算的方法。

Conclusion: 预算受限的分布式语义探索与RAG协调可在通信和移动约束下有效提升多机器人探索性能，并保持可证明的近似保证。

Abstract: Multi-robot exploration under communication and mobility constraints requires balancing information gain with reachability. We present a distributed semantic exploration framework that combines Dirichlet mapping, budget-constrained trajectory selection, and Resource-Aware distributed Greedy (RAG) coordination. Each robot clusters frontiers, computes terrain- and heading-aware least-cost paths with a single budget-limited Dijkstra search, and selects feasible paths by marginal information gain, represented by the expected reduction in Dirichlet entropy. Each robot's per-round selection is exact, the team's joint per-round information gain is at least half the centralized optimum, and the bound degrades gracefully under limited communication. Ten-run gridworld ablations show improved coverage, semantic mIoU, and map certainty with lower entropy and traversal cost per unit distance than shortest-distance frontier selection. Budget-sensitivity experiments show that an intermediate budget best balances path reach and receding-horizon execution. In photorealistic full-system simulation against five coordination rules run in their native published forms, the proposed method attains the highest mean coverage and semantic accuracy while being the only one that respects the per-trajectory mobility budget.

</details>


### [116] [ATOM: Abstract Transition and Option Modeling - Experience-Informed Hierarchical Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17555)
*Changxuan Yao,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ATOM：一种分层导航框架，将高层图规划与可复用的低层option策略结合，面向由3D场景图等层次结构表示的大规模3D环境中的重复导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模3D环境中的重复导航若每次使用单体MDP规划，计算代价高；需要利用层次化抽象与策略复用来提升效率。

Method: ATOM在层次图表示上运行，例如3DSG的Rooms与Places层；每条有向抽象转移关联一个导航option。对每个转移，ATOM计算并存储可复用的option策略及其状态相关cost-to-go。跨重复导航任务，ATOM维护option起始状态的光滑经验分布，并用其渐进细化对应抽象转移代价。

Result: 消融研究显示，相对于单体MDP基线，ATOM将累计重复规划时间降低97.5%，同时轨迹保持在Global Places A*最短路径参考约7%以内。经验驱动的细化使抽象转移代价估计误差进一步降低10.3%。F1Tenth实车实验验证ATOM成功完成全部5个导航查询，其中43%的跨区域option执行复用了先前生成的解。

Conclusion: ATOM通过分层抽象转移、可复用option策略与经验驱动的代价细化，实现了高效的大规模3D重复导航，同时保持接近最短路径的轨迹质量，并具备实车可行性。

Abstract: We propose ATOM (Abstract Transition and Option Modeling), a hierarchical navigation framework that combines higher-level graph planning with reusable lower-level option policies for efficient repeated navigation in large-scale 3D environments represented by hierarchical structures such as 3D scene graphs (3DSGs). ATOM operates on a hierarchical graph representation, implemented using, for instance, the Rooms and Places layers of a 3DSG, where each directed abstract transition is associated with a navigation option. For each transition, ATOM computes and stores a reusable option policy along with its state-dependent cost-to-go. Across repeated navigation tasks, ATOM maintains a smoothed empirical distribution over option initiation states and uses it to progressively refine the corresponding abstract transition cost. Ablation studies show that ATOM reduces cumulative repeated-planning time by 97.5% relative to a monolithic MDP baseline while maintaining trajectories within approximately 7% of a Global Places A* shortest-path reference. Experience-informed refinement further reduces abstract transition-cost estimation error by 10.3%. Physical experiments validate ATOM on an F1Tenth vehicle, successfully completing all five navigation queries with 43% of inter-region option executions reusing previously generated solutions.

</details>


### [117] [Sim4RAD: A MATLAB Toolkit for Design-Phase Sizing, Actuator Selection and URDF-Model Verification of Serial Robotic Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17534)
*Jingchen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Sim4RAD 是一套用于执行器选型与验证的 MATLAB 工具包：从少量参数建立刚体模型，通过静力、动力学、随机运动和可达/灵巧度四类分析给出关节规格，并可用 URDF 模型对详细设计进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行器选型是早期且影响重大的决策；关节扭矩与速度规格会在 CAD 模型出现前就限定电机、减速器和制动器，因此需要可靠工具支持选型及后续验证。

Method: 该工具包从少量参数构建刚体模型，提供四类分析：静力分析结合杠杆臂采样与姿态搜索，得到给定负载包络下的额定扭矩和可复现峰值扭矩；动力学流程在四种约束（含执行器扭矩-速度包络）之一下重塑轨迹；随机分析基于大量随机运动，使规格依赖一类运动而非单一轨迹；工作空间与灵巧度分析区分可达位置与可达方式。将合成模型替换为 URDF 文件后，同一套分析即可用于验证。

Result: 对 7 自由度拟人臂比较三种布局（肩轴顺序不同、末端腕关节是否与负载同轴）。半臂长处的静力需求在三种布局中几乎相同，且主要用于确定关节规格而非采样运动；重排肩轴不改变最坏扭矩（55 N m），可达体积差异在 8% 以内。末端腕关节与负载同轴可消除其重力扭矩，使相邻腕关节降低近三分之一，并在 5 m/s 扫描中使最后一个关节需求降低十倍，但灵巧度下降（均值 0.570 降至 0.348）。同一机械臂在 5 m/s 目标下所需扭矩是 2 s 运动的 20 至 37 倍：必须先固定场景，而非轨迹。

Conclusion: 验证应使用详细设计的 URDF 模型；该工具包已在给定 https URL 提供。

Abstract: Actuator sizing is an early and consequential decision: joint torque and velocity specifications fix the motors, gearboxes and brakes before any CAD model exists. Sim4RAD is a MATLAB toolkit for that phase and for the verification that follows it. It builds a rigid-body model from a handful of parameters, and four analyses supply the specification: a static analysis combining lever-arm sampling with a posture search, giving a rated torque at a chosen payload envelope and a reproducible peak torque; a dynamics pipeline that reshapes a trajectory under one of four constraints, including the actuator torque-speed envelope; a stochastic analysis over many random motions, so a specification rests on a class of motions, not one trajectory; and a workspace and dexterity analysis that separates where the arm can reach from how it can reach there. Replacing the synthesized model by a URDF file turns the same analyses to verification. A 7-DOF anthropomorphic arm is compared in three layouts differing in the shoulder axis order and in whether the last wrist joint is coaxial with the payload. The static demand at half arm length is nearly the same in all three, and it sizes the joints rather than the sampled motion; reordering the shoulder axes leaves the worst-case torque unchanged at 55 N m and the reachable volume within 8 percent. A last wrist joint coaxial with the payload removes its gravity torque, reduces the adjacent wrist joint by almost a third and cuts the last joint demand tenfold in a 5 m/s scan, at the price of dexterity (mean 0.570 to 0.348). The same arm needs 20 to 37 times more torque for a 5 m/s target than for a 2 s motion: the scenario, not the trajectory, must be fixed first. Verification should use the URDF model of the detailed design; the toolkit is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [118] [Box-WAM: Box Trajectories as Compact State Representations for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17526)
*Qinxi Yu,Wenzhou Ding,Gloria X. Wang,Albert J. Zhai,Shenlong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BoxWAM，用任务相关物体的有向包围盒轨迹替代稠密未来视频帧，作为轻量级世界动作建模表示，在降低计算成本的同时提升动作预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型通过显式预测动作与未来世界状态，在多种机器人操作任务中优于仅动作模型；但全帧视频预测在训练和推理阶段计算代价高昂。核心问题是：何种紧凑状态表示能保留未来预测带来的规划与监督收益？

Method: BoxWAM将未来表示从稠密视频帧替换为任务相关物体的有向包围盒轨迹，显式想象以物体为中心的未来运动，从而提供轻量预测目标并捕捉机器人动作引起的关键物理变化。

Result: 包围盒轨迹预测显著降低计算成本，同时提高动作预测性能。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心的紧凑状态可成为机器人操作中视频式世界动作建模的高效替代方案。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) are a category of policy models that ex- plicitly predict both actions and future world states. They have been shown to outperform action-only models across various robotic manipulation tasks. How- ever, representing the future with full-frame video prediction can be computation- ally expensive during both training and inference. This raises a central question: what compact state representation preserves the planning and supervision bene- fits of future prediction? In this paper, we propose BoxWAM, which replaces dense future frames with oriented bounding box trajectories of task-relevant ob- jects. By explicitly imagining object-centric future motion, our method provides a lightweight prediction target that captures the key physical changes induced by robot actions. We show that box trajectory prediction substantially reduces com- putational cost while improving action prediction performance, suggesting that object-centric compact states can serve as an efficient alternative to video-based world action modeling for robotic manipulation. Result videos can be found on this https URL

</details>


### [119] [YUBI-Real2Sim: 3DGS Scene Verification for Reliable Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17521)
*Hiroki Nishizawa,Takehiko Ohkawa,Nicola Scianca,Tatsuya Matsushima,Shigeo Morishima,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出YUBI-Real2Sim统一框架，基于3DGS与MuJoCo在双鱼眼腕部视角下进行真实到仿真策略评估，在9组场景-任务对比中复现8组真实策略排序，并系统分析3DGS伪影对VLA策略评估可靠性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人上评估VLA策略成本高、耗时且难以复现，因此需要real-to-sim仿真实现可扩展评估。3DGS虽可快速生成真实到仿真场景，但可靠策略评估所需的3DGS重建质量尚未被量化。依赖腕部鱼眼相机的策略（如YUBI手持夹爪数据）在广角视图中易受孔洞、漂浮物等视觉伪影影响，直接降低任务表现。

Method: 提出YUBI-Real2Sim统一框架，集成200度鱼眼相机采集、3DGS训练、3D基础模型物体建模与MuJoCo物理仿真。构建跨两个双臂机器人平台、三个场景、三个任务及两个基于pi0.5策略的基准，测量real-to-sim策略排序一致性，并评估策略对反事实3DGS伪影（天花板孔洞、漂浮物、扭曲）和采集扰动的敏感性。进一步在场景留出评估协议下分析新视角质量分数能否指示策略排序可靠性。

Result: 该框架在9组场景-任务对比中复现了8组真实世界策略排序。基准结果显示性能具有任务依赖性，且基于新视角质量分数的策略排序可靠性预测存在局限；3DGS伪影与采集扰动会影响策略评估。

Conclusion: 使用Real2Sim进行策略评估时必须评估重建质量，尤其需关注双鱼眼腕部视图下的3DGS伪影。当前结果尚不足以建立通用可靠的场景接受准则。

Abstract: Evaluating VLA policies on real robots is costly, time-consuming, and difficult to reproduce, making real-to-sim simulation essential for scalable evaluation. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables rapid real-to-sim scene generation, the reconstruction quality required for reliable policy evaluation remains unquantified. In particular, policies relying on wrist-mounted fisheye cameras (e.g., YUBI handheld gripper data) are heavily exposed to visual artifacts (holes, floaters) in wide-angle views, directly degrading task performance. To address this, we present YUBI-Real2Sim, a unified framework for 3DGS-based scene reconstruction and real-to-sim policy evaluation under dual fisheye wrist views. We develop an integrated pipeline combining 200-degree fisheye camera capture, 3DGS training, 3D foundation models for object modeling, and MuJoCo-based physical simulation, reproducing the observed real-world policy ordering in eight of nine scene-task comparisons. Using this framework, we construct a benchmark across two bimanual robot platforms, three scenes, three tasks, and dual pi0.5-based policies. This systematically measures real-to-sim rank agreement and evaluates policy sensitivity against counterfactual 3DGS artifacts (ceiling holes, floaters, warping) and capture disturbances. We further analyze whether novel-view quality scores can indicate policy-ranking reliability under a scene-held-out evaluation protocol. The results show task-dependent performance and limitations of quality-based reliability prediction, highlighting the need to assess reconstruction quality when using Real2Sim for policy evaluation. These results do not yet establish a generally reliable scene-acceptance rule. Project Page: this https URL .

</details>


### [120] [An Optimal Algorithm for Changing from Longitudinal to Latitudinal Formation of Autonomous Aircraft Squadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17511)
*Paulo André Sperandio Giacomin,Elder Moreira Hemerly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将自主飞行器编队从纵向队形转换为横向队形的算法，基于预定义三维基本机动动作动态定义机动，并证明其时间效率最优且无碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主飞行器编队在执行着陆、起飞、避障和地理参考等任务时，需要同时具备纵向和横向队形，因此必须实现两种队形之间的安全高效转换。

Method: 利用一组预定义的三维基本机动动作动态定义转换机动，设计从纵向到横向的队形变换算法，并对算法进行时间复杂度分析和正确性证明。

Result: 时间复杂度分析表明算法效率最优；仿真显示队形变换过程无碰撞；正确性证明保证转换后的横向队形特征。

Conclusion: 该算法能够高效、安全地实现自主飞行器编队从纵向到横向的队形转换，适用于需要同时使用两种队形的任务场景。

Abstract: This work presents an algorithm for changing from longitudinal to latitudinal formation of autonomous aircraft squadrons. The maneuvers are defined dynamically by using a predefined set of 3D basic maneuvers. This formation change is necessary when the squadron has to perform tasks demanding both formations, such as landing, lift-off, obstacle avoidance and georeferencing. The time complexity analysis of the transformation algorithm reveals that its efficiency is optimal. Simulations show that the formation change is done without collision, and the proof of correctness ensures its latitudinal formation features.

</details>


### [121] [From Orbit Matching to Docking: A Hierarchical Reinforcement-Learning Approach to Multiphase Rendezvous Trajectory Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17468)
*Mariko Storey-Matsutani,Anatoly Zlotnik,Lee Holguin,Zigfried Hampel-Arias,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层强化学习框架，用五个阶段专用策略和高层管理器策略实现从轨道匹配到对接的自主三自由度RPO，并与固定顺序调用启发式进行端到端对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间任务复杂度增加，RPO需跨越轨道匹配、远距离接近、近距离机动和终端对接等多个动态、目标与约束不同的阶段；现有强化学习方法通常只覆盖部分序列或依赖人工定义控制器切换条件。

Method: 采用分层强化学习：为轨道匹配、远距离接近、中距离接近、近距离接近和对接分别训练五个专用策略；再学习高层管理器策略，根据航天器状态在各阶段控制器间选择；面向三自由度RPO，覆盖从轨道匹配到对接的完整轨迹。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果；研究在全端到端交会序列上评估，并与固定顺序调用启发式比较轨迹性能和过渡行为，同时分析各阶段策略及完整分层系统的训练与推理指标。

Conclusion: 该分层强化学习框架可覆盖完整RPO轨迹，并通过学习型高层管理器实现状态驱动的阶段切换；其相对固定顺序启发式的具体性能优势仍需实验数据验证。

Abstract: As the complexity of space missions increases, autonomous spacecraft guidance must accommodate transitions between mission phases with distinct dynamics, objectives, and constraints. Rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) are particularly challenging because they span multiple operating regimes, from orbital-scale matching and far-range approach to close-range maneuvering and terminal docking. Existing reinforcement-learning approaches generally address only a subset of this sequence or rely on manually defined switching conditions between controllers. This study presents a hierarchical reinforcement-learning framework for autonomous, three-degree-of-freedom RPO that spans the complete trajectory from orbit matching to docking. Five specialized policies are trained for orbit matching, far-range approach, mid-range approach, close-range approach, and docking. A learned high-level manager policy then selects among these phase-specific controllers based on the evolving spacecraft state. The hierarchical approach is evaluated over the full end-to-end rendezvous sequence and compared with a fixed sequential-invocation heuristic to assess trajectory performance and transition behavior. Training and inference metrics are also analyzed for the individual phase policies and the complete hierarchical system.

</details>


### [122] [Act4R: Action-conditioned 4D reconstruction for kinematics-free control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17462)
*Zhaoliang Zhang,Chenhao Yu,Zhimin Shao,Xijun Liu,Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Act4R，基于3D基础模型从2D观测与动作信号预测机器人3D场景流，并支持逆控制；在真实与仿真数据上优于先前SoTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人运动精确控制长期困难；建模复杂机器人结构耗时且需专用传感器与人工标注；希望利用从2D观测提升的4D特征，按动作信号密集映射机器人空间运动。

Method: Act4R以3D基础模型为基础，引入空间-动作编码器，将当前2D机器人状态与动作信号编码为表示未来机器人3D状态的嵌入；当前与未来空间嵌入解码为点图，估计动作条件场景流；在Transformer编码器中进行空间-动作融合，并用空间对齐损失引导未来流预测；在给定实体少量样本训练后，通过优化动作信号实现逆控制。

Result: 在公开真实数据集和仿真合成数据上评估；多种机器人平台实验显示，Act4R在场景流预测上优于先前SoTA；逆控制中动作精度更高，仿真闭环控制表现也更好。

Conclusion: Act4R能有效学习动作到未来3D场景流的映射，并支持逆控制，在跨机器人平台场景流预测与闭环控制中具有优势。

Abstract: Precise control of movement across various robotic embodiments is a longstanding challenge. Modeling complex robotic structures requires significant time, dedicated sensors, and manual labeling. Recent advancements in 4D representation learning offer an interesting alternative: can we leverage 4D features lifted from 2D observations to densely map a robot's spatial movement based on the action signal? To this end, we propose Act4R, a framework that builds on 3D foundation model to predict a robot's 3D scene flow with any given action. Act4R introduces a Spatial-Action Encoder, which encodes the current 2D robot state and its action signals into embeddings that represent future robot state in 3D. Then, both the current and future spatial embeddings are decoded into point maps to estimate action-conditioned scene flow. To effectively learn the mapping between action and future state, Act4R introduces spatial-action fusion in its transformer-based encoder, and a spatial alignment loss that guides the encoder for future flow prediction. After training on some samples of a given embodiment, Act4R can perform inverse control by optimizing the signal to achieve the desired scene flow. We evaluate Act4R on a public real-world dataset and synthetic data collected in simulator. Experiments on various robotic platforms show that Act4R outperforms the previous SoTA in scene flow prediction. Our method also performs better in inverse control, both on the action accuracy metric and in closed-loop control in simulation.

</details>


### [123] [Smoothness Ranks Skill, Not Success: An Audit of Published Trajectory-Smoothness Curation Metrics Under Episode-Length Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17427)
*Jaiveer Bassi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文检验RINSE提出的轨迹平滑度指标SAL与TED在真实robomimic数据上是否受回合长度混杂影响，并评估其筛选示范数据对下游模仿学习的影响；结果表明这些指标在长度控制后大多失效，TED甚至有害，且筛选结论不能跨策略配置迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹平滑度被当作廉价、免训练的模仿学习示范筛选信号，但已有指标可能只是捕捉回合长度或任务成功相关的运动幅度差异，而非真正可迁移的筛选准则。

Method: 在真实robomimic数据上复现SAL和接触感知TED，加入回合长度控制与固定长度回合分析；比较SAL、TED、平均jerk及随机筛选；用筛选子集训练GMM行为克隆策略（10个种子）和扩散策略（5个种子），并检查轨迹运动特征。

Result: can/mh上SAL的AUROC为0.952，但回合长度单独达0.957；在等时长区间内SAL降至0.628，而jerk升至0.896，square/mh复现该混杂。can/mg每回合固定150步时，SAL接近随机（0.454），TED相对任务成功反向（0.376），jerk为0.706。GMM策略中，TED筛选比同规模随机筛选显著更差（0.240 vs. 0.360，p=0.0001，Bonferroni校正后仍显著），SAL和jerk与不筛选无显著差异。扩散策略下TED仍最差但5个种子不足以分辨，jerk显著优于两个基线，说明筛选决策不跨策略配置迁移。

Conclusion: SAL和TED的筛选效果主要受回合长度与运动幅度混杂驱动；TED会反向偏好失败轨迹，因为成功操作需要接触中持续的大偏差运动，而失败常是低幅悬停。因此，这些平滑度指标不能作为稳定、免训练的示范筛选信号，筛选结论依赖策略配置，需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Trajectory smoothness has been proposed as a cheap, training-free signal for curating imitation-learning demonstrations, most recently by rinse, which introduces Spectral Arc Length (SAL) and a contact-aware Trajectory-Envelope Distance (TED). We test whether these published metrics survive episode-length controls on real robomimic data. On can/mh, SAL separates better from worse operators with AUROC 0.952, but episode length alone achieves 0.957, and inside a duration-matched band SAL falls to 0.628 while raw mean jerk, the baseline rinse argues against, rises to 0.896. The confound replicates on square/mh. On can/mg, where every episode is exactly 150 steps, SAL is at chance (0.454) and TED is inverted (0.376) against task success, while jerk reaches 0.706. Training GMM behavior-cloning policies on each metric's curated subset (10 seeds), curating by TED costs roughly a third of downstream success relative to random selection of the same size (0.240 vs. 0.360, p=0.0001, surviving Bonferroni correction), while SAL and jerk are indistinguishable from not curating. Under a diffusion policy TED still ranks worst, though its deficit is not resolvable at five seeds, and jerk becomes significantly better than both baselines, so the curation decision does not transfer unchanged across the two policy configurations we tested. Inspecting trajectories shows why TED inverts: successful manipulation requires sustained high-deviation motion through contact, while failure is low-amplitude hovering that a smoothed envelope follows closely.

</details>


### [124] [MIRROR: Reflecting Hidden Symmetries from Offline Robot Interaction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17422)
*Shiqi Liu,Yihang Yao,Peide Huang,Mengdi Xu,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIRROR 提出从离线交互数据中学习已知对称群在 POMDP 观测与动作上的联合变换，通过对抗式一步转移元组匹配耦合观测-动作变换，并用群一致性正则约束群公理；在 Robosuite 操作任务上提升对称质量与行为克隆成功率，并零样本部署到真实 Kinova GEN3 机械臂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素观测下，即使简单物理对称也会产生复杂且依赖视角的图像变换，难以手工指定。现有对称感知方法通常依赖预定义变换表示，并需要针对感知设置和任务进行预处理。

Method: 提出 MIRROR 框架，从离线交互数据中学习已知对称群在部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）中对观测和动作的联合作用。方法通过一步转移元组上的对抗式元组匹配耦合观测变换与动作变换，并加入显式群一致性正则化，惩罚违反群运算公理的行为；学到的变换用于帧平均行为克隆（frame-averaged BC）。

Result: 在四个 Robosuite 桌面操作任务上，MIRROR 在四个对称质量指标中的三个取得最低聚合误差，其中 compatibility error 相比适配的 LaLiGAN 基线降低 61%。使用学习变换进行 frame-averaged BC 后，四个任务平均成功率从 80.48% 提升到 85.12%，单任务最高提升 9.66 个百分点。作者还在高度图和分割掩码观测下重复对称发现与策略训练，并将所得策略零样本部署到真实 Kinova GEN3 机械臂。

Conclusion: MIRROR 无需针对传感设置和任务手工设计变换或预处理，即可在像素 POMDP 中学习观测-动作联合对称变换，提升策略学习效果，并展现出向真实机器人零样本迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Symmetry-aware robot policy learning requires coupled observation-action transformations. For pixel observations, even simple physical symmetries can induce complex, viewpoint-dependent image transformations that are difficult to specify by hand. Existing symmetry-aware methods often rely on representations with predefined transformations, requiring preprocessing tailored to the sensing setup and task. We introduce MIRROR, a framework that learns how a known symmetry group operates jointly on observations and actions in partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) from offline interaction data. MIRROR couples these transformations through adversarial tuple matching over one-step transition tuples, while explicit group-consistency regularization penalizes violations of the group-operation axioms.
