On four Robosuite tabletop manipulation tasks, MIRROR achieves the lowest aggregate error on three of four symmetry-quality metrics, including a 61% reduction in compatibility error (which measures consistency under transformation composition) relative to an adapted LaLiGAN baseline. Using the learned transformations in frame-averaged behavior cloning (BC) yields higher mean success rates than vanilla BC on all four tasks, increasing average success from 80.48% to 85.12%, with per-task gains up to 9.66 percentage points. We also repeat symmetry discovery and policy training in simulation with heightmap and segmentation-mask observations, then deploy the resulting policy zero-shot on a real Kinova GEN3 arm. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [125] [ClearNav: Perception-Aligned Quadrotor Flight Using Differential-Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17419)
*Aditya Azad,Arthur Huang,Guanrui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ClearNav 是一种面向小型四旋翼在杂乱环境中安全自主飞行的导航策略。它通过奖励约束将运动限制在机载相机可观测方向，并在障碍物附近惩罚速度，从而显著降低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主四旋翼在杂乱环境中飞行对巡检、搜索等任务至关重要。仿真训练并迁移到硬件的策略虽能在开阔空间高速飞行，但当障碍物间距变小时仍会碰撞。作者认为多数碰撞源于运动方向未被机载相机观测：航向与行进方向不一致，以及避障时的横向偏移，都会把飞行器带向视野外的障碍物。

Method: 提出 ClearNav 导航策略，训练中加入三项奖励/惩罚：1）前向航向目标，使航向与可观测方向对齐；2）横向速度惩罚，抑制横向偏移；3）障碍物附近速度惩罚，使进入视野的障碍物仍可被规避。

Result: 仿真中，在 18 个未见保留场景、每个策略 2304 次配对飞行下，ClearNav 相比当前最优方法将成功率提升 6.8 个百分点，碰撞率降低 65%，最高速度达 5 m/s。仅使用近距障碍速度惩罚项且在匹配指令速度下，碰撞率仍降低 70%。在静态场景中效果显著，在移动障碍物上方向一致但套件级不显著。真实杂乱环境中也验证了策略能使四旋翼成功且安全地导航。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使小型无人机仅具备有限传感器和计算能力，也能在杂乱环境中实现更安全的飞行，并具备真实环境部署可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous quadrotor flight in cluttered environment is an key capability for tasks like inspection and search. Navigation policies trained in simulation and transferred to hardware can fly at high speed in open space, but they can still collide as obstacle clearance shrinks. We attribute most of these collisions to a single mechanism, namely motion in directions the onboard camera does not observe. Heading that is not aligned with the direction of travel and lateral excursion during obstacle avoidance both carry the vehicle toward obstacles outside its field of view. We introduce ClearNav, a navigation policy trained with two reward terms that constrain motion to the observed direction, a forward-looking heading target and a penalty on lateral velocity. A third term penalizes speed near obstacles so that an obstacle entering the field of view remains avoidable. In simulation, across 18 held-out scenarios with 2304 paired flights per policy unseen in training, ClearNav improves success by 6.8 points, cuts the collision rate by 65% relative to the state of the arts, and flies at maximum 5m/s. For the proximity term alone under matched commanded speeds, the collision reduction still holds at 70%. The effect is significant on static scenes and consistent in direction on moving obstacles, though not significant there at the suite level, showing that safer flight in clutter is possible even on the limited sensors and compute of small drones. We also validate our navigation policy in a real-world cluttered environment, showing that it enables the quadrotor to navigate through the cluttered environment successfully and safely.

</details>


### [126] [Can a Frontier VLM Serve as a Robot Policy for Memory-Dependent Manipulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17350)
*Jack B. Jedlicki,Amish Sethi,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 冻结的前沿视觉语言模型 GPT-6-Astra 无需 RoboMME 特定训练，直接作为机器人策略，在 16 项记忆依赖操作任务上取得 92.1% 成功率，超过人类基线 90.5% 和现有最佳系统，表明单一通用模型可同时承担长时程规划、记忆与末端执行器控制的大部分功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究通用视觉语言模型能否直接作为具身智能体的机器人策略，从而统一传统上分离的长时程规划、记忆和末端执行器控制，并减少对任务特定训练或分层系统的依赖。

Method: 在 RoboMME 基准的 16 项记忆依赖操作任务上，使用冻结的 GPT-6-Astra 作为策略；模型将整个 episode 保留在上下文中，直接输出每个末端执行器 waypoint 和夹爪命令，不进行 RoboMME 特定训练。

Result: 在 800 个测试 episode 上成功率为 92.1%，高于使用 oracle 技能接口的人类得分 90.5%，远超最佳排行榜条目 60.8% 以及同一模型与 RoboMME 训练策略结合的分层系统 79.1%；在 16 项任务中的 13 项达到或超过人类水平。63 次失败集中在两项任务，主要为过早完成以及按钮按压时机错误或非预期按压。

Conclusion: 该结果模糊了长时程规划、记忆和末端执行器控制之间的传统分工，并提出在具身系统中单一通用模型能够接管多少机器人技术栈的问题。

Abstract: We use a frozen frontier vision-language model, GPT-6-Astra, directly as the robot policy on RoboMME, a benchmark of 16 memory-dependent manipulation tasks spanning temporal, spatial, object, and procedural memory. The model keeps the whole episode in context and outputs every end-effector waypoint and gripper command, with no RoboMME-specific training. On the 800 test episodes it succeeds in 92.1%, above the previously reported human score of 90.5%, which was obtained with an oracle skill interface, and far ahead of the best leaderboard entry (60.8%) and of a hierarchical system that pairs the same model with a RoboMME-trained policy (79.1%). It matches or exceeds the human score on 13 of the 16 tasks. Its 63 failures concentrate in two tasks and are mostly premature completions and mistimed or unintended button presses. These results blur the usual division between long-horizon planning, memory and end-effector control, and raise the question of how much of this stack a single general-purpose model can take over in embodied systems. Project website: this https URL . Code, prompts and all episode logs: this https URL .

</details>


### [127] [Evaluating Long-Horizon Robot Planning under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17326)
*Harsh Muriki,Gayathri Aravindan,Yuchen Zheng,Sonia Chernova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Habitat中七个长期家庭任务基准，评估ReAct、VLM-TAMP和Tru-POMDP在变化初始记忆与环境配置下的表现，发现ReAct总体成功率最高，但准确初始记忆并不总带来最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人需在内部模型和外部世界不完美的情况下成功执行任务，因此需评估不同规划器在变化初始记忆和环境配置下的鲁棒性与知识利用方式。

Method: 在Habitat中构建包含七个长期家庭任务的基准，变化初始机器人记忆与环境配置；在共享执行框架下评估ReAct、VLM-TAMP和Tru-POMDP的适应性，覆盖90个任务-环境-记忆组合、共1350个回合；测量任务成功、部分完成和动作效率，并基于规则分析执行轨迹。

Result: ReAct取得最高总体任务成功率（45.3%）和完成度（71.7%），但准确的初始记忆并不总是为每个规划器带来最高性能；轨迹分析识别出重复的放置错误、前提条件错误和冗余动作。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了所评估规划器在利用初始知识和应对不确定性方面的差异。

Abstract: Household robots must successfully navigate im-perfect circumstances, both in their internal models and the external world. We introduce a benchmark of seven long-horizon household tasks in Habitat that vary initial robot memory and environment configurations. We evaluate adaptations of ReAct, VLM-TAMP, and Tru-POMDP within a shared execution framework across 90 tasks-environment-memory combinations, totaling 1,350 episodes. Our evaluation measures task success, partial completion, and action efficiency, along with rule-based analysis of execution traces. ReAct achieves the highest overall task success (45.3%) and completion (71.7%), while having accurate initial memory does not consistently yield the highest performance for every planner. We identify recurring placement errors, precondition errors, and redundant actions through trace analysis. These findings highlight differences in how the evaluated planners use initial knowledge and respond to uncertainty.

</details>


### [128] [Improving Policy Learning from Delayed Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17298)
*Xiru Zhu,David Meger,Gregory Dudek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究人类监督式机器人模仿学习中专家干预延迟问题，并提出“从延迟干预中学习”（LfDI）方法，利用干预位置、时机与有限延迟信息学习代价函数，从而为无专家数据状态提供额外监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往模仿学习研究多关注次优专家（专家策略会犯错），但通常假设专家在错误发生后立即干预。本文考虑现实中专家干预可能存在延迟，并检验延迟干预对策略学习的影响。

Method: 提出LfDI：根据专家在何处、何时进行干预学习一个代价函数，并结合专家提供的关于干预延迟程度的有限信息；再用该代价函数推断没有专家数据状态下的额外监督，引导策略避开可能触发干预的状态。

Result: 结果表明，从延迟专家干预中学习整体上不如从非延迟干预中学习有效，且延迟干预在展示错误规避行为方面效果更差。在仿真环境中，LfDI优于未考虑干预延迟的其他方法。

Conclusion: 延迟专家干预会削弱模仿学习效果，而显式建模干预延迟并学习干预代价函数，可为策略提供额外监督，提升在存在延迟干预场景下的学习表现。

Abstract: In this work, we explore the idea of expert intervention delays in human-supervised robotic systems for imitation learning system. Previous works in imitation learning have primarily examined the impact of suboptimal experts, where the expert policy makes mistakes, but assumed expert interventions occur immediately after errors are made. Although delayed expert intervention can be viewed as a form of noise that might improve policy robustness, our results show that learning from delayed expert interventions is overall less effective than learning from non-delayed expert interventions; delayed expert interventions are less effective at demonstrating error avoidance behaviors. To address this, we propose Learning from Delayed Intervention (LfDI), which learns a cost function based on where and when the expert intervenes, combined with limited information about the extent of intervention delays provided by the expert. This cost function is then used to infer additional supervision for states without expert data, guiding the policy to avoid states that would likely trigger interventions. We evaluate our method, Learning from Delayed Intervention (LfDI), on simulated environments and show that it outperforms other methods that do not account for intervention delays.

</details>


### [129] [Phase-Conditioned Affordance Fields for SE(3)-Equivariant Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17281)
*Xinzhe Chen,Liqi Huang,Sihua Ren,Haowen Sun,Mingyang Li,Zeyang Liu,Xingyu Chen,Xuguang Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出相位条件可操作场（PAF），在观测点云上学习逐点可操作性分数，并将其用于构造相位条件锚点与逐点特征，从而在保持全局SE(3)等变性的同时为视觉运动策略引入局部几何归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SE(3)-等变视觉运动策略依赖全局等变假设，即把整个观测视为受单一刚体变换支配；但真实操作任务中任务相关区域会随物体变化，该假设不再成立。

Method: 学习观测点云上的PAF，即逐点分数。其分数加权质心作为可学习的、相位条件锚点，将观测和动作围绕当前活跃区域规范化，替代固定夹爪坐标系参考；分数本身作为逐点特征附加。该方法引入局部几何归纳偏置，同时保留策略的全局SE(3)等变性。

Result: 在多个MimicGen任务及不同空间随机化下，PAF一致提升底层策略成功率；在多物体真机任务中优于基线，并在光照变化下保持领先。

Conclusion: PAF通过相位条件可操作场缓解了全局等变假设在真实操作中的局限，为SE(3)-等变视觉运动策略提供有效的局部区域自适应能力，并提升泛化性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: SE(3)-equivariance provides a useful geometric inductive bias for visuomotor policies, encoding that actions covary with rigid transformations of the observation. However, existing SE(3)-equivariant visuomotor policies build this bias on a global equivariance assumption that treats the entire observation as governed by a single rigid transformation, which breaks down in real-world manipulation tasks where the task-relevant region changes across different objects. We address this by learning a phase-conditioned affordance field (PAF) over the observed point cloud, a per-point score that the SE(3)-equivariant policy uses in two complementary ways. Its score-weighted centroid serves as a learned, phase-conditioned anchor that canonicalizes the observation and the action around the active region, replacing the fixed gripper-frame reference, and the score itself is appended as a per-point feature. This introduces a local geometric inductive bias, while preserving the policy's global SE(3) equivariance. We validate on multiple MimicGen tasks under varying spatial randomization, and PAF consistently improves the success rate of the underlying policy. On multi-object real-robot tasks, PAF outperforms baselines and keeps its lead under lighting variations.

</details>


### [130] [ReForesight: Occlusion-Robust Re-Identification and Future Occupancy Forecasting for Dynamic Semantic Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17279)
*Didar Seyidov,Faith Ogunwoye,Hajira Amjad,Danil Tolkachev,Yuri Sarkisov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReForesight 是一个开源 ROS 2 工具包，在基于粒子的语义建图流程中加入实例级未来占据预测与遮挡后重识别能力，使机器人能预判行人和车辆未来位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享通道的机器人需要知道人未来会在哪里，但语义占据地图通常只显示当前占据状态，且遮挡会导致目标身份丢失。

Method: 将实例级未来占据与遮挡后重识别接入粒子语义建图流程；使用 36.6k 参数 MLP 对每个被跟踪行人或车辆预测未来最多 3 秒位姿，并由 Kalman 回退和静止门控保护；把目标自身点云移动到预测位姿，在地图坐标系中形成 ghost 点云，无需占据解码器；可选跟踪器利用流程已计算的关键点描述子和度量 3D 门控在遮挡后重新关联目标。

Result: 在四个留出的 CODa 序列上，MLP 将平均 3 秒最终位移误差从 Kalman 的 0.728 m 降至 0.662 m，并在每个序列上优于相近规模 GRU 的 0.692 m，单 CPU 核每实例耗时 0.04 ms；代码、全部模型和无 ROS 演示已发布，并在户外清洁机器人上进行了现场演示。

Conclusion: 该工具包以低计算开销实现了实例级未来占据预测和遮挡后重识别，并通过真实户外机器人应用验证了实用性。

Abstract: Robots sharing walkways need to know where people will be, yet semantic occupancy maps show only where they are. We present ReForesight, an open-source ROS 2 toolkit that adds instance-level future occupancy and re-identification after occlusion to a particle-based semantic mapping pipeline. A 36.6k-parameter MLP predicts each tracked person or vehicle up to 3 s ahead, guarded by a Kalman fallback and a stationary gate, and the object's own points are moved to the forecast pose, forming a "ghost" point cloud in the map frame without an occupancy decoder. An optional tracker re-associates objects after occlusion using keypoint descriptors the pipeline already computes and a metric 3D gate. On four held-out CODa sequences, the MLP lowers the mean 3 s final displacement error from 0.728 m (Kalman) to 0.662 m and beats a similar-size GRU (0.692 m) on every sequence, at 0.04 ms per instance on one CPU core. Code, all models, and a ROS-free demo are released; field demonstrations use an outdoor cleaning robot.

</details>


### [131] [Physical SLAM: Physics-aware Simultaneous Localisation and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17260)
*Guangming Wang,Zhishuo Liu,Haibing Wu,Wanli Ma,Yixiong Jing,Olaf Wysocki,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Physical SLAM，一种从单目 RGB-D 视频中联合重建动态高斯辐射场并推断物理动力学的物理感知 SLAM 方法，可支持可变形物体的观测帧渲染与动作条件仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是从移动相机拍摄的单目视频中重建物理世界可预测的 4D 表示，不仅重建动态高斯辐射场，还要推断其潜在物理动力学，并预测可变形物体在外力作用下的未来状态。

Method: 方法将场景分解为用于相机定位的静态背景高斯场，以及由弹簧-质量物理模型驱动的动态物体高斯场，该模型建模弹性形变、惯性和阻尼。为融合视觉观测与物理动力学，引入虚拟观测力，将仿真表面节点逐渐拉向观测深度点，避免直接替换节点位置；同时基于完整重建观测窗口内聚合的仿真误差更新弹簧-质量模型物理参数。进一步设计物理感知高斯变换与表面对齐策略，使动态高斯沿弹簧-质量模型的变形物理表面变换，并在基于渲染的优化中约束其与表面保持对齐。

Result: 在 PhysTwin 基准上，重建和预测 PSNR 分别比 PhysTwin 提升 10.4% 和 11.0%；在 TUM RGB-D 基准上，相比 4DGS-SLAM，平均相机跟踪误差降低 20.6%，SSIM 提升 3.6%，LPIPS 降低 8.3%。

Conclusion: 该方法支持观测帧渲染和动作条件仿真，将 SLAM 地图扩展到描述可变形物体如何响应操作，实现了物理感知的 4D 高斯场重建与预测。

Abstract: To reconstruct a predictive 4D representation of the physical world from monocular video captured by a moving camera, we jointly reconstruct dynamic Gaussian radiance fields and infer their underlying physical dynamics. This paper proposes \emph{Physical SLAM}, a physics-aware method for constructing 4D Gaussian fields of deformable objects and predicting their future state when subjected to external actions, given a sequence of RGB-D images. Our method separates the scene into a static background Gaussian field for camera localization and a dynamic object Gaussian field driven by a spring--mass physical model of elastic deformation, inertia, and damping. To integrate visual observations with physical dynamics, we introduce virtual observation forces that gradually pull simulated surface nodes toward observed depth points, avoiding abrupt replacement of simulated node positions. Meanwhile, the physical parameters of the spring--mass model are updated from simulation errors aggregated over the full reconstruction observation window, enabling the reconstructed dynamics to align with observations. To couple appearance, geometry, and physics, we further design a physics-aware Gaussian transformation and surface alignment strategy, where dynamic Gaussians are transformed along the deforming physical surface of the spring--mass model and constrained to remain aligned with the surface during rendering-based optimization. The resulting representation supports observed-frame rendering and action-conditioned simulation, extending SLAM maps to describe how deformable objects respond to manipulation. On the PhysTwin benchmark, our method improves reconstruction and prediction PSNR over PhysTwin by 10.4\% and 11.0\%, respectively. On the TUM RGB-D benchmark, it reduces average camera tracking error by 20.6\%, improves SSIM by 3.6\%, and reduces LPIPS by 8.3\% compared with 4DGS-SLAM.

</details>


### [132] [Value-Preserving Action Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17252)
*Max Muchen Sun,Jueun Kwon,Benjamin Riviere,Todd Murphey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出值保持动作量化VAQ，证明动作量化导致的值函数误差受量化动作下状态可达性重建误差紧约束，并将VAQ与MCTS结合为VAQ-MCTS，用于行为克隆中的搜索式策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索式策略学习（如MCTS）能以运行时计算换取数据效率和对新环境的适应能力，但机器人连续动作空间通常需离散化/量化。传统基于重构的量化只能表示动作本身，而策略学习还要求量化动作保持其对未来状态和值的影响；动作重构误差不足以刻画这一点。

Method: 作者从理论上证明：由动作量化引起的值函数误差，其紧上界取决于量化动作下状态可达性的重建误差。据此提出VAQ：通过保持状态可达性来量化动作。进一步将VAQ与MCTS结合，形成值保持的搜索式行为克隆框架VAQ-MCTS。实验以多种物体推动任务作为受控基准进行评测。

Result: VAQ-MCTS在标准设定下匹配或超过当前最先进的行为克隆方法，同时显著提升数据效率、对异构演示的鲁棒性，并支持对新环境的零样本适应。硬件实验中，仅用10分钟演示数据即完成全部30次真实物体推动试验。

Conclusion: 结果表明，以状态可达性为中心的值保持动作量化能有效连接连续控制与搜索式策略学习，提升行为克隆的数据效率、鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Search-based policy learning, such as Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS), offers the flexibility to trade runtime computation for data efficiency and adaptation to novel environments. For robot learning, however, applying search requires discretizing the continuous action space and associated dynamics, typically through action quantization. While reconstruction-based quantization is sufficient for representing the continuous action space alone, policy learning additionally requires the quantized actions to preserve their effects on future states and values. We show that such requirement is not necessarily captured by action reconstruction error. Instead, we prove that the error in the value function resulting from action quantization is tightly bounded by the reconstruction error of state reachability under the quantized actions. Based on these results, we propose value-preserving action quantization (VAQ), which quantizes actions by preserving state reachability. We further combine VAQ with MCTS (VAQ-MCTS) as a value-preserving, search-based policy learning framework for behavioral cloning. Using a wide range of object pushing tasks as a controlled benchmark, we evaluate VAQ-MCTS against state-of-the-art behavioral cloning methods. VAQ-MCTS matches or exceeds their performance under standard settings, while substantially improving data efficiency and robustness to heterogeneous demonstrations, while enabling zero-shot adaptation to novel environments. Hardware experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness of VAQ-MCTS, completing all 30 real-world object pushing trials with only 10 minutes of demonstration data.

</details>


### [133] [LiDAR-based Humanoid Navigation in Dynamic, Non-Convex Environments via Spatio-Temporal Safe Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17232)
*Shubham S. Kumbhar,Panagiotis Artemiadis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向人形机器人的实时导航框架，直接从LiDAR粒子表示构建时空安全走廊，并结合LIP规划与WBC，实现动态非凸环境中的安全运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人导航方法常通过简化凸障碍边界丢弃复杂自由空间，且未考虑实时动态占用预测，难以在动态、非凸环境中安全导航。

Method: 从LiDAR粒子表示直接构建时空安全走廊，将其与基于线性倒立摆LIP的规划及全身控制WBC紧耦合，无需 restrictive 几何假设即可绕行并进入移动非凸结构。

Result: 在Digit人形机器人仿真中，于24×24 m²场地内绕行和进入移动非凸障碍的成功率为96%；消融实验表明迭代多面体生成在狭窄走廊中显著优于单次迭代基线，并验证了对杂乱、传感器噪声、控制延迟和力扰动的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够实现动态非凸环境下人形机器人的实时、鲁棒导航，并优于依赖简化几何假设和单次走廊生成的方法。

Abstract: Navigating humanoid robots through dynamic, non-convex environments requires tightly coupling onboard perception with footstep planning and dynamic locomotion control. Existing methods frequently discard complex free space through simplified convex obstacle bounds and fail to account for real-time dynamic occupancy predictions. This paper presents a real-time framework for humanoid navigation that directly constructs spatio-temporal safe corridors from LiDAR-based particle representations and couples them with Linear Inverted Pendulum (LIP)-based planning and Whole Body Control (WBC) to enable navigation around and within moving non-convex structures without restrictive geometric assumptions. Simulations on the Digit humanoid yield a 96% success rate in navigating around and inside moving non-convex obstacles in a 24 by 24 m^2 arena. Ablation studies confirm that the proposed iterative polytope generation significantly outperforms single-iteration baselines in narrow corridors, while evaluations demonstrate robustness to clutter, sensor noise, control delays, and force perturbations.

</details>


### [134] [DexNinja: Learning Robust Dexterous Cutting Policy with a Real-to-Sim-to-Real Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17208)
*Haozhe Lou,Runyi Yang,Wanqi Zhong,Caiyun Liu,Yicheng Liu,Wenhao Liu,Wenlong Ma,Jiawei Xia,Kanzhi Wu,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Hang Zhao,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexNinja 是一个 real2sim2real 框架，利用少量真实演示重建对象并在类别约束下随机化物理参数，再通过自定义可微分仿真器生成密集且真实的灵巧切割训练数据，从而提升食物切片任务的成功率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人切割食物属于高接触、可变形弹塑性且会改变拓扑的交互任务，其行为随姿态、接触模式和切割阶段变化。真实数据采集昂贵且会造成食物污染或损坏，难以规模化；仿真虽可替代，但在物理与感知上存在显著 sim-to-real gap，切割中接触、摩擦、材料与弹性响应的小误差会快速累积。

Method: DexNinja 提出 real2sim2real 框架：一是从真实捕获中重建物体实例；二是在类别级约束下随机化具有物理意义的参数；三是使用自定义可微分仿真器，耦合机器人动力学、触觉接触与可变形切割，生成增强的灵巧操作回合。

Result: 在灵巧食物切片任务上，使用数十条真实演示与数百条增强仿真数据时，成功率可超过 60%；仅用真实数据时，增强数据通过 sim-to-real 可持续提升成功率超过 20%；所得策略还能泛化到形状和尺寸变化的分布外物体。

Conclusion: DexNinja 表明，少量真实演示结合物理参数随机化与可微分仿真生成的增强数据，可有效缓解切割任务中的 sim-to-real gap，并支持可扩展的灵巧食物切割策略学习与泛化。

Abstract: Cutting a piece of food looks effortless in human hands. For a robot, however, every cut is a high-contact interaction with a deformable, elasto-plastic object whose behavior changes with pose, contact mode, and cutting stage. Collecting real-world data is expensive and wasteful: each trial typically contaminates or damages the food, so obtaining scalable real world data for dexterous cutting is expensive and difficult. Simulation is an appealing alternative, but a large sim-to-real gap exists in both physics and perception. This gap is especially severe for topology-changing interactions like cutting, where small errors in contact, friction, or material and elasticity responses quickly compound. We propose \textsc{DexNinja}, a real2sim2real framework that turns a handful of real demonstrations into a dense and realistic training distribution. \textsc{DexNinja} (i) reconstructs object instances from real captures, (ii) randomizes physically meaningful parameters under category-level constraints, and (iii) produces augmented dexterous manipulation episodes using a custom differentiable simulator that couples robot dynamics, tactile contact, and deformable cutting. We evaluate \textsc{DexNinja} on a dexterous food-slicing task and show that (1) with tens of real demonstrations and hundreds of augmented simulator data, the success rate could reach more than 60\%. (2) the augmented data consistently improves success rates when only using real data with more than 20\% via sim-to-real, and (3) the resulting policies generalize to out-of-distribution objects with varying shapes and sizes.

</details>


### [135] [Generative Flow on Symbolic Abstractions for Embodiment Capability Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17174)
*Youwei Yu,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FOSA：利用生成流网络在符号抽象空间学习具身相关的任务生成器，发现机器人能力前沿上的可执行任务，并将任务策略蒸馏为语言条件策略，从而提升未见复杂长时程任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂长时程机器人行为难以实现，因为成功执行依赖机器人物理可供性所支撑的技能。现有训练任务分布多由手工设计或大语言模型采样，侧重语义合理性而非具身可行性，导致课程过难、冗余，或忽略非显而易见但可学习的行为。

Method: FOSA 将抽象策略参数化为生成流网络（GFlowNet），用于构造符号任务骨架；每个骨架被编译为仿真环境，以低层策略学习性能作为抽象策略的奖励信号，从而学习有依据的任务生成器并发现具身特定能力。

Result: 在异构机器人平台上，FOSA 能发现靠近机器人经验能力前沿的多样且可执行任务；将发现的任务策略蒸馏为单一语言条件策略后，FOSA 在未见复杂与长时程任务上取得性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过符号抽象上的生成流进行具身特定能力发现，可生成更可行且有效的训练课程，并蒸馏为通用语言条件策略，提升复杂长时程机器人行为；代码与演示视频已公开。

Abstract: Achieving complex, long-horizon robot behavior remains challenging because successful execution depends on skills grounded in a robot's physical affordances. Existing training-task distributions are often handcrafted or sampled from large language models (LLMs), relying on semantic plausibility rather than embodiment-specific feasibility. This mismatch can produce curricula that are too difficult, redundant, or blind to non-obvious but learnable behaviors. We propose Generative Flow on Symbolic Abstractions (FOSA), a framework for discovering embodiment-specific capabilities by learning a grounded task generator. FOSA parameterizes an abstract policy as a Generative Flow Network that constructs symbolic task skeletons. Each skeleton is compiled into a simulation environment, where low-level policy-learning performance provides the reward signal for the abstract policy. Across heterogeneous robot platforms, FOSA discovers diverse and executable tasks near the robot's empirical capability frontier. After distilling the discovered task policies into a single language-conditioned policy, FOSA improves performance on unseen complex and long-horizon tasks. The code and demonstration videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [136] [Force-Modulated Dexterity: Unified Compliance for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17161)
*Ying Yuan,David Held,Jitendra Malik,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出力调制灵巧性框架 FOMO-Dex，通过任务几何与接触结构定义坐标并施加力驱动力修正，提升接触丰富操作的遥操作演示采集与策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作需要协调运动与接触力；遥操作中操作者能指定运动，但难以在无接触力反馈时复现人类细微的力调整，导致接触力过大、演示数据质量与效率受限。

Method: FOMO-Dex 采用统一的柔顺控制公式，将名义动作表示在由任务几何和接触结构定义的坐标中；在选定方向施加力驱动的运动修正，同时保持其余命令分量不变。

Result: 在卡片拾取、剪纸、拧螺丝和螺母螺栓装配中，相比刚性控制减少过大接触力；用户研究显示短暂练习后每小时可采集更多成功演示；模仿修正动作提升卡片拾取成功率；基于 FOMO-Dex 演示训练的策略在螺母螺栓和拧螺丝任务中比刚性控制器演示获得更大旋转。

Conclusion: FOMO-Dex 通过力调制遥操作改善接触丰富操作的数据采集与策略学习，降低接触力并提升下游任务成功率与操作幅度。

Abstract: Teleoperation is widely used to collect high-quality demonstrations for robot learning. Dexterous manipulation, however, requires coordinated control of motion and contact forces. Operators can specify motion, but struggle to reproduce the subtle force adjustments of human manipulation without feeling the robot's contacts. We introduce Force-Modulated Dexterity (FOMO-Dex), a unified compliance formulation for contact-rich manipulation. FOMO-Dex expresses nominal actions in coordinates defined by task geometry and contact structure. It applies force-driven motion corrections along selected directions while leaving the remaining command components unchanged. Across card picking, paper cutting, screwdriving, and nut-and-bolt assembly, FOMO-Dex reduces excessive contact forces compared with stiff control. A user study shows that operators collect more successful demonstration data per hour after brief practice. For policy learning, imitating the corrected actions improves card-picking success over imitating raw operator commands. Policies trained on FOMO-Dex demonstrations also achieve more rotation in nut-and-bolt manipulation and screwdriving than those trained on demonstrations collected with the stiff controller. Videos are shown on this https URL .

</details>


### [137] [D-World: An Explicit World-Language-Action Model with Recursive Self-Improvement under Dual Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17114)
*Xuan Wen,Baichao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 D-World：一种基于可执行数字孪生与测量驱动递归自我改进（RSI）的显式世界-语言-动作模型，使冻结 LLM 在机器人任务中持续修正世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿 LLM 已能进行空间推理并编程机器人，但机器人策略背后的世界模型是隐式的，存在于网络权重或预测视频中，无法直接检查或修正。

Method: 基于感知-规划-行动分解：感知智能体用生成的视觉与几何代码将场景写成可执行数字孪生；规划智能体在仿真中搜索动作，并在每个动作块执行后，根据机器人测量修正孪生中的物体状态与命名物理参数；仅当重放最新动作块不使任何测量残差超过容差时才接受参数修正，且每个动作块执行前都在更新后的孪生中校验；结构不匹配则返回感知重建。该测量锚定的修正与修订循环称为递归自我改进（RSI），其中 LLM 保持冻结而其所作用的世界持续改进。作者将兼顾任务推进与暴露模型误差的动作选择建模为对偶控制问题，表明把证据纳入孪生的成本决定探测何时值得，因此低成本局部修正可抵消谨慎控制器的退缩倾向。

Result: 在双臂机器人上，毛巾与烤面包机任务展示了局部修正可避免重建：例如将面包的渲染深度残差从 22.4 mm 降至 1.5 mm，使执行得以继续。每个回合运行真-仿-真循环，记录的孪生可离线重拟合与重渲染为更高保真环境用于后训练。

Conclusion: D-World 通过可解释、可修正的显式数字孪生实现了冻结 LLM 下的世界模型自我改进；感知与规划仅通过孪生和语言通信，因此二者均可替换为学习模型；由于推理主导循环，更快模型可加速整个循环，且记录孪生可复用于后训练。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) now reason about space well enough to program robots, yet the world models behind robot policies are implicit, held in network weights or predicted video, and cannot be inspected or corrected directly. We present D-World, an explicit world-language-action model built on the Sense, Plan and Act decomposition. A perception agent writes the scene as an executable digital twin using generated vision and geometry code. A planning agent searches for actions in simulation and, after each executed action chunk, revises the twin's object states and named physical parameters against what the robot measured. A parameter revision is accepted only if replaying the latest chunk does not increase any measured residual beyond its tolerance, and every chunk is checked in the updated twin before it runs. Structural mismatches return to perception for reconstruction. We call this measurement-grounded cycle of correction and revision recursive self-improvement (RSI), in which the LLM stays frozen while the world it acts in improves. We model the choice of actions that both advance the task and expose model error as a dual control problem and show that the cost of incorporating evidence into the twin sets the threshold at which probing pays, so cheap local revision counteracts the turn-off of cautious controllers. Each episode runs a real-to-sim-to-real loop, and its recorded twins can be refitted and re-rendered offline into higher-fidelity environments for post-training. Because perception and planning communicate only through the twin and language, either can be replaced by a learned model, and since inference dominates the loop, the loop speeds up with faster models. On a dual-arm robot, towel and toaster tasks show local revisions, such as one reducing the bread's rendered-depth residual from 22.4 to 1.5 mm, that let execution continue without reconstruction.

</details>


### [138] [Interactive Navigation Beyond Reachability: Benchmark and Agentic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17089)
*Delun Li,Xueyao Wei,Rui Chen,Yifei Wang,Junzhi Yu,Wenjie Song,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出交互式导航新任务，统一了开门改变连通性与开容器改变目标可见性及其顺序依赖，并发布3K回合的MultINav-Bench基准与基于状态条件交互图的三阶段智能体MultINav-Agent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航任务通常假设回合内可通行性与物体状态固定不变；然而到达目标可能需要先开门进入房间，再打开容器以显露隐藏目标，因此需要超越单纯可达性的交互式导航。

Method: 将通道引起的空间连通性变化、容器引起的目标可见性变化及其顺序依赖统一建模；构建MultINav-Bench，含3K回合ObjectGoal任务（通道、容器、混合），在MolmoSpaces中运行，带交互必要性标注（必需/非必需），并提供自动化数据生成流水线与分别评估导航成功率、交互有效性和代价的协议；提出MultINav-Agent三阶段框架，基于持久的状态条件交互图：语义交互感知定位实体与交互状态，视觉语言模型依据连通性和目标可观测性的预期变化选择任务相关动作，交互后验证更新图以指导后续导航、探索或交互。

Result: 在MultINav-Bench上的实验表明，MultINav-Agent在通道、容器和混合任务中均持续优于适配的基线方法。

Conclusion: 交互式导航需显式建模状态变化与顺序依赖；所提基准与智能体框架能有效评估并解决该类任务，验证了状态条件交互图与三阶段智能体设计的有效性。

Abstract: Existing embodied navigation tasks commonly assume that traversability and object states remain fixed during an episode. Yet reaching a goal may require opening a door to access a room and then opening a container to reveal a hidden target. We formulate interactive navigation beyond reachability, unifying channel-induced changes in spatial connectivity, container-induced changes in target visibility, and their sequential dependencies. We introduce MultINav-Bench, a 3K-episode ObjectGoal benchmark in MolmoSpaces spanning channel, container, and mixed tasks, with interaction-necessity annotations covering required and unnecessary episodes. Its data generation pipeline automates task construction across multiple task types. The evaluation protocol separately measures navigation success, interaction effectiveness, and cost. We further propose MultINav-Agent, a three-stage agentic framework built on a persistent, state-conditioned interaction graph. Semantic interactive perception grounds entities and interaction states; a vision-language model selects task-relevant actions based on expected changes in connectivity and target observability; and post-interaction verification updates the graph to guide subsequent navigation, exploration, or interaction. Experiments on MultINav-Bench show that MultINav-Agent consistently outperforms adapted baselines across channel, container, and mixed tasks.

</details>


### [139] [Declarative Reactive Robot Behaviors from Imperative Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17083)
*Vincent Berenz,Tasha Kim,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究表明，借助 Playful 反应式脚本语言、合适上下文与可复用编程指南，LLM 编码代理可从不充分自然语言描述生成机器人行为的正确声明式/反应式程序，并能从通用机器人操作系统 API 联合设计命令式模块与声明式行为逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 主要训练于命令式语言，现有 LLM 机器人编程工作集中于目标导向任务与计划；但许多机器人应用需要开放式、连续、无终止条件的行为，更适合声明式与反应式编程，而 LLM 对此几乎没有训练。论文旨在探究能否弥合这一缺口。

Method: 使用 Playful 反应式脚本语言，让 LLM 编码代理基于欠指定自然语言描述、合适上下文和可复用编程指南生成程序。指南由专家一次性编写，编码设计原则，将代理从命令式默认倾向引导至反应式模式，并可跨机器人与行为泛化。代理从通用机器人操作系统 API 出发，联合设计命令式组件（Python 感知、执行与评估模块）和声明式组件（组合行为逻辑的反应式脚本）。

Result: LLM 代理能够生成正确的声明式与反应式机器人行为程序；该声明式特性支持通过对话轮次进行增量细化，也支持一次性生成完整行为。

Conclusion: 该工作表明可弥合 LLM 在反应式机器人编程上的训练缺口；在可复用指南辅助下，LLM 能生成开放式机器人行为，并具备跨机器人与行为的泛化潜力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are predominantly trained on imperative programming languages, and existing work on LLM-based robot programming reflects this: it focuses on goal-directed tasks expressed as plans. However, many robotic applications require not tasks but behaviors--open-ended, continuous patterns of sensory-motor engagement with no termination condition (e.g., greeting visitors, patrolling an area). Such behaviors are better served by declarative and reactive programming, on which LLMs have virtually no training. We investigate whether this gap can be bridged. Using Playful, a reactive scripting language, we show that an LLM-based coding agent can generate correct declarative and reactive programs for robotic behaviors. These programs are produced from under-specified natural language descriptions, given suitable context and a set of reusable programming guidelines. The guidelines encode design principles that redirect the agent from imperative defaults toward proper reactive patterns. Developed once by an expert, they generalize across robots and behaviors. Starting from only the generic robot operating system API, the agent jointly designs both the imperative component (Python modules for sensing, acting, and evaluating) and the declarative component (the reactive script that composes them into behavior logic). The declarative nature of reactive programming supports both incremental refinement through conversational turns and one-shot generation of complete behaviors.

</details>


### [140] [Task-Space Motion-Force Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Precise and Compliant Control under Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17069)
*Quang-Nam Nguyen,Ludovic Righetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种统一NMPC框架，在任务空间运动与力约束下直接规划并控制机器人关节，可跨平台、跨任务泛化，并通过任务空间阻抗与微分稳定性/无源性分析实现高层力反馈闭环，在冗余机械臂和四足机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NMPC控制律隐含于OCP求解中，OCP设计通常依赖特定平台与任务，需要大量专家知识和调参；同时，基于优化的方法常将交互视为刚性接触，使力矩解对机器人动力学与环境建模误差敏感。

Method: 提出统一NMPC框架，直接规划与控制关节以满足任务空间运动与力需求，并跨多机器人平台和任务泛化；研究控制器内在的任务空间阻抗，分析其在未建模交互下的部分收缩性以实现微分稳定性与微分无源性；进而将力反馈环引入高层规划层，形成力反馈NMPC。

Result: 在物理硬件上验证：在冗余机械臂上实现混合运动-力控制，在四足机器人上实现柔顺运动；同时保留NMPC核心优势，包括硬约束满足、最优冗余利用以及突发交互下的实时规划。

Conclusion: 该统一NMPC框架通过任务空间阻抗与高层力反馈闭环，实现了精确的运动-力跟踪，缓解了长期存在的力反馈NMPC难题，并展示了跨平台、跨任务的通用性与硬件可行性。

Abstract: In Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), the control law is implicitly determined by the solution of an Optimal Control Problem (OCP). Consequently, OCP design is often tailored to specific platforms and tasks, demanding extensive expert knowledge and tuning effort. To address this, we propose a unified NMPC framework that directly plans and controls the robot joints subject to task-space motion and force requirements and is capable of seamlessly generalizing across multiple robotic platforms and diverse tasks. Furthermore, optimization-based methods often treat interactions as rigid contacts, making the torque solution sensitive to the modeling errors of the robot dynamics and the environment. We study the intrinsic task-space impedance of our proposed controller, its partial contraction for differential stability and differential passivity under unmodeled interactions. As a result, our framework enables closing the force-feedback loop in the high-level planning layer to achieve precise motion-force tracking performance, addressing the long-standing challenge of force-feedback NMPC. Finally, we validate our framework on physical hardware, demonstrating hybrid motion-force control on a redundant manipulator and compliant locomotion on a quadruped, while preserving core NMPC advantages such as hard constraint satisfaction, optimal redundancy exploitation, and real-time planning under unforeseen interactions.

</details>


### [141] [MOSAIC: Mode- and Support-Aware Inference-Time Control of Robot Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17001)
*Sabrina Bodmer,Rahel Rickenbach,René Zurbrügg,Melanie N. Zeilinger,Colin Jones*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MOSAIC 是一种面向机器人扩散模型的、模式与支撑感知的无梯度推理时控制框架，通过优化扩散模型噪声输入分布，在不重训练的情况下适配并组合冻结的生成先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式策略（如扩散模型）能提供表达力强的数据驱动机器人行为先验，但执行时的场景常与训练数据不匹配，并带来策略未学习过的约束与目标。现有推理时引导方法依赖梯度，可能将样本推离学习先验；或依赖采样过程，存在低效、模式坍塌、模式平均导致分布外预测等问题。

Method: MOSAIC 将初始噪声和所有随机反向步扰动视为预训练去噪过程的联合输入，并用完全去噪输出计算重要性权重来适配该输入分布。它通过自适应惩罚平衡物理可行性与对学习支撑的一致性，并采用按模式的的重要性加权更新以保留模式、避免破坏性平均。相同形式还可通过共享耦合代价联合引导多个独立训练的扩散模型，实现无需联合模型的协调行为。

Result: 在导航、灵巧抓取、未见几何下的运动规划，以及使用独立训练机器人先验的协作箱体操作中评估。结果表明，MOSAIC 能在新的部署约束下适配并组合冻结扩散先验，且无需重新训练。

Conclusion: MOSAIC 提供了一种无梯度、模式与支撑感知的推理时控制方法，可适配并组合多个冻结扩散先验，在保持模式与先验支撑的同时满足新约束，避免重训练。

Abstract: Generative policies such as diffusion models provide expressive data-driven priors for robot behavior, yet the situations a robot faces at execution time rarely match its training data and impose constraints and objectives the policy never learned. Existing inference-time guidance methods either rely on gradients that can push samples away from the learned prior, or on sampling procedures that can be inefficient, collapse modes, or average them into out-of-distribution predictions. We introduce MOSAIC, a mode- and support-aware framework for gradient-free inference-time control of robotic diffusion models. MOSAIC treats the initial noise and all stochastic reverse-step perturbations as a joint input to the pretrained denoising process and adapts a distribution over this input using importance weights computed from fully denoised outputs. Physical feasibility and consistency with the learned support are balanced using adaptive penalties, while mode-wise importance-weighted updates preserve modes and avoid destructive averaging. The same formulation can jointly steer multiple independently trained diffusion models through shared coupling costs, enabling coordinated behavior without requiring a joint model. We evaluate MOSAIC on navigation, dexterous grasping, motion planning with unseen geometry, and collaborative box manipulation with independently trained robot priors. Across these settings, MOSAIC adapts and composes frozen diffusion priors under new deployment constraints without retraining.

</details>


### [142] [Joint-Feasibility Margin Feedback for Bounded-Input Second-Order Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16978)
*Siyuan Yang,Shaoming He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出符号局部联合可行性裕度，提前预测并选择候选控制输入，避免多个二阶控制障碍函数（HOCBF）在有界加速度下耗尽共同控制权限而导致硬QP不可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个二阶CBF约束共享控制权限时，即使当前状态可行，也可能因有界加速度而联合不可行；需要一种能识别并避免硬HOCBF二次规划失效的机制。

Method: 定义符号局部联合可行性裕度，其符号精确判断当前障碍半空间是否与允许输入集相交；对单位尺度距离约束，该裕度具有加速度单位，并可通过支撑函数对偶识别加权约束冲突。对九个任务导向候选控制输入预测一步裕度，选择达到期望裕度的最小偏差候选或预测裕度最大的候选，再求解同一硬HOCBF二次规划。

Result: 受控一步案例中，在硬QP命令预测的状态下，两个约束单独可行但联合不可行，而裕度反馈控制器保持下一时刻裕度为正。20组配对初始条件下，低压任务中控制器保持硬QP命令；关键任务中在硬QP问题变为不可行前以正裕度行动，轨迹在该时刻仍可行并完成任务。源代码已公开。

Conclusion: 基于联合可行性裕度一步预测的反馈控制可在硬HOCBF二次规划失效前采取可行控制，保障多约束系统的安全性与任务完成能力。

Abstract: Multiple second-order control barrier function constraints can exhaust their common control authority under bounded acceleration, even when the current state remains feasible. We define a signed local joint-feasibility margin whose sign exactly identifies whether the current barrier half-spaces intersect the admissible input set. For unit-scaled distance constraints, it has acceleration units and a support-function dual that identifies weighted constraint conflicts. We predict the margin one sample ahead for nine task-directed candidates, selecting either the least-deviating candidate that attains a desired margin or the candidate with the largest predicted margin before solving the same hard HOCBF quadratic program. A controlled one-step case shows that, at the state predicted under the hard-QP command, two constraints remain individually feasible but become jointly infeasible, whereas the margin-feedback controller retains a positive next margin. Separately, across 20 paired initial conditions, the margin-feedback controller retains the hard-QP command in a low-pressure task and acts at positive margin in a critical task before the hard-QP problem becomes infeasible; its trajectories remain feasible at that time and complete the task. Source code is publicly available. this https URL

</details>


### [143] [UniLAP: A Unified Latent Action Policy for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16969)
*Lei Yao,Qiao Feng,Mutian Tong,Qihao Yun,Lap-Pui Chau,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 UniLAP：一种基于流匹配的统一连续潜在动作策略，可绕过推理时对未来帧的依赖，并在多类操作任务和真实部署中超越现有潜在动作与 VLA 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型虽能从无动作视频中学习操作先验，但离散潜在表示存在粒度瓶颈，难以刻画细粒度动作；连续替代方案多作为世界模型代理，仍需额外规划步骤才能生成可执行动作。

Method: UniLAP 用流匹配建模连续潜在动作空间，并通过流自适应目标蒸馏 LAM 教师，继承其动作表示与多模态可行行为分布；同时提取无标签运动目标，将潜在表示与任务无关外观解耦，保留运动学线索，形成具身无关的潜在流形，便于适配新平台。

Result: 在 1.1B 参数规模下用多源视频预训练，UniLAP 在手部姿态预测、单臂操作、人形机器人操作和真实世界部署中均优于当前最先进的潜在动作与 Vision-Language-Action 基线。

Conclusion: UniLAP 通过连续潜在动作建模与流匹配蒸馏，缓解了离散粒度瓶颈和额外规划依赖，实现了细粒度、具身无关且可部署的机器人操作策略。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) can capture manipulation priors from action-free videos, but discrete latents usually impose a granularity bottleneck, limiting their ability to represent fine-grained action. Recent continuous alternatives mitigate this, but they are typically used as proxies for world modeling and require a further planning step to produce executable actions. We thus introduce UniLAP, a unified continuous latent-action policy that models the latent space via flow matching and bypasses reliance on future frames during inference. By distilling a LAM teacher with a flow-adaptive objective, UniLAP inherits the teacher's action representation while retaining the multimodal distribution of feasible behaviors. To decouple latent representations from task-irrelevant appearance, we extract a label-free motion target to isolate relevant kinematic cues from spurious visual dynamics. With the established embodiment-agnostic latent manifold, UniLAP can be easily adapted to novel platforms. Pretrained on multi-source videos at a compact 1.1B parameter scale, UniLAP outperforms state-of-the-art latent-action and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) baselines across hand pose forecasting, single-arm and humanoid manipulation, and real-world deployment.

</details>


### [144] [Human-Like Redundancy Resolution for Real-Time Bimanual Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16855)
*Chao Tan,Ming Shin Cindy Sung,Shuxian Liang,Chaoqun Wan,Zhen Huang,Xian-Sheng Hua*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合自适应肘部引导与奇异鲁棒腕部跟踪的类人冗余解析框架，用于实时双臂人形机器人遥操作，在保持腕部跟踪精度的同时稳定肘部构型并增强奇异鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 7自由度机械臂对同一腕部位姿存在多组肘部构型，仅跟踪腕部会导致肘部漂移或构型突变；固定拟人约束虽能稳定肘部，却会限制接近奇异构型时所需的冗余调整。因此需兼顾腕部跟踪精度、肘部可预测性与奇异鲁棒性。

Method: 提出类人冗余解析框架：通过肩-腕几何重定向生成机器人尺度腕部目标；设计自适应肘部偏移准则，以偏移方向引导姿态，并按偏移幅值缩放引导，使人类手臂伸直时逐步释放冗余自由度；采用分层速度求解器，优先腕部跟踪，并将自适应肘部引导投影到腕部任务零空间。

Result: 在固定腕部目标消融中，自适应引导相较移除该引导将平均肘部方向误差降低54.4%；在工作空间边界压力测试中，奇异鲁棒跟踪相较未阻尼伪逆将峰值原始关节速度需求降低97.9%；真实双臂任务与跨平台重定向验证了跨机器人形态的适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效提升人形机器人双臂遥操作的肘部构型稳定性与奇异鲁棒性，并具备跨平台、跨形态应用潜力。

Abstract: Real-time bimanual humanoid teleoperation requires accurate wrist tracking while keeping arm configurations predictable. Because a 7-DoF arm admits multiple elbow configurations for the same wrist pose, wrist-only tracking can cause elbow drift or abrupt configuration changes. Fixed anthropomorphic constraints can stabilize elbow posture, but they can also block the redundant adjustments needed near singular configurations. We propose a human-like redundancy-resolution framework that combines adaptive elbow guidance with singularity-robust wrist tracking. Shoulder-wrist geometric retargeting generates robot-scale wrist targets. An adaptive elbow-offset criterion uses the offset direction to guide posture and scales this guidance by the offset magnitude, so the redundant degree of freedom is progressively released as the human arm straightens. A hierarchical velocity solver prioritizes wrist tracking and projects the adaptive elbow guidance into the wrist-task null space. In a fixed-wrist-target ablation, adaptive guidance reduces mean elbow-direction error by 54.4% compared with removing this guidance. In a workspace-boundary stress test, singularity-robust tracking reduces peak raw joint-velocity demand by 97.9% compared with the undamped pseudoinverse. Real-world bimanual tasks and cross-platform retargeting show applicability across robot morphologies.

</details>


### [145] [CopyQuad: Quadrupedal Skill Transfer from a Single Side-View Video Without a Depth Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16737)
*Mohamad H. Danesh,Raina Hu,Mengxue Xu,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CopyQuad 提出一种面向四足机器人模仿的侧视视频到机器人流程，省去单目深度或逐片段3D重建的“提升”阶段，将侧视相机缺失的轴直接固定为机器人自身站姿宽度，并在硬件上零样本部署多种步态与手势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频到机器人流程需用学习式提升阶段恢复侧视相机看不到的一个轴，但该轴在矢状面主导运动、肢体几乎位于图像平面的设定中可能并不关键，且逐片段优化与深度估计会引入误差和额外复杂度。

Method: 使用冻结的2D关键点检测器和固定解析重定向，将单个未标定侧视视频转化为机器人参考轨迹；足端目标按构造可达，因此无需逐片段轨迹优化。采用单一阶段索引奖励结构，每个行为仅由共享扫描得到的三个标量控制，学习狗步行、马步行、马小跑和两个非周期手势。

Result: 训练无关刚性测试中，视频深度模型使动物髋距变化达自身均值的40%–107%，单图像度量模型达22%–58%，但该距离本不应变化。运动捕捉显示狗步行真实横向爪移动峰峰值为4.5 cm，平均偏差约1.4 cm，低于策略已有3.5 cm横向足误差；修复到可行性的深度从不优于常数，未修复估计则使机器人倒地。

Conclusion: 在侧视、矢状面主导的四足模仿场景中，缺失的横向轴低于流程分辨率，直接固定为机器人站姿宽度比学习式深度/重建提升更简单且更有效。

Abstract: Video-to-robot pipelines for quadrupedal imitation insert a learned lifting stage, either a monocular depth model or a per-clip 3D reconstruction, to recover the one axis a side-view camera cannot see. We present CopyQuad, which omits that stage and pins the missing axis to the robot's own stance width instead. The setting is side-view footage of sagittally dominated motion, where limb motion is almost entirely in the image plane. A frozen 2D keypoint detector and a fixed analytic retargeting then turn a single uncalibrated clip into a robot reference whose foot targets are reachable by construction, so no per-clip trajectory optimization is needed. One phase-indexed reward structure, whose only per-behavior freedom is three scalars from a shared sweep, learns a dog walk, a horse walk, a horse trot and two non-periodic gestures, all deployed zero-shot on hardware. We then measure what omitting the axis costs. In a training-free rigidity test, a video depth model varies the distance between the animal's hips by $40$ to $107\%$ of its own mean and a single-image metric model by $22$ to $58\%$, though that distance cannot move. Motion capture puts the true lateral paw travel of a dog walk at $4.5$ cm peak to peak, a mean deviation near $1.4$ cm against the $3.5$ cm of lateral foot error our policies already leave, so the discarded axis sits below the pipeline's resolution. Depth repaired to feasibility never beats the constant, and the unrepaired estimates put the robot on the ground.

</details>


### [146] [Belief Space Planning for Risk Signal Temporal Logic via Mixed-Integer Convex Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16696)
*Shaohang Han,Joris Verhagen,Kaier Liang,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究高斯信念空间中的 Risk STL 机器人规划，提出基于信息形式信念参数化与凸松弛的混合整数凸规划方法，并证明其可靠、完备且保持全局最优输入轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际机器人需从噪声测量估计状态；当测量信息增益依赖状态时，可通过信念空间规划主动降低不确定性，但该问题通常非凸，梯度法既不完整也不能保证全局最优。

Method: 采用 Risk STL (RiSTL) 描述机器人时空任务，在高斯信念空间中对信念进行信息形式参数化，并对信念动力学进行凸松弛，从而构造混合整数凸规划 (MICP)。

Result: 理论证明该 MICP 是可靠且完备的，且所采用的凸松弛保持全局最优输入轨迹；数值仿真验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为高斯信念空间中的 RiSTL 规划提供了具备全局最优保证的凸优化框架，并通过数值仿真验证了其可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifies robot tasks with spatio-temporal requirements, and its robustness semantics allow planning by trajectory optimization. In practice, robots estimate their state from noisy measurements, and when the information gained from a measurement depends on the state, the robot can actively reduce its uncertainty by planning in the belief space. The resulting problem is, however, generally non-convex, for which gradient-based methods are neither complete nor globally optimal. In this paper, we consider Risk STL (RiSTL) planning in Gaussian belief space and show that an information-form parameterization of the belief, together with a convex relaxation of the belief dynamics, yields a mixed-integer convex program (MICP). We prove that the MICP is sound and complete, and that the relaxation preserves the globally optimal input trajectories. We validate the effectiveness of our method in numerical simulations.

</details>


### [147] [FEM-Validated Real-Time Modelling of Soft Tactile Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16667)
*Silvia Terrile,Kaichen Shi,Taejun Kwon,Jiseok Lee,Nathan Lepora,Yeejin Lee,Saekwang Nam,Efi Psomopoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种数据高效框架，将少量真实实验验证、高保真有限元仿真与图神经网络结合，用于平面软触觉结构二维触觉变形的实时高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软触觉传感器精确建模困难：有限元方法计算昂贵，实时仿真器对超弹性变形保真度不足；同时反复测试易损坏脆弱传感器，难以采集大规模物理数据集。

Method: 采用验证与训练解耦策略：仅用82个相机跟踪接触构型校准和验证ANSYS有限元模型；再由该模型生成768个合成接触场景训练图神经网络，并评估从标记变形到接触定位的正向预测以及从接触构型到标记变形的逆向推断。

Result: 验证后的有限元在超过90%验证案例中误差低于0.5 mm；GNN代理模型平均绝对误差为0.32 mm，推理达毫秒级，而有限元仿真为分钟级；完全基于合成有限元数据训练的模型在二维触觉结构真实测量上仍保持亚毫米精度，表现出在该平面设置内的强sim-to-real迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该框架可在无需大量物理测试的情况下实现实时变形预测，并可集成到实时可视化流程中，支持虚拟原型和基于仿真器的设计探索。

Abstract: Accurate modelling of soft tactile sensors remains difficult because finite element methods (FEM) are computa- tionally expensive, while real-time simulators often lack fidelity for hyperelastic deformation. Large physical datasets are also impractical to collect because repeated testing can damage fragile sensors. We present a data-efficient framework that combines limited real-world validation, high-fidelity FEM, and graph neural networks (GNNs) for accurate real-time prediction of two-dimensional tactile deformation in a planar soft tactile structure. The key idea is to decouple validation from training: 82 camera-tracked contact configurations are used only to tune and validate an ANSYS FEM model, which then generates 768 synthetic contact scenarios for GNN training. We evaluate both forward prediction from marker deformation to contact localisation and inverse inference from contact configuration to marker deformation. The validated FEM achieves sub-0.5 mm error in more than 90% of validation cases, while the learned GNN surrogate reaches a mean absolute error of 0.32 mm with millisecond-scale inference, compared with minute-scale FEM simulation. Models trained entirely on synthetic FEM data retain sub-millimetre accuracy on real measurements from the 2D tactile structure, demonstrating strong sim-to-real transfer within the tested planar setting. We further integrate the learned model into a real-time visualisation pipeline, enabling rapid deformation prediction for virtual prototyping and simulator-based design exploration without extensive physical testing.

</details>


### [148] [What Limits Cross-Task Generalization in World Action Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16641)
*Chen Zhao,Zipeng Ye,Yihan Lin,Yuchen Xie,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文将世界动作模型解耦为世界模型与逆动力学模型，并固定逆动力学模型来直接检验跨任务泛化；发现世界模型预测的未来在动作恢复上失败，主要错在夹爪与交互几何。为此提出VITA损失，用冻结的AoE对生成未来恢复动作并惩罚偏差，将生成引向动作相关细节；在两个世界模型、三个操作基准上将生成未来执行成功率从近零提升至47.0%，并可迁移到WAM提升原生控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型即使在预测出看似合理的操作未来时，跨任务泛化仍然困难。作者希望直接测试该差距，而非仅观察生成质量，因此需要解耦未来生成与动作恢复，以定位失败来源。

Method: 提出AoE：一个在大规模机器人数据上预训练的相机空间逆动力学模型，作为固定恢复接口，用于从不同未来来源（记录未来或世界模型生成未来）恢复动作。基于此诊断，提出VITA损失：将生成未来通过冻结AoE，惩罚恢复动作与真实动作的差异，从而将生成监督集中到夹爪和交互几何等动作相关区域。

Result: 从未见任务的记录未来恢复的动作执行效果几乎与重放演示相当，而世界模型预测的未来在所有基准上崩溃。失败并非泛化性生成失败，而是集中在夹爪和交互几何，生成容量被用于任务无关背景。VITA在两个世界模型和三个操作基准上，将生成未来的执行成功率从近零提升至47.0%，并迁移到WAM获得一致的原生控制增益。

Conclusion: 跨任务泛化瓶颈可直接归因于世界模型生成未来中动作相关细节的缺失，尤其是夹爪与交互几何。通过固定逆动力学模型提供动作恢复监督，VITA能有效重定向未来生成并提升下游执行，适用于世界模型与WAM。

Abstract: Cross-task generalization remains difficult for world action models (WAMs), even when their predicted futures depict plausible manipulation. This gap can be attributed directly: we make this gap directly testable by decoupling a WAM into a world model and an inverse-dynamics model (IDM), then holding the IDM fixed while replacing the source of future images. We introduce Align Once, Evaluate Everywhere (AoE), a camera-space inverse-dynamics model pretrained on large-scale robot data, which provides this recovery interface for different future sources across tasks and environments. Through it, we find that actions recovered from recorded futures of unseen tasks execute almost as well as replayed demonstrations, whereas futures predicted by world models collapse across benchmarks. The failures are not generic: they concentrate on the gripper and interaction geometry while generation capacity is spent on task-irrelevant background. This diagnosis suggests that supervision should target exactly these regions. We therefore introduce the vision-to-action (VITA) loss, which passes generated futures through the frozen AoE and penalizes discrepancies in the recovered actions, redirecting generation toward action-relevant detail. Across two world models and three manipulation benchmarks, this simple change raises the execution success of generated futures from near zero to 47.0%. The same loss also transfers to WAMs and yields consistent gains in native control.

</details>


### [149] [Language2Drive: Do Vision-Language-Action Models Really Follow the Instructions in Autonomous Driving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16634)
*Zihan You,Zehua Fan,Wenqi Lyu,Aoqi Wang,Ni Wang,Yifei Yang,Linge Zhao,Huan Li,Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Jueyu Chen,Xiangyu Li,Qianshan Wei,Jieyuan Pei,Yuxin Wang,Zhiwen Chen,Jiaqi Li,Wenxuan Song,Yanyuan Qiao,Benjin Zhu,Haoang Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Language2Drive 基准，用“配对反事实单元”（PCU）固定初始驾驶场景、仅改变自然语言指令，检验 VLA 自动驾驶模型是否真正按语言指令选择可行行为，而非仅完成安全/合规轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环基准主要评估路线完成、安全与规则遵守，未回答语言是否真正决定车辆执行哪种可行行为；安全成功的轨迹仍可能违反甚至忽略乘客指令。

Method: 提出 Language2Drive 基准，核心评估单元为 PCU：保持初始驾驶场景不变，仅替换指令，从而区分孤立指令成功与反事实配对成功，并覆盖四类指令跟随范围。在闭环 CARLA 仿真、重建真实场景与开环驾驶基准中实例化评估，并发布带专用地图的 CARLA-Language2Drive 模拟器。

Result: 在多种评估设置下测试代表性驾驶 VLA，发现强驾驶能力与弱语言控制之间的显著错配：93.2% 的成功 rollout 在替代可行指令下没有成功对应项；失败主要由“单侧完成”主导，即模型反复执行偏好行为，但在同一场景要求反事实替代行为时失败。

Conclusion: 当前评估存在两个根本局限：传统基准广泛使用的孤立成功与驾驶分数会显著高估语言跟随能力；现有驾驶 VLA 在匹配反事实测试下缺乏可靠指令跟随能力，因此需要采用反事实配对式评估。 

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models increasingly use natural-language instructions to condition autonomous driving behavior. Yet existing closed-loop benchmarks primarily measure route completion, safety, and rule compliance, leaving a fundamental question unresolved: does language actually determine which feasible behavior the vehicle executes? A trajectory may be safe and successful while still violating or even ignoring the passenger's instruction. To address this, we introduce \ltd, a benchmark designed to directly test this capability. Its key evaluation unit, the paired counterfactual unit (PCU), holds the initial driving scene fixed while changing only the instruction. This design enables us to distinguish isolated instruction success from counterfactual paired success across four instruction-following scopes. To test whether this distinction persists across evaluation regimes, we instantiate Language2Drive in complementary closed-loop CARLA simulation, reconstructed real-world scenes, and open-loop driving benchmarks, including our released CARLA-Language2Drive simulator with purpose-built maps. Evaluating representative driving VLAs across these settings reveals a striking mismatch between strong driving competence and weak language control: 93.2% of successful rollouts do not have a successful counterpart under the alternative feasible instruction. This failure is dominated by one-sided completion, where a model repeatedly executes one preferred behavior but fails when the same scene requires the counterfactual alternative. These results expose two fundamental limitations of current evaluation: isolated success and driving scores, which are widely used by conventional driving benchmarks, can substantially overstate language-following capability, while current driving VLAs themselves lack reliable instruction following under matched counterfactual tests.

</details>


### [150] [Design and Analysis of a Nonlinear Parallel Mechanism Coupling for Dynamic Humanoid Legs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16604)
*Rohit Kumar,Heiner Peters,Felix Wiebe,Franek Stark,Janne Janzen,Dennis Mronga,Shubham Vyas,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种串联-并联腿设计，结合膝关节并联机构、踝关节并联机构与非线性膝-踝耦合，并将执行器靠近髋部，以降低远端惯性并提升动态运动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要轻量腿部机构以实现敏捷、动态且高能效的运动；通过近端驱动降低远端惯性是常见目标，但保持力矩能力与高效力传递的紧凑传动仍难以实现。

Method: 设计一种新型串联-并联腿：包含膝关节并联机构、踝关节并联机构以及非线性膝-踝耦合，所有执行器靠近髋部。膝关节屈曲时，耦合被动辅助踝背屈以利于足部离地；伸展时辅助跖屈以利于蹬地，并通过膝传动重新分配踝负载。推导耦合的非线性传动模型，并在仿真与硬件上验证。

Result: 共享传动使两个关节可相互利用执行器能力：在负载下缓解膝关节峰值力矩需求，在快速运动中缓解踝关节峰值速度需求。对于6 kg腿，足端有效反射质量为0.37 kg，显示出极低的远端腿惯性。

Conclusion: 该设计通过近端执行器布置与非线性膝-踝耦合传动，实现了低远端惯性和更优的动态运动潜力，适用于动态人形机器人。

Abstract: Humanoids increasingly demand lightweight leg mechanisms for agile, dynamic, and energy-efficient locomotion. Reducing distal inertia through proximal actuation has thus become a common design goal. Yet, compact transmissions that preserve torque capability and efficient force transmission remain hard to achieve. This paper presents a novel series-parallel leg design combining a knee parallel mechanism, an ankle parallel mechanism, and a nonlinear knee-ankle coupling where all actuators placed closer to the hip. As the knee flexes, the coupling passively assists ankle dorsiflexion for foot clearance and, as it extends it assists plantarflexion for push-off, redistributing ankle load through the knee transmission. We derive the coupling's nonlinear transmission models and validate them in simulation and on hardware. The design shows that the shared transmission lets both joints draw on each other's actuator capacity by easing the knee's peak torque demand under load and the ankle's peak speed demand during fast motion. %Together With the leg's proximal actuator placement, the leg achieves an effective reflected mass of 0.37kg at the foot for a 6kg leg, demonstrating very low distal leg inertia for dynamic humanoid robots.

</details>


### [151] [Learning Reachability-Based Safety Representations for Real-Time Robot Navigation in Unknown and Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16522)
*Bojan Derajić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出离线训练神经隐式表示 Hamilton-Jacobi 可达性值函数，并在线用于优化控制器，实现未知动态环境下的安全机器人导航；方法涵盖 MPC 终端约束、CBF 安全滤波器及面向高维系统的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠几何碰撞检测不足以保障安全：机器人即使离障碍物尚远，也可能因自身速度、朝向或障碍物运动而无法避免碰撞。HJ 可达性可通过值函数符号区分安全与不可避免碰撞状态，但在线计算每个新观测环境的可达性代价过高。

Method: 值函数等于直接计算的约束（如符号距离）减去非负的动力学修正；只学习该修正，可在近似误差下仍将表示障碍物排除在预测安全集之外。五种方法：前两种将学习函数作为模型预测控制的终端约束，分别处理静态局部环境和预测障碍运动；第三、四种将其作为控制屏障函数安全滤波器的屏障候选，分别处理静态环境和通过相对坐标下的成对屏障处理数量变化的移动障碍；第五种面向控制仿射动力学与多面体输入，利用 HJ Hamiltonian 在顶点取最大，通过可并行的顶点输入树搜索生成训练标签，并以 HJ 变分不等式损失训练屏障候选。

Result: 仿真与地面、空中机器人硬件实验表明，相比几何基线和手工设计基线，该方法具有更好的避碰性能，尤其在短预测时域和移动障碍物场景中。

Conclusion: 离线神经隐式 HJ 可达性表示可实时为优化控制器提供安全信息，并借助顶点搜索与变分不等式损失扩展到最多 13 个状态的控制仿射系统。

Abstract: Safe robot navigation in unknown, dynamic environments requires more than geometric collision checking: a robot still far from an obstacle may be unable to avoid it because of its speed, orientation, or the obstacle's motion. Hamilton--Jacobi (HJ) reachability analysis captures this in a value function whose sign separates states that admit a safe continuation from those where collision is unavoidable, but computing it online for every newly observed environment is too expensive. This dissertation trains neural implicit representations offline on reachability solutions to give optimization-based controllers this information in real time. Since the value function never exceeds the constraint, it equals a directly computed constraint, such as a signed distance, minus a nonnegative dynamics correction. Learning only this correction keeps represented obstacles outside the predicted safe set despite approximation errors. The first two of five methods use the learned function as a terminal constraint in model predictive control, for static local environments and predicted obstacle motion. The next two use it as a barrier candidate in control barrier function safety filters, for static environments and, through pairwise barriers in relative coordinates, for a varying number of moving obstacles. Simulations and hardware experiments on ground and aerial robots show better collision avoidance than geometric and hand-designed baselines, especially for short horizons and moving obstacles. The fifth method replaces the grid-based offline solver, which limits the first four to low-dimensional models. For control-affine dynamics with polytopic inputs, the HJ Hamiltonian is maximized at a vertex, so a parallelizable tree search over vertex inputs can generate training labels. With a loss from the HJ variational inequality, these labels yield barrier candidates for systems with up to 13 states.

</details>


### [152] [Trust-Calibrated VLA Policy Post-Training with Evolving Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16517)
*Wenzhan Li,Yiran Qin,Heng Zhou,Huirui Wang,Yulan Guo,Ruimao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MERL，一种信任校准的强化学习后训练框架，通过在线更新的世界模型与奖励代理头，并依据模拟器可靠性动态调节想象数据比例、 rollout 视界及 chunk 级信任，缓解VLA精炼中的可靠性与效率矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型通常经模仿学习预训练，部署超出示范覆盖时需RL后训练。现有精炼范式存在可靠性—效率张力：无模型方法避免模拟器偏差但交互密集，基于模型的方法可能利用不可靠想象，即使模拟器在自适应更新。

Method: MERL将策略优化与在线更新的模拟器耦合，模拟器由世界模型和奖励代理头组成。跨阶段上，模拟器可靠性控制想象数据比例与rollout视界；rollout内，chunk级信任控制回放选择与优化权重。对于无精确grounded continuation的递归想象，使用残差预测器估计信任，而无需查询未来真实结果。

Result: 在扰动的LIBERO-PRO任务上，MERL达到79.7%平均成功率，高阈值学习速度较匹配的无模型控制提升10.9%，并在三个训练种子下优于同协议外部基线。每任务50个示范的固定数据适应在两个物理机器人任务上达到80.0%平均成功率。

Conclusion: MERL通过信任校准有效平衡VLA强化学习后训练的可靠性与效率，在仿真扰动任务和真实机器人少样本适应中均表现优越。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are often pretrained via imitation learning, making reinforcement learning (RL) post-training important when deployment moves beyond demonstration coverage. Existing refinement paradigms expose a reliability--efficiency tension: model-free refinement avoids simulator bias but is interaction-intensive, whereas model-based refinement can exploit unreliable imagination even as its simulator adapts. We propose Model-Evolving Reinforcement Learning (MERL), a trust-calibrated framework that couples policy optimization with an online-updated simulator comprising a world model and a reward proxy head. Across stages, simulator reliability controls the imagined-data ratio and rollout horizon; within rollouts, chunk-level trust controls replay selection and optimization weight. For recursive imagination without exact grounded continuations, a residual predictor estimates trust without querying future ground truth. On perturbed LIBERO-PRO tasks, MERL reaches 79.7% average success, improves high-threshold learning speed by 10.9% over the matched model-free control, and outperforms same-protocol external baselines across three training seeds. Fixed-data adaptation from 50 demonstrations per task achieves 80.0% average success on two physical-robot tasks.

</details>


### [153] [DeTac: Dual-branch Decoupling of Texture and Force Signals in Vision-Based Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16463)
*Zhirui Zhang,Yunkai Tang,Jiahang Li,Fan Fei,Jinnuo Zhang,Kairan Ding,Jieji Ren,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeTac双分支生成框架，从单张混合触觉图像中同时解耦出干净 shading 与 marker 图像，并构建真实配对数据集，在解耦精度、下游任务与跨传感器泛化上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的触觉传感器对灵巧操作至关重要，但现有基于marker的设计在单次观测中耦合了表面纹理与接触力信号；已有数据驱动解耦方法依赖串行mask修复，存在因物理凹陷导致的mask尺寸困境、误差残留以及缺乏真实配对数据监督等问题。

Method: 提出DeTac双分支生成框架，并行从单张混合触觉图像中提取干净shading图像和marker图像；利用全图合成天然绕过mask依赖，并引入跨分支协同优化实现相互监督以抑制误差残留；同时使用定制硬件构建严格对齐的真实世界配对触觉数据集，为训练提供高质量监督。

Result: 大量实验表明DeTac取得当前最优的解耦精度，显著促进下游表面重建与力估计任务，并展现出跨传感器泛化能力。

Conclusion: DeTac有效解决了marker-based触觉图像解耦中的mask依赖、误差累积和监督数据缺失问题，为高精度触觉感知及下游操作任务提供了可泛化的解耦方案。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors enable fine-grained perception for dexterous robotic manipulation, but current marker-based designs tightly couple surface texture and contact force signals within a single observation. Existing data-driven decoupling methods rely on serial mask-based inpainting, which suffers from mask-sizing dilemmas caused by physical indentation effects, error retention, and lack of real paired data for supervision. To address these challenges, we propose DeTac, a novel dual-branch generative framework that concurrently extracts clean shading and marker images from a single mixed tactile image. DeTac leverages full-image synthesis to naturally bypass mask dependency and introduces a cross-branch synergistic optimization that enables mutual supervision, effectively preventing error retention. Furthermore, we construct a strictly aligned, real-world paired tactile dataset using custom hardware to provide high-quality supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeTac achieves state-of-the-art decoupling accuracy, significantly facilitating downstream surface reconstruction and force estimation tasks while exhibiting cross-sensor generalization.

</details>


### [154] [SHOES: Sim-to-real Hardware-adaptation via On-policy Expansion of Skills for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16457)
*Kota Tsuruhara,Tsung-Yuan Chen,Kento Kawaharazuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SHOES：冻结仿真训练的名义运动策略，仅在线微调轻量且恒等初始化的调制模块，并引入 SafeBox 工作空间感知路点采样器，使足式机器人适应穿鞋导致的形态变化，并在 Unitree Go2 仿真与真实实验中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动控制器通常针对固定形态训练，而穿鞋会改变足部几何、质量和摩擦等地面接触界面，导致策略性能下降；因此需要一种样本高效、能保持初始行为并适应硬件形态变化的在线适应框架。

Method: SHOES 冻结仿真中训练好的名义策略，只在机器人上微调轻量、恒等初始化的调制模块，以保留初始运动行为并实现高效硬件适应；同时提出 SafeBox，一种工作空间感知的路点采样器，将在线适应限制在用户定义的工作空间内。

Result: 在配备鞋子的 Unitree Go2 仿真中，SHOES 以有限额外适应显著恢复因穿鞋引起的形态变化后的运动性能；在相同适应预算下优于仅 FiLM、仅 Linear Adapter 和残差强化学习基线；并在穿人类鞋子的 Unitree Go2 真实实验中进一步评估硬件部署效果。

Conclusion: SHOES 能有效适应 footwear-induced morphology changes，在有限适应预算下恢复并提升运动性能，且具备真实机器人硬件部署潜力。

Abstract: Humans expand their locomotion capability by wearing appropriate footwear, such as hiking boots for mountains or skates for ice. Footwear changes the ground-contact interface and enables traversal of otherwise difficult terrain. In contrast, locomotion controllers are typically trained for a fixed morphology, and their performance can degrade when shoes alter foot geometry, mass, and friction. We propose SHOES, a sample-efficient framework for adapting locomotion policies to footwear-induced morphology changes. SHOES freezes a simulation-trained nominal policy and fine-tunes only lightweight, identity-initialized modulation modules on the robot, preserving the initial behavior while enabling efficient hardware adaptation. We also introduce SafeBox, a workspace-aware waypoint sampler that constrains online adaptation within a user-defined workspace. The simulation results with a shoe-equipped Unitree Go2 show that SHOES substantially recovers locomotion performance after footwear-induced morphology changes using limited additional adaptation. Under the same adaptation budget, SHOES outperforms FiLM-only, Linear Adapter-only, and residual-RL baselines. Real-world experiments on a Unitree Go2 fitted with human shoes further evaluate the effectiveness of SHOES for hardware deployment.

</details>


### [155] [Estimating the Causal Impact of Social Navigation Signals in Corridor Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16411)
*Francesco Baldo,Luca Castri,Gloria Beraldo,Nicola Bellotto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文用因果推断评估走廊人机交互中社交导航信号是否真正提高通行成功率，发现忽略混杂因素的观察性估计会错误判断信号有害，而因果介入估计显示其有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机共享环境中，机器人不仅需要决定如何导航，还需判断何时发出社交信号；传统基于观察的推断易受混杂因素偏差影响，可能错误估计信号与任务结果之间的关系，因此需要因果框架来区分相关性与因果效应。

Method: 构建走廊场景的因果DAG，纳入机器人动作、行人占用、可用空间、任务结果及受控混杂因素（静态障碍）；估计社交导航信号对通行成功率的介入效应，并与仅条件于机器人动作、未显式建模干预的观察性估计进行比较。

Result: 观察性估计错误地表明社交信号有害；在考虑混杂变量后，因果估计显示社交信号有益，结论发生反转。

Conclusion: 忽略混杂会导致错误结论；因果模型对于社会机器人导航中情境感知的信号决策至关重要。

Abstract: Deploying robots in human-shared environments requires deciding not only how to navigate, but also when dedicated social signals are worth emitting. Classic observation-based inference can be misleading in such settings: confounding factors may bias the estimated relationship between a signal and the task outcome, leading to incorrect decisions. Causal inference provides a framework for disentangling these relationships by modelling the mechanisms linking observations, actions, and outcomes, and by estimating interventional effects after accounting for confounding paths. We study a human-robot interaction scenario in a corridor and use a causal model to estimate whether a social navigation signal causally improves transit success. We represent the interaction as a causal DAG involving robot action, pedestrian occupancy, available space, task outcome, and a controlled confounder (static obstacles). We compare the interventional estimate with the observational estimate obtained by conditioning only on the robot action, which does not explicitly model interventions. Our results show that accounting for confounding variables can reverse the conclusions drawn from naive observational analysis: the observational estimate wrongly suggests that the social signal is detrimental, whereas the causal estimate shows that it is beneficial. This discrepancy highlights how failing to account for confounding can lead to incorrect conclusions and demonstrates the importance of causal models for context-aware signalling decisions in social robot navigation.

</details>


### [156] [LABIT: Long-Horizon Robotic Assembly Benchmark for Industrial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16398)
*Constantin Schempp,Christian Friedrich,Luca Kofler,Laura Suhm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向长时程工业装配的机器人基准LABIT，覆盖DIN标准装配任务，并提供复杂度分类、性能指标及基于传统力位控制的基线评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人装配基准多聚焦单一任务，难以评估现代制造中多步骤、相互关联的装配与子装配流程，尤其是前序误差会影响后续操作时的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建LABIT基准，包含拧紧、放置、插入、卡扣等DIN规范装配任务，涵盖高/低公差条件；设计基准测试协议与基于装配复杂度分类的新性能指标，提供零件清单及CAD模型；以传统力位控制作为基线。

Result: 在基线上采用传统力位控制进行评估，报告了按基元、子任务、装配类别和子装配划分的可靠性及得分指标。

Conclusion: LABIT可作为工业机器人长时程装配研究与开发的基础工具，用于评估智能机器人系统在连续装配步骤中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robot learning has undergone significant advancements in the domain of compliant control and robotic assembly. The development of novel methodologies has transcended the capacity to address discrete tasks, such as grasping or inserting, and has evolved to encompass the comprehensive construction of entire assemblies. Conventional benchmarks, which are predominantly focused on single tasks, are no longer adequate to address the complexity and interconnected nature of modern manufacturing processes. Consequently, the necessity for benchmarks to encompass a broader spectrum of assembly and subassembly steps is becoming apparent. In light of these developments, we propose LABIT, an industrial robotic assembly benchmark for long-horizon manipulation. This benchmark encompasses DIN-norm assembly tasks, including screwing, placing, inserting, and snapping, under both high and low tolerance conditions. The successive nature of these assembly steps implies that prior errors can influence subsequent manipulation. Consequently, this benchmark is particularly relevant for assessing the robustness of intelligent robotic systems. The proposed benchmarking protocols and novel performance metric, based on a taxonomy of assembly complexity in conjunction with the comprehensive list of parts and their corresponding CAD models, serve as essential tools for research and development in the field. To provide a baseline, we evaluate the benchmark using traditional force-position control and report reliability and score metrics per primitive, subtask, assembly category, and subassembly. Accompanying data is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [157] [RoboBugBench: Benchmarking Automated Repair of Real Robot Software Under an Audited Behavioral Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16369)
*Alexey Tuzikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboBugBench——一个基于固定版本 ROS 2 导航栈、含 33 个真实且经执行验证缺陷的机器人修复基准，用编译加物理仿真行为而非单元测试来评分，并揭示评分 oracle 本身构成可观测性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体越来越多地修改机器人软件，但其修复能力无法衡量：该领域缺乏可继承的 oracle——机器人缺陷以行为形式呈现，而机器人代码库几乎不含能捕获该缺陷的测试。

Method: 构建 RoboBugBench：33 个真实、经执行验证的缺陷，位于固定版本 ROS 2 导航栈；候选补丁通过编译并在物理仿真中驱动机器人来评分，而非运行单元测试。三个前沿模型各获得 1 次尝试/实例；由于 oracle 是构造而非继承的，对其做对抗式审计，并给出该审计产生的分解，包括伪造补丁套件与准则对比。

Result: 修复率 24.2%–36.4%，模型间无显著成对差异；难度随 oracle 而非领域变化：进程存活判定的实例为 66.7%，行为判定的仅 22.2%（实例级 p=0.0027）。抑制失败但未修复缺陷的伪造补丁被出厂规则接受 11/11，而同一数据上的分布规则拒绝其中 4 个且无额外误拒。

Conclusion: 残余部分是可观测性界限——观测通道所丢弃的信息无法由任何谓词恢复，故区分度必须靠插桩而非更严格准则来换取；修复率只是界：被 oracle 无法区分之处抬高，又被一致性机制在运行间噪声下拒绝正确修复所压低。作者发布语料、准则集、伪造套件与缓存遥测。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly modify robot software, but whether they can repair it is unmeasured, because the domain supplies no oracle to inherit: a robot bug manifests as behavior, and robot repositories ship almost no tests that capture it. We present RoboBugBench, a benchmark of 33 real, execution-validated defects in a pinned ROS 2 navigation stack, in which a candidate patch is graded by compiling it and driving the robot through a physics simulation rather than by running a unit test. Three frontier models, each given 1 attempt per instance, repair 24.2%-36.4%, with no pairwise difference significant; difficulty tracks the oracle rather than the domain, at 66.7% on instances graded by process survival against 22.2% on those graded by what the robot did (instance-level p = 0.0027). Because the oracle is constructed rather than inherited we audit it adversarially, and report the decomposition that audit yields: counterfeit patches which suppress a failure without repairing the defect are accepted by the shipped rule 11/11, while a distributional rule over the same data rejects 4 at no cost in false rejects. The residual is an observability bound (what the observation channel discards, no predicate over it can recover), so discrimination must be bought with instrumentation rather than stricter criteria, and our repair rates are bounds: inflated by what the oracle cannot distinguish, deflated by unanimity's rejection of correct fixes under run-to-run noise. We release the corpus, criterion batteries, counterfeit suite and cached telemetry.

</details>


### [158] [PINT: A Unified Model with Shared Predictive Intent for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16355)
*Xinyu Guo,Chao Jing,Li Yi,Xingyu Chen,Zhengxing Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PINT 提出一种统一模型，通过共享的 chunk 级 Predictive Intent 协调视觉语言模型（VLM）的语义推理、视频生成模型（VGM）的世界建模和动作生成，同时保留各专家模型的专用路径；无需具身预训练即在仿真与真实双臂任务中取得强 ID 表现并显著提升 OOD 泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接通过多层共享注意力融合 VLM 与 VGM 的统一模型，容易削弱两类专家模型的专用能力，难以同时发挥语义推理和世界建模优势。

Method: PINT 在 VLM 序列中插入可学习 Intent Tokens 形成 chunk 级 Predictive Intent，并用其条件化 VGM 与 Action Expert（AE）。方法仅替换 VGM 的 prompt 条件，并在推理时保留 VGM，由 AE 通过单向注意力读取其表示。Predictive Intent 不预先规定编码内容，而是由 VLM、VGM、AE 的原生预测目标联合学习，以获得同一即将执行任务的不同互补视角。训练分两阶段：Stage 1 冻结 VGM，使未来视频预测塑造 Predictive Intent 中的任务相关未来动态；Stage 2 固定已学 Intent，适配 VGM 与 AE。

Result: 无需具身预训练，PINT 在分布内（ID）表现强，并显著提升分布外（OOD）泛化：在 RoboTwin 2.0-Hard 上达到 15.5%，优于 Fast-WAM 的 1.8% 和 StarVLA 的 3.2%；在 LIBERO-Plus 上达到 79.5%，优于 51.5% 和 75.0%。在五个真实世界双臂任务中，PINT 达到 68% ID 和 24% OOD 成功率，对比预训练 Motus 的 35%/9% 和预训练 π0.5 的 67%/35%。

Conclusion: PINT 通过共享 chunk 级 Predictive Intent 有效协调 VLM、VGM 与 AE 的专用能力，在不进行具身预训练的情况下取得强 ID 性能并大幅改善仿真 OOD 泛化；真实世界 OOD 仍低于预训练 π0.5，表明其泛化能力虽显著提升但仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Generalist robot manipulation can benefit from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) for semantic reasoning and video generation models (VGMs) for world modeling. However, existing unified models that directly fuse them through shared attention at multiple layers can make it harder to draw on their specialized capabilities. We introduce PINT, a unified model that coordinates semantic reasoning, world modeling, and action generation through a shared chunk-level Predictive Intent while preserving expert-specific pathways. PINT inserts learnable Intent Tokens into the VLM sequence to form this representation, conditioning both the VGM and an Action Expert (AE). It replaces only the VGM's prompt conditioning and retains the VGM at inference, where the AE reads its representations through one-way attention. Rather than prescribing what Predictive Intent should encode, PINT learns it jointly from the native prediction objectives of the VLM, VGM, and AE, which provide complementary views of the same upcoming task execution. Training uses two stages: Stage 1 freezes the VGM so that future video prediction shapes Predictive Intent with task-relevant future dynamics, while Stage 2 fixes the learned Intent and adapts the VGM and AE. Without embodied pretraining, PINT achieves strong in-distribution (ID) performance and substantially improves out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization, reaching 15.5% on RoboTwin 2.0-Hard compared with 1.8% for Fast-WAM and 3.2% for StarVLA, and 79.5% on LIBERO-Plus compared with 51.5% and 75.0%, respectively. Across five real-world bimanual tasks, PINT reaches 68% ID and 24% OOD success, compared with 35%/9% for pretrained Motus and 67%/35% for pretrained $\pi_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [159] [Toward Study on the Effects of Robot's Humorous Apology on Human's Trust Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16334)
*Gayoung Kim,Go-Eum Cha,Wonse Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究计划通过2x2被试间在线实验，检验幽默机器人道歉对信任恢复的作用及错误严重性的调节效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人错误会降低用户信任与依赖意愿，道歉可助于信任恢复，但不同道歉策略与错误严重性的影响尚不明确，尤其是幽默道歉的适用边界。

Method: 采用2（错误严重性：低 vs. 高）×2（道歉策略：通用 vs. 幽默）被试间设计，在开源 Warehouse Robot Interaction Simulator 中进行在线协作任务，参与者经历机器人错误与道歉后报告信任、信任恢复、交互体验和复用意愿。

Result: 目前为研究计划，尚无结果；假设幽默道歉在低严重错误后比通用道歉带来更强的信任恢复，但在高严重错误后该优势减弱或反转。

Conclusion: 研究旨在揭示幽默机器人道歉的信任修复效果受错误严重性调节，为不同错误情境下的机器人道歉策略设计提供依据。

Abstract: Robot errors can disrupt human-robot collaboration by reducing user trust and willingness to rely on the robot. Robot apologies can support trust recovery after such errors, but their effects may vary with apology strategy and error severity. This study therefore presents a user experiment based on a 2x2 between-subjects factorial design to examine the effect of humorous robot apologies on user trust recovery and the moderating role of error severity. The design crosses robot error severity (low vs. high) with apology strategy (generic vs. humorous). An online experiment will be conducted using the open-source Warehouse Robot Interaction Simulator. Participants will complete a collaborative task, experience a robot error followed by an apology, and report trust, trust recovery, interaction experience and reuse intention. We hypothesize that humorous apologies will produce greater trust recovery than generic apologies after low-severity errors, but that the advantage of humor will weaken or reverse after high-severity errors.

</details>


### [160] [Imitation Learning of Avoidance Modes for Efficient Multimodal Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16326)
*Changyu Lee,Byung-Kwan Lee,Kanghoon Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SideNet-MPC：用图神经网络模仿多模态 MPC（MM-MPC）专家进行避让侧决策，替代随决策关键障碍物数量指数增长的模式枚举，每控制周期最多求解两个 OCP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MM-MPC 通过为每种避让侧组合求解一个最优控制问题（OCP）来避免移动障碍物间的局部极小，但计算成本随决策关键障碍物数量指数增长。目标是在保留专家行为与安全约束的同时大幅降低计算量。
