Method: 将 MM-MPC 作为专家，训练 GNN 预测其最小代价模式，即为每个障碍物选择一个避让侧。图的边编码障碍物对之间、车辆首次到达任一障碍物时刻的预测间隙，使阻挡偏好通行的邻居可反转提议侧。保留专家选择规则，即比较最小代价模式与上一执行模式，并用网络提议替代最小代价模式。因此每周期最多求解两个 OCP，且实际输入仍来自带控制障碍函数（CBF）约束的 OCP，而非直接来自网络。

Result: 在 100 次种子匹配的混合交通闭环蒙特卡洛中，SideNet-MPC 达到专家成功率的 93%，单线程每控制周期耗时 18.6 ms，比专家在 16 个并行线程上的 35.5 ms 少 48%。在受控间隙扫描中，其提议侧在与专家相同的间隙区间发生反转；而无成对边的提议器在相同数据训练下从不反转，并在确定性场景的阻塞门处全部死锁。

Conclusion: SideNet-MPC 以学习到的模式决策替代组合枚举，在保持 OCP/CBF 安全保证和接近专家成功率的同时显著降低计算成本；成对边建模对于避让侧反转和避免死锁至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal model predictive control (MM-MPC) avoids local minima among moving obstacles by solving one optimal control problem (OCP) per combination of avoidance sides, so its computational cost grows exponentially with the number of decision-critical obstacles. We propose SideNet-MPC, which replaces this enumeration with a mode decision learned by imitating MM-MPC as an expert. A graph neural network is trained to predict the expert's minimum-cost mode, one avoidance side per obstacle. Its edges encode, for each obstacle pair, the gap predicted for the moment the vehicle first reaches either obstacle, so a neighbor that blocks a preferred passage can reverse the proposed side. The expert's selection rule compares its minimum-cost mode with the previously executed mode; SideNet-MPC keeps this rule and substitutes the network proposal for the minimum-cost mode. Hence at most two OCPs are solved per control period, and every applied input still comes from an OCP with control barrier function constraints rather than from the network. In $100$ seed-matched closed-loop Monte Carlo runs of mixed traffic, SideNet-MPC reaches $93\%$ of the expert's success rate and needs \SI{18.6}{ms} per control period on a single thread, 48\% less than the expert's \SI{35.5}{ms} on sixteen parallel threads. In a controlled gap sweep, its proposed side reverses in the same gap interval as the expert's, whereas proposers without pairwise edges, trained on the same data, never reverse and all deadlock at the blocked gates of a deterministic scene.

</details>


### [161] [Constraint-Decomposed Impedance Control from Generative Policy for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16316)
*Kyungseo Park,Byeongdo Lim,Jungbin Lim,Minseok Choi,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种约束分解阻抗控制方法：利用生成策略的采样多样性估计末端可行运动方向，并通过PCA确定可行扭转子空间以构造6×6阻抗增益矩阵，在保持任务执行的同时减少违反运动学约束的接触载荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开门、拧螺栓、拉抽屉等关节物体操作任务会对机器人末端执行器运动施加运动学约束，神经策略可能无法满足这些约束；混合力位控制严格将力与位置控制分离到正交子空间，而阻抗控制虽通过虚拟惯量、阻尼和刚度调节机械阻抗，但为机器人策略选择任务合适的阻抗增益仍然困难。

Method: 针对关节物体操作，从同一观测下生成策略产生的轨迹集合中推断扭转空间中的可行与受约束子空间；对末端执行器扭转进行主成分分析（PCA）确定可行扭转子空间，并据此形成6×6阻抗增益矩阵。对许多常见关节物体，可行扭转子空间与物体构型无关，因此只需估计一次并在操作中固定增益；对不满足该独立性的少数情况，估计一个包含局部可行子空间的更高维、构型无关扭转子空间，从而仍可使用固定增益策略。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的单自由度及多自由度物体实验表明，与各向同性阻抗控制相比，所提方法能持续降低违反约束的接触载荷，同时维持任务执行。

Conclusion: 该方法通过生成策略采样实现约束分解阻抗控制，可高效估计任务适配的固定阻抗增益，提升关节物体操作中受运动学约束时的安全性与合规性。

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation tasks such as opening doors, tightening bolts, and pulling drawers impose kinematic constraints on a robot end-effector's motion that neural policies may fail to satisfy. Unlike hybrid force-position control that strictly separates force and position control into orthogonal subspaces, impedance control modulates a mechanical impedance system in the form of a virtual inertia, damping, and stiffness. However, selecting task-appropriate impedance gains remains challenging for robot policies. In this paper, we address this problem for the case of manipulating articulated objects. Our constraint-decomposed impedance control method uses the sampling diversity of a generative policy to estimate the feasible motion directions in twist space. The key idea is to infer the feasible and constrained subspaces of twists from an ensemble of trajectories generated by the policy from the same observation. Principal component analysis (PCA) of the end-effector twists determines the feasible twist subspace, which is then used to form 6x6 impedance gain matrices. For many common articulated objects, the feasible twist subspace is independent of the object configuration, so the subspace and resulting gains are estimated once and held fixed during manipulation, minimizing computational overhead. For less common cases where this independence does not hold, we instead estimate a configuration-independent twist subspace of higher dimension that contains the local feasible subspaces, allowing the same fixed-gain strategy to be used. Experiments across simulated and real-world single- and multi-DoF objects show that the proposed method consistently reduces constraint-violating contact loads relative to isotropic impedance control while maintaining task execution.

</details>


### [162] [Listen Before Act: Audio-Conditioned Robot Manipulation with ListenVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16313)
*Jiaqi Fan,Rui Xu,Jiakun Xia,Xuyu Fan,Shuailei Ma,Yuefeng Wang,Qing Wang,Jiarui Zhang,Yili Fu,Xu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ListenVLA：基于 GR00T N1.7 的音频条件 VLA 策略，并构建 LIBERO-Audio 基准，实现声音引导的机器人操作，在仿真与真实任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型主要依赖视觉与语言，难以利用视觉无法可靠推断、且常需通过物理交互才能引发的听觉线索；许多操作任务依赖此类声音信息。

Method: ListenVLA 在因果音频历史上融合语义音频嵌入与波形声学线索，通过音频 token 和特征调制条件化连续动作生成；引入音频-动作绑定目标，用上下文匹配示教将听觉差异与示教动作差异关联，在共享纯噪声动作输入和固定非音频观测下监督不同音频条件下 flow-velocity 预测的短时差异，并结合完整动作块监督。另构建 LIBERO-Audio 八任务基准，涵盖基于声音的目标选择、听觉事件响应及揭示声学证据的物理交互。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Audio 和三个真实世界操作任务上表现优异；消融实验支持绑定监督与音频调制的贡献，音频交换测试表明声音会影响动作预测。

Conclusion: ListenVLA 证明音频条件化与音频-动作绑定能有效增强 VLA 的声音引导操作能力，为依赖听觉线索的机器人操作提供了新方案与评测基准。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced robotic manipulation by connecting visual perception and language instructions to action generation. However, many manipulation tasks depend on auditory cues that cannot be reliably inferred from visual observations and may need to be elicited through physical interaction. To address this gap, we present ListenVLA, an audio-conditioned VLA policy built on GR00T N1.7 for sound-guided manipulation. ListenVLA combines semantic audio embeddings with waveform-derived acoustic cues over a causal audio history and conditions continuous action generation through audio tokens and feature modulation. Moreover, we introduce an audio-action binding objective that uses context-matched demonstrations to link auditory differences to demonstrated action differences. The objective supervises short-horizon differences between flow-velocity predictions under different audio conditions, with shared pure-noise action inputs and fixed non-audio observations, alongside full-chunk action supervision. Furthermore, we develop LIBERO-Audio, an eight-task benchmark covering sound-based target selection, responses to auditory events, and physical interactions that reveal acoustic evidence. Experiments on LIBERO-Audio and three real-world manipulation tasks show excellent performance. Ablation studies support the contributions of binding supervision and audio modulation, while audio swap tests show that sound influences action predictions. Our code and videos are available at this https URL

</details>


### [163] [ReAP: From Visual Failure Recognition to Action-Conditioned Risk Prediction for Runtime Failure Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16309)
*Jingyuan Liu,Yunpeng Qing,Changxu Wei,Zhi Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReAP 是一种面向通用机器人策略的未来感知运行时监控框架，通过结合视觉失败识别与动作条件失败预测，在执行前预警潜在失败，并兼顾检测准确性与预警及时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在执行中仍较脆弱，可靠部署需要运行时监控及时发出失败警告；现有监控器多基于当前观测或策略信号估计风险，未显式预测候选动作的未来后果。

Method: ReAP 首先在成功与失败轨迹上训练视觉检测器，学习与失败相关的表征空间并估计当前风险；随后使用动作条件预测器在该空间中预测未来表征，从而估计未来失败风险。运行时，检测器与预测器分别提供当前状态失败识别和未来感知风险评估的互补信号。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界中对多种机器人策略的实验表明，ReAP 能有效平衡失败检测准确率与预警及时性。

Conclusion: ReAP 能够在动作执行前进行失败风险预判，为通用机器人策略的可靠部署提供更及时、准确的运行时监控。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, generalist robot policies remain brittle during execution. Reliable deployment therefore requires runtime monitoring that provides timely warnings of failure. Existing monitors commonly estimate risk from current observations or policy signals without explicitly predicting the future consequences of proposed actions. To anticipate such risks before action execution, we introduce ReAP, a future-aware runtime monitoring framework that combines visual failure recognition with action-conditioned failure prediction. Specifically, ReAP first trains a visual detector on successful and failed rollouts to learn a failure-relevant representation space and estimate current risk. An action-conditioned predictor then forecasts the resulting future representation in this space to estimate future failure risk. At runtime, the detector and predictor provide complementary signals for current-state failure recognition and future-aware risk assessment. Experiments across diverse robot policies in simulation and the real world demonstrate that ReAP effectively balances failure-detection accuracy and warning timeliness.

</details>


### [164] [PACE: Temporal-Role Tactile Conditioning for Chunk-Based Insertion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16308)
*Zhiyuan Chen,Shixiong Xu,Shuai Chen,Yangwei You*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACE 是一种面向 chunk 式插入策略的时序角色触觉条件框架，将共享触觉流分为持续接触状态、突发接触事件和接触后果预测三种时序角色，从而在保持动作 chunk 连续性的同时改善接触丰富的插入操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的插入要求机器人结合视觉对齐与触觉调整。现有 visuotactile chunk 策略将触觉反馈压缩到单一的生成时融合通路中，导致持续接触状态、突发接触事件和未来接触后果都被隐含建模在同一表示中，难以区分不同触觉反馈的时序作用。

Method: PACE 将共享触觉流组织为三种时序角色：持续接触状态用于条件化 action-chunk 生成；突发接触事件用于修正已生成的 chunk；接触后果预测在训练阶段塑造动作表示。该设计保留 chunk 级动作连续性，并为不同时序角色的接触反馈分配独立通路。

Result: 在五个真实机器人插入任务中，PACE 平均成功率为 90.7%，比仅视觉和生成时触觉融合基线高出超过 44 个百分点；在 ManipulationNet 基准的六种几何间隙配置中保持 88.7% 成功率，包括工业级 0.1 mm 间隙。消融实验证实三种时序角色的互补贡献；机制分析表明 PACE 能分离正常插入进展与需要立即修正的接触事件；跨信号与跨架构实验表明该时序角色原则可扩展到不同物理反馈信号和策略骨干。

Conclusion: PACE 通过显式区分触觉反馈的持续状态、突发事件与未来后果，有效提升 chunk 式接触丰富插入策略的成功率、鲁棒性与泛化性。

Abstract: Contact-rich insertion requires robots to combine visual alignment with tactile adjustment. However, existing visuotactile chunk-based policies collapse tactile feedback into a single generation-time fusion pathway, leaving persistent contact states, abrupt contact events, and future contact consequences implicitly modeled in a single representation. We present PACE (Phase-Aware Contact-Event Conditioning), a temporal-role tactile conditioning framework for chunk-based insertion policies. PACE organizes a shared tactile stream into three temporal roles: persistent contact states condition action-chunk generation, abrupt contact events correct the generated chunk, and contact-consequence prediction shapes action representations during training. This design preserves chunk-level action continuity while assigning distinct pathways to different temporal roles of contact feedback. Across five real-robot insertion tasks, PACE achieves an average success rate of 90.7%, outperforming vision-only and generation-time tactile-fusion baselines by over 44 percentage points; on the ManipulationNet benchmark, PACE maintains 88.7% success across six geometry-clearance configurations, including an industrial-grade 0.1-mm clearance. Ablations confirm the complementary contributions of the three temporal roles, mechanistic analyses show that PACE separates normal insertion progress from contact events requiring immediate correction, and cross-signal and cross-architecture experiments indicate that this temporal-role principle extends across different physical feedback signals and policy backbones. Videos and materials are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [165] [Same Goal, Different Words: Spatial Instruction Sensitivity in World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16242)
*Ali-Adeeb Abbas,Esmaeil Seraj,Behrad Toghi,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究评估世界-动作模型在等价空间目标描述下保持操作意图的一致性，发现指令表述方式会显著影响任务成功率，且聚合指标会掩盖模型对措辞和空间关系的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型应在相同空间目标的不同等价描述下保持一致的操纵行为；现有评估可能未充分检验其可引导性、语言理解与视觉 grounding，导致对模型能力的判断不完整。

Method: 固定初始状态和任务目标，在四个 RoboLab 场景中，对三个模型共 864 次 rollout 进行评估；比较通过参考物体而非待移动物体描述空间关系的指令变体，并加入独立的语言理解测试与 rollout 分析。

Result: 用参考物体描述关系比用待移动物体描述平均降低稳定放置率 12.2 个百分点；指令改写的影响随空间关系变化，聚合分数会隐藏措辞敏感性。独立语言理解测试发现关系反转，且加入图像会破坏原本正确的纯文本解释。Rollout 分析还区分了空间放置与指令遵循：移动错误物体也可能满足所要求关系；稳定放置率从任意时刻的 43.2% 降至回合结束时的 21.8%。

Conclusion: 这些发现要求更严格地评估世界-动作模型的可引导性、语言理解和 grounding：应测试等价指令间的一致性，验证目标物体是否被移动，并检查所请求的空间安排是否持续到 rollout 结束。

Abstract: World-action models should preserve the intended manipulation across equivalent descriptions of a spatial goal. We evaluate this consistency in 864 rollouts from three models across four RoboLab scenes, holding initial states and task goals fixed. Describing relations through the reference object rather than the object-to-move lowers the rate of reaching a stable placement by 12.2 percentage points on average. The effect of instruction rephrasing varies across spatial relations, so aggregate scores can hide sensitivity to wording. Standalone tests of the policies' language understanding identify relation reversals and show that adding images can disrupt otherwise correct text-only interpretations. Rollout analysis also distinguishes spatial placement from instruction following: moving the wrong object can satisfy the requested relation, and rates of stable placement fall from 43.2% at any time to 21.8% at episode end. These findings motivate a more thorough evaluation of world-action models' steerability, language understanding, and grounding. Evaluations should test consistency across equivalent instructions, verify that the intended object moved, and check whether the requested arrangement persists to the end of the rollout.

</details>


### [166] [Embodied Skill Bench: Benchmarking How Well Agents with Embodied Skills Perform on Physical Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16238)
*Zhi Han,Zihan Guo,Chenxi Zeng,Zicai Cui,Yue Xin,Ming Zhou,Dongzhan Zhou,Weinan Zhang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出具身技能（embodied skills）与 EmbodiedSkillBench 基准，在 100 个扰动 LIBERO-Pro 任务上评测发现：技能只补足智能体尚不具备的能力；随智能体变强，技能作用从提供能力转为降低成本，而 Agent-Directed Reuse（ADR）可在接近原生性能下减少 token 与耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略、感知模型与过程性知识等模块因接口、具身假设和使用条件各异而难以复用，且这些模块在物理任务上的实际收益尚不明确。

Method: 将上述模块封装为具身技能，为智能体技能补充显式具身假设与使用条件，基于 1,052 个资源构建 250 个动作、感知与经验技能；提出 EmbodiedSkillBench，在闭环观测-动作循环中运行智能体并记录成功率、动作数、token 数与真实耗时（wall-clock），支持原生控制、纯技能执行与 ADR 三种模式；ADR 中智能体保留原生控制，对每个技能逐一决定调用、修改或忽略。

Result: 在 100 个扰动 LIBERO-Pro 任务上评测四个智能体：技能增益取决于其超出智能体原有能力的程度。纯技能执行使 GPT-5.6-Sol 从 11% 升至 63%，使 Kimi-K3 与 Claude-Opus-5 各提升约 20 个百分点，并将 token 用量降低 58%–85%；但四者随后均落在 61%–64% 区间，GPT-6-Astra 从原生控制的 92% 降至 61%。ADR 下 GPT-6-Astra 回升至 85%，距原生控制 7 个百分点以内，且 token 少用 14.1%、耗时减少 11.9%；以 Jev 作为负责运动方向与夹爪动作的技能时达 81%，token 减少 50%、耗时减少 57%。

Conclusion: 随着智能体能力增强，具身技能的角色由“补足缺失能力”转向“降低执行成本”：强智能体（如 GPT-6-Astra）原生控制已能解决多数任务，强制只用技能反而得不偿失；ADR 式选择性复用可在性能接近原生的情况下显著降低 token 与时间开销。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly control robots using VLA policies, perception models, and procedural knowledge. These modules are difficult to reuse because their interfaces, embodiment assumptions, and conditions of use differ, and their benefits on physical tasks remain unclear. We package them as embodied skills that extend agent skills with explicit embodiment assumptions and conditions of use, building 250 action, perception, and experience skills from 1,052 resources. On this basis we introduce EmbodiedSkillBench, which runs an agent in a closed observation-action loop and records success, actions, tokens, and wall-clock time. It supports three modes: native control, skill-only execution, and Agent-Directed Reuse (ADR), a mode we propose in which the agent keeps its native controls and decides for each skill whether to invoke, modify, or ignore it. Across four agents on 100 perturbed LIBERO-Pro tasks, we find that skills help an agent only as far as they exceed what it can already do. Skill-only execution lifts GPT-5.6-Sol from 11% to 63% and Kimi-K3 and Claude-Opus-5 by about 20 points, while cutting their token usage by 58% to 85%. Yet all four agents then end up between 61% and 64%, and GPT-6-Astra falls from 92% under native control to 61%. This suggests that as agents grow stronger, the role of embodied skills shifts from supplying missing capability to reducing cost: GPT-6-Astra already solves most tasks through native control, so restricting it to skills removes more than it adds. Under ADR, which lets it keep native control and call skills selectively, GPT-6-Astra recovers to 85%, within 7 points of native control, while using 14.1% fewer tokens and 11.9% less time. With Jev as a skill choosing motion directions and gripper actions, it reaches 81% with 50% fewer tokens and 57% less time. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [167] [MSC: Multi-Stage Caster-aware Control Framework with Uncertainty-Aware NMPC and RL-based Execution Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16199)
*Jinho Park,Jeongtae Lee,Jeonghyeok Lim,Keun Ha Choi,Kyung-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多阶段脚轮感知控制（MSC），通过不确定性感知NMPC与强化学习执行层耦合，提升工业AMR/SDV在低速或大曲率机动下的脚轮状态估计、未对齐抑制与轨迹跟踪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由旋转脚轮因机械简单、承载能力强而广泛用于工业AMR/SDV，但低速或高曲率机动时脚轮未对齐会引入强接触非线性，扩大指令速度$(v, \omega)$与实际运动之间的规划-执行差距。现有脚轮感知策略高度依赖状态估计可靠性，在不确定过渡工况下易导致执行不稳定。

Method: MSC是一种规划-执行一体化分层框架，包含两个核心机制：其一，不确定性感知NMPC（CU-MPC）利用预测不确定性动态调度脚轮感知代价与输入正则化，抑制过于激进的机动；其二，基于强化学习的执行层在部分可观测条件下估计并预测脚轮状态，将高层速度指令转化为适应当前接触条件的可执行轮速。

Result: 在Isaac Sim和真实机器人上验证表明，MSC相较现有解耦补偿方法具有明显比较优势：显著提高脚轮状态估计精度，减少脚轮未对齐，并增强脚轮敏感机动下的跟踪鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MSC通过规划层与执行层的耦合，以及不确定性感知与部分可观测下的脚轮状态预测，有效缩小规划-执行差距，提升自由脚轮移动机器人在挑战工况下的执行稳定性与跟踪鲁棒性。

Abstract: Freely swiveling casters are widely adopted in industrial AMRs/SDVs for their mechanical simplicity and load capacity. However, caster misalignment during low-speed or high-curvature maneuvers induces strong contact nonlinearities, widening the planning-execution gap between commanded $(v, \omega)$ and realized motion. Existing caster-aware strategies are highly sensitive to state-estimation reliability, causing unstable execution in uncertain transition regimes. To address this, we present Multi-Stage Caster-aware Control (MSC), a planning-execution integrated hierarchical framework featuring two core mechanisms. First, an uncertainty-aware NMPC (CU-MPC) dynamically schedules caster-aware costs and input regularization using predictive uncertainty to suppress overly aggressive maneuvers. Second, a reinforcement learning-based execution layer estimates and predicts caster states under partial observability, transforming high-level velocity commands into physically executable wheel speeds adapted to current contact conditions. Validations in Isaac Sim and real robots demonstrate that MSC offers a clear comparative advantage over existing decoupled compensation approaches by significantly improving caster-state estimation accuracy, minimizing misalignment, and enhancing tracking robustness in caster-sensitive maneuvers.

</details>


### [168] [One-Shot Learning for Double-Shot Latte Art: Adapting Cobot Pouring Skills with Dynamic Movement Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16179)
*Zain Khan,Victor Hernandez Moreno,Dinh Tung Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于分阶段笛卡尔动态运动基元（CDMP）的单次演示学习系统，用于复现拉花动作，并在精度、泛化到不同杯量方面优于单CDMP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉花需要高灵巧性与精确性，是具有商业价值的复杂操作任务，因此值得研究如何通过单次演示实现可泛化的机器人学习与复现。

Method: 将一次咖啡师演示分割为五个功能不同的阶段，每个阶段用独立的笛卡尔动态运动基元（CDMP）编码并拥有各自参数；以单CDMP作为基线进行对比，并通过单一空间偏移与缩放配方实现跨杯量适配。

Result: 相比单CDMP基线，分阶段编码将过渡端点误差降低3.2–4.5倍，将拉花笔画形状误差降低2.7倍，在倾倒阶段复现的拉花壶倾斜角RMS低于1度；学习到的倾倒动作可从280mL杯泛化到400mL杯，且无需重新演示。

Conclusion: 基于阶段的CDMP方法不仅适用于拉花，还可推广到任何其阶段具有不同空间或时间要求的演示任务，并能够调整复现动作以适应不同任务需求。

Abstract: Latte art is a complex task requiring dexterity and precision, making it a challenging and commercially relevant task. A one-shot learning system is employed in which a single barista demonstration is segmented into five functionally distinct phases, each encoded as an independent Cartesian Dynamic Movement Primitive (CDMP) with its own parameters. Against a single-CDMP baseline, per-phase encoding reduces transition endpoint error by 3.2-4.5 times and art-stroke shape error by 2.7 times, reproducing jug tilt to below 1 degree RMS in the pouring phases. The learnt pour scales from a 280mL to a 400mL cup through a single spatial offset and a scaled recipe, with no re-demonstration. The phase-based CDMP approach generalises beyond latte art to any demonstration task whose phases have distinct spatial or temporal requirements whilst demonstrating the ability to adapt reproductions to suit different task requirements.

</details>


### [169] [Extending Wire-Driven Parallel Robots to Multi-Floating-Link Systems with an Inter-Body Wire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16173)
*Shintaro Inoue,Kento Kawaharazuka,Temma Suzuki,Sota Yuzaki,Keita Yoneda,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出引入体间线缆实现多浮动连杆并联线驱动，并通过双体机器人完成假人救援搬运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统并联线驱动系统通常假设单一末端执行器，在共享工作空间中因线缆与连杆干涉，难以同时操控多个浮动连杆；研究希望将其扩展到多体系统，以支持大物体操作与救援等任务。

Method: 引入体间线缆（inter-body wire），构建多浮动连杆并联线驱动系统；进行实机实验，由两个机体支撑并抬起假人，并将其转移到担架上。

Result: 实机实验中，两个机体协作支撑并抬起假人，完成将其转移到担架上的救援动作。

Conclusion: 表明并联线驱动可从单末端执行器扩展到多浮动连杆系统，并显示其在大物体操作和救援支持等更广泛任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Parallel wire-driven systems are a framework in which multiple wires are connected to an end effector, and the end effector is driven by the tensions of those wires. Because they can transmit large forces, up to the breaking strength of the wires, over lengths on the order of several tens of meters, they can realize powerful actuation in large workspaces. In recent years, studies have also investigated connecting wires to external environments such as trees and columns and forming parallel wire actuation on site. However, conventional parallel wire-driven systems often assume a single end effector, and because of interference between wires and links in a shared workspace, it has been difficult to handle multiple floating links simultaneously. In this study, we realize multi-floating-link parallel wire actuation by introducing an inter-body wire. In real-robot experiments, the two bodies supported and lifted a mannequin and transferred it onto a stretcher as a rescue motion. This shows that parallel wire actuation can be extended from a single end effector to a multi-floating-link system, and indicates its potential for broader tasks such as the manipulation of large objects and rescue support.

</details>


### [170] [FlowVLA: Enabling World Dynamics Understanding through Pluggable 3D Flow Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16160)
*Junhyeong Go,Mingyu Jung,Woobin Im,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowVLA 是一个重建引导、可插拔的 3D 流学习框架，旨在让 VLA 模型在训练阶段获得与动作相关的 3D 动态理解，同时不增加推理时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLA 模型主要依赖 2D 图像-文本先验，无法准确表征物理动作对应的 3D 世界基本动态，过度依赖静态 2D 视觉线索限制了机器人操作的鲁棒性。

Method: FlowVLA 仅在训练时利用时间对齐的 3D 流，不要求显式 3D 传感器输入，也不改变推理架构；提出模块化 3D 流学习框架与双层级流监督：点级流预测任务关键 3D 点的未来位移，TCP 级流聚合这些运动以对齐机器人 TCP 轨迹，从而耦合场景级运动与精确控制信号。训练时仅添加轻量辅助头，推理时丢弃。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，FlowVLA 持续提升操作性能、零样本鲁棒性，并优化数据效率；不引入额外 3D 传感器输入、架构修改或推理时计算。

Conclusion: 通过训练时 3D 流学习，FlowVLA 能以可插拔方式赋予 VLA 模型内在的动作动力学理解，在保持常规 2D VLA 骨干计算效率的同时，提高机器人操作表现与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D reconstruction enable the extraction of temporally consistent 3D motion from RGB observations; however, current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models still predominantly rely on 2D image-text priors. Therefore, they do not accurately represent the fundamental dynamics of the 3D world associated with physical actions. This over-reliance on static 2D visual cues limits the capacity of VLA models in achieving robust robot manipulation. To address these limitations, we introduce FlowVLA, a reconstruction-guided, pluggable 3D flow learning framework aimed at empowering VLA models with an inherent understanding of action-relevant dynamics. Instead of requiring explicit 3D sensor inputs or altering the inference-time architecture, our method is designed to utilize temporally aligned 3D flow only during training. This allows the model to learn the dynamic evolution of the physical scene while preserving the computational efficiency of conventional 2D VLA backbones. To bridge scene-level dynamics with robotic manipulation, we propose a modular 3D flow learning framework with dual-level flow supervision. Specifically, point-level flow guides the model to forecast the future displacements of task-critical 3D points, while TCP-level flow aggregates these motions to align with the robot's TCP trajectories. This dual-level flow supervision encourages the model to focus on semantically meaningful dynamic regions, effectively coupling scene-level motion with precise control signals. Extensive evaluations across simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that FlowVLA consistently improves manipulation performance, zero-shot robustness, and optimizes data efficiency. FlowVLA adds only lightweight auxiliary heads during training and discards them at inference. Therefore, FlowVLA introduces no additional 3D sensor inputs, architectural modifications, or inference-time computation.

</details>


### [171] [Directional Pullback Regularization for Long-Horizon Rollout of Latent World Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16139)
*Shatadal Mishra,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Nejib Ammar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出方向性回拉正则化（DPR），在解码状态空间定义运动学感知的各向异性黎曼度量，并经解码器雅可比回拉到潜空间，以改善自动驾驶潜空间长时程rollout；在多个训练步长下优于无约束和欧氏回拉基线，3步监督下10秒终点位移误差降低约44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶世界模型通常在无约束潜空间学习，但车辆受非完整约束，不能瞬时纯侧向运动；作者探究是否可通过给潜空间转移赋予运动感知几何，而非重新设计预测器架构，来提升长时程rollout质量。

Method: 以无约束潜空间rollout为基线，提出DPR：在解码状态空间构造运动学感知各向异性黎曼度量，经解码器雅可比拉回至潜流形，使解码为侧向运动的潜方向代价更高，同时保留编码器-动力学-解码器主干；对比ULR、EPR消融和完整DPR。

Result: 在10秒开环自车运动rollout上，训练rollout长度为1、3、5、10步时，DPR的10秒终点位移误差均低于ULR和EPR；3步监督下相对ULR降低约44%；10步等长时程训练下，DPR同时改善位移误差、侧向运动一致性和前向运动保持。

Conclusion: 通过回拉式运动学感知各向异性几何约束潜空间转移，可有效提升自动驾驶长时程latent rollout的精度与运动一致性/控制相关折中，且无需修改预测器架构。

Abstract: Learned world models for autonomous driving operate in unconstrained latent spaces, yet the physical systems they represent are governed by strict non-holonomic constraints: ground vehicles cannot realize instantaneous pure lateral motion. We study long-horizon latent rollout modeling for autonomous driving and ask whether rollout quality can be improved by assigning a motion-aware geometry to latent transitions, rather than by redesigning the predictor architecture. Starting from an unconstrained latent rollout baseline, we introduce Directional Pullback Regularization (DPR), a training time method that defines a kinematics-aware anisotropic Riemannian metric in decoded state space and pulls it back through the decoder Jacobian to the latent manifold. The resulting latent-space metric assigns higher cost to latent directions that decode to lateral vehicle motion, while preserving the original encoder-dynamics-decoder backbone. This yields a clean three-way comparison among an Unconstrained Latent Rollout model (ULR), an Euclidean Pullback Regularization ablation (EPR), and the full directional method (DPR). We evaluate all models on open-loop ego-motion rollout over a common 10-second test horizon, using training rollout lengths of 1, 3, 5, and 10 steps. Across all four training regimes, DPR achieves lower 10-second final displacement error than both ULR and EPR. The strongest relative accuracy gain appears for models trained with 3-step rollout supervision, where DPR reduces the 10-second final displacement error by approximately 44 percent relative to ULR. The strongest overall control-relevant tradeoff is obtained for longer rollout-trained models, especially at 10 steps, where DPR simultaneously improves displacement error, lateral motion consistency, and forward motion preservation relative to both ULR and EPR.

</details>


### [172] [Constrained Differentiable Policy Learning for Safe Quadrotor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16119)
*Yan Rui Tan,Praveen Krishnapur,Zeyu Feng,Han Bao,Yew-Soon Ong,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将有障碍物避障建模为显式约束，通过可微仿真与原始-对偶梯度下降-上升训练四旋翼策略，并提出有界间隙约束函数与log-mean-exp聚合，实现无需手工调参的安全-性能权衡，并证明收敛到可行策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微仿真虽能利用解析梯度高效优化策略，但安全通常以加权惩罚加入目标，需人工调节任务性能与约束满足的折中，难以直接指定安全水平。

Method: 将避障作为显式约束，在可微仿真器中用原始-对偶梯度下降-上升优化四旋翼策略；提出基于有界间隙（bounded clearance）的约束函数，在安全边界附近提供有效梯度并抑制大违规对原始-对偶优化的破坏；采用log-mean-exp聚合轨迹与训练批次中的每步安全违规，以单一约束和单一对偶变量平滑强调关键违规；理论证明该优化在温和假设下收敛到可行策略。

Result: 实验表明，相较于固定惩罚和现有策略学习基线，方法取得更好的安全-性能折中，并能泛化到更密集的分布外环境；学习到的策略无需微调即可零样本迁移到Gazebo/PX4和物理四旋翼。

Conclusion: 通过显式约束、有界间隙约束函数和LME聚合，可在可微仿真中有效进行约束策略优化，直接指定安全水平并兼顾任务完成，且具备仿真到现实的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation enables efficient policy optimization through analytic gradients, yet safety is commonly incorporated as a weighted penalty, requiring manual tuning of the trade-off between task performance and constraint satisfaction. We instead formulate obstacle avoidance as an explicit constraint and train quadrotor policies using primal--dual gradient descent--ascent through a differentiable simulator. We introduce two key components to enable effective constrained training. First, we propose a bounded clearance-based constraint function that provides informative gradients around the safety boundary while preventing large violations from destabilizing primal--dual optimization. Second, we introduce log-mean-exp (LME) aggregation to smoothly emphasize critical per-step safety violations across a trajectory and training batch within a single constraint governed by one dual variable. Together, these components enable the desired safety level to be specified directly while balancing constraint satisfaction and task completion. We theoretically prove that the proposed optimization with differentiable simulation converges to feasible policy under mild assumptions. Experiments demonstrate improved safety--performance trade-offs over fixed-penalty and existing policy-learning baselines, as well as generalization to denser out-of-distribution environments. Finally, the learned policy transfers zero-shot to Gazebo/PX4 and a physical quadrotor without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [173] [PathLift: Depth-Conditioned Metric Lifting for Visual-Navigation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16103)
*Gwanjun Shin,Hyeongseok Suh,Hongmo Jung,Jongwoo Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PathLift 是一种逐路点、深度条件适配器，可将预训练视觉导航策略输出的无量纲轨迹提升为带度量的、深度可行的路径；它在仿真和真实机器人上均一致提升闭环导航成功率，且无需测试时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉导航策略通常只告诉机器人往哪个方向走，而不给距离；部署时需额外提升步骤将其无量纲轨迹转为控制器可跟踪的度量路径。现有标量提升方法采用统一缩放，忽略逐路点修正；而将单目深度注入策略的方法会继承深度模型在障碍物附近和近距离处的预测误差。

Method: PathLift 作为逐路点、深度条件适配器，消费显式且传感器级深度源（RGB-D、立体视觉或投影 LiDAR），在规范化先验上拟合每个路点的幅值与横向残差，并通过单次前馈将策略的无量纲意图转换为深度可行的度量路径，无需测试时优化。

Result: 在仿真和真实轮式机器人上，使用立体视觉与 LiDAR 深度时，PathLift 在多种视觉导航策略上均一致提升闭环导航性能，并提升真实世界任务成功率。

Conclusion: 通过显式深度条件进行逐路点提升，PathLift 避免了统一缩放和单目深度误差传播，可将策略的无量纲意图直接转为深度可行度量路径，从而无需测试时优化地改善真实部署表现。

Abstract: Generalist visual-navigation policies tell a robot which direction to go but not how far. A separate lifting step is needed at deployment to convert their unitless trajectory into a metric path that a controller can track; existing scalar lifters apply uniform scaling that misses per-waypoint corrections, while in-policy approaches that inject monocular depth into the policy inherit the prediction errors of the depth model near obstacles and at close range. We present PathLift, a per-waypoint depth-conditioned adapter that lifts a pre-trained visual-navigation policy by consuming an explicit, sensor-grade depth source (RGB-D, stereo, or projected LiDAR) and fitting per-waypoint magnitude and lateral residuals to a canonicalized prior. PathLift consistently improves closed-loop navigation across diverse visual-navigation policies in simulation and on a real wheeled robot under both stereo and LiDAR depth. By turning the policy's unitless intent into a depth-feasible metric path in a single feed-forward pass, the adapter lifts real-world success without test-time optimization.

</details>


### [174] [A Hardware-Partitioned micro-ROS Mini-Vehicle for Operator-Supervised Neural Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15966)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种低成本微型阿克曼车辆，用于嵌入式神经驾驶研究，采用硬件分区的“感知-决策-执行”架构，在真实闭环神经驾驶中验证了其延迟、鲁棒性与定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为嵌入式 AI、无线控制和操作员监督的自动驾驶研究提供一个可复现、低成本且兼顾实时安全控制的微型车辆平台。

Method: 车辆被组织为硬件分区智能体：ESP32 摄像头节点通过 Wi-Fi 向主机电脑传输帧，主机运行神经网络推理与状态估计；ESP32-S3 底盘控制器在 micro-ROS 客户端下以 100 Hz 每轮 PID 执行安全关键执行；学习组件离车运行，并由操作员通过手柄监督。研究者在真实闭环神经驾驶中表征完整流水线。

Result: 无线传感而非神经推理是延迟主导因素，捕获到执行的中位延迟为 59.5 ms；平台通过固件看门狗在无线波动时保持最后安全指令，完成 12 次自主会话且零碰撞、零人工干预；编码器-IMU 融合使里程计闭环误差降低 22%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，经过精心分区的微控制器级车辆能够支持嵌入式 AI、无线控制与操作员监督自动驾驶的可复现实验；平台软件已开源。

Abstract: We present a low-cost miniature Ackermann vehicle for embedded neural-driving research, organized as a hardware-partitioned perceive-decide-act agent: an ESP32 camera node streams frames over Wi-Fi to a host computer, where neural inference and state estimation run, while an ESP32-S3 chassis controller executes safety-critical actuation in a 100 Hz per-wheel proportional-integral-derivative (PID) loop beneath a micro-ROS client. The partition isolates real-time control and safety on the vehicle while learning-based components run off-board, supervised by an operator from a gamepad. We characterize the complete pipeline during real closed-loop neural driving. Wireless sensing, rather than neural inference, dominates latency, with a 59.5 ms median capture-to-actuation delay; the platform absorbed wireless variability by holding the last safe command under a firmware watchdog, operating across twelve autonomous sessions with zero collisions or manual interventions; and encoder-inertial-measurement-unit (IMU) fusion cut odometry loop-closure error by 22%. These results indicate that carefully partitioned microcontroller-class vehicles support reproducible experiments in embedded AI, wireless control, and operator-supervised autonomous driving. The platform software is open source.

</details>


### [175] [Jacobian State Augmented Viable Kinematic Safety Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15936)
*Siddharth Singh,Joshua Vaughan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于雅可比的运动学安全滤波器，引入任务空间速度增广状态并过滤任务空间加速度，通过嵌套二次规划与保守可行性包络内近似，在保证关节位置和速度约束的同时维持底层滤波器可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测安全滤波（PSF）能改善约束满足并减少短视单步滤波导致的可行性损失；基于生存性/viability的方法虽可保证未来所有时刻存在可容许控制，但计算开销显著。因此需要兼顾安全性与计算效率的安全滤波方法。

Method: 设计Jacobian-based kinematic safety filter，使用含任务空间速度的增广状态，并对任务空间加速度进行滤波；提出嵌套二次规划（QP）形式，定义viability envelope的保守内近似，并在线计算可行规划器，以保持低层滤波器可行性并执行关节位置与速度约束。

Result: 在简化2R平面机械臂仿真中验证，相比基线CBF-QP滤波器显著提升；相比传统PSF和Reference Governor方法，计算时间改善且可行性违反显著减少；在仿真6-DoF机械臂上的简化演示表明方法对更高自由度系统具有可扩展潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法在运动学层面提供了一种计算高效的安全滤波方案，可减少可行性违反，并具有向高自由度机械臂扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Safety Filters (SF) are leveraged to design certifiably safe controllers, which ensures forward invariance on a defined safe set when the filter is feasible. Predictive Safety Filters (PSF) embed these constraints to a closed-loop model-based optimization method to improve constraint satisfaction, and reduce feasibility loss caused by myopic, one-step filtering. Related viability-based methods emphasize approximating a viability kernel which ensures admissible control for all future time, but requires significant computational expense. This work introduces a Jacobian-based kinematic safety filter leveraging an augmented state variable with task-space velocity and filters task-space acceleration to preserve the feasibility of a low-level filter while enforcing joint position and velocity. The proposed method introduces a nested Quadratic Program (QP) formulation, defines a conservative inner approximation of viability envelope and computes a viable planner online. The method is validated in a simplified 2R planar manipulator simulation environment and shows significant improvement compared to a baseline Control Barrier Function Quadratic Program (CBF-QP) based filter. The experiments highlights the improvements in terms of computation time compared to traditional PSF and Reference Governor approach while also showing significant reduction in feasibility violations. Lastly, a simplified demonstration on a simulated 6-DoF manipulator highlights the potential scalability of the method to higher degree-of-freedom setups.

</details>


### [176] [Efficient Hybrid Hard--Soft Signal Temporal Logic MPC for Driving under Multimodal Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15934)
*Kumar Manas,Adrian Paschke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合硬-软STL MPC框架，用于交互交通中自动驾驶运动规划：硬约束保证安全与交规，软STL鲁棒项优化间隙、舒适与交互质量，并通过可达性认证筛选和向量化符号构建降低计算成本。六场景闭环蒙特卡洛显示最高严格成功率、无碰撞与规则违反，规划延迟约为未优化版的四分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互交通中，自动车辆规划需同时确保安全、遵守交规并在多模态行为不确定性下保持舒适通行。若将所有要求作为硬约束会降低可行性，而全部作为代价又可能以牺牲安全关键要求换取通行效率。因此需要兼顾硬安全保证与软性能优化的规划方法。

Method: 提出混合硬-软信号时序逻辑（STL）模型预测控制（MPC）框架：针对每个保留的行为假设优化单一共享自车轨迹，而非分支特定的应急策略；将最小距离与速度谓词作为显式硬约束，用软STL鲁棒性项在可行驾驶走廊内塑造间隙、舒适性和交互质量。进一步识别符号图构建开销，引入可达性认证的相关性筛选与向量化符号构建，以降低计算成本，同时保持可行集和目标不变。

Result: 在六个高速公路与交叉口场景的闭环蒙特卡洛评估中，该方法在所比较规划器中取得最高严格成功率，未观察到碰撞或规则违反，规划延迟约为未优化公式的四分之一。消融研究表明两层作用分离：硬约束定义安全包络，软项在包络内改善间隙与运动质量。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在多模态交互场景中同时提供安全与交规的硬保证，并通过软STL项优化交互质量和运动舒适性；结合可达性筛选与向量化符号构建，可显著降低符号MPC计算开销，具有较好的实时性与安全性。

Abstract: Motion planning for automated vehicles in interactive traffic has to simultaneously ensure safety, respect traffic rules, and maintain comfortable progress under multi-modal behavior uncertainty. Imposing all these requirements as hard constraints reduces feasibility, whereas treating them all as costs allows safety-critical requirements to be traded against progress. We propose a hybrid hard--soft Signal Temporal Logic (STL) MPC framework that optimizes a single shared ego trajectory against every retained behavior hypothesis: minimum-distance and speed predicates are imposed as explicit hard constraints, while soft STL robustness terms shape clearance, comfort, and interaction quality within the resulting feasible driving corridor. This retains robustness to discrete interaction modes without branch-specific contingent ego policies. We further identify substantial symbolic graph-construction overhead and introduce reachability-certified relevance screening and vectorized symbolic construction to reduce computational cost while preserving the feasible set and objective. Closed-loop Monte Carlo evaluation over six highway and intersection scenarios shows the highest strict success rate among the compared planners with no observed collisions or rule violations, at roughly a quarter of the planning latency of the unoptimized formulation. An ablation study separates the two layers: the hard constraints define the safety envelope, while the soft terms improve clearance and motion quality within it.

</details>


### [177] [REFLEX: A Learned On-Board Reflex for Robust Autonomous Collision Recovery of Soft Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15867)
*Jakob Hermeler,Luca Girardi,Gabriel Maquignaz,Stefan Leutenegger,Stefano Mintchev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出REFLEX碰撞恢复飞行模式，完全运行于飞控上，无需接触传感器或伴飞计算机；通过本体感受循环策略在无位置、速度、航向和高度估计下稳定飞行器，并在恢复后重新初始化平移估计与可信门控后交还控制权，实现高速撞墙后的自主恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耐撞机架只能让四旋翼承受碰撞，无法保证碰撞后的自主恢复；高速撞击后，机载估计器中的创新门控常会拒绝有效外部速度测量，导致恢复失败。

Method: REFLEX不依赖接触传感器或伴飞计算机，仅使用飞控本体感受信息；采用循环策略在无位置、速度、航向和高度估计时稳定飞行器。激活期间重新初始化平移估计，并通过可信门控检查估计器一致性后再将控制交还原厂自驾仪。策略在仿真中用PPO训练，训练回合由47次真实碰撞后的状态初始化。

Result: 硬件实验中，REFLEX在5.03 ± 0.09 m/s速度下对22次撞墙实现19次恢复，而匹配的原厂自驾仪仅14次中恢复1次；其中包括单次飞行中连续15次恢复。另一探索性飞行中，同一飞行内还实现了两次额外恢复，速度分别为6.02和6.05 m/s，将此前自主撞墙恢复速度提高64%。

Conclusion: REFLEX证明无需额外传感器或机载计算机，仅靠飞控端本体感受策略即可在高速碰撞后实现自主恢复，并在恢复后可靠地重新初始化估计并交还控制，显著优于原厂自驾仪。

Abstract: Collision-resilient airframes allow quadrotors to survive impacts, but do not ensure autonomous post-collision recovery. Innovation gating in stock onboard estimators often rejects valid external velocity measurements after high-speed impacts, compromising recovery. We present REFLEX, a collision-recovery flight mode that runs entirely on the flight controller, requiring neither contact sensors nor a companion computer. Its proprioceptive recurrent policy stabilizes the vehicle without position, velocity, heading, or altitude estimates. The translational estimate is reinitialized while REFLEX is active, and a trust gate checks estimator consistency before returning control to the stock autopilot. The policy is trained in simulation using proximal policy optimization, with episodes initialized from post-impact states measured in 47 real collisions. In hardware experiments, REFLEX recovered 19 of 22 wall impacts at 5.03 $\pm$ 0.09 m/s, compared with 1 of 14 for the matched stock autopilot. These included 15 consecutive recoveries in a single flight. A separate exploratory flight demonstrated two additional recoveries at 6.02 and 6.05 m/s within the same flight, exceeding the previously reported velocity for autonomous wall-collision recovery by 64%.

</details>


### [178] [P-BFM: Perceptive Behavioral Foundation Models for Humanoid Control via Unsupervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15833)
*Xue Wang,Yitang Li,Yiming Zhang,Siqin Wang,Junhan Sun,Zhihao Liu,Yaojie Shen,Jingbo Yuan,Hao Zhao,Qingdu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 P-BFM：一种感知式行为基础模型，将人形机器人控制分解为地形无关的意图与地形条件化的实现，并引入显式根指令 ERI 分离全局移动与行为意图，从而在无需地形特定演示的情况下实现同一意图在不同地形上的适配行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界人形控制中，同一行为意图在不同地形约束下需要根本不同的动作；用与地形匹配的演示覆盖这种变化，需要枚举行为与地形几何的组合空间，代价极高。同时，纯潜空间控制虽能保留局部运动模式，却可能丢失精确的全局行进信息。

Method: P-BFM 将控制分解为地形不变意图和地形条件化实现：用前向-后向表示将运动参考、目标状态和奖励函数映射到共享意图空间，且不依赖地形信息；感知策略学习在周围地形几何下实现各意图。进一步提出显式根指令 ERI，将全局运输（如朝向与平面速度）从行为意图中分离，局部全身适应仍由感知策略完成。

Result: 在多种挑战性地形上，P-BFM 在运动保真度、根控制精度和扰动恢复方面优于强基线。单个冻结策略支持运动跟踪、目标到达、奖励推断、遥操作和地形感知恢复，并能零样本迁移到 Unitree G1，使用机载深度感知。

Conclusion: P-BFM 表明，将意图与地形条件化实现解耦，并结合显式根指令，可使一个固定意图产生多种地形适宜行为，如步高、步幅、姿态、落脚点和接触变化，且无需地形特定演示；这为可泛化、可复用的真实世界人形行为基础模型提供了路径。

Abstract: Behavioral foundation models (BFMs) provide humanoids with reusable repertoires of whole-body behaviors; in real-world humanoid control, the same behavioral intent may require fundamentally different motions under different terrain constraints. Covering such variation with terrain-matched demonstrations requires enumerating a combinatorial space of behaviors and terrain geometries. We introduce P-BFM, a perceptive behavioral foundation model that factorizes humanoid control into terrain-invariant intent and terrain-conditioned realization. A forward-backward representation maps motion references, target states, and reward functions into a shared intent space without terrain information, while a perceptive policy learns how to realize each intent under the surrounding terrain geometry. We further identify a complementary limitation of purely latent control: behavioral latents can preserve local motion patterns while losing precise global progress. We therefore introduce Explicit Root Instructions (ERI), which separates global transport (e.g., heading and planar velocity) from behavioral intent, while leaving local whole-body adaptation to the perceptive policy. This decomposition leads to a characteristic capability absent from conventional BFMs: one fixed intent induces different, terrain-appropriate behaviors, including changes in step height, stride, posture, foothold placement, and contacts, without terrain-specific demonstrations. Across challenging terrains, P-BFM improves motion fidelity, root-control precision, and perturbation recovery over strong baselines. A single frozen policy further supports motion tracking, goal reaching, reward inference, teleoperation, and terrain-aware recovery, and transfers zero-shot to a Unitree G1 using onboard depth sensing.

</details>


### [179] [ReTrace: Cross-Stage Failure Attribution for Self-Improving Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15814)
*Yue Yu,Bin Zhu,Junhui Li,Jiayu Wang,Shijie Zhou,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ReTrace，一种无需演示或参数更新的机器人操作自我改进框架，通过自主物理交互与回溯推理定位跨阶段失败根源并生成可复用技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态基础模型已能直接控制机器人，但在涉及跨阶段依赖的复杂操作中，局部成功的动作可能导致后续阶段失败；这类失败信号难以用于有效自我改进。

Method: ReTrace 在自主交互中回溯下游失败至早期决策，提出针对性纠正，并通过后续交互验证；跨多次尝试将验证有效的纠正逐步固化为可复用的操作技能，指导后续执行。无需演示数据和模型参数更新。

Result: 在 Franka Research 3 上评估四个真实操作任务。两个强跨阶段依赖任务中，GPT-6 Astra-low 插头插入成功率从 0% 提升至 80%，倒球从 0% 提升至 100%；GPT-6 Sol-xhigh 分别从 0% 提升至 10% 和从 0% 提升至 90%。学习到的技能还可跨模型与推理配置迁移，并在多数设置下降低推理时决策成本。

Conclusion: ReTrace 能通过自主交互和回溯推理实现机器人操作自我改进，在跨阶段依赖任务上显著提升成功率，并具备迁移性与推理效率优势。

Abstract: General-purpose multimodal foundation models are increasingly capable of directly controlling robots, yet improving their manipulation capabilities through autonomous interaction remains challenging. A key challenge to such self-improvement is learning from failures in complex manipulation tasks involving cross-stage dependencies, where an action may succeed locally but cause a later stage to fail. To address this challenge, we introduce ReTrace, a self-improvement framework for robotic manipulation through autonomous physical interaction and retrospective reasoning, without provided demonstrations or model-parameter updates. ReTrace retrospectively traces downstream failures to earlier decisions, proposes targeted corrections, and evaluates them through subsequent interactions. Across attempts, ReTrace progressively consolidates validated corrections into a reusable manipulation skill that guide subsequent execution. We evaluate ReTrace on four real-world manipulation tasks using a Franka Research 3 robot. On the two tasks with strong cross-stage dependencies, GPT-6 Astra-low improves from 0% to 80% success on plug insertion and from 0% to 100% on ball pouring, while GPT-6 Sol-xhigh improves from 0% to 10% and 0% to 90%, respectively. The learned skills also transfer across model and reasoning configurations and reduce inference-time decision cost in most evaluated settings.

</details>


### [180] [$Δ$ Flow Matching for Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15794)
*Kuangji Zuo,Tuo An,Xinying Guo,Gen Li,Mengfei Zhao,Jiaqi Bai,Bofan Lyu,Zhuoyuan Yu,Jinghan Yang,Jiayi Li,Jindou Jia,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Δ-flow：用近期历史加轻噪声构造生成源，替代固定高斯先验，将传输学习为小残差 Δ，实现高效训练、单步推理，并提升机器人基础模型的效率、泛化与动作平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 与世界动作模型 WAM 的 flow matching 均从固定高斯先验开始生成动作块或未来视觉状态，忽略过去与未来状态及动作之间的时序连续性；这导致训练成本高、采样需迭代，WAM 因额外视觉分支尤为昂贵。

Method: Δ-flow 以近期历史状态/动作作为源，并加入轻度噪声，使源更接近目标；模型只需学习从历史到未来的小残差 Δ，从而支持单步推理。方法集成到 VLA 策略 π0.5 及 WAM 策略 ImageWAM、Fast-WAM；支持 LoRA 微调；动作历史源可在不重训练的情况下用于预训练 WAM 策略，并在推理时替换高斯动作源；进一步研究动作表示与视觉信息对 Δ-flow 中历史到未来预测的影响。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，ImageWAM 1 个 epoch 达 90% 成功率，而基线需 6 个 epoch；Fast-WAM 2 个 epoch 达 95%，而基线需 8 个 epoch。LoRA 微调后，π0.5 1 个 epoch 超过基线 5 个 epoch 的性能。单步推理分别带来 1.54×、2.49×、4.45× 加速；相比一步高斯采样，动作 jerk 降低 4.5×。LIBERO-Plus 上达到 SOTA 泛化性能。推理时替换高斯动作源可基本保持甚至略微提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: Δ-flow 简单而有效，可提升机器人基础模型的训练与推理效率、泛化能力和动作平滑性，并能无需重训练地应用于预训练 WAM 策略。

Abstract: Flow matching is a common generative recipe for robot foundation models. Vision-language-action models (VLAs) generate action chunks, while world-action models (WAMs) additionally generate future visual states. Both start generation from a fixed Gaussian prior, despite temporal continuity between past and future states and actions. This requires costly training and iterative sampling, especially for WAMs with an additional visual branch. We introduce $\Delta$-flow, which constructs the source from recent history with mild noise rather than from a fixed Gaussian prior. This moves the source closer to the target, reducing transport to a small residual $\Delta$ that can be learned efficiently and enables single-step inference. We integrate $\Delta$-flow into the VLA policy $\pi_{0.5}$ and the WAM policies ImageWAM and Fast-WAM. On LIBERO, ImageWAM reaches 90% success in one epoch rather than six, and Fast-WAM reaches 95% in two epochs rather than eight. With LoRA fine-tuning, $\pi_{0.5}$ exceeds its baseline's five-epoch performance after one epoch. Single-step inference gives $1.54\times$, $2.49\times$, and $4.45\times$ speedups on $\pi_{0.5}$, ImageWAM, and Fast-WAM, respectively. It preserves action smoothness, achieving a $4.5\times$ reduction in action jerk compared with one-step Gaussian sampling. $\Delta$-flow reaches state-of-the-art generalization performance on LIBERO-Plus. Action-history sources can be applied to pretrained WAM policies without retraining. Replacing the Gaussian action source at inference largely preserves task success with a single sampling step and can slightly improve it. We further study how action representation and visual information in action learning affect history-to-future prediction in $\Delta$-flow. These results show that $\Delta$-flow is simple yet effective, improving the efficiency and generalization of robot foundation models.

</details>


### [181] [Hand2Robot: Object-Centric 4D Hand Reconstruction from Human Demonstrations for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15769)
*Zetao Du,Weiqing Wang,Hanbing Zhang,Yimiao Zhou,Ye Shi,Liang Wang,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Hand2Robot：一个以物体为中心的 4D 手部重建框架，从人类演示视频生成机器人动作监督，用于机器人策略后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示能捕获任务相关操作，但手部运动不能直接作为机器人动作监督；仅做手部重建与运动重定向不足以保证手-物交互的物理一致性。

Method: 给定人类执行目标任务的视频，Hand2Robot 提取交互片段、重建物体几何，并通过手-物交互优化与运动重定向生成机器人动作监督。物体跟踪方面，结合观测置信度与时间运动约束，从逐帧位姿候选中选择全局一致的物体位姿轨迹；手部精化方面，以物体几何和运动为参考，结合分阶段 2D 对齐与物体局部接触约束，精化手部轨迹和手指关节配置。

Result: 在 DexYCB 和 HO3D-v3 上，物体位姿精度与手-物交互质量均有提升；在三个真实机器人灵巧操作任务上，重建并重定向的人类演示能有效补充有限机器人数据，用于目标任务的策略后训练。

Conclusion: Hand2Robot 表明，通过物体中心的手-物交互优化与重定向，可从人类视频中获得物理一致且可用于机器人策略后训练的动作监督。

Abstract: Human demonstrations can capture task-relevant manipulation without using the target robot, but the recorded hand motions cannot directly serve as robot action supervision. Hand reconstruction and motion retargeting provide a direct route to such supervision, yet recovering hand motion alone does not ensure physically consistent hand-object interactions. To address this issue, we introduce Hand2Robot, an object-centric 4D hand reconstruction framework for robot policy post-training. Given videos of humans performing the target task, Hand2Robot extracts interaction clips, reconstructs object geometry, and generates robot action supervision through hand-object interaction optimization and motion retargeting. For object tracking, we combine observation confidence with temporal motion constraints to select a globally consistent object pose trajectory from per-frame pose candidates. For hand refinement, we use object geometry and motion as a reference and combine staged 2D alignment with object-local contact constraints to refine hand trajectories and finger joint configurations. Experiments on DexYCB and HO3D-v3 show improved object pose accuracy and hand-object interaction quality. Experiments on three real-robot dexterous manipulation tasks further show that reconstructed and retargeted human demonstrations can effectively supplement limited robot data for target-task policy post-training.

</details>


### [182] [SurgiCLAW: An Interpretable Atomic-Skill Agent for Precision Robotic Micromanipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15766)
*Angen Ye,junjie xie,Yujia Song,Junkai Huang,Dapeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SurgiCLAW 分层技能框架：将多模态大模型限制于术中视觉理解、结构化解释和原子手术技能选择，底层物理执行交由可验证的确定性控制程序；在脑机接口植入任务上，固定测试集技能选择准确率 94.6%、Macro-F1 93.0%，50 次闭环真机试验任务成功率 88.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期多模态大模型与视觉-语言-动作模型提升了机器人长时程任务理解与规划能力，但在手术机器人中，端到端低层动作预测仍受限于精度不足、决策不透明和数据稀缺。

Method: SurgiCLAW 采用分层技能式框架：MLLM 负责术中视觉理解、结构化解释与原子手术技能选择，确定性控制程序负责可验证的物理执行。针对脑机接口植入这一高精度任务，构建 state-to-skill invocation 数据集，并通过视觉和状态扰动扩增。模型先通过监督微调学习结构化技能调用，再利用动作正确性、操作前置条件和格式有效性三个奖励进行优化。评估包括子任务成功率、离线模型比较、真机性能和消融实验。

Result: 在固定测试集上，SurgiCLAW 达到 94.6% 技能选择准确率和 93.0% Macro-F1；在 50 次闭环真机植入试验中，任务成功率为 88.0%。

Conclusion: SurgiCLAW 能提升动作选择、操作安全性和输出可解析性，在精密手术操作中实现高精度且可验证的分层控制，并展现出真实机器人应用潜力。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and vision--language--action models have provided robots with new capabilities for long-horizon task understanding and planning. In surgical robotics, however, end-to-end prediction of low-level actions remains limited by insufficient precision, opaque decisions, and scarce data. This paper presents SurgiCLAW, a hierarchical skill-based framework for precision surgical manipulation. SurgiCLAW confines an MLLM to intraoperative visual understanding, structured explanation, and atomic surgical-skill selection, while delegating physical execution to verifiable deterministic control programs. For brain--machine-interface implantation, a representative high-precision surgical task, we construct a state-to-skill invocation dataset and expand it through visual and state perturbations. The model first learns structured skill invocation through supervised fine-tuning and is then optimized using three rewards for action correctness, operation prerequisites, and format validity, improving action selection, operational safety, and output parseability. We evaluate subtask success, offline model comparison, real-robot performance, and ablations. On a fixed test set, SurgiCLAW achieves 94.6% skill-selection accuracy and 93.0% macro-averaged F1 (Macro-F1). Across 50 closed-loop real-robot implantation trials, it achieves an 88.0% task success rate.

</details>


### [183] [Competitive Task Allocation with Temporal Proposal Windows and Improved Artificial Potential Fields for Dual-Arm Industrial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15763)
*Surya Prakash S.K,Bhuvan Narula,Jagannath Prasad Sahoo,Jayakumar VK,Praful Hambarde,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向双臂系统的基于距离的竞争式任务分配框架，融合实时视觉感知与无碰撞运动规划，并在仿真与Kinova Gen3 Lite硬件上验证其在效率、碰撞风险与工作空间覆盖率上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业自动化需要能在异构物体布局中协同操作的自主协作系统。现有系统依赖预设轨迹和刚性分配策略，导致工作空间利用率次优并存在碰撞风险。

Method: 框架采用基于距离的竞争式任务分配，结合实时视觉感知和无碰撞运动规划；引入时间窗口机制，使每条机械臂在连续5个视觉帧上评估物体检测后再提交最优距离选择，并支持动态重分配和0.01m阈值精度的空间冲突消解。运动规划使用改进人工势场（iAPF），包含指数目标吸引、臂间排斥、回位（home-seeking）和速度阻尼力，实现双臂协调运动。系统集成YOLOv8 OBB检测（30 FPS）与10 Hz控制循环。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法在距离效率、碰撞风险和工作空间覆盖率上优于市场机制、轮询和固定分配策略。使用两台6-DOF Kinova Gen3 Lite的硬件实验中，同构场景下相比固定分配平均速度提升约47%，且零碰撞；混合物体场景验证了通用处理能力，时间开销增加约11%，但换来了更高的安全裕度。

Conclusion: 该框架具备实际部署能力，适用于工业装配与分拣等需要双臂协同、实时感知和无碰撞规划的操作任务。

Abstract: Modern industrial automation demands autonomous collaborative systems capable of coordinated manipulation in workspaces with heterogeneous object arrangements. Existing systems rely on predetermined trajectories and rigid allocation strategies, causing suboptimal workspace utilization and collision risks. This paper presents a distance-based competitive task allocation framework for dual-arm systems, integrating real-time vision perception with collision-free motion planning. A temporal window mechanism enables each arm to evaluate object detections over five consecutive vision frames before committing to optimal distance-based selection, featuring dynamic reallocation and spatial conflict resolution at 0.01m threshold accuracy. An improved Artificial Potential Field (iAPF) framework provides coordinated dual-arm movements through exponential goal attraction, inter-arm repulsion, home-seeking, and velocity damping forces. Simulation results validate superiority over market-based, round-robin, and fixed allocation strategies in distance efficiency, collision risk, and workspace coverage. Hardware experiments using two 6-DOF Kinova Gen3 Lite manipulators demonstrate approximately 47% average velocity improvement over fixed allocation in homogeneous scenarios with zero collisions, while mixed object scenarios confirm universal handling with approximately 11% time overhead offset by superior safety margins. The framework integrates YOLOv8 OBB detection at 30 FPS with 10 Hz control loops, demonstrating practical deployment capability for industrial assembly and sorting operations.

</details>


### [184] [Kinematic Reward, Measured Effort: Hardware-Validated RL Morphology Optimization of a Planar 2R Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15756)
*Gunesh Gupta,Arvind Kumar Mishra,Sohom Chakrabarty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出基于 RSL-RL PPO 的强化学习框架，以 Yoshikawa 运动学可操作度指数为核心奖励，优化 2R SCARA 机械臂构型；先在圆形任务中验证可恢复解析最优解，再用于无解析最优的椭圆和矩形路径，并迁移到 2R 硬件验证关节负载降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yoshikawa 可操作度指数常用于机械臂最优构型设计，但既有优化多停留在仿真中的纯运动学分析，很少在硬件上验证其实际效果。

Method: 使用 RSL-RL PPO 强化学习框架，以 Yoshikawa 运动学可操作度指数为核心奖励，优化 2R SCARA 型机械臂的构型；对圆形任务采用解析闭式最优解验证方法能否恢复该最优解，并将方法扩展到无解析最优的椭圆和矩形路径；随后将 RL 优化构型迁移到 2R 硬件机械臂，验证关节负载降低效果。

Result: 相比最差构型，所提方法得到的最优构型在椭圆路径下使第二关节负载降低 38.4 ± 0.6%，在矩形路径下降低 41.3 ± 0.3%，并使第一关节负载降低 15%。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅能恢复圆形任务的解析最优构型，还能推广到无解析最优的复杂路径，并通过 2R 硬件实验证明最优构型可显著降低关节负载。

Abstract: Yoshikawa's Manipulability Index is generally used as a classical measure to define an optimal morphology of a manipulator. However, any such optimization is almost always studied as a purely kinematic quantity in simulation and is rarely validated on hardware. We present a reinforcement-learning framework with RSL-RL PPO that optimizes the morphology of a 2R SCARA-type manipulator arm with Yoshikawa's kinematic manipulability index as a core reward, and the analytically derived closed-form optimum for the circular task is used to verify that our method recovers it. Then the same method is applied to custom elliptical and rectangular paths having no analytical optimum. The RL-optimized morphologies are then transferred to a 2R hardware manipulator to validate its effect in reduction of joint load in the hardware. Compared to the worst morphology, the optimum morphology delivered by our method reduces second joint load by 38.4 +/- 0.6% for the ellipse path and 41.3 +/- 0.3% for the rectangle, and first joint load by 15%.

</details>


### [185] [Model-Based Trailer-Sway Suppression for Autonomous Passenger Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15747)
*Alec McFadyen,Pablo Covarrubias De La Torre,Connor McGovern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究针对轻型车辆牵引挂车时的挂车摆振问题，建立显含舌载的横摆平面线性状态空间模型，设计LQR抑制铰接角发散，并用仅依赖牵引车测量的卡尔曼滤波估计挂车横摆率和铰接角，最后在MuJoCo中验证其在多种速度和舌载（含负舌载）下可显著减小摆振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挂车摆振是轻型乘用车拖挂事故的重要原因，但当前进入自动化时代的自动驾驶系统尚未有效解决。货物后置会降低甚至反转经牵引装置传递的舌载，导致牵引车后轴垂直载荷和可用侧向轮胎力不足，使耦合系统横摆稳定性下降，进而引发挂车横向发散振荡；在恶劣的速度与载荷组合下，可能发展为折叠/折刀工况并失去方向控制。

Method: 建立轻型车辆-挂车组合的横摆平面线性状态空间模型，将舌载参数化为显式决定稳定性的量；基于该模型综合线性二次调节器（LQR）以抑制铰接角发散；由于消费级挂车无传感器，使用卡尔曼滤波仅从牵引车测量中估计控制器所需的挂车横摆率和铰接角；随后在MuJoCo多体仿真中实现并评估，覆盖多种纵向速度和舌载条件，包括负舌载工况。

Result: 结果表明，利用牵引车IMU和LiDAR数据，通过LQR控制车辆转向可显著降低挂车振荡；该方法在较宽速度与舌载范围内有效，并覆盖负舌载区域。

Conclusion: LQR控制的防摆系统是未来自动驾驶车辆中可行且有效的控制方法。

Abstract: Trailer sway is a persistent contributor to severe crashes among light passenger vehicles towing trailers, yet it remains largely unaddressed by the autonomous vehicle systems now entering this new era of automation. Improper cargo placement rearward of the trailer axle reduces, and can invert, the tongue load transmitted through the hitch. The resulting loss of vertical load at the tow vehicle's rear axle diminishes the lateral tire force available there and degrades yaw stability of the coupled system, admitting a divergent lateral oscillation of the trailer known as trailer sway. Under adverse combinations of speed and loading, this oscillation can progress to jackknifing, in which the articulation angle diverges rapidly and directional control is lost. This work develops a linear state-space model of the yaw-plane dynamics of a light vehicle-trailer combination, parameterized to expose tongue load as an explicit stability-determining quantity. From this model a linear quadratic regulator is synthesized to suppress articulation-angle divergence. Because consumer trailers carry no sensors, the two trailer states the controller needs, trailer yaw rate and articulation angle, are estimated by a Kalman filter from measurements taken only on the tow vehicle. The controller is then implemented and evaluated in a MuJoCo multibody simulation across a range of longitudinal speeds and tongue-load conditions, including the negative-tongue-load regime. Results show a significant decrease in trailer oscillation when controlling a given vehicle's steering with an LQR using tow-vehicle IMU and LiDAR data. These findings indicate that LQR controlled anti-sway systems are a viable control method for future autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [186] [Multimodal Sensor-Gated Mid-Air Launch and Vision-Based Recovery of a Child UAV on a Hovering Mothership Drone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15740)
*Aashish Sahu,R. Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种母机-子四旋翼空中发射/回收系统，用上视单光束LiDAR、RTK和载机推力等非视觉传感对任务转换进行门控，以确认子机安全就位；室外10次任务9次成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从悬停载机发射并回收微型无人机时，载机下降前必须可靠确认子机已安全坐稳。视觉仅提供相对引导，不能直接反映载荷转移，且下视相机在触地前可能丢失基准标记。

Method: 构建母机-子四旋翼系统，任务转换由互补非视觉传感门控：回收甲板上视单光束LiDAR基于子机遮挡足迹和穿越速度，采用滞回与去抖输出二值占用状态；RTK定位约束载机悬停；载机推力提供载荷转移的本体感知。室外使用F450级母机与Q250级子机进行相机引导回收；MuJoCo中开展完全自主基准标记回收仿真。

Result: 室外10次任务中9次成功，释放与捕获期间保持RTK固定解。MuJoCo仿真中触地距甲板中心0.077 m，标记检测在最终约0.2 m以上丢失；载机推力在释放时变化-17.0 N、捕获时+17.3 N，与子机18.0 N悬停推力误差在6%以内。

Conclusion: 结果表明，视觉引导接近、测距与本体感知信息佐证最终对接决策的传感架构可行。

Abstract: Launching a micro unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) from a hovering carrier and recovering it in flight requires reliable confirmation that the child is securely seated before the carrier is allowed to descend. This is challenging because vision provides relative guidance but does not directly indicate load transfer, and a downward-facing camera can lose its fiducial near touchdown. This letter presents a mothership-child quadrotor system in which mission transitions are gated by complementary nonvisual sensing. An upward-facing single-beam LiDAR on the recovery deck provides a binary occupancy state using hysteresis and a debounce interval derived from the child's occluding footprint and crossing speed; real-time kinematic (RTK) positioning conditions the carrier hover; and carrier thrust provides a proprioceptive indication of load transfer. In ten outdoor missions using an F450-class mothership and a Q250-class child with camera-guided recovery, nine completed successfully while RTK-fixed positioning was maintained through release and capture. A complementary MuJoCo study of fully autonomous fiducial-based recovery achieved touchdown 0.077 m from the deck center, while marker detection was lost within the final approximately 0.2 m above the docked configuration. In simulation, carrier thrust changed by -17.0 N at release and +17.3 N at capture, agreeing within 6% with the child's 18.0 N hover thrust. The results support a sensing architecture in which vision guides the approach, while range and proprioceptive information corroborate the final docking decision.

</details>


### [187] [Distributed area coverage path planning subject to the inclusion of a headland path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15739)
*Mogens Plessen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种考虑地头路径的分布式区域覆盖路径规划确定性方法，可为多机器人生成满足多种约束并协同完成全覆盖的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非凸区域、含障碍、多机器人、仓库及机械约束下的区域覆盖路径规划问题，并强调纳入地头路径的必要性。

Method: 给定一般非凸且可能含障碍的区域、多机器人、至少一个机器人仓库，以及各机械的工作幅宽、工作量限制、行程限制、非完整动力学约束和可能的起止仓库分配，确定性计算每台机械的分布式路径，使其整体实现全覆盖；并讨论变体，包括无约束仓库分配可达到全局最优解。

Result: 方法生成可协同实现全覆盖的分布式路径；某些变体在无约束仓库分配下可达到全局最优；展示了地头路径对区域覆盖的重要性，以及与Boustrophedon模式方法相比的优势。

Conclusion: 地头路径的纳入对区域覆盖至关重要，所提方法相较Boustrophedon模式方法更具优势。

Abstract: A deterministic method for the problem of distributed area coverage path planning subject to the inclusion of a headland path is presented. Given a, in general, non-convex area to be covered that may, in general, include obstacle areas, multiple robots, at least one robot depot, and constraints on each machinery such as a working width, work capacity limits, travel capacity limits, nonholonomic dynamical constraints and, possibly, a robot start and end depot assignment, distributed paths for each machinery are calculated that collectively enable full area coverage. Variations are discussed, in particular, a scenario where unconstrained depots assignment can attain a global optimal solution to the problem. The importance of inclusion of a headland path for area coverage, as well as the benefits in comparison to an alternative Boustrophedon-pattern based approach are highlighted.

</details>


### [188] [Geometry-Aware Predictive Control for Walk-to-Run Bipedal Obstacle Crossing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15735)
*Aashish Sahu,R. Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种几何感知预测控制框架，用于双足机器人在行走与跑步模式下的越障，通过五阶段轨迹生成与约束MPC跟踪实现协调的骨盆和摆动腿运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对双足机器人在行走/跑步中跨越障碍时，需要兼顾几何约束、横向间隙、身体对齐、步态转换与运动平滑性，并避免离散控制器切换的问题。

Method: 将越障过程分为预越障、前腿通过、跨立、后腿通过和恢复五个可解释阶段；摆动腿抬升随障碍高度自适应，横向站距加宽和骨盆重定位随障碍深度调整；采用连续步态参数演化实现走-跑平滑过渡；用约束MPC跟踪生成参考，调节运动平滑性、间隙可行性、触地行为和横向容许性。

Result: 在降阶仿真中，于行走和跑步条件下测试五个难度递增障碍；机器人成功跨越前四个障碍，最小机体-障碍间隙分别为0.036、0.036、0.059和0.017 m；第五个最难障碍导致碰撞，标定所提降阶公式的当前操作边界。

Conclusion: 该方法为智能、障碍感知的双足运动提供了一种紧凑、可解释且计算高效的基座。

Abstract: This paper presents a geometry-aware predictive control framework for bipedal obstacle crossing across walking and running regimes. The proposed approach integrates a phase-structured trajectory generator with a constrained model predictive control (MPC) tracking layer to generate coordinated pelvis and swing-foot motions during obstacle traversal. The crossing process is represented through five interpretable phases: pre-crossing, leading-leg clearance, straddle, trailing-leg clearance, and recovery. Within this structure, swing-foot elevation is adapted as a function of obstacle height, while lateral stance widening and pelvis repositioning are introduced as functions of obstacle depth to improve transverse clearance and body alignment. A continuous gait-parameter evolution enables smooth walk-to-run transition without discrete controller switching. The generated references are tracked using MPC to regulate motion smoothness, clearance feasibility, touchdown behavior, and lateral admissibility. The framework is evaluated in reduced-order simulation over five progressively difficult obstacles under walking and running conditions. The robot successfully crosses the first four obstacles, achieving minimum body-obstacle clearances of 0.036, 0.036, 0.059, and 0.017 m, respectively. The fifth and most demanding obstacle leads to collision, identifying the current operating boundary of the proposed reduced-order formulation. Overall, the method provides a compact, interpretable, and computationally efficient basis for intelligent obstacle-aware bipedal locomotion.

</details>


### [189] [Recovering Low-Bit Failures in Vision-Language-Action Policies via Quantization-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15675)
*Seongmin Park,Jaewon Lee,Yujun Heo,Yoonsik Kim,Seheon Choi,Hyoung-rock Kim,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA策略量化后任务完成率下降的问题，提出离线强化学习方法QCRL，通过进度优势估计与量化感知源条件化利用rollout结果信号，在INT4/NVFP4量化下恢复甚至超过FP性能并带来内存与速度收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化可降低VLA策略部署成本，但会损害任务完成能力。对5个VLA模型、4050次真实试验的系统评估表明：QAT通常优于PTQ，但在长时程任务及视觉/语义偏移下仍显著落后于FP；且其模仿目标未显式利用rollout成功与否及部分完成信息。

Method: 提出Quantization-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (QCRL)，一种离线RL方法：耦合基于进度的优势估计与量化感知源条件化。FP rollout提供超越动作级蒸馏的参考经验；进度缩放终端惩罚区分部分完成；量化感知条件化在量化策略内选择有利参考行为。评估INT4 W4A16权重仅量化（π0.5、GR00T N1.5）与NVFP4 W4A4权重-激活联合量化（π0.5）。

Result: 仅用分布内数据，π0.5在INT4 W4A16下于三种视觉偏移条件达到FP平均得分的99.3%，并节省42.9%模型内存；NVFP4 W4A4下达到同一FP基线的100.7%，使用NVFP4/FP8部署获得1.73×加速。硬件收益相对Jetson Thor上的FP16。

Conclusion: QCRL通过利用rollout结果信号而非仅模仿/蒸馏，有效弥合量化VLA与FP之间的性能差距，并在视觉偏移与低精度部署下保持任务完成率，同时实现显著内存节省与推理加速。

Abstract: Quantization lowers deployment costs of vision-language-action (VLA) policies but can compromise task completion. Across five VLA models and 4,050 real-world trials, we systematically reveal that quantization-aware training (QAT) generally improves on post-training quantization (PTQ) but leaves substantial gaps to full precision (FP) on long-horizon tasks and under visual and semantic shifts, while its imitation-based objective does not explicitly use rollout success or partial completion. To exploit these outcome signals, we introduce Quantization-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (QCRL), an offline RL method coupling progress-based advantage estimation with quantization-aware source conditioning. FP rollouts supply reference experience beyond action-level distillation, progress-scaled terminal penalty distinguishes partial completion, and quantization-aware conditioning selects advantageous reference behavior within the quantized policy. We evaluate weight-only INT4 W4A16 on $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.5 and joint weight-activation NVFP4 W4A4 on $\pi_{0.5}$. Using only in-distribution data, QCRL reaches 99.3% of FP's mean score across three visual-shift conditions for $\pi_{0.5}$ with INT4 W4A16, alongside 42.9% model-memory savings. With NVFP4 W4A4, it reaches 100.7% of the same FP baseline, alongside a $1.73\times$ speedup using NVFP4/FP8 deployment. Hardware gains are relative to FP16 on Jetson Thor.

</details>


### [190] [Imitation Learning via Discrete and Rhythmic Primitive Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15639)
*Rafid Saif,Auke Ijspeert,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Neural Dynamic Composition (NDC)，一种仅用原始轨迹监督、在给定每个基元一个示例的条件下组合 DMP 的监督框架，并在 2D 手写与 3D 机器人任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作可分解为语义子任务，但运动基元组合常依赖人工设计或样本效率低的强化学习；现有监督方法受限于标准数据集缺少 MP 标签，且难以同时泛化离散与节律运动。

Method: NDC 将多个 DMP 的并行组合视为类别式基元选择的可微松弛；使用 warp-invariant 损失以在时空变化下识别基元；采用分阶段训练课程联合学习选择策略与 DMP 参数，将离散和节律运动统一到一个框架中。

Result: 在 2D 手写和 3D 机器人任务上评估，NDC 在多数任务和指标上优于基线；消融实验表明各设计选择对系统性能均有贡献。

Conclusion: NDC 表明仅需原始轨迹与每个基元一个示例，即可通过可微、形变不变的分阶段监督学习实现 DMP 组合，并统一处理离散与节律运动，为长时程操作提供有效方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation is inherently composed of semantically meaningful sub-tasks. While Movement Primitives (MPs) can model these components, their composition typically relies on manual engineering or sample-inefficient reinforcement learning. Existing supervised methods are limited by the lack of MP labels in standard datasets and often fail to generalize across discrete and rhythmic movements. We propose Neural Dynamic Composition (NDC), a supervised framework to compose Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) using only raw trajectories for supervision, given a single example of each primitive. Our approach exploits parallel combinations of DMPs as a differentiable relaxation of categorical primitive selection, and utilizes a warp-invariant loss formulation to identify primitives despite spatial or temporal variations. A staged training curriculum is employed to learn the selection policy and DMP parameters, unifying discrete and rhythmic movements in one framework. Evaluated on 2D handwriting and 3D robotic tasks, our method outperforms baselines on most tasks and metrics. An ablation study demonstrates how each design choice contributes to the system's performance.

</details>


### [191] [StreamingWAM: Streaming World Action Modeling via Chunk-Wise Staircase Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15585)
*Shaojie Ren,Jingwen Sun,Zezhi Liu,Hongjin Chen,Xin Jin,Wei Zou,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出StreamingWAM，一种面向世界动作模型（WAM）机器人控制的流式扩散框架，联合解决执行不连续、抖动与重规划延迟问题，并在仿真和真实任务中取得有竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于预训练视频生成世界模型的WAM在通用和开放式机器人控制中很有前景，但实际部署常出现不连续和抖动执行。现有方法通常只单独提升推理效率或增强跨预测块的动作连续性，未能同时解决二者。

Method: StreamingWAM采用流式扩散框架，核心包括：噪声阶梯用于分块动作预测，前缀展开模块用于跨块连续性，密集视频历史记忆用于时间一致的条件建模。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0及真实机器人任务上，StreamingWAM提升了静态和动态设置下的动作连续性，性能具有竞争力；稳态重规划延迟降至仿真72.9 ms、真实机器人57.3 ms，并产生更平滑的动作过渡，同时流式阶梯降低了对执行视野K的敏感性。

Conclusion: StreamingWAM通过联合优化跨块连续性与推理效率，有效缓解WAM部署中的不连续和抖动问题，实现更平滑、低延迟且对执行视野更鲁棒的机器人控制。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs), often built upon pretrained video-generation world models, have emerged as a promising framework for general-purpose and open-ended robot control. However, their real-world deployment often suffers from discontinuous and jerky execution. Existing methods typically address this issue by either improving inference efficiency or enhancing action continuity across prediction chunks, without jointly addressing both challenges. To bridge this gap, we propose StreamingWAM, a streaming-diffusion framework for WAM-based robot control. StreamingWAM combines a noise ladder for chunk-wise action prediction, a prefix rollout module for cross-chunk continuity, and dense video-history memory for temporally consistent conditioning. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate that StreamingWAM improves action continuity across static and dynamic settings, achieving competitive performance while reducing steady-state replanning latency to 72.9 ms in simulation and 57.3 ms on the real robot. The method also produces smoother action transitions, while the streaming staircase reduces sensitivity to the execution horizon K.

</details>


### [192] [SIGN-MAPPO: State-Intent-Aware Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Driving under Imperfect V2X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15577)
*Zide Fan,Jiahui Yuan,Kaixin Zhang,Junhao Fan,Xiaohe Li,Yiru Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SIGN-MAPPO，用状态-意图感知图多智能体强化学习解决不完美V2X、HV意图不可通信及交叉决策不一致下的混合交通无信号交叉口协同驾驶问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通无信号交叉口协同驾驶面临三类挑战：V2X通信不完美、人类驾驶车辆（HV）意图无法直接通信、CAV独立生成的穿越决策可能不兼容。

Method: 提出State-Intent-Aware Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Driving under Imperfect V2X（SIGN-MAPPO），包含三个协同机制：每个有决策资格的CAV根据自身状态和可获得的同伴状态记录重建接收者特定的交通图，共享图注意力策略生成原始Stop/Go意图并附加预测的HV意图特征；CAV意图通过带时间戳的V2V消息交换；本地冲突感知仲裁层将各CAV意图转换为安全可执行动作，以应对同伴信息可能不一致。

Result: 在SUMO主实验中，SIGN-MAPPO达到97.53%完成率、0.10%碰撞率和35.28 s平均交叉口通行时间。消融研究证实HV意图预测与CAV意图交换具有互补贡献。训练策略在评估的通信延迟和丢包范围内保持鲁棒；跨场景测试中所有测试设置均实现完全交通清空，但在复合不可预测HV行为下安全裕度收窄。

Conclusion: SIGN-MAPPO通过图注意力、意图交换和冲突仲裁有效提升不完美V2X下混合交通无信号交叉口协同驾驶的安全性与效率，但面对复合不可预测HV行为时安全裕度仍会下降。

Abstract: Cooperative driving at mixed-traffic unsignalized intersections is challenged by imperfect vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication, human-driven vehicle (HV) intentions that cannot be directly communicated, and the potential incompatibility of independently generated crossing decisions. This paper proposes State-Intent-Aware Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Driving under Imperfect V2X (SIGN-MAPPO) to address these challenges through three coordinated mechanisms. Each decision-eligible connected and automated vehicle (CAV) first reconstructs a receiver-specific traffic graph from its ego state and the peer-state records available to it, from which a shared graph-attention policy generates a raw Stop/Go intention augmented with predicted HV-intent features. CAV intentions are then exchanged through timestamped vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) messages, and a local conflict-aware arbitration layer converts each CAV's intention into a safe executable action despite potentially inconsistent peer information. In the principal Simulation of Urban MObility (SUMO) evaluation, SIGN-MAPPO achieves a 97.53% completion rate, a 0.10% collision rate, and a mean intersection traversal time of 35.28 s. Ablation studies further confirm the complementary contributions of HV-intent prediction and CAV-intent exchange to this performance. The trained policy also remains robust under the evaluated communication-delay and packet-loss ranges, and cross-scenario tests show complete traffic clearance across the tested settings, though safety margins narrow under compound unpredictable HV behavior.

</details>


### [193] [Look Before You Move: Agent-Interface-Aligned Supervision for Open-World Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15572)
*Ronghe Qiu,Teli Ma,Jiaming Zhou,Zeying Gong,Yiting He,Qiang Huang,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AIDA数据采集管线，使教师通过部署智能体的技能接口直接选择并执行动作，从而为开放世界移动操作训练VLM智能体提供动作选择与主动视觉示范；所得AIDA-OWMM在OWMM上达到71.76%全任务成功率，并可零样本迁移至真实移动机器人取得38%端到端成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界移动操作要求机器人根据语言指令找到、接近并将物体移动到目标容器，训练VLM智能体需要同时具备动作选择与主动视觉的示范。现有流程常将规划轨迹转为高层动作标签，但最短路径进展对“该看哪里”的监督有限，难以学习主动视觉与目标恢复行为。

Method: 提出Agent-Interface Data Alignment (AIDA)数据采集管线：教师直接在已部署智能体的技能接口上选择并执行动作。针对移动操作，设计能够保持目标可见、恢复丢失目标并接近以抓取或放置的教师。加入基于模板的空间推理监督，并使用统一执行框架在训练与推理时组织观测、历史与输出。

Result: 所得智能体AIDA-OWMM在170个留出OWMM回合上取得71.76%的全任务成功率。教师敏感性分析表明，主动视觉示范可提升学生智能体成功率。仿真训练的智能体无需额外训练便在真实移动机器人上取得38%端到端成功率。将AIDA应用于EmbodiedBench的导航子集也验证了该管线对导航任务的效用。

Conclusion: AIDA通过让教师在部署技能接口中直接示范动作与主动视觉，为开放世界移动操作提供了有效的数据对齐与训练方案，能够提升VLM智能体全任务成功率并展现仿真到真实的零样本迁移潜力，同时可推广至导航任务。

Abstract: Open-world mobile manipulation requires a robot to find an object, approach it, and move it to a target receptacle from a language instruction. Training vision-language model (VLM) agents for these tasks requires demonstrations of both action selection and active vision. Existing pipelines often convert planned trajectories into high-level action labels, but shortest-path progress provides limited supervision for deciding where to look. We introduce Agent-Interface Data Alignment (AIDA), a data collection pipeline in which a teacher directly selects and executes actions through the deployed agent's skill interface. For mobile manipulation, we design teachers that maintain target visibility, recover lost targets, and approach for picking or placing. We add template-based spatial reasoning supervision and use a shared harness to organize observations, history, and outputs during training and inference. The resulting agent, AIDA-OWMM, achieves 71.76% full-task success on 170 held-out OWMM episodes. Teacher sensitivity analysis shows that active-vision demonstrations improve student success. The simulation-trained agent achieves 38% end-to-end success on a real mobile robot without additional training. Applying AIDA to a navigation subset of EmbodiedBench further demonstrates the pipeline's utility for navigation tasks.

</details>


### [194] [Anticipatory Textile MMG-Strain Sensing for Confirm-Then-Assist Soft Knee Exosuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15563)
*Zheng Ma,Xiao Jin,Zhenhua Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种针织纺织接口，在同一500 Hz时钟同步采集三通道MMG阵列与前置应变传感器，用于运动起始附近的意图识别，并驱动软气动膝关节的确认-辅助控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软穿戴机器人需在运动起始时提供辅助，但现有信号模糊：sEMG可早期检测神经肌肉驱动，却受汗液和电极移位影响；运动学传感器仅在肢体运动后响应。

Method: 将三通道机械肌图（MMG）阵列与针织应变传感器集成于单一纺织接口，以500 Hz同步采样；利用MMG激活早于膝运动，并用应变斜率符号判断运动方向，无需训练或个体校准；融合信号驱动两阶段“确认-辅助”策略控制软气动膝。

Result: 5名参与者中，坐-站时MMG激活比应变检测的膝运动提前322–344 ms（各通道中位数）。650次转换中649次可由应变斜率符号判断方向，无需训练/校准；四分类平衡准确率由90.3%提升至95.6%。4名参与者240次试验中，控制器在80次坐-站中78次进入辅助，80次等长共收缩中0次进入；但在80次站-坐中27次误进入并在保持超时退出，需拮抗屈肌传感器解决起始方向问题。

Conclusion: 在同一时钟下共采样肌肉声学与纺织应变，可在运动起始附近发出充气指令，提升软穿戴机器人辅助的及时性与方向判别能力；但站-坐方向仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Soft wearable robots must commit to assistance around movement onset, when the available signals are ambiguous. Surface electromyography (sEMG) detects neuromuscular drive early but degrades under sweat and electrode shift, and kinematic sensors respond only once the limb moves. This letter presents a knitted textile interface that co-samples a three-channel mechanomyography (MMG) array and an anterior strain sensor on a single synchronous 500 Hz clock. Across five participants, MMG activation preceded strain-detected knee motion during sit-to-stand by per-channel medians of 322-344 ms. Sampled on the same clock, the sign of the strain slope resolved movement direction on 649 of 650 completed transitions with no training or per-participant calibration, raising four-class balanced accuracy from 90.3% to 95.6%.
