The fused signals drove a two-stage confirm-then-assist policy for a soft pneumatic knee. Across 240 trials in four participants, the onboard controller entered the assist state on 78 of 80 sit-to-stand trials and on none of the 80 isometric co-contractions. It also entered the assist state on 27 of 80 stand-to-sit trials and left it at the hold timeout, motivating an antagonistic flexor gauge to resolve this direction at onset. Co-sampling muscle acoustics with textile strain on one clock lets the inflation command be issued around movement onset.

</details>


### [195] [Stereo-Planner: Learning Stereo-to-Trajectory Planning for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Quadrotor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15560)
*Hongming Shen,Junjie Lu,Xun Chen,Wei Wang,Xinhang Xu,Bailing Tian,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Stereo-Planner，一个仅依赖双目 RGB 的端到端视觉到轨迹规划框架，通过状态与尺度条件化查询潜在双目几何，实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，并在仿真与真实四旋翼上验证高速自主飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航系统多依赖专用深度传感器或显式深度重建；直接从 RGB 图像学习轨迹生成面临仿真到现实的域差异挑战，且难以在真实环境中零样本部署。

Method: 将轨迹生成建模为对潜在双目几何的状态与尺度条件化查询。Scene Representor 利用预训练视觉基础模型提取跨仿真与真实环境共享的几何线索；轻量 Trajectory Decoder 通过交错注意力将轨迹-状态交互与场景特征检索分离，并引入双目基线作为度量尺度线索。模型完全在仿真中通过最小化可微轨迹代价训练，无需微调即可部署到现实。

Result: 仿真环境中指令速度 8 m/s 时成功率达 95%，跨场景泛化强；仅用双目 RGB 感知即可与基于深度的规划器竞争。在搭载 NVIDIA Orin NX 的紧凑四旋翼上实现最高 7 m/s 自主飞行，机载视觉到动作平均推理时间 24.27 ms。

Conclusion: Stereo-Planner 表明，利用预训练视觉基础模型与双目几何条件化查询，可在不依赖深度传感器且无需真实微调的情况下实现高速、跨场景的零样本仿真到现实视觉导航；软件、仿真环境与硬件设计将开源。

Abstract: Existing navigation systems commonly rely on dedicated depth sensors or explicit depth reconstruction, while learning to generate trajectories directly from RGB images remains challenging due to the sim-to-real domain gap. In this work, we propose Stereo-Planner, an end-to-end vision-to-trajectory framework that enables zero-shot sim-to-real planning from stereo RGB observations. The key idea is to formulate trajectory generation as state- and scale-conditioned querying of latent stereo geometry. A Scene Representor incorporates a pretrained vision grounding foundation model to extract geometric cues shared across simulated and real-world environments, supporting generalization across visual domains. A lightweight Trajectory Decoder then translates these representations into trajectories through interleaved attention, separating trajectory-state interaction from scene-feature retrieval and incorporating the stereo baseline as a metric-scale cue. With these designs, Stereo-Planner is trained entirely on simulated data by minimizing differentiable trajectory costs and deployed directly in real-world environments without fine-tuning. Benchmark results demonstrate strong cross-scene generalization, with a 95% success rate in simulation environments at a commanded speed of 8 m/s, and competitive performance against depth-based planners using only stereo RGB observations for scene perception. Furthermore, deployment on a compact quadrotor equipped with an NVIDIA Orin NX enables autonomous flight at speeds of up to 7 m/s, with a mean onboard vision-to-action inference time of 24.27 ms. The software, simulation environments, and hardware designs will be open-sourced at this https URL .

</details>


### [196] [Analytic Robot Self-Model: Learning Screw-Axis Kinematics for Rendering and Model-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15558)
*Chen Xu,Ning Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种显式解析机器人自模型：无需变形网络或URDF/CAD模板，仅用多视角图像、关节读数和稀疏末端位姿学习度量螺旋轴，得到闭式前向运动学与解析雅可比，并在仿真机械臂、四足和真实机械臂上验证控制与预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉自建模可让机器人从观测中学习身体模型，但现有模型多隐式编码运动学，无法暴露可用于计算和控制的度量关节参数；且对图像拟合得好，并不代表运动学准确。

Method: 方法无需变形网络和URDF/CAD模板。利用多视角图像与关节读数建立高斯外观，并提供刚体连杆分配的运动证据；同时用稀疏末端执行器位姿独立辨识度量螺旋轴。由此得到闭式前向运动学和解析雅可比，可外推到拟合范围之外。

Result: 在仿真七轴机械臂、十二关节四足和真实六轴机械臂上验证。仿真中实现毫米级到达与拾放，以及四足闭环行走。真实机械臂可渲染留出构型，末端位置预测中位误差6.4 mm，优于标定标称模型的11.7 mm，并支持带外部标记反馈的到达。控制实验表明仅图像拟合不足：两个仅关节轴不同的Panda模型PSNR仅差0.54 dB，但末端预测误差相差13.7倍。

Conclusion: 显式解析自模型能从视觉和稀疏位姿中恢复准确度量运动学，并支持闭式计算、外推和控制；图像拟合质量本身不能保证运动学正确性。

Abstract: Visual self-modelling lets robots learn body models from observations, but such models usually encode kinematics implicitly rather than exposing metric joint parameters for computation and control. Moreover, a good fit to images does not by itself establish accurate kinematics. Here we present an explicit analytic robot self-model that requires neither deformation networks nor URDF/CAD templates. Multi-view images and joint readings establish Gaussian appearance and provide motion evidence for rigid link assignments, whereas sparse end-effector poses independently identify metric screw axes. The resulting closed-form forward kinematics and analytic Jacobian can be evaluated beyond the fitting ranges. We validate the model on a simulated seven-axis arm, a simulated twelve-joint quadruped and a real six-axis arm. In simulation, it achieves millimetre-level reaching and pick-and-place, as well as closed-loop quadruped walking. On the real arm, it renders held-out configurations, predicts end-effector positions with a median error of 6.4 mm, compared with 11.7 mm for a calibrated nominal model, and supports reaching with external marker feedback. A controlled comparison shows that image fit alone is not enough: two Panda models differing only in their joint axes are 0.54 dB apart in PSNR, yet their end-effector prediction errors differ 13.7-fold.

</details>


### [197] [PointWalker: Mapless Quadruped Locomotion Directly From Raw LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15548)
*Sicen Li,Zhen Chu,Chao Li,Jun Zheng,Qiuguo Zhu,Jun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PointWalker：一种无地图强化学习框架，直接用四个LiDAR的原始点云作为单一策略输入，实现四足机器人跨异构地形并零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖地形图或深度图，但地图维护成本高、易受里程计漂移和异常值影响，且会被透视格栅误导；深度相机在光照和表面材质变化下性能下降。因此需要一种无需地图、能直接利用原始点云感知稀疏或可透视支撑地形的控制方法。

Method: 将体素化点云用线性自注意力编码，并通过交叉注意力与本体感觉融合，使地形几何直接进入策略，无需建图或落脚点估计。采用PPO一次训练，结合地形感知奖励和透视格栅双通道仿真，得到覆盖七类地形的单一策略。

Result: 仿真中七类地形平均成功率达90.1%；在Deep Robotics X30上以50 Hz机载零样本部署，可跨越0.8 m沟壑、攀爬0.8 m平台和45°钢格栅楼梯、承载50 kg负载，并完成2.5 km山路路线。

Conclusion: 无地图、端到端点云强化学习可让四足机器人在异构地形中实现鲁棒运动，并具备真实场景零样本部署能力。

Abstract: Quadruped robots deployed for inspection and field missions must cross heterogeneous terrain, from stairs, slopes, and gaps to high platforms, stepping stones, and balance beams, where safe support is sparse or see-through. Existing methods interpose a terrain map or a depth image between sensing and control. Maps are costly to maintain, corrupted by odometry drift and outliers, and misled by see-through gratings, while depth cameras degrade with lighting and surface material. In this paper, we present PointWalker, a mapless framework whose single reinforcement learning policy directly consumes raw point clouds from four light detection and ranging (LiDAR) sensors. Voxelized points are encoded by linear self-attention and fused with proprioception through cross-attention, so terrain geometry reaches the policy without maps or foothold estimation. One proximal policy optimization (PPO) run, with terrain-aware rewards and dual-channel simulation of see-through gratings, yields one policy for all seven terrain classes with a 90.1% mean success rate in simulation. Deployed zero-shot on a Deep Robotics X30 at 50 Hz onboard, it crosses 0.8-m gaps, climbs 0.8-m platforms and 45° steel-grating stairs, carries a 50-kg payload, and completes a 2.5-km mountain route.

</details>


### [198] [Targeting the Right Situations: Mutation Testing of Fuzzy-Powered Situation Hyperspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15122)
*Nawshin Mannan Proma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究在SCALOFT仿真框架中通过变异测试验证，基于模糊逻辑的风险优先情境生成相比均匀随机生成能获得更高变异得分，更有效暴露地下矿用自主无人机软件中难以检测的故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下矿山自主无人机运行ODD广阔且不确定，安全验证困难；随机测试情境覆盖效率低，将算力用于低风险场景，难以暴露关键边缘案例；需验证模糊逻辑风险估计是否与真实系统故障相关。

Method: 基于风险优先的Situation Coverage，使用SCALOFT仿真框架开展变异测试；注入固定软件变异体，在两种策略下评估：均匀随机情境生成与模糊引导的风险优先情境生成。

Result: 模糊引导方法获得更高变异得分，比随机测试更有效地暴露难以检测的故障。

Conclusion: 模糊逻辑风险估计与真实故障暴露具有有效性/相关性，风险优先情境生成可提升地下矿用自主无人机安全验证的故障检测效率，尤其针对关键边缘案例。

Abstract: Ensuring the safety of autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating in underground mines is challenging due to the vast and uncertain Operational Design Domain (ODD). Traditional validation approaches, such as random testing, often result in inefficient situation coverage, allocating computational resources to low-risk scenarios while failing to expose critical edge cases. Building on prior work in risk-prioritized Situation Coverage, this paper investigates whether Fuzzy Logic-based risk estimation effectively correlates with real system failures. To this end, we conduct a mutation testing study within our SCALOFT simulation framework. A fixed set of software mutants is systematically injected and evaluated under two testing strategies: (i) uniformly random situation generation and (ii) Fuzzy-Guided risk-prioritized situation generation. Experimental results show that the Fuzzy-Guided approach achieves a higher mutation score and is more effective at exposing difficult-to-detect faults compared to random testing.

</details>


### [199] [Modeling and Control of a Rigid-soft Hybrid-link Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15073)
*Zewen He,Taiki Ishigaki,Ko Yamamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于逆运动学与柔顺性优化的混合连杆人形机器人控制框架，采用分段常应变（PCS）模型描述柔性结构连续变形，并通过仿真与实验验证其站立、跳跃、行走及平衡控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理柔性在人体和机器人运动中具有重要作用；含被动柔性元件的刚性-柔性混合连杆人形系统可储能并缓解冲击，但其运动设计与控制极具挑战，相关研究仍有限。

Method: 使用分段常应变（PCS）模型表示柔性结构的连续变形，提出逆运动学与柔顺性优化相结合的控制框架；通过前向动力学仿真验证站立、跳跃和行走动作；并搭建带叶片弹簧假肢腿的双足人形机器人实验平台，利用动作捕捉系统测量假肢变形。

Result: 仿真验证了所提方法在站立、跳跃、行走中的可行性；实验在双支撑和单支撑情况下验证了站立平衡控制。

Conclusion: 所提基于PCS模型与逆运动学/柔顺性优化的控制框架可有效用于刚性-柔性混合连杆人形系统的运动控制与平衡控制。

Abstract: Physical flexibility plays a significant role not only in human motions but also in robot motions. For example, a humanoid robot with passive and flexible elements, which can be regarded as a rigid--soft hybrid-link system, has the advantages of storing energy and mitigating impact in a dynamic motion. However, the motion design and control of a hybrid-link system are challenging to achieve and have only been discussed in the literature. In this study, we propose the framework of inverse kinematics and compliance optimization-based control for a hybrid-link humanoid system that represents the continuous deformation of a flexible structure using the piece-wise constant strain (PCS) model. The proposed method was validated using forward dynamics simulations for standing, jumping, and walking motions. Then, we built an experimental setup using a biped humanoid robot with leaf-spring type prosthesis leg, in which the prosthesis deformation was measured by a motion capture system. Using this setup, we experimentally validated the proposed method for standing balance control in both double- and single-support cases.

</details>


### [200] [Training-Free Multi-Agent Cooperative Embodied Search from Uncertain Vision-Language Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15038)
*Fan Pu,Keshu Wu,Jiwan Jiang,Shiyu Shen,Zhiwen Fan,Zhengzhong Tu,Jiachen Li,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量、免训练、不确定性感知的协作多智能体多目标搜索框架，在无地图环境中将MLLM作为假设生成器，并用滚动时域混合整数线性规划实现协同导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界视觉语言导航（VLN）需在部分可观测条件下行动，地图结构、语义线索和目标位置可能不完整或被基础模型错误推断，因此需要一种不依赖端到端策略学习、能处理不确定性并支持多智能体多目标搜索的方法。

Method: 框架将高层语义推理与低层执行分离，以可扩展图优化为骨干。多模态大语言模型（MLLM）仅作为假设生成器，提出语义区域、目标位置线索和可能可导航连接；这些输出被视为不确定先验，并利用在线观测和图上下文证据进行细化。协作导航被建模为滚动时域多智能体混合整数线性规划，采用字典序目标：先最大化目标导向奖励，再减少旅行距离、不确定边使用和重复访问。

Result: 在100个Matterport3D测试用例上的实验表明，该框架相比代表性基线方法提升了目标覆盖率和搜索效率；共享信息与联合规划使各智能体工作量更均衡。由于在抽象图上运行且不依赖具身特定策略参数，框架可适应不同数量的智能体和目标，泛化到未见环境，并兼容不同感知模块与低层控制器而无需任务特定重训练。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将MLLM限制为不确定性假设生成器并结合图优化与滚动时域多智能体规划，可在无地图、部分可观测条件下实现高效、可扩展、免训练的协作多目标搜索。

Abstract: Real-world Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to act under partial observability, where map structure, semantic cues, and target locations may be incomplete or incorrectly inferred by foundation models. This paper presents a lightweight, training-free, and uncertainty-aware framework for cooperative multi-agent multi-target search in mapless environments. Instead of learning an end-to-end navigation policy, the framework separates high-level semantic reasoning from low-level execution through a scalable graph-optimization backbone. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) serve only as hypothesis generators, proposing semantic zones, target-location cues, and possible navigable connections. These outputs are treated as uncertain priors and further refined using online observations and graph-context evidence. Cooperative navigation is then formulated as a rolling-horizon multi-agent mixed-integer linear program with a lexicographic objective that first maximizes target-directed reward and then reduces travel distance, uncertain-edge use, and repeated visits. Experiments derived from 100 Matterport3D test cases show that the proposed framework improves target coverage and search efficiency over representative baseline methods, while shared information and joint planning enable more balanced workloads across agents. By operating on an abstract graph without relying on embodiment-specific policy parameters, the framework scales across varying numbers of agents and targets, generalizes to unseen environments, and accommodates different perception modules and low-level controllers without task-specific retraining.

</details>


### [201] [Design and Evaluation of a Collaborative Teleoperation Interface for Multi-Operator Multi-Robot Systems in Challenging Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15001)
*Alperen Kenan,Juan José García Cárdenas,Adriana Tapus,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究设计并评估共享可视化协作遥操作界面，以改善多操作员多机器人系统中的协调、态势感知与任务绩效；实验表明同地共享界面在完成时间、碰撞次数、态势感知和认知负荷方面均最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂且难以进入环境中的任务需要互补型多机器人系统，但当前部署通常为每个机器人分配操作员，依赖操作员间通信手动协调异构平台，且接口不兼容，导致认知负荷增加并限制跨厂商机器人的可扩展性。

Method: 设计共享可视化协作遥操作界面，并开展16名参与者的对比用户研究，比较三种条件：同地操作员+共享界面、远程操作员+共享界面、同地操作员+独立界面；参与者在核电站数字孪生中控制两个仿真机器人完成灾难响应任务，记录绩效指标、生理数据和行为测量以评估任务绩效、认知负荷与操作员态势感知。

Result: 同地共享界面条件任务完成最快（292.8 s），优于远程操作（351.6 s）和独立界面（385.1 s），碰撞最少（每轮0.9次），态势感知准确率最高（91.1%）。物理同地存在与认知负荷降低相关，NASA-TLX分数从同地条件的38.2升至远程操作的57.7，生理指标也一致支持该结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，同地存在与共享界面分别提供互补收益：同地存在支持工作负荷调节，共享界面支持相互态势感知，从而提升多操作员多机器人遥操作性能。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems that combine the complementary capabilities of specialised platforms are critical for complex missions in challenging, inaccessible environments. Current deployments assign an operator or operator team to each robot, with collaboration relying on operator-to-operator communication to manually coordinate heterogeneous platforms with incompatible interfaces. This operator-dependent approach increases cognitive load and limits scalability across robots from different manufacturers. This study designs and evaluates a shared-visualisation collaborative teleoperation interface to improve operator coordination, situational awareness, and the task performance in multi-operator, multi-robot systems. A comparative user study with 16 participants compared three teleoperation conditions: co-located operators with a shared interface, remote operators with a shared interface, and co-located operators with individual interfaces. Participants controlled two simulated robots in a nuclear power plant digital twin to complete disaster response tasks. Performance metrics, physiological data, and behavioural measures were recorded to assess task performance, cognitive load, and operator awareness across conditions. The co-located shared-interface condition achieved the fastest mission completion (292.8 s), outperforming remote operation (351.6 s) and individual interfaces (385.1 s), with the fewest collisions (0.9 per trial) and highest situational awareness accuracy (91.1 %). Physical co-presence was associated with reduced cognitive load, with NASA-TLX scores increasing from 38.2 in co-located to 57.7 in remote operation, supported by consistent physiological indicators. These results demonstrate that co-presence and shared interfaces provide complementary benefits, supporting workload regulation and mutual awareness respectively in multi-operator, multi-robot teleoperation.

</details>


### [202] [Barrier-Constrained Guidance For Real-time Asynchronous VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14997)
*Wenbo Yu,Jifan Li,Wenke Xia,Yuchen Li,Yizhuo Zhang,Pei Ren,Zhengping Che,Di hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FreeBar 是一种无需训练的实时推理插件，通过将流式 VLA 的分块对齐建模为屏障引导的动作空间约束，以极低额外推理成本实现稳健异步执行，并显著提升成功率与降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步执行虽能让 VLA 模型实时运行，但会在提交执行的动作流与预测的下一动作分块之间引入边界错位。现有延迟感知方案通常依赖大量重训练、架构修改或固定前缀 inpainting，在低延迟关键场景中计算开销过大。

Method: FreeBar 将基于 flow 的 VLA 中动作分块对齐转化为屏障引导的动作空间约束。它引入受控制屏障函数（CBF）启发的轻量一致性约束，约束相邻动作分块，并闭式求解校正量，避免通过 VLA 动作头进行昂贵反向传播。生成过程中逐步收紧一致性边界，使生成分块与已提交执行流对齐，同时保留早期生成灵活性与分块内未来动作灵活性。

Result: 在 Kinetix、RoboCasa 及多种真实机器人平台、多个 VLA 主干上，FreeBar 相比朴素异步执行平均提升成功率 10%–15%，在高动态场景中增益更大。它保持实时计算效率，每动作分块推理延迟比 RTC 降低超过 45%。作为通用插件，FreeBar 在不同延迟环境、基础策略和超参数设置下均表现稳健。

Conclusion: FreeBar 以无需训练、低开销、通用插件的形式有效缓解异步 VLA 执行中的动作分块边界错位问题，在提升任务成功率的同时保持实时推理效率，适合实际机器人部署。

Abstract: While asynchronous execution enables VLA models to operate in real-time environments by predicting the next chunk during execution, it inevitably introduces boundary misalignment with the committed action stream. Existing delay-aware solutions address this at the prohibitive cost of extensive retraining, architectural modifications, or fixed-prefix inpainting that inflates computation when low latency is vital. To address these problems, we propose FreeBar, a training-free real-time inference plugin that enables robust asynchronous execution with only marginal additional inference cost by casting chunk alignment in flow-based VLAs as barrier-guided action-space constraint. Specifically, FreeBar introduces a lightweight Control Barrier Function (CBF)-inspired consistency constraint between consecutive action chunks and solves the resulting correction in closed form, avoiding costly backpropagation through the VLA action head. By progressively tightening the consistency bound during generation, FreeBar aligns generated chunks with the committed execution stream while preserving early-stage generative and within-chunk future action flexibility,. Across Kinetix, RoboCasa, and diverse real-world robotic platforms with multiple VLA backbones, FreeBar improves success rates over naive asynchronous execution by 10%--15% on average, with larger gains in highly dynamic scenarios. It also preserves real-time computational efficiency, decreasing inference latency per action chunk over 45% than RTC. Additionally, as a universal plugin, FreeBar remains robust across distinct latency environments, base policies, and hyperparameter settings.

</details>


### [203] [EnactOpt: Diagnosis-Guided Harness Evolution for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14977)
*ZhiYuan Feng,Zhuoshi Pan,Zhaolu Kang,Zhongxia Chen,Shuo Yang,Sen Ye,Heng Wang,Han Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EnactOpt 通过有限执行反馈联合演化可复用文本技能与上下文构建代码，在不更新底层模型和运行时的前提下改进具身智能体的执行框架，提升任务成功率并展现出泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于基础模型的具身智能体依赖执行框架来组织观测、引导动作并利用执行反馈；失败不仅源于模型能力，也源于框架如何呈现证据和引导使用。此类模型外部机制可作为无需参数更新的适配目标，但任务特定交互经验收集代价高；仅凭任务级结果难以区分相关证据是缺失于模型输入还是已提供但未被有效使用，且证据呈现与动作引导相互耦合。

Method: 提出 EnactOpt：利用有限执行反馈，在固定底层模型与运行时的情况下，联合演化可复用文本技能和上下文构建代码。它将动作反馈与实际模型输入及后续动作相关联，以诊断相邻决策；通过结构化情节摘要和提示级编辑算子指导联合修订，并对候选执行框架进行兼容性检查与基于验证的保留筛选。

Result: 在 VLABench、ALFWorld 和 Embodied Agent Interface (EAI) 上，EnactOpt 相较初始执行框架将任务成功率提升约 10–27 个百分点，并优于所评估的 GEPA、TextGrad 和 Trace2Skill 基线；增益还扩展到未用于执行框架演化或候选筛选的任务条件。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过联合优化证据呈现方式与基于证据的行动方式，可将有限的执行经验转化为可复用的行为改进，为具身智能体适应提供了一条不依赖模型参数更新的互补路径。

Abstract: Embodied agents built on foundation models rely on an execution harness to organize observations, guide actions, and incorporate execution feedback into subsequent decisions. Failures can arise not only from limited model capabilities, but also from how the harness presents evidence and guides its use. These model-external mechanisms provide an adaptation target without updating model parameters, but task-specific interaction experience can be costly to collect. Task-level outcomes alone do not reveal whether relevant evidence was missing from the model input or was available but inadequately used. Moreover, these two aspects are coupled: effective action guidance may require evidence that the current context omits, while exposing that evidence alone does not ensure an appropriate response. We introduce EnactOpt, which uses limited execution feedback to jointly evolve reusable textual skills and context-construction code while keeping the underlying models and runtime fixed. It diagnoses adjacent decisions by relating action feedback to the actual model input and subsequent action. Structured episode summaries guide joint revisions through prompt-level edit operators, and candidate harnesses undergo compatibility checks and validation-based retention. Across VLABench, ALFWorld, and Embodied Agent Interface (EAI), EnactOpt improves task success by approximately 10-27 percentage points over the initial harnesses and outperforms the evaluated GEPA, TextGrad, and Trace2Skill baselines. These gains extend to task conditions not used for harness evolution or candidate selection. The results support a complementary route to embodied adaptation: turning limited execution experience into reusable behavioral improvements by jointly refining how evidence is presented and acted upon.

</details>


### [204] [FoundationVIO: Enforcing Metric Scale in Feed-forward Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14951)
*Jin Rhee,Yuhao Zhang,Frank Dellaert,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FoundationVIO，将惯性传感与几何基础模型（GFM）预测在因子图中融合，以恢复度量尺度，并构建实时视觉惯性 SLAM 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFM 能直接预测稠密几何与相机位姿，但预测的几何尺度模糊：独立预测仅局部一致且为 up-to-scale，缺乏直接物理测量约束；即使训练时已知尺度，也不能直接测量尺度。因此，基于 GFM 的 SLAM 轨迹与重建会继承该尺度问题。

Method: 受经典视觉惯性里程计启发，FoundationVIO 将 IMU 惯性测量与 GFM 预测通过因子图优化融合，恢复度量尺度重建；并加入轻量级基于 GFM 的回环检测机制，形成完整视觉惯性 SLAM 系统。

Result: 在三个具有知名传感器系统的数据集上评估：里程计估计与经典最先进 VIO 方法相当，稠密重建优于 GFM-enabled SLAM 方法；加入回环后能获得全局一致的位姿与重建。方法兼容常见相机与 IMU，在消费级笔记本 GPU 上约实时运行。

Conclusion: FoundationVIO 通过惯性信息解决 GFM 尺度模糊问题，实现度量尺度、全局准确的视觉惯性 SLAM，并具备实际部署潜力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Feed-forward geometric foundation models (GFMs) directly predict dense geometry and camera poses from batches of images. Emerging visual SLAM methods chain together consecutive GFM batches to form dense point cloud maps. A core challenge is how to address the ambiguous geometric scale of GFM predictions: independent predictions are locally consistent up-to-scale but not constrained by any direct physical measurements. Models trained on data with known scale improve the consistency of GFM reconstructions but again do not directly measure it. Consequently, trajectories and 3D reconstructions from GFM-enabled SLAM methods inherit this issue. Motivated by parallels with classical visual-inertial odometry, we introduce FoundationVIO which integrates inertial sensing with GFM predictions to recover metric scale reconstruction using a factor-graph based approach. We demonstrate odometry estimates competitive with classical state-of-the-art VIO methods and accurate dense reconstructions that outperform GFM-enabled SLAM methods. We also add a minimal GFM-based loop-closure mechanism to create a full Visual-Inertial SLAM system capable of creating globally accurate reconstructions and pose estimates. We evaluate our method on three datasets with well known sensor systems and demonstrate its compatibility on ubiquitous camera and IMU sensors. The method runs at approximately real-time on a consumer-grade laptop GPU. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [205] [CFG-Policy: Unified Diffusion Policy Learning Framework via Classifier-free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14936)
*Shutong Ding,Zhaoyang Liu,Yimiao Zhou,Jingya Wang,Jiayuan Gu,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CFG-Policy 通过无分类器引导组合探索与利用两个扩散策略，在提升强化学习任务性能的同时保持行为多样性和预训练能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在机器人控制、视觉-语言-动作模型和世界模型智能体中日益普及，其多模态动作分布有利于强化学习探索。但用强化学习优化扩散策略会降低行为多样性并限制探索能力；后训练中直接微调预训练策略可能破坏已获得的能力，而动作空间残差又缺乏多模态探索所需表达力。在线与离线到在线扩散强化学习共同面临的核心挑战是：在提升任务性能的同时，保留持续适应所需的行为多样性与能力。

Method: 提出 CFG-Policy，一个统一、即插即用的框架，将探索与利用分离为两个生成策略，并在采样时通过无分类器引导组合其 score 或 velocity fields。在线强化学习中，冻结的探索分支维持行为多样性，利用分支从任务奖励中学习；离线到在线强化学习中，预训练的利用分支保持冻结，可训练分支引导组合行为。该方法保留底层强化学习目标。

Result: 在 5 个 MuJoCo 任务上结合 QSM 与 QVPO 提升性能；在 4 个 RoboTwin 操作任务上结合 Flow-SDE+PPO 提升性能。

Conclusion: CFG-Policy 能统一应对在线与离线到在线扩散强化学习中性能提升与行为多样性、能力保持之间的权衡，即插即用，保留原始强化学习目标并取得更优表现。

Abstract: Diffusion policies are increasingly used in robotic control and manipulation, vision-language-action models, and world-model-based agents. Their ability to represent multimodal action distributions offers rich opportunities for exploration in reinforcement learning (RL). However, optimizing diffusion policies with RL can reduce the behavioral diversity and further limit their exploration capability. In RL post-training, directly fine-tuning a pretrained policy may also disrupt its acquired capabilities, while action-space residuals may lack the expressive power for multimodal exploration. Together, these issues reveal a common challenge across online and offline-to-online diffusion RL: improving task performance while retaining the behavioral diversity and capabilities needed for continued adaptation. We introduce CFG-Policy, a unified, plug-and-play framework that separates exploration and exploitation into two generative policies and combines their score or velocity fields during sampling through classifier-free guidance. For online RL, a frozen exploration branch sustains behavioral diversity while an exploitation branch learns from task rewards; for offline-to-online RL, a pretrained exploitation branch remains frozen while a trainable branch steers the composed behavior. CFG-Policy preserves the underlying RL objectives and improves performance with QSM and QVPO on 5 MuJoCo tasks and with Flow-SDE+PPO on 4 RoboTwin manipulation tasks. Project page is at this https URL .

</details>


### [206] [Edge-Native Temporal--Graph Dual-Expert Inference for Dexterous Grasp Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14926)
*Jiajian Zheng,Bin Zhang,Wen Fan,Yanan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出仅用内置关节角与预校准执行器负载反馈的边缘原生DualExpert管线，实现多指独立Stop预测与物体识别，并在Flash上保持高精度、低延迟与低功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指手接触物体时间不同，全局闭合命令会使早接触手指过载，而其他手指仍未接触；密集触觉阵列虽可解决该问题，却增加硬件、校准、带宽与计算负担。

Method: 提出边缘原生管线，仅用内置关节角和预使用校准的执行器负载反馈识别被抓物体，并为每根手指预测独立Stop事件。以4个采样及其一阶差分构成4×20接触演化窗口。核心为异构双专家DualExpert：TCN建模短期接触演化并提供全部5个Stop概率；FGFN编码手指拓扑，仅参与物体分类。采用固定0.7/0.3类别概率融合。

Result: 在225个留出试验上达到93.78%轨迹级分类准确率，较单独TCN和FGFN分别提升4.00和2.22个百分点；TCN Stop路径F1为95.25%。数据集包含5个物体实例的1500条自动采集轨迹。Flash部署中，混合加速器执行保持93.78%分类准确率和94.84% Stop F1，平均推理时间2.890 ms，平均样本到停止延迟9.172 ms。50次物理试验中识别率96.00%、抓取成功率98.00%，完整嵌入式板功耗约0.80 W。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅依赖本体关节角与执行器负载反馈即可实现多指独立停止预测和物体识别，兼顾分类/Stop精度、推理延迟与功耗，适合边缘部署的多指抓取系统。

Abstract: Multi-finger hands contact objects at different times, so a global close command can overload early-contact fingers while others are still moving. Dense tactile arrays address this problem but add hardware, calibration, bandwidth, and computation. We present an edge-native pipeline that uses only built-in joint-angle and pre-use-calibrated actuator-load feedback to recognize the grasped object and predict an independent Stop event for each finger. Four samples and their first differences form a 4-by-20 contact-evolution window. At its core is a heterogeneous dual-expert architecture, termed DualExpert, that assigns complementary tasks to a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) and a Finger Graph Filter Network (FGFN). The TCN models short-term contact evolution and supplies all five Stop probabilities, while the FGFN encodes finger topology and contributes only to object classification. Fixed 0.7/0.3 class-probability fusion reaches 93.78\% trajectory-level classification accuracy on 225 held-out trials, improving the standalone TCN and FGFN by 4.00 and 2.22 percentage points, respectively. The TCN Stop path attains 95.25\% F1. The dataset contains 1,500 automatically collected trajectories from five object instances. In Flash deployment, mixed accelerator execution retains 93.78\% classification accuracy and 94.84\% Stop F1 with 2.890~ms mean inference time and 9.172~ms mean sample-to-stop latency. Across 50 physical trials, the system achieves 96.00\% recognition and 98.00\% grasp success while the complete embedded board draws approximately 0.80~W.

</details>


### [207] [Funnel planning and control for manipulators using neural Signed Configuration Distance Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14917)
*Bernhard Wullt,Christos K. Verginis,Per Mattsson,Mikael Norrlöf,Thomas B. Schön*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于神经符号配置距离函数（nSCDF）的漏斗规划与控制方法，提升机械臂鲁棒运动规划的效率和避障性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KDF规划器虽具鲁棒性和漏斗内无碰撞保证，但漏斗计算开销大，限制其表达能力和性能；需要高效计算无碰撞球与梯度，并兼顾路径长度与间隙。

Method: 提出nSCDF以高效估计无碰撞球和梯度；在规划器中利用nSCDF生成平衡路径长度与间隙的名义轨迹；设计利用nSCDF几何信息的漏斗控制器，跟踪轨迹并保证避障。

Result: 数值实验中，名义路径生成快2倍，路径长度缩短20%，间隙提高5倍；使用nSCDF进行漏斗控制还改善了运动性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: nSCDF能有效克服KDF漏斗计算瓶颈，实现更高效、更安全且更鲁棒的机械臂规划与控制。

Abstract: Robust motion planning and control for manipulators is the problem of guaranteeing safe execution and collision-free motion under disturbances. The Kinodynamic Motion Planning via Funnel Control (KDF) is a recently proposed planner that comes with robustness guarantees and is particularly attractive for collision-free control as it guarantees closed-loop evolution inside a funnel, i.e., a collision-free region around a nominal trajectory. Despite its attractive theoretical properties, the current approach for computing funnels is computationally demanding, which limits its expressiveness and performance. We address its current limitations by proposing a novel funnel planning and control solution. Our key tool to enable this is a neural Signed Configuration Distance Function (nSCDF) which allows us to estimate collision-free balls and gradients in a computationally efficient way. We use the nSCDF in our planner to compute a nominal trajectory, where path length and clearance are balanced. To track the trajectory with collision-avoidance guarantees, we design a funnel controller that exploits the geometric information from the nSCDF. From numerical experiments, we observe that our planner produces nominal paths $2$ times faster, while at the same time obtaining $20 \%$ shorter path length and $5$ times the clearance. Finally, we also observe improved motion performance and robustness properties when using the nSCDF for funnel control.

</details>


### [208] [Scalable Multi-Human Cooperative Manipulation via Commander-Executor Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14911)
*Buzhen Huang,Xingchen Wu,Yuanwang Yang,Chenyi Xie,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出层级式指挥官-执行器框架，用于多智能体角色依赖、长时程、顺序协作的人形物体操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体协作操作多能处理简单协同搬运任务，难以应对角色依赖、长时程和顺序协作操作等复杂场景。

Method: 框架包含指挥官与执行器：指挥官融合指令和场景信息进行语义与几何联合规划，将任务分解为角色特定子任务，并生成以物体为中心的共享协调计划，指定各智能体的执行时序、目标位置和操作动作；执行器依据高层策略与局部观测，通过单人到协作课程学习底层物理策略，实现协调的人-物交互，其中物体轨迹提供共享协调目标，根位置与抓取指令提供角色依赖执行线索。

Result: 在多种物体操作任务上评估，方法能执行组合不同协作类型的长时程任务，并从双智能体扩展到多智能体协作；大量实验表明其成功率优于当前最优方法。

Conclusion: 所提层级指挥官-执行器框架能有效实现复杂多智能体协作物体操作，具备长时程、角色依赖和可扩展性优势，代码将公开。

Abstract: Multi-human cooperative object manipulation is a highly challenging topic in humanoid control. While existing methods can handle simple collaborative carrying tasks, they struggle with role-dependent, long-horizon, and sequential cooperative manipulation. To address this issue, we propose a hierarchical commander-executor framework that enables multiple agents to accomplish complex collaborative tasks in distinct ways. Specifically, a commander combines instructions and scene information for joint semantic and geometric planning. It then decomposes the task into role-specific subtasks and generates shared object-centric coordination plans, specifying execution timing, target positions, and manipulation actions for each agent. Conditioned on the commander's high-level strategy and each agent's local observations, the executor learns a low-level physics-based policy through a solo-to-cooperative curriculum to realize coordinated human-object interactions. The planned object trajectory provides a shared coordination target, while root positions and grasping instructions specify role-dependent execution cues. We evaluate our method on a variety of object manipulation tasks, demonstrating its ability to execute long-horizon tasks that combine different types of collaboration and to scale from two-agent to multi-agent cooperation. Extensive experiments further show that our approach achieves higher success rates compared to state-of-the-art methods. Code will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [209] [Test-Time Compute for Humanoid Behavior Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14896)
*Weiji Xie,Junsong Wu,Zibo Zhou,Yueru Chen,Xingyi Wang,Zhenhua Xiong,Weinan Zhang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出测试时命令优化 TTCO，通过测试时计算适配人形行为基础模型 BFMs 的输入命令，缩小命令-执行差距，提升全身位置精度，并可生成训练高层 loco-manipulation 策略所需的动作标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形行为基础模型 BFMs 虽能执行复杂动作，但在 loco-manipulation 等下游任务中存在系统性的命令-执行差距，即实际执行动作偏离期望动作，且现有方法通常需要重训练。

Method: TTCO 在测试时对输入命令进行采样优化，通过 BFM 与机器人动力学的闭环仿真搜索使执行轨迹更接近期望运动的命令；进一步分析测试时计算缩放，推导近似计算最优策略；并利用记录到的机器人轨迹生成 BFM 特定动作标签，无需原始遥操作人体运动命令。

Result: 在 AMASS 数据集上的三个 BFMs 中，TTCO 将模拟中的平均全局身体位置误差降低 79-84%；测试时计算增加可持续带来性能增益；仿真与真机实验表明生成的标签可有效训练高层 loco-manipulation 策略，并支持跨 BFMs 复用已有演示。

Conclusion: 测试时计算可在不重训练 BFM 的情况下改善命令执行精度，TTCO 为提升人形全身 loco-manipulation 提供有效途径，同时支持动作标签生成与跨模型演示复用。

Abstract: Humanoid Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) can execute impressive motions, but downstream applications such as loco-manipulation demand high whole-body positional accuracy. Existing BFMs suffer from a systematic command-execution gap, where executed motions deviate from desired ones. We show that additional test-time compute can improve execution without retraining the BFM. First, we introduce Test-Time Command Optimization (TTCO), which adapts the input command to the specific BFM to produce an executed trajectory closer to the desired motion. TTCO finds such commands by sampling-based optimization through closed-loop simulations of the BFM and robot dynamics. Across three BFMs on the AMASS dataset, TTCO reduces mean global body-position error by 79-84% in simulation. Second, we analyze test-time compute scaling and derive an approximate compute-optimal strategy that can sustain performance gains as the compute budget increases. Third, TTCO can generate BFM-specific action labels from recorded robot trajectories without requiring the original teleoperation body-motion commands. Simulation and real-robot experiments show that these labels are effective for training high-level loco-manipulation policies. This method also supports reusing existing demonstrations across BFMs.

</details>


### [210] [LETS: Low-Overhead External-Torque Sensing for Force-Feedback Bilateral Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14889)
*Zonghao Guo,Dongchen Guan,Yubo Wang,Peng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LETS：一种物理引导学习的外力矩估计方法，用于双边遥操作力反馈；仅用URDF和关节反馈，将刚体动力学与学习残差结合，实现实时、低成本且更准确的外力矩估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边遥操作中的力反馈日益重要，而外力矩估计质量直接影响其性能。商用机械臂厂商提供的外力矩估计往往不准确，使用末端六维力/力矩传感器又会增加成本与负载。

Method: LETS将刚体动力学分配给递归牛顿-欧拉逆动力学，仅用学习补偿机器人特定残差。方法仅依赖机器人URDF模型和关节反馈，刻意不使用控制指令，从而将外力矩估计与控制输入解耦；无需专用末端六轴力/力矩传感器，使用12分钟自由运动数据即可在1分钟内完成训练。

Result: 在双边遥操作中，LETS实现实时外力矩估计并支持稳定力反馈。其自由空间误差比厂商估计低69%，显著减少主从端耦合振荡；接触误差低28%，提供更准确的触觉反馈。

Conclusion: LETS是一种无需额外力/力矩传感器、训练高效且精度更高的物理引导学习外力矩估计方案，可有效提升双边遥操作力反馈的稳定性与透明性。

Abstract: Bilateral teleoperation with force feedback is becoming increasingly important, and the quality of external-torque estimation is critical to this process. However, manufacturer-provided disturbance-torque estimates on commercial robotic arms are often inaccurate, while the use of end-effector force/torque sensors adds cost and payload. We propose LETS, a physics-guided learning approach that assigns rigid-body dynamics to recursive Newton-Euler inverse dynamics and reserves learning for only the robot-specific residual. LETS uses only the robot's URDF model and joint feedback, deliberately excluding control commands and thereby decoupling external-torque estimation from control-command inputs. It trains on 12 minutes of free-motion data in under one minute, without requiring a dedicated end-effector six-axis force/torque sensor. In bilateral teleoperation, LETS achieves real-time external-torque estimation that enables stable force feedback. Its free-space error is 69% lower than the manufacturer-provided estimate, substantially reducing coupled oscillations between leader and follower; its contact error is 28% lower, yielding more accurate haptic feedback.

</details>


### [211] [Verify, Act, Distill: Turning Test-Time Compute into Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14885)
*Kaneyoshi Hiratsuka,Satoshi Yamamori,Jun Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Verify, Act, Distill 框架，将测试时计算（TTC）的验证收益通过蒸馏内化到视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中，从而提升无验证器单候选生成性能，并进一步放大后续 TTC 的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TTC 方法在推理时对多个候选动作评分，能提升 VLA 执行性能，但策略保持冻结，因此每次执行都需重新计算收益，无法将改进固化到模型中。

Method: 固定验证器对 flow-matching VLA 生成的候选动作打分，执行加权聚合结果；将成功适应回合中的观测和聚合动作块，以标准 flow-matching 损失直接蒸馏到 LoRA 参数中。该方法复用 TTC 执行中已产生的监督，无需额外强化学习或专门为教师生成 rollouts。

Result: 在 LIBERO 40 个任务和 SmolVLA 5k 检查点上，每任务仅用 10 个回合蒸馏，即将无验证器单候选生成的平均成功率从 54.1% 提升到 70.6%，保留约 74% 的八候选聚合改进。对蒸馏后策略应用相同聚合，成功率从 76.4% 提升到 82.3%，LIBERO-Long 从 44.0% 提升到 57.8%，单候选策略在含分布偏移的 LIBERO-plus 上也有提升。

Conclusion: 将验证作为学习信号而非仅作为执行时选择器，既能保留 TTC 的改进，又能重新放大后续 TTC 的效果。

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC), which scores multiple candidate actions at inference time, is a powerful way to improve the execution performance of vision-language-action (VLA) models, but existing methods keep the policy frozen, so the gain must be recomputed at every execution. We propose Verify, Act, Distill, a framework that consolidates the gains of TTC into the VLA itself. A fixed verifier scores candidates generated by a flow-matching VLA, the weighted aggregate is executed, and the observations and aggregated chunks from successful adaptation episodes are distilled directly into LoRA parameters with the standard flow-matching loss. This reuses supervision that TTC has already produced during execution, without additional reinforcement learning or rollouts dedicated to teacher generation. On the 40 LIBERO tasks with a SmolVLA 5k checkpoint, distilling from only 10 episodes per task raises the mean success rate of verifier-free single-candidate generation from 54.1% to 70.6%, retaining about 74% of the improvement obtained by aggregating eight candidates. Applying the same aggregation to the distilled policy further raises the success rate from 76.4% to 82.3%, with a particularly large gain on LIBERO-Long (44.0% to 57.8%), and the single-candidate policy also improves on LIBERO-plus, which contains distribution shifts. These results show that using verification as a learning signal, rather than only as an execution-time selector, both retains the improvements of TTC and re-amplifies the effect of subsequent TTC.

</details>


### [212] [Predict in Chunks, Revise in Spans: History-conditioned Action Regeneration for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14864)
*Chenyang Wang,Yuntian Wang,Jiaying Tang,Chunzheng Li,Yang Liu,Siao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CMAP 是一种视觉运动模仿学习策略，通过基于历史条件与不确定性引导的局部片段重生成来精炼连续动作块，避免全局重生成，并在仿真与真实操作任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块通过联合预测未来动作序列提升时序一致性，但现有策略在仅局部出错时仍会重新生成整个动作块，可能覆盖原本有效的预测。

Method: 提出 Contextual Masked Action Policy（CMAP）。其核心是历史条件动作带：将已执行命令与掩码的未来位置结合，生成粗略动作预测和逐步不确定性分数；随后不确定性引导的跨度重生成机制选择高不确定性的连续片段，并以已执行历史和保留的未来预测为上下文重建该片段。通过联合重建邻近动作建模局部时序依赖，同时保留所选片段之外的预测。

Result: 在九个仿真和四个真实世界操作任务上，CMAP 的整体性能优于多个竞争基线；消融实验验证了关键组件的互补贡献；留出轨迹分析表明局部重生成降低了所选片段内的预测误差。

Conclusion: CMAP 表明，面向动作块的局部、不确定性引导重生成能有效精炼预测并提升视觉运动模仿学习性能，同时避免不必要的全局替换。

Abstract: Action chunking improves temporal coherence in visuomotor imitation learning by jointly predicting sequences of future actions. However, many existing policies regenerate entire action chunks even when errors are localized, potentially replacing otherwise valid predictions. We introduce Contextual Masked Action Policy (CMAP), a visuomotor policy that refines continuous action chunks through history-conditioned local regeneration. Specifically, CMAP introduces a history-conditioned action tape that combines executed commands with masked future positions to produce coarse action predictions and per-step uncertainty scores. An uncertainty-guided span regeneration mechanism then selects a high-uncertainty contiguous span and reconstructs it using the executed history and retained future predictions as context. By jointly reconstructing neighboring actions, CMAP models local temporal dependencies while preserving all predictions outside the selected span. Extensive experiments on nine simulated and four real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that CMAP achieves superior overall performance over several competitive baselines. Ablation studies validate the complementary contributions of its key components, while held-out trajectory analysis shows that local regeneration reduces prediction errors within selected spans.

</details>


### [213] [DEFT-VLA: Stage-Localized Force Specialization for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14854)
*Zan Huang,Yibo Qiu,Kan Ding,Xuanping Zhang,Ao Tian,Ronald X Xu,Mingzhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DEFT-VLA，通过双专家与接触局部化力注意力课程，在接触丰富操作中按阶段融合视觉与力觉，提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作存在阶段依赖的感官冲突：接近阶段依赖视觉与本体感觉，力觉信息少且可能干扰；接触后力觉关键，但无关视觉细节与相关机器人状态可能成为捷径。因此需将力条件适应仅作用于接触阶段。

Method: DEFT-VLA保留任务适配的基专家处理视觉主导的非接触行为，引入力感知接触专家处理关键接触阶段，将力条件适应局部化。进一步提出接触局部化力注意力课程：仅对接触专家进行力注意力后训练，逐步恢复视觉并掩蔽状态token以抑制捷径。

Result: 在移液枪头安装、双臂移液枪吸液和白板擦拭的真实机器人实验中，DEFT-VLA有效利用力反馈提升接触阶段性能，同时保持强非接触行为和对留出抓取高度的鲁棒性；每个任务总体成功率较最强基线提升20-40个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将力条件适应局部化到接触阶段并配合课程抑制捷径，能有效解决阶段依赖的感官冲突，提升接触丰富操作的整体成功率。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation poses a stage-dependent sensory conflict: vision and proprioception are essential for localization and approach, when force is largely uninformative and potentially distracting, whereas force becomes critical after contact, when irrelevant visual details and correlated robot states may become shortcuts. We propose DEFT-VLA (Dual-Expert Force-specialized Training for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation), which retains the task-adapted base expert for vision-dominated non-contact behavior and introduces a force-aware contact expert for critical contact stages, localizing force-conditioned adaptation to where it is needed. We further introduce a contact-localized force-attending curriculum that applies force-attending post-training only to the contact expert, progressively restoring vision and masking state tokens to suppress shortcuts. Real-robot experiments on pipette tip attachment, bimanual pipette aspiration, and whiteboard wiping show that DEFT-VLA effectively exploits force feedback to improve contact-stage performance while maintaining strong non-contact behavior and robustness to held-out grasp heights. It improves overall task success by 20-40 percentage points over the strongest baseline on each task.

</details>


### [214] [Learning Dynamic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14848)
*Nga Kiu Kelly Ho,Yilin Liu,Hao Xu,Niloy J. Mitra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从单一初始手-物状态生成完整4D抓取序列的框架，解耦手部运动生成与物体运动恢复：用条件流匹配预测序列级接触图和手姿态，再通过无训练、接触引导加权配准恢复刚体物体运动。在DexYCB和Open-CHOIR上，相比LatentHOI运动更平滑、野外接触与穿透指标持平或更优，每帧约快30倍，15人研究中约2/3强制成对比较偏好本方法；但新几何上的精确接触闭合仍是关键开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成的逼真4D手-物抓取序列对角色动画、扩展现实和具身智能体很重要。抓取需要手连贯地接近、接触并抬升或操作物体。现有方法通常只建模最终静态抓取，或依赖语言、目标抓取、物体轨迹等额外条件来指定交互。本文研究更欠约束的问题：仅从单一初始手-物状态生成完整抓取运动。

Method: 框架将手部运动生成与物体运动恢复解耦。输入为初始腕坐标系下的物体点云和初始参数化手配置；条件流匹配预测序列级接触图以及每个归一化序列时间上的手姿态。该时间索引公式表示完整运动，同时避免自回归展开的误差累积。随后推断附着帧，并通过无训练、接触引导的加权配准恢复刚体物体运动。

Result: 在留出的DexYCB物体以及Open-CHOIR从野外视频重建的未见物体几何上评估。与LatentHOI相比，本方法在所有设定中生成更平滑的手和物体运动；在共享初始化下，野外接触和穿透指标持平或更优，并且每生成帧约快30倍。15名参与者的强制选择成对比较中，约三分之二偏好本方法。新几何上的精确接触闭合仍是关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效从单一初始手-物状态生成完整抓取序列，在平滑性、效率和用户偏好方面表现良好；但泛化到新物体几何时，精确接触闭合尚未解决，是未来工作的重要方向。

Abstract: Synthesizing believable 4D hand-object grasping sequences is important for character animation, extended reality, and embodied agents. Grasping requires a hand to approach, contact, and lift or manipulate an object coherently. Existing synthesis methods often model only the final static grasp or rely on additional conditions, such as language, target grasps, or object trajectories, to specify the interaction. We instead study the underconstrained problem of generating a complete grasping motion from a single initial hand-object state. Our framework decouples hand-motion generation from object-motion recovery. Given an object point cloud in the initial wrist frame and the initial parameterized hand configuration, conditional flow matching predicts a sequence-level contact map and a hand pose at each normalized sequence time. This time-indexed formulation avoids the error accumulation of autoregressive rollout while representing the complete motion. We then infer an attachment frame and recover rigid-object motion through training-free, contact-guided weighted registration. We evaluate on held-out DexYCB objects and unseen object geometries reconstructed from in-the-wild videos by Open-CHOIR. Compared with LatentHOI, our method produces smoother hand and object motion in every setting, matches or improves on its contact and penetration metrics in the wild under a shared initialization, and is about 30x faster per generated frame. In a 15-participant study, our motions were preferred in roughly two thirds of (forced-choice pairwise) comparisons. Precise contact closure on novel geometry, however, remains a key open challenge.

</details>


### [215] [Push the Limit: Learning High-Speed Sprinting and Aggressive Cornering on a Full-Scale Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14840)
*Huaxing Huang,Ruidong Li,Jinchao Han,Ziyang Dong,Wenhao Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种学习框架，使全尺寸双足人形机器人以超过7 m/s的速度冲刺，并以躯干侧倾达25°完成高速急转；通过因果多头注意力、航向方向奖励与技能条件多对抗运动先验判别器，解决高速步态中的高频偏航振荡和技能先验冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约4 m/s以上，周期性脚部冲击会引发高速步态固有的高频偏航率振荡，破坏 flat-MLP 策略依赖的本体感觉反馈历史，并使传统偏航率跟踪奖励与机器人自然振荡动力学相冲突；此外，单一共享风格先验无法区分被指令技能，并损害低速技能保真度。

Method: 采用因果多头注意力（MHA）显式建模高频时序反馈；用航向方向奖励替代振荡偏航率，仅监督低频速度方向，从而在高速下实现敏捷转向而不抑制自然步态振荡；引入技能条件多对抗运动先验（MAMP）判别器，使风格奖励以被指令技能为条件。

Result: 消融实验确认各机制均关键：以平坦历史MLP替代结构化时序编码会限制最高速度；回归偏航率跟踪会阻碍高速转向；单一共享判别器会使原地转向退化为准静态站立。该框架在仿真与真机部署中实现了稳定高速冲刺和激进转弯。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了原始冲刺速度与参考运动保真度之间的差距，证明双足人形机器人可在保持自然步态的同时完成高速冲刺和高速急转。

Abstract: We present a learning-based framework that enables a full-scale bipedal humanoid to sprint at over 7 m/s with motion-prior-guided gait and perform aggressive cornering at high speed with a pronounced trunk lean of up to $25^\circ$. Achieving this combination is challenging: above approximately 4 m/s, periodic foot impacts induce severe, high-frequency yaw-rate oscillations intrinsic to high-speed gaits. This high-frequency temporal structure corrupts the history of proprioceptive feedback that flat-MLP policies rely on, while rendering conventional yaw-rate tracking rewards counterproductive, as they fight against the robot's natural oscillatory dynamics. Moreover, a single style prior shared across skills cannot tell which skill is commanded and inherently compromises the low-speed skill fidelity. To overcome these limitations, we adopt a causal multi-head attention (MHA) module to explicitly model the high-frequency temporal feedback. In place of the oscillatory yaw rate, we design a heading-direction reward that supervises only the low-frequency velocity direction, allowing agile turning at high speed without suppressing natural gait oscillations. We further introduce skill-conditioned multi-adversarial-motion-prior (MAMP) discriminators that condition the style reward on the commanded skill. Ablation experiments confirm that each mechanism is essential: replacing structured temporal encoding with a flat-history MLP caps the top speed; reverting to yaw-rate tracking prevents high-speed turning; and a single shared discriminator makes in-place turning collapse into a quasi-static stance. Our framework bridges the gap between raw sprinting speed and reference-motion fidelity, demonstrating stable high-speed sprinting and aggressive cornering in both simulation and real-robot deployment.

</details>


### [216] [Scaling Latent Motor Adaptation to Coordinated Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14833)
*Seung-Bin Joo,Alessandro Albini,Luca Wiehe,Perla Maiolino,Daniel Olmeda Reino,João F. Henriques*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究双臂RMA能否将基于潜在动力学的自适应策略从单臂扩展到协调双臂操作，在ManiSkill接触丰富任务中验证鲁棒性并实现直接sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人操作中物体质量、摩擦、尺度和形状不确定会导致固定假设下训练的策略性能退化；RMA等方法通过交互历史推断潜在动力学来适应，但其能否扩展到高维控制、耦合接触和共享物体受力的双臂场景尚不明确。

Method: 在三个接触丰富的ManiSkill任务上评估双臂RMA，随机化YCB物体属性；策略使用观测-动作历史和双腕深度图像进行潜在条件化，并与无潜在条件、其他设置匹配的stationary PPO对比；同时在双Franka提升任务上测试无需真实世界微调的直接sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 双臂RMA在双臂抓取和提升任务上保持接近privileged policy的性能，而stationary PPO成功率低；所有方法在长时程堆叠任务上仍受限；双Franka提升任务实现了直接sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 潜在条件化为将鲁棒控制扩展到协调双臂系统提供了有效结构，但长时程任务仍需额外的时序结构。

Abstract: Robust manipulation under uncertain object dynamics and geometry remains a core challenge in real-world robotics. Policies trained under fixed assumptions often degrade when object mass, friction, scale, or shape vary. Embedding-based adaptation methods such as Rapid Motor Adaptation (RMA) address this by conditioning actions on latent representations of environment dynamics inferred from interaction history. We investigate whether this paradigm scales from single arm settings to coordinated bimanual manipulation, where higher dimensional control, coupled contacts, and shared object forces introduce additional complexity. We evaluate bimanual RMA on three contact-rich ManiSkill tasks with randomized YCB object properties. Bimanual RMA, using observation-action history and dual wrist depth images, retains privileged policy performance on bimanual picking and lifting, while an otherwise matched stationary PPO policy without latent conditioning achieves low success; all methods remain limited on long-horizon stacking. We further demonstrate direct sim-to-real transfer on a dual Franka lifting task without real-world finetuning. Together, these results suggest that latent conditioning provides useful structure for scaling robust control to coordinated bimanual systems, while longer horizon tasks require additional temporal structure.

</details>


### [217] [Beyond Fixed Visual History: Learning Motion-Adaptive Temporal Receptive Fields for Complex-Terrain Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14753)
*Hao Zhang,Yuping Yan,Yitian Hong,Luying Feng,Tianhong Wang,Fengqi Dai,Wei Chen,Yaochu Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TempoLoco 是一种体感知动态视觉感知框架，根据四足机器人当前运动状态组织视觉记忆：从长深度缓存中学习多个互补时间视图，在共享特征空间编码，并利用本体感觉历史与多尺度地形证据动态聚合，从而让单一策略在复杂地形中按运动能力对齐视觉预判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉控制器通常以固定时间模式组织深度历史，忽略同一时间跨度会随速度、姿态和步态相位变化而对应不同空间范围；因此同一视觉历史在某些运动状态下不足，在另一些状态下过时或错位。

Method: 提出 TempoLoco：从长深度缓冲区学习多个互补的时间视图，在共享特征空间中编码，并基于本体感觉历史和多尺度地形证据进行动态聚合，实现体感知的动态视觉记忆；由单一策略执行，覆盖楼梯、平台、间隙、离散障碍、粗糙地面和斜坡等地形。

Result: 实验显示 TempoLoco 成功率达 78.4%，优于最强固定历史基线的 58.2% 和统一多尺度融合的 62.1%；在九类地形上完成 41/45 次仿真试验，并在搭载固定臂的 Unitree B2 上完成 23/25 次真实世界试验。

Conclusion: 体感知动态视觉感知能有效对齐视觉预判与机器人当前运动能力，提升复杂地形下四足运动的鲁棒性与可部署性。

Abstract: Versatile quadruped locomotion depends on a tight coupling between what the robot perceives and what its body can execute. Yet existing visual controllers typically organize depth history using fixed temporal patterns, overlooking that the same temporal span corresponds to different spatial ranges as the robot's speed, posture, and gait phase change. Consequently, the same visual history may be insufficient in one motion state but outdated or misaligned in another. To address this limitation, we propose TempoLoco, a body-aware dynamic visual perception framework that organizes visual memory according to the robot's current locomotion state. TempoLoco learns multiple complementary temporal views from a long depth buffer, encodes them within a shared feature space, and dynamically aggregates them using proprioceptive history and multi-scale terrain evidence. This enables a single policy to align visual anticipation with the robot's current motion capabilities across complex terrains, including stairs, platforms, gaps, discrete obstacles, rough ground, and slopes. From the experiment analysis, TempoLoco achieves a 78.4% success rate, compared with 58.2% for the strongest fixed-history baseline and 62.1% for uniform multi-scale fusion. In separate capability evaluations, it completes 41/45 simulated trials across nine terrain types and 23/25 real-world trials on a Unitree B2 with a fixed arm, demonstrating robust and deployable locomotion through body-aware dynamic visual perception.

</details>


### [218] [BridgeWAM: Bridging Video and Action Experts via Latent Bridge Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14746)
*Jiaxin Fang,Ruitong Liu,Zeyu Lai,Xingyu Wang,Mingyuan Jia,Pingyue Sheng,Weikang Wan,Mengdi Xu,Yuxiang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Bridge-of-Experts (BoE) 并实例化为 BridgeWAM：用 32 个 Latent Bridge Queries (LBQs) 将视觉、语言、本体感觉先验聚合到共享有限潜空间，解耦 video/action 专家深度，以超轻动作头提升成功率、泛化并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 World Action Models (WAMs) 常通过逐层密集 Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention 耦合 video 与 action experts，导致动作预测暴露于无关视觉特征、专家深度被迫匹配、选择性利用视觉特征受限且计算冗余。

Method: 引入 BoE/BridgeWAM：通过 32 个 LBQs 聚合多模态先验至共享有限潜空间；联合 video-action 监督使动作梯度引导 Video Expert 学习更适合动作预测的表征，Action Expert 通过 cross-attention 选择性检索任务相关信息；解耦专家深度，并在动作去噪时复用 LBQs；最终为 5.06B 模型，含 30 层 Video Expert 与 2 层 Action Expert。

Result: LIBERO 成功率达 98.25%，LIBERO-Plus 汇总成功率达 72.24%；相较 Fast-WAM，仿真 profiling 中单样本推理 FLOPs 降低 28.72%，真实机器人延迟降至 77.1 ms（2.47 倍加速）；消融支持联合表征适配与统一 LBQ conditioning；定性结果显示在扰动下更优先关注目标物体与任务目的地。

Conclusion: BridgeWAM 以超轻动作头实现有效任务执行与强泛化，验证了解耦专家深度、复用 LBQs 及联合表征适配等设计有效性，并显著降低推理计算量与真实机器人延迟。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) commonly couple video and action experts through dense, layer-wise Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention, exposing action prediction to irrelevant visual features while enforcing matched expert depths. This coupling hinders selective visual-feature use and introduces redundant computation. We introduce Bridge-of-Experts (BoE), instantiated as BridgeWAM, which aggregates visual, language, and proprioceptive priors through 32 Latent Bridge Queries (LBQs) into a shared, limited latent space. Joint video-action supervision lets action gradients guide the Video Expert toward representations better suited for action prediction, while the Action Expert learns to selectively retrieve task-relevant information through cross-attention. Decoupling expert depths and reusing LBQs during action denoising yield a 5.06B model with a 30-layer Video Expert and a two-layer Action Expert, achieving 98.25% success on LIBERO and 72.24% pooled success on LIBERO-Plus, demonstrating effective task execution and strong generalization with an ultralight action head. Ablations support joint representation adaptation and unified LBQ conditioning. Relative to Fast-WAM, BridgeWAM reduces single-sample inference FLOPs by 28.72% in simulation profiling and real-robot latency to 77.1 ms (a 2.47-fold speedup). Qualitative experimental results further indicate preferential attention to target objects and task destinations across perturbations.

</details>


### [219] [B-Human '26 - Scalable Team Play up to 11 vs. 11](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14733)
*Arne Hasselbring,Tim Laue,Thomas Röfer,Philip Reichenberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: B-Human在2026年新成立的人形足球联赛中级组中多次夺冠，并完成11台人形机器人的演示赛；论文介绍从标准平台组迁移所需的调整，并重点阐述其行为架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-Human此前参加标准平台组，机器人尺寸约为人形组一半、重量不足三分之一；为适应新人形足球联赛并展示大规模多机器人比赛能力，需要完成系统迁移与行为架构扩展。

Method: 首先概述从标准平台组转向人形组所需的调整；核心部分介绍其行为架构，并借鉴已长期使用11名球员的RoboCup联赛概念。

Result: B-Human在2026年人形足球联赛中级组中赢得多个比赛；除3人或5人常规比赛外，还成功进行了11台人形机器人的演示赛。

Conclusion: 11人演示赛体现了行为架构的战术灵活性以及整个系统的稳健性。

Abstract: B-Human won multiple competitions in the Middle Division of the new Humanoid Soccer League in 2026. In addition to regular matches with three or five players per team, we ventured into uncharted territory by playing demo matches with eleven humanoid robots, highlighting the tactical flexibility of our behavior and the overall robustness of our system. As B-Human formerly competed in the Standard Platform League with robots half the size and less than a third the weight, we begin by outlining the adjustments needed for this transition. The central section of this paper details our behavior architecture, drawing on concepts from RoboCup leagues that have used 11 players for much longer.

</details>


### [220] [CAG-WAM: Causal Action-Gain World Action Models for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14720)
*Peizheng Guo,Cheng Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 世界动作模型（WAM）结合动作生成与未来预测，但候选动作对应的未来包含动作引发与环境驱动的变化，直接评估预测未来不能充分刻画该动作带来的未来变化。为此，本文建立结构因果模型（SCM），提出因果动作增益（CAG）及 CAG-WAM，在训练中结合 GRPO，在推理中用于评估和修正动作，实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 WAM 中，候选动作对应的未来可能同时包含动作引发的变化和环境自身演化，直接评估预测未来会混淆二者。例如，一个使有利环境未来恶化的动作仍可能产生有利结果，而一个改善不利环境未来的动作仍可能产生不利结果。因此需要因果视角来分离环境演化对候选动作未来评估的影响。

Method: 本文为 WAM 中的动作评估建立结构因果模型（SCM），形式化动作引发与环境驱动变化如何共同影响未来评估，并揭示环境演化影响候选动作未来评估的后门路径。通过干预与后门调整，为刻画候选动作对应的未来变化提供因果基础。据此提出 CAG-WAM：对每个候选动作，在同一当前观测下预测动作条件未来和环境未来，将其评估差异定义为因果动作增益（CAG）。训练时，将 CAG 融入组相对策略优化（GRPO）以指导机器人策略学习；推理时，用 CAG 评估初始动作，并在需要时利用预测未来信息进行修正。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验支持 CAG-WAM 的有效性。

Conclusion: CAG-WAM 通过因果动作增益分离动作引发与环境驱动的未来变化，为 WAM 中的动作评估与机器人决策提供有效方法。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine action generation with future prediction, allowing predicted futures to support action evaluation and robot decision making. However, the future associated with a candidate action may contain action-induced and environment-driven changes. Directly evaluating the predicted future may not fully characterize the future change associated with the candidate action. For example, a candidate action that worsens a favorable environmental future may still yield a favorable outcome, while one that improves an unfavorable environmental future may still yield an unfavorable outcome. To analyze this issue, we formulate a Structural Causal Model (SCM) for action evaluation in WAMs. The SCM formalizes how action-induced and environment-driven changes jointly shape future evaluation, and reveals a backdoor path through which environmental evolution can also affect the future evaluation associated with a candidate action. Intervention and backdoor adjustment account for this path, providing a causal basis for characterizing the future change associated with each candidate action. Based on this analysis, we propose Causal Action-Gain World Action Model (CAG-WAM). For each candidate action, CAG-WAM predicts an action-conditioned future and an environmental future under the same current observation, and defines their evaluation difference as causal action gain (CAG) for characterizing the future change associated with the candidate action. During training, we incorporate CAG into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to guide robot policy learning. During inference, we evaluate the initial action with CAG and, when needed, revise it using predicted future information. Experiments on multiple benchmarks support the effectiveness of CAG-WAM.

</details>


### [221] [C2Flight: A Closed-Loop Co-Simulation Framework for Early-Stage Evaluation of Heterogeneous UAV Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14715)
*Xizhu Chen,Shuyuan Zhang,Yixuan Li,Huazhong Yang,Yu Wang,Jincheng Yu,Hongyang Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: C2Flight：一种面向异构无人机计算系统的闭环协同仿真框架，可在系统集成前评估算法、硬件、通信与拓扑对闭环飞行的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期设计需在完整系统可用前评估各设计选择对闭环飞行的综合影响；但机器人反馈回路使设计选择相互耦合，孤立组件指标难以预测任务性能，现有执行驱动方法直到计算系统集成后才提供系统级设计反馈。

Method: 将目标系统建模为参数化数据流图，通过统一节点接口集成待设计硬件模型与可独立访问的物理硬件；图运行时捕捉缓冲、激活、资源共享、通信及局部时序对闭环飞行行为的联合影响。

Result: 与机载及无线离板物理计算流水线对比验证了时序保真度和闭环预测准确性；两个案例研究展示了部署方案与加速器位宽选择的早期探索。

Conclusion: C2Flight 能在全系统集成前揭示系统级设计权衡，支持快速早期设计迭代优化。

Abstract: Early-stage design of heterogeneous UAV computing systems requires evaluating how algorithm, hardware, communication, and system topology affect closed-loop flight before the complete system is available. However, design choices interact through the robotic feedback loop, making task performance difficult to predict from isolated component metrics, while existing execution-driven approaches postpone system-level design feedback until the computing system is integrated. This work presents C2Flight, a closed-loop co-simulation framework that represents target systems as parameterized dataflow graphs and integrates models of hardware under design with independently accessible physical hardware through a unified node interface. Its graph runtime captures the combined effects of buffering, activation, resource sharing, communication, and local timing on closed-loop flight behavior. Validation against onboard and wireless offboard physical computing pipelines confirms its timing fidelity and closed-loop prediction accuracy. Two case studies demonstrate early-stage exploration of deployment and accelerator bit-width choices. These results show that C2Flight exposes system-level design trade-offs before full-system integration, enabling rapid early-stage design refinement.

</details>


### [222] [PRISM: Precision Forcing for Generalist Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14704)
*Chenghao Yue,Peiyan Li,Hao Wang,Lei Lv,Jiahao Chen,Huaping Liu,Yu Luo,Yuanlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出主流 VLA 策略在精密操作中不可靠，根源是流匹配训练目标在上下文与流时间上平均监督，导致关键决策时刻监督不足，称为 Flow-Time Starvation。作者提出 PRISM，通过精度感知重加权与内部化，联合调整监督分配和 VLM 条件表示，在仿真与真机任务中将平均端到端成功率从 38.3% 提升至 86.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将预训练视觉语言模型与扩散/流匹配动作专家耦合的 VLA 范式，在亚毫米级线缆插入等精密操作中表现不稳定。其训练目标在上下文和流时间上平均流匹配误差，对决定任务成败的少数关键时刻监督不足；由于推理时对局部监督的速度场进行积分，不同流时间上同等惩罚的误差对最终动作影响不等，因此物理容差收紧时性能会数量级下降。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为 Flow-Time Starvation，并提出 Precision-aware Reweighting and Internalization for Skilled Manipulation（PRISM）这一 precision-forcing 策略。PRISM 离线评估上下文相关的精度需求，在误差后果严重且动作信号可见的流时间上增强流匹配监督，并将该精度需求内化到 VLM 的条件表示中，使策略能够识别何时需要更高精度。

Result: 仿真和真机实验表明，PRISM 在驱动连接器插入、双臂连接器对接和螺钉拧紧任务中保持或提升通用任务性能，同时将平均端到端成功率从 38.3% 提高到 86.7%，且无需新增传感器或观测模态。

Conclusion: 通过联合对齐监督分配与表示学习同上下文相关的精度需求，PRISM 能显著扩展现有具身策略的精密操作边界，并缓解 Flow-Time Starvation 带来的性能退化。

Abstract: Coupling a pretrained vision-language model (VLM) with a diffusion or flow-matching action expert has become the mainstream vision-language-action (VLA) paradigm for embodied policies, yet they remain unreliable once a task demands precise manipulation, e.g., submillimeter cable insertion. We attribute this weakness to a training objective that averages flow-matching errors across contexts and flow times, allocating little supervision to the few decisive moments determining task success. Along the generative path, inference integrates a locally supervised velocity field, so equally penalized errors at different flow times can affect the executed action unequally. Consequently, precision-task performance deteriorates as physical tolerance tightens by orders of magnitude. We call this supervision imbalance across contexts and flow times Flow-Time Starvation. The remedy is to jointly align supervision allocation and representation learning with context-dependent precision demand, enabling the policy to recognize when greater precision is needed. We instantiate this precision-forcing strategy as Precision-aware Reweighting and Internalization for Skilled Manipulation (PRISM), which scores precision demand offline, strengthens flow supervision where errors are consequential and action signals are visible, and internalizes that demand in the VLM condition representation. Simulation and real-robot experiments show that PRISM preserves or improves general-task performance while raising mean end-to-end success from 38.3% to 86.7% across drive connector insertion, bimanual connector mating, and screw tightening, substantially extending the precision boundary of existing embodied policies without new sensors or observation modalities.

</details>


### [223] [BGC-Nav: Cross-Altitude Multi-UAV Collaborative Navigation via Belief-Guided Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14702)
*Jiuwu Hao,Yueyang Wu,Liguo Sun,Haolin Song,Zao Zhang,Pin Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BGC-Nav：一种免训练的跨高度协作框架，通过 Belief-Guided Coupling 实现高空无人机全局信念引导与低空无人机自主探索的鲁棒空中视觉语言导航与搜救。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外单无人机搜救受部分可观测性限制，跨高度协作具有潜力；但现有协作导航依赖精确目标定位和刚性目标分派，忽略高空观测不确定性，并抑制低空探索自主性。

Method: BGC-Nav 采用免训练协作范式：高空无人机维护编码任务条件先验的 Global Belief Memory；低空智能体通过 Local Exhaustion Memory、Local Attraction Augmentation 和 Directional Belief Prior 将其融入决策，且信念仅作为建议性偏置而非刚性命令。

Result: 在 ESARBench 上，BGC-Nav 持续优于最先进的单智能体与协作导航方法。

Conclusion: BGC-Nav 通过不确定性感知的信念引导耦合，实现高层全局信息与低层自主探索的平衡，为跨高度空中搜救协作提供了有效且免训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN), single Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) search-and-rescue in outdoor scenarios remains challenging due to partial observability, making cross-altitude collaboration a promising direction. However, existing collaborative navigation methods rely on precise target localization and rigid goal-dispatching paradigms, ignoring high-altitude observation uncertainty and suppressing low-altitude exploration autonomy. To address these issues, we introduce BGC-Nav, a training-free cross-altitude collaboration framework that enables robust navigation via Belief-Guided Coupling (BGC). Specifically, the high-altitude UAV maintains a Global Belief Memory encoding task-conditioned priors, which the low-altitude agent incorporates into decision-making via Local Exhaustion Memory (LEM), Local Attraction Augmentation (LAA), and Directional Belief Prior (DBP) as an advisory bias rather than a rigid command. Extensive experiments on ESARBench show that BGC-Nav consistently outperforms state-of-the-art single-agent and collaborative methods. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [224] [DexCast: Learning Real-World Dexterous Control from Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14691)
*Zihan Gao,Yuhong Zhang,Mengzhe Wang,Kaisheng Liu,Shengpeng Li,Siyao Xiao,Runqing Cheng,Xiao Lin,Zhifang Liu,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEXCAST 是一个无需遥操作、可直接从单目 RGB-D 人类视频中学习灵巧机器人手控制的框架；通过联合手-物交互重建与 DexHand 运动感知模块，结合扩散策略，在多种真实灵巧操作任务中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧模仿学习通常依赖昂贵且需人工遥操作的机器人演示，耗费大量人力；同时，高维机器人手运动建模与用于未来动作生成的时序上下文提取仍是关键挑战。

Method: 提出 DEXCAST 框架，利用联合手-物交互重建从单目 RGB-D 视频直接学习灵巧机械手控制，无需任何遥操作；并设计 DexHand Motion Perception Module（DMPM），以紧凑潜在表示捕捉关节间协调与时序依赖，作为扩散策略的条件。

Result: 大量真实世界实验表明，DEXCAST 在多种灵巧操作任务上取得卓越性能。

Conclusion: DEXCAST 证明了无需遥操作、从人类单目 RGB-D 视频中学习灵巧机器人手操作的可行性与有效性，为可扩展的灵巧技能学习提供了新路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D hand-object interaction (HOI) perception have opened new opportunities for learning dexterous robotic skills from human videos. However, existing dexterous imitation learning methods often rely on costly and manually teleoperated robot demonstrations, requiring substantial human effort. Therefore, we build DEXCAST, a framework that learns dexterous robotic hand control directly from monocular RGB-D videos by leveraging joint HOI reconstructions, without requiring any teleoperation. Furthermore, how to model high-dimensional robotic hand motion and extract temporal context to inform future action generation remains a key challenge. To this end, we propose a DexHand Motion Perception Module (DMPM) to capture inter-joint coordination and temporal dependencies in compact latent representations that condition our diffusion policy. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that DEXCAST achieves exceptional performance across diverse dexterous manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [225] [Successful but Unsafe: An Analytical Benchmark for Revealing Collisions in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14682)
*Hieu D. Pham,Quoc-An Luong,Dung D. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出VLN-Safe基准，揭示传统VLN评估中任务成功率会掩盖碰撞安全问题；在滑动与阻挡两种机制下系统评估碰撞，并提出免训练碰撞防护，显著减少碰撞并提升导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN智能体通常只按是否到达目标和路径效率评估，几乎不反映碰撞行为；连续VLN常用滑动机制，碰撞后智能体可沿障碍物滑动，可能掩盖不安全动作对导航性能的影响。因此需要专门分析碰撞安全，并研究碰撞预判信号与可行安全替代。

Method: 构建VLN-Safe基准，在R2R-CE上采用两种互补评估机制：滑动（sliding，符合常见VLN评估实践）和阻挡（blocking，碰撞动作阻止前进并直接暴露后果）。评估六个已发表智能体，并分析预判即将碰撞的信号。基于发现，提出免训练碰撞防护：否决高风险前进动作，执行逃逸机动，再将控制权交还原策略。

Result: 滑动机制下，38.5-56.8%的成功episode包含接触；切换为阻挡后，成功率下降11.7-36.1个百分点。碰撞预判中，深度几何和近期运动历史比策略派生信号更有信息量，而策略偏好动作附近的安全替代很少。阻挡下，碰撞防护在五个recurrent、topological和vision-language-action智能体上将碰撞步数减少62-98%，成功率提升6.9-29.3个百分点；滑动下成功率不变，无碰撞成功占比提升4.1-12.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了任务成功与碰撞安全导航之间的显著差距，并表明轻量级碰撞感知干预可在不牺牲甚至提升导航性能的同时改善安全性。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) agents are typically evaluated by whether they reach a target and how efficiently they follow an instructed route, yet these metrics provide little information about collision behavior. Moreover, continuous VLN evaluation commonly uses a sliding regime, where an agent can slide along obstacles after a collision, potentially masking the effect of unsafe actions on navigation performance. We introduce VLN-Safe, a benchmark for analyzing collision safety under two complementary evaluation regimes: sliding, reflecting common VLN evaluation practice, and blocking, where collision-inducing actions prevent forward motion and more directly expose their consequences. Across six published agents on R2R-CE, 38.5-56.8% of successful episodes contain contact under sliding, while switching to blocking decreases success rate by 11.7-36.1 percentage points. We further analyze signals for anticipating imminent collisions and find that depth geometry and recent movement history are more informative than policy-derived signals, while safe alternatives near the policy's preferred action are rarely available. Motivated by these findings, we introduce a training-free collision guard that vetoes risky forward actions, executes an escape maneuver, and returns control to the original policy. Under blocking, the guard reduces collision steps by 62-98% and improves success rate by 6.9-29.3 percentage points across five recurrent, topological, and vision-language-action agents. Under sliding, it leaves success rate unchanged while raising the share of collision-free successes by 4.1-12.4 points. These results reveal a substantial gap between task success and collision-safe navigation and show that lightweight collision-aware intervention can improve both safety and navigation performance.

</details>


### [226] [Global Optimality of Pose Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14655)
*Shuan Ding,Ruishuang Chen,Mengfan Cao,Zaiyue Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究位姿图优化（PGO）的优化景观，证明当噪声小于一个可先验验证的显式上界时，全局最优解附近具有良性吸引域，且该吸引域内唯一局部极小值即为全局最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PGO 在机器人状态估计、定位、建图和导航中具有基础地位，但其非凸且可能存在多个局部极小值，导致全局最优性分析困难。

Method: 通过刻画 PGO 的优化景观，给出全局最优性的充分条件。具体包括：识别包含全局最优解的吸引域（BOA），并显式刻画其半径受噪声控制；基于一阶最优性条件，证明满足噪声控制判据的局部极小值为全局最优；结合两者证明在噪声小于显式先验上界时，BOA 内仅存在一个局部极小值且为全局最优。

Result: 理论证明：若噪声小于显式且可先验验证的界，则优化景观在全局最优解附近良性且具有吸引力；BOA 内唯一局部极小值即全局最优解。多种随机情形下的数值仿真验证了该理论，表明所得条件可作为认证 PGO 全局最优性的有效判据。

Conclusion: 本文为 PGO 全局最优性提供了理论保证和可验证的噪声阈值判据，说明在低噪声条件下 PGO 的优化景观是良性的，并得到数值实验支持。

Abstract: Pose Graph Optimization (PGO) is a classical and widely applied problem in state estimation for robotics, playing a fundamental role in localization, mapping, and navigation. Yet, it is nonconvex in nature and may have many local minimizers, thus difficult to analyze. In this paper, we investigate the optimization landscape and establish a sufficient condition of global optimality for PGO. The ultimate goal of this paper is to theoretically confirm that the optimization landscape is benign and attractive around the global minimizer, if noise is smaller than an explicit and a priori bound. To reveal such an insight, this paper develops three main technical results. First, we identify a proper basin of attraction (BOA) containing global minimizer, and characterize its geometric property by showing that its radius is controlled by noise in an explicit way. Then, by exploring the first order optimality conditions of PGO, we prove that a local minimizer is globally optimal if a noise-controlled criterion is satisfied. Finally, by combining above two results, we prove that one and only one local minimizer exists in the BOA and this minimizer is globally optimal, when the noise is smaller than an explicit bound that can be verified a priori. Numerical simulations validate the proposed theory under various random cases, showing that the obtained conditions provide an effective criterion for certifying the global optimality of PGO.

</details>


### [227] [VbotNav-0: Asynchronous Cloud-Edge Dual-Policy Navigation across Indoor and Outdoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14634)
*Shiqi Tan,Yujun Zhang,Keyu Li,Shuaixi Pan,Siyu Zhou,Hailong Qin,Yinan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VbotNav-0 是一个云边双策略框架，通过异步的策略间条件化衔接目的地级推理与局部运动决策，实现室内外连续导航；在 145 个场景 258 个 episode 的闭环评测中达到 55.04% 成功率和 55.03% SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续室内外导航需要把目的地级推理与必须响应实时观测的运动决策连接起来，同时让局部感知-动作闭环在云端更新之间不中断。

Method: 设计云边双策略：云端策略根据视觉历史、导航指令和路线 token 生成子目标引导；边缘策略将可用引导与当前 RGB、LiDAR 观测结合，生成局部轨迹。采用统一路线表示连接室内路径与室外地图路线；训练数据混合导航示范、纠正数据以及接地的视觉问答（VQA）数据。

Result: 在 145 个场景、258 个 episode 的闭环评测中，VbotNav-0 取得 55.04% 成功率与 55.03% 路径长度加权成功率（SPL）。

Conclusion: 异步的策略间条件化使局部感知-动作循环可在云端更新间隙持续运行，从而支持室内外连续导航。

Abstract: Continuous indoor-outdoor navigation connects destination-level reasoning with motion decisions that must respond to current observations. We present VbotNav-0, a cloud-edge dual-policy framework that links these processes through asynchronous policy-to-policy conditioning. The cloud policy produces subgoal guidance from visual history, navigation instructions, and route tokens; the edge policy combines available guidance with current RGB and LiDAR observations to generate local trajectories. This formulation allows the local perception-action loop to continue between cloud updates. A common route representation connects indoor paths and outdoor map routes, and the training recipe combines navigation demonstrations, corrective data, and grounded visual question answering (VQA). In the reported closed-loop evaluation of 258 episodes across 145 scenes, VbotNav-0 achieves 55.04% success rate and 55.03% success weighted by path length.

</details>


### [228] [AnyRank: Reliable Anytime Policy Ranking via Sequential Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14603)
*Apurva Badithela,David Snyder,Tenny Yin,Jade Sceats,George Pappas,Nikolai Matni,Masha Itkina,Haruki Nishimura,Anirudha Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AnyRank：一种用于机器人策略多策略排序的序贯方法，可自适应分配试验、在任意停止时间给出部分排序并控制错误排序概率，可选利用不完美先验；相比现有统计严谨方法最多减少 50% 硬件评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件实验成本高昂，机器人策略通常仅能基于有限硬件试验的经验性能进行评估。有限样本统计不确定性可能导致错误结论，尤其是在使用固定批量评估数据对多个策略进行排序时。

Method: AnyRank 结合序贯检验与多重假设图检验：自适应地将试验分配给难以区分的策略；在任意停止时间提供部分排序，并约束错误排序概率；可选利用先验信息（专家直觉、少量留出硬件评估、模拟器或动作条件视频世界模型中的策略 rollout）以更高效地优先安排硬件评估。先验可以是不完美的，其排序不准确不会破坏 AnyRank 的统计保证。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界策略评估基准中，AnyRank 相比现有统计严谨方法最多减少 50% 的硬件评估，同时避免当前最优基准所用启发式方法产生的错误排序。

Conclusion: AnyRank 为机器人策略评估提供了统计可靠且样本高效的多策略排序方法，并能安全利用不完美先验信息来加速硬件评估。

Abstract: Due to the high cost of hardware experiments, robot policies are predominantly evaluated by comparing their empirical performance on a limited number of hardware trials. However, finite-sample statistical uncertainty can lead to erroneous conclusions, especially when ranking multiple policies using a fixed batch of evaluation data. We propose AnyRank: a sequential method for multi-policy ranking that (i) adaptively allocates trials to hard-to-separate policies, (ii) provides (partial) rankings at any stopping time while bounding the probability of an incorrect ranking, and (iii) optionally exploits prior information to prioritize hardware evaluations more efficiently. This prior information could be informed from expert intuition, a small set of held-out hardware evaluations, or from policy rollouts in a simulator or an action-conditioned video world model. Notably, the prior can be imperfect; while a strong prior can provide efficiency gains on hardware, any inaccuracies in prior policy rankings do not invalidate the statistical assurances of AnyRank. We achieve the aforementioned properties through novel theoretical contributions that combine sequential testing with multiple-hypothesis graphical testing approaches. Experiments in simulation and real-world policy evaluation benchmarks show that AnyRank requires up to 50% fewer hardware evaluations compared to existing statistically rigorous approaches, while guarding against the incorrect rankings produced by heuristic methods used by state-of-the-art benchmarks.

</details>


### [229] [StateSkill-Agent: An Effective and Efficient Embodied Agent via State-conditioned Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14601)
*Changxu Wei,Jingyi Zhang,Zhiyu Zheng,Tianyi Lin,Yixian Zhang,Yunpeng Qing,Jingyuan Liu,Zhi Hou,Jiaxing Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出StateSkill——将有限状态机引入具身智能体技能建模，并配合StateSkill-Evolve与StateGRPO形成技能与模型协同进化的统一学习框架，在多种具身任务上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建能够理解并求解具身任务的具身多模态大模型智能体，通过学习、抽象和复用可迁移、可泛化的行为模式来提升任务表现。

Method: 提出StateSkill技能表示，将具身交互经验建模为有限状态机：技能由任务进度状态、状态转移及状态条件引导组成，使智能体随任务进展动态调整技能并保持上下文紧凑且与阶段相关；进一步提出经验驱动的StateSkill-Evolve，通过对新成功与失败的状态引导轨迹进行回溯分析迭代精炼技能；并提出StateGRPO，将StateSkill演化与基于状态条件GRPO的模型优化整合为统一迭代学习回路，实现技能与模型能力协同进化、相互增强。

Result: 大量实验验证了所提方法及训练模型StateSkill-Agent在多种具身任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: StateSkill框架通过有限状态机建模、经验驱动演化与状态条件GRPO的统一学习，实现了具身技能与模型能力的协同提升，并在多种具身任务中取得良好效果。

Abstract: In this work, we aim to develop an embodied MLLM agent that understands and solves embodied tasks by learning, abstracting, and reusing transferrable and generalizable behavioral patterns. To this end, we propose StateSkill, a new skill representation for embodied agents, which introduces the concept of "finite-state machines" into skill modeling for effective and efficient skill construction and learning. The core idea of StateSkill is to model embodied interaction experiences as a finite-state machine, where a skill is organized around a set of task-progress states, state transitions, and state-conditioned guidance, enabling embodied agents to dynamically adjust their skills as the task progresses while keeping skill contexts compact and stage-relevant. We further develop StateSkill-Evolve, an experience-driven evolution mechanism that iteratively refines StateSkill through retrospective analysis of new successful and failed state-guided trajectories.
