Upon this, we introduce StateGRPO, which integrates StateSkill evolution and model optimization via state-conditioned GRPO into a unified iterative learning loop, enabling state-conditioned skills and model capabilities to co-evolve and reinforce each other. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed methods and trained model (i.e., StateSkill-Agent) across various embodied tasks.

</details>


### [230] [Load-Aware Quadruped Navigation with Online Contact-based Geometry Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14596)
*Qi Yin,XiaoLi Zhang,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段载荷感知导航学习框架，使四足机器人携带未知几何尺寸物体时能在杂乱环境中自适应导航；第一阶段用强化学习训练LOADNav，第二阶段用模仿学习训练CoGE在线估计载荷几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有载荷感知运动研究多关注保持步态稳定，而自主导航系统通常忽略所携物体几何，采用固定足印膨胀，导致导航过于保守。为填补载荷几何对导航影响这一空白，提出载荷感知导航学习框架。

Method: 采用两阶段学习框架。Phase 1：通过强化学习训练载荷感知导航策略LOADNav，使机器人根据所携物体几何尺寸调整轨迹与间隙行为。Phase 2：通过模仿学习训练在线几何预测器CoGE，从偶发接触事件中估计未知载荷几何。

Result: 在包含不同尺寸与形状载荷的杂乱环境中评估。导航策略表现出明确的载荷感知行为；CoGE能够从偶发接触事件估计物体几何，持续细化估计并相应调整导航行为。无需任何载荷先验知识，即可缩小与几何感知策略之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了未知载荷条件下的载荷感知导航，避免固定足印膨胀带来的过度保守，提升了四足机器人在杂乱环境中携物自主导航的适应能力。

Abstract: Navigating cluttered environments while carrying objects remains a challenging problem for quadruped robots. Compared with works that focused on load-aware locomotion adaptation to maintain gait stability, existing autonomous navigation systems generally ignore the influence of carried objects' geometries and use fixed footprint inflation, leading to overly conservative navigation. To fill this knowledge gap, we propose a two-phase load-aware navigation learning framework. In Phase 1, we train LOADNav, a load-aware navigation policy via reinforcement learning, enabling the robot to adapt its trajectory and clearance behavior according to the geometric size of the carried object. In Phase 2, we train CoGE, an online geometry predictor via imitation learning, which estimates unknown payload geometry from occasional contact events. We evaluate the framework in cluttered environments with loads of varying sizes and shapes. The results show that our navigation policy exhibits clear load-aware behaviors, and the online geometry predictor estimates object geometry from occasional contact events, refining its estimate and adapting the navigation behavior accordingly. Without requiring any prior knowledge of the payload, it narrows the gap to geometry-aware policies.

</details>


### [231] [ToF-Wheel Odometry: Navigating Degeneration of Localization With Limited Sensing Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14590)
*Xu Yang,Wenhao Li,Zhengyu Wei,Zening Li,Longxiang Huang,Bo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ToF-轮式里程计（TWO）：一种紧耦合滤波框架，将轮式运动学与ToF相机几何约束结合，并通过退化感知的方向性协方差塑形提升轮式里程计在轮滑、地形倾斜及几何退化场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式里程计为地面机器人提供高效运动先验，但在轮滑或地形诱导倾斜等瞬态异常下易失效；在滤波式里程计中，这些异常若与受限几何可观测性（如无纹理走廊）同时出现，常导致滤波器发散。现有在线协方差调参多依赖启发式策略，难以可靠区分运动学异常与传感器噪声。

Method: TWO采用紧耦合滤波框架，融合轮式运动学与ToF相机几何约束。其核心是退化感知、方向性的协方差塑形策略，以几何条件化膨胀替代启发式在线协方差调参。方法将后验到先验的校正分解为轮式可解释子空间与正交残差，并根据ToF更新的可观测性各向异性地调整过程协方差：若几何在非运动学方向退化，则将残差抑制为传感器噪声；若几何区分度高，则将其接纳为有效运动学异常。此外引入帧内反馈环，在当前帧内回溯修复过程协方差，以降低突发扰动下的结构滞后。

Result: 在挑战性场景中的大量实验表明，与静态高斯滤波和自适应高斯滤波相比，TWO提升了轨迹精度与鲁棒性，能够在运动学可靠性与有限感知能力之间取得稳健折中。

Conclusion: TWO通过几何条件化的方向性协方差塑形与帧内反馈修复，有效缓解了轮式里程计在瞬态异常和几何退化下的滤波发散问题，为地面机器人提供了一种更鲁棒的紧耦合ToF-轮式里程计方案。

Abstract: Wheel odometry provides an efficient motion prior for ground robots but is prone to transient violations such as wheel slip or terrain-induced tilt. In filtering-based odometry, these violations frequently trigger divergence when they coincide with constrained geometric observability (e.g., featureless corridors). We address this challenge by proposing ToF-Wheel Odometry (TWO), a tightly-coupled filtering framework that integrates wheel kinematics with geometric constraints from Time-of-Flight (ToF) cameras. The primary contribution of this work is a degeneracy-aware, directional covariance-shaping strategy that replaces heuristic online covariance tuning with geometry-conditioned inflation. The core idea is to infer process noise from geometric corrections with a strict reliability check. We decompose the posterior-to-prior correction into a wheel-explainable subspace and an orthogonal remainder. The process covariance is anisotropically adjusted according to the observability of the ToF update: if the geometry is degenerate in non-kinematic directions, the remainder is suppressed as sensor noise; if the geometry is distinct, it is admitted as a valid kinematic violation. Additionally, we introduce an intra-frame feedback loop that retrospectively repairs the process covariance within the current frame to reduce structural lag during bursty disturbances. Extensive experiments on challenging scenarios demonstrate that TWO improves trajectory accuracy and robustness compared to static and adaptive Gaussian filtering, robustly navigating the trade-off between kinematic reliability and limited sensing capabilities.

</details>


### [232] [Flow-Support-Weighted Proposals for Test-Time Verified VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14588)
*Chen Zhao,Zipeng Ye,Haitao Shen,Guanlin Li,Yuchen Xie,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出动作动态采样（ADS），在训练阶段缓解视觉-语言-动作模型微调后出现的候选动作提案收缩问题，从而提升测试时采样扩展与验证器选择效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时采样本应让单一动作预测变成多个可执行候选，但下游微调使流匹配解码器集中于主导示范动作，导致重复采样高度冗余，验证器只能收到同一决策的多个副本；作者将此称为提案收缩，并视为测试时扩展的关键限制。

Method: ADS 在训练侧缓解该问题：每步训练中，在全新基础噪声下从当前策略积分 K 条额外轨迹，用 Hutchinson 迹估计器沿路径计算流支持度并打分，再通过分离的有限集 KL 项将策略局部动作分布校正到该加权集合；训练成本增加，部署成本不变。

Result: 在仿真与四个真实机器人任务中，ADS 提升提案多样性且不带来高斯扰动那样的成功率损失：RoboCasa 上 oracle best-of-8 成功率从 57.6% 提升至 63.0%，仿真中固定验证器提升 2.4 至 4.9 个百分点，真实世界操作 TokenPB 最高提升 13.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: ADS 通过训练期校正动作提案分布来缓解提案收缩，在不增加部署成本的情况下提升候选多样性与测试时验证收益。

Abstract: Test-time sampling promises to turn one action prediction into a set of distinct executable candidates that a verifier can choose among. Downstream fine-tuning works against that promise. A vision-language-action model trained to solve one task distribution concentrates its flow-matching decoder around the dominant demonstrated action, so repeated samples differ only marginally and the verifier receives many copies of one decision. We call this proposal contraction and identify it as a key constraint on test-time scaling: repeated samples can remain highly redundant as the candidate budget grows. Action Dynamic Sampling (ADS) alleviates the constraint on the proposal side, during training. At each training step, ADS integrates $K$ additional trajectories from the current policy under fresh base noise, scores each by the flow support along its path with a Hutchinson trace estimator, and corrects the policy's local action distribution toward this weighted set through a detached finite-set KL term. Training cost rises; deployment cost is unchanged. Across simulation and four physical-robot tasks, ADS raises proposal spread without the success penalty that Gaussian perturbation incurs, lifts oracle best-of-8 success on RoboCasa from 57.6% to 63.0%, improves fixed verifiers by 2.4 to 4.9 points in simulation, and raises TokenPB by up to 13.0 points on real-world manipulation.

</details>


### [233] [PC7 Model: Dynamics of Expanding and Contracting Soft Robots using Piece-wise Constant 7D Deformation Based on Similarity Transformation Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14586)
*Yamato Kudo,Taiki Ishigaki,Ko Ayusawa,Ko Yamamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于SIM(3)的7维分段常值变形模型PC7，用于表示软体机器人及人工肌肉的伸缩变形，并基于Euler-Poincaré方程推导动力学，通过仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有分段常值应变PCS模型基于SE(3)定义6维变形向量，主要面向Cosserat杆，无法描述人工肌肉等软体致动器的膨胀与收缩变形。因此需要扩展模型以同时刻画伸缩变形。

Method: 在PCS模型基础上，将变形向量由6维扩展为7维，并基于SIM(3)相似变换群定义分段常值7D变形；新增维度对应SIM(3)中的缩放参数，用于表示伸缩变形。利用SE(3)与SIM(3)的共同数学性质，将PCS的运动学与动力学直接推广到PC7，并使用Euler-Poincaré方程严格推导PC7动力学方程。

Result: 通过动力学仿真验证所提模型，仿真对象包括薄圆柱结构和McKibben型人工肌肉致动器；摘要未给出具体定量性能指标。

Conclusion: PC7模型能够在统一框架下描述软体结构的伸缩变形及传统Cosserat杆类变形，为人工肌肉等软体致动器提供数学严谨的建模与仿真方法。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel soft robot model named piece-wise constant 7D deformation (PC7) model that can represent expanding and contracting deformation of, for example, artificial muscle actuator. In the proposed model, we newly define the 7D deformation vector based on SIM(3) - 3D similarity transformation group - whereas a 6D deformation vector was defined for Cosserat rod based on SE(3) - the special Euclid group - in the piece-wise constant strain (PCS) model proposed in the previous study. The additional dimension of the 7D deformation vector represents expanding and contracting deformation, which comes from the scaling parameter in SIM(3). Common mathematical properties between SE(3) and SIM(3) allow us to directly extend the kinematics and dynamics of the PCS model to those of the PC7 model. In particular, we derive the dynamical equation of the PC7 model based on Euler-Poincare equation in a mathematically precise manner. We validate the proposed model through dynamical simulations assuming a thin cylinder structure or McKibben-type artificial muscle actuator.

</details>


### [234] [Remembering What Matters for Long-Horizon Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14583)
*Hao Wu,Tianyu Shao,Shengju Qian,Yizhou Zhao,Fan Zhang,Penghao Zhao,Weiyan Wang,Yufei Huang,Fan Xu,Kun Wang,Xin Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对基于离散视觉token的自回归机器人视频世界模型在长时程推演中不可靠的问题，作者提出免训练的上下文调度方法 ReCAP：保留初始场景锚点与最近完整视觉-动作块，驱逐陈旧生成历史，从而在不重训练的情况下提升长时程预测质量与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归机器人视频世界模型在长 rollout 下仍不可靠，且保留完整生成历史未必提升预测质量。作者研究 Full Context 解码中可能的“记忆角色冲突”：持续场景信息、近期视觉-动作状态与陈旧生成历史具有不同预测作用，却在注意力中相互竞争。

Method: ReCAP（Receding Context with Anchor Preservation）是一种面向长时程机器人视频世界模型的免训练上下文调度方法。冻结预训练 tokenizer、世界模型和视觉解码器；保留初始场景锚点以及一段连续的近期完整视觉-动作块，同时驱逐较旧的生成历史。该调度保持块内结构与时间顺序，无需额外模块或参数更新，并使活跃上下文增长与 rollout 长度无关。

Result: 在 RT-1、BridgeV2、LIBERO-90 和 CALVIN 上，基于冻结自回归世界模型评估，ReCAP 相比 Full Context 提升了视觉保真度、动作条件一致性和机器人结构保持。BridgeV2 上 LPIPS 降低 63.3%；H=64 系统基准中逻辑 KV 占用降低 73.3%，p50 延迟降低 6.51%。注意力分析显示保留的近期状态获得更多注意力，而历史干预表明更多陈旧上下文与更高预测误差相关。

Conclusion: 结果支持将推理时上下文调度作为提升长时程机器人视频预测质量和计算效率的实用方法，且无需重新训练。不过，观察结果与记忆角色冲突假设一致，但尚不能将其与上下文长度和位置处理效应完全区分。

Abstract: Autoregressive robot video world models based on discrete visual tokens remain unreliable over long rollout horizons. Retaining the entire generated history does not necessarily improve prediction quality. We investigate a possible memory-role conflict in Full Context decoding: persistent scene information, recent visual-action states, and stale generated history compete for attention despite their different predictive roles. This perspective motivates ReCAP (Receding Context with Anchor Preservation), a training-free context-scheduling method for long-horizon robot video world models. ReCAP keeps the pretrained tokenizer, world model, and visual decoder frozen. It preserves the initial scene anchor and a contiguous window of recent complete visual-action blocks while evicting older generated history. The schedule preserves within-block structure and temporal order, requires no additional modules or parameter updates, and bounds active-context growth independently of rollout length. We evaluate ReCAP on RT-1, BridgeV2, LIBERO-90, and CALVIN using frozen autoregressive world models. Across evaluated environments and horizons, ReCAP improves visual fidelity, action-conditioned consistency, and robot structure preservation relative to Full Context. On BridgeV2, it reduces LPIPS by 63.3%. In the system benchmark at H=64, it reduces the logical KV footprint by 73.3% and p50 latency by 6.51%. Attention analyses show increased attention to retained recent states, while history interventions associate larger amounts of stale context with higher prediction error. These observations are consistent with the memory-role-conflict hypothesis, although they do not isolate it from context-length and position-handling effects. Our results support inference-time context scheduling as a practical approach to improving long-horizon robot video prediction and computational efficiency without retraining.

</details>


### [235] [In View or Out of View? A Vision-Aware Filter that Infers and Reacts to the Human's Field of View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14577)
*Mengxue Fu,Ya-Chuan Hsu,Yinlong Dai,Erdem Bıyık,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种视觉感知滤波器，使机器人能够推断人类在交互中看到和未看到的区域，并据此调整动作，从而提升人机协作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互中，视觉对人类预测机器人行为至关重要；但现有的人类感知规划器要么忽略人类视野，假设人类能观察整个场景，要么刻意让机器人始终处于人类可见范围内。

Method: 给定名义机器人策略与交互模型，在短时域内展开策略以预测机器人状态更新，并根据动作对机器人可见性的影响重新加权：提高使机器人进入或离开人类视线的动作权重。该框架以在线估计与过滤流程反复更新对人类视野的理解，并基于人类视觉局部扰动机器人动作。

Result: 在协作烹饪与驾驶协调场景的仿真及一项12人用户研究中，视觉感知滤波器同时改变了性能与偏好：烹饪时参与者完成的内视野辅助子任务数量超过三倍，驾驶时与故意避开其视野的车辆发生的碰撞更少。

Conclusion: 通过在线推断人类视野并据此过滤机器人动作，可有效利用可见性提升人机协作的性能与用户体验。

Abstract: During human-robot interaction, sight plays an important role. When the human sees a robot, they can observe the robot's actions and better predict the robot's future behaviors. Conversely, when the human cannot see the robot, they become uncertain about the robot's intent and plans. In this paper, we focus on the human's field of view. Existing human-aware planners either ignore this field of view and assume the human can observe the entire scene, or intentionally design their interactions so the robot is always visible to the human. By contrast, we enable robots to infer what the human has and has not seen during interaction, i.e., the human's field of view. We then apply this estimate as a vision-aware filter on the robot's actions. Given a nominal robot policy and a model of the interaction, we roll out the policy over a short horizon to see how the robot's state updates. We then reweight the actions based on how those actions impact the robot's predicted visibility: higher weights are assigned to actions that bring the robot into (or away from) the human's line of sight. In practice, our framework provides an online estimation and filtering process that repeatedly updates its understanding of the human's field of view and then locally perturbs robot actions based on the human's vision. We evaluate our vision-aware filter on robots that assist humans in collaborative cooking and coordinate with them in driving environments. Across simulations and a 12-participant user study, we find that our vision-aware filter changes both performance and preference: participants completed over three times as many subtasks with in-view assistance while cooking, and had fewer collisions with cars that purposely stayed out of their view while driving.

</details>


### [236] [Chunk-wise Model-based Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14564)
*Weixi Song,Donglin Wang,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于分块世界模型与分块策略的模型强化学习方法，通过单次前向生成H步假设轨迹并直接优化价值展开，避免不稳定且昂贵的BPTT，同时减少循环复合误差与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的强化学习虽通过无导数规划/想象训练或动力学梯度策略更新实现了较高样本效率，但普遍存在循环复合误差、推理时延迟高以及沿时间反向传播（BPTT）不稳定等问题。

Method: 将分块世界模型（chunk world model）作为可微的训练时评估器来优化分块演员（chunk actor），无需循环调用模型；通过世界模型与策略的协同分块预测，在单次前向传播中生成H步假设展开（hypothetical rollouts），并直接优化价值展开（value expansion），从而避免昂贵且不稳定的BPTT。

Result: 在HumanoidBench的19个高维人形任务上进行了评估，并将方法部署到真实世界用于四足/双足行走。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在避免BPTT的同时实现快速学习，并在高维仿真任务与真实机器人任务上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning has shown superior sample efficiency by leveraging the learned latent dynamics either in the form of derivative-free planning/imagined rollout training or dynamics gradient-based policy updates. While these approaches achieve comparatively fast learning, they all suffer, to varying degrees, from issues of recurrent compounding errors, high inference-time latency, and instability in backward propagation through time (BPTT). In this work, we propose a model-based RL approach that uses the chunk world model as a differentiable training-time evaluator to optimize the chunk actor without recurrent model invocation. Through the collaborative chunk-wise prediction of the world model and policy, we can generate H-step hypothetical rollouts in a single forward pass and directly optimize value expansion, avoiding the costly and unstable BPTT. We evaluate our method on 19 challenging high-dimensional humanoid tasks from HumanoidBench and deploy our method to the real-world for quadruped bipedal walking. Project Website: this https URL

</details>


### [237] [From Video to Vision: Learning Generalizable Humanoid Carrying from Human Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14561)
*Ziluo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MOVER多阶段Teacher-Assistant-Student强化学习框架，通过视频重定向、分阶段训练和Dagger-PPO蒸馏，使人形机器人无需动捕系统即可基于机载RGB-D相机完成不同位置的物体搬运，并成功迁移到真机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频或动捕数据的RL人形搬运方法依赖上层模块实时生成参考轨迹，导致只能从指定位置沿参考轨迹搬运，空间泛化差，且策略仅能在动捕工作室评估。需要一种无需动捕、可泛化到不同物体与位置、可实机部署的人形物体搬运方法。

Method: 提出MOVER多阶段Teacher-Assistant-Student RL训练框架。首先采集视频数据，利用GENMO和GMR重定向，并结合数据增强泛化到不同形状和尺寸的物体。第一阶段基于DeepMimic训练人形从指定位置沿指定轨迹搬运物体；第二阶段放松参考运动跟踪并调整奖励，使其学习从不同位置搬运；第三阶段采用Dagger-PPO蒸馏，去除本体感知位置观测和参考关节位置，使部署策略不依赖动捕全局位置信息，可直接从机载RGB-D相机感知物体位姿并完成搬运。

Result: 设计系列仿真实验，比较Stage 1与Stage 2以及蒸馏前后的成功率；并将策略迁移到真实人形，在无动捕设置下成功搬运不同位置的物体。

Conclusion: MOVER通过分阶段训练与蒸馏有效降低对参考轨迹和动捕系统的依赖，提升空间泛化能力，并实现真机无动捕的人形物体搬运。

Abstract: Most existing approaches rely on video or motion-capture data for reinforcement learning (RL) training; however, all of these methods require an upper-level module to generate reference trajectories in real time. In object carrying tasks, the humanoid can only lift objects from designated locations along the reference trajectory, resulting in poor spatial generalization. Moreover, existing policies can only be evaluated inside a motion-capture studio. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{MOVER}, a multi-stage, Teacher-Assistant-Student, RL training framework for humanoid object carrying. We first collect data from videos and employ GENMO and GMR to retarget the video data, combining this with data augmentation techniques to generalize the data across objects of varying shapes and sizes. In the \textbf{first stage}, building upon the DeepMimic \cite{peng2018deepmimic} motion-training framework, we train the humanoid to transport objects from specified locations along designated trajectories. In the \textbf{second stage}, we relax the tracking of the reference motion and adjust the reward so that the humanoid learns to transport objects from different positions. In the \textbf{third stage}, we apply a Dagger-PPO distillation method to distill away the proprioceptive position observations and reference joint position of the humanoid. This allows the deployed policy to operate without relying on global position information from a motion-capture system; instead, the humanoid can directly perceive the object's pose from its onboard RGB-D camera to complete the carrying task. We design a series of simulation experiments to compare the success rates across stages (Stages 1 vs. 2) and before versus after distillation. We further transfer the policy to a real humanoid, successfully transporting objects at varying positions in a motion-capture-free setting.

</details>


### [238] [Before We Act: Learning Action-Conditioned Team-Response Beliefs for Decentralized Multi-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14551)
*Zihao Li,Yanan Zhou,Ranpeng Qiu,Guoqiang Ren,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CARE（协同动作—响应信念估计）框架，从局部历史学习队友响应信念，将队友适应性预测转化为去中心化动作选择，并在多机器人操作任务中取得最高平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人操作中，动作价值取决于队友如何响应，因此需要显式建模队友适应性以改进协同决策。

Method: CARE使用预测性信念编码器表示交互并条件化名义动作；通过配对的反应式与重放延续，分离候选动作的直接优势与队友适应带来的效用；候选条件读出同时学习响应分布与总优势；校准选择决定采用备选动作或保留名义动作。分析刻画了响应改变偏好动作的条件，以及预测与来源误差如何限制可恢复收益。

Result: 在表示、候选与数据固定时，显式响应监督相比通过分支效用学习相同信息，能提升候选排序并降低选择遗憾；收益集中在队友适应改变偏好动作的决策上。在RoboFactory、MARS Control Track与物理协作任务中，CARE在评估策略中平均成功率最高，兼具强名义策略与选择性协调调整。

Conclusion: CARE表明，对队友适应的预测可有效转化为去中心化动作选择。

Abstract: In multi-robot manipulation, an action's value depends on how teammates respond. We present \method{} (Cooperative Action--Response Belief Estimation), a framework for learning \emph{team-response beliefs} from local history. A predictive belief encoder represents the interaction and conditions a nominal action. Matched reactive and replay continuations separate each candidate's direct advantage from the utility contributed by teammate adaptation. A candidate-conditioned readout learns this response distribution alongside total advantage, and calibrated selection chooses an alternative or retains the nominal action. Our analysis characterizes when response changes the preferred action and how prediction and source errors limit the recoverable gain. With the representation, proposals, and data fixed, explicit response supervision improves candidate ranking and reduces selection regret relative to learning the same information through branch utilities. The benefit is concentrated in decisions where teammate adaptation changes the preferred action. Across RoboFactory, the MARS Control Track, and physical collaboration tasks, CARE achieves the highest average success among the evaluated policies, combining a capable nominal policy with selective coordination adjustments. Together, these results show that CARE turns predictions of teammate adaptation into effective decentralized action selection. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [239] [GG-Nav: Goal-oriented Grounding Chain-of-Thought Elicits Reasoning in Navigation Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14541)
*Shaohao Zhu,Wensi Huang,Siqi Zhang,Yuqiang Yang,Meng Wei,Chenyang Wan,Tianle Liu,Tai Wang,Jinming Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GG-Nav，通过结构化思维链监督微调提升目标导向导航，解决流式观测利用不足与目标定位不准问题，并在多个基准和真实机器人上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）导航基础模型在目标导向导航中性能不佳，动机研究发现关键问题在于对流式观测利用不充分以及目标定位不精确。

Method: 提出GG-Nav导航框架，通过监督微调学习结构化思维链（CoT），包含两阶段：Observation-to-Evidence，在相邻决策步之间的中间帧上推理并保留目标相关证据；Evidence-to-Decision，融合当前观测与累积证据来定位目标，生成导航动作或澄清问题。

Result: 在HM3D ObjectNav、VL-LN、CoIN基准上成功率分别超越SOTA 3.1%、16.6%、5.3%；消融显示累积中间观测证据减少11.6%证据缺失失败，显式定位减少49.4%停止错误；在Unitree Go2机器人上部署，展现未见真实环境的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: GG-Nav有效提升目标导向导航性能，结构化CoT与证据累积、显式定位是关键，且具备真实世界零样本泛化潜力。

Abstract: Emerging strong Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have advanced navigation foundation models. However, these models still have unsatisfactory performance on goal-oriented navigation settings. In this paper, we first conduct a motivating study revealing that the key issue lies in the insufficient utilization of streaming observations and imprecise target localization. To tackle these challenges, we propose GG-Nav, a simple yet effective navigation framework that learns a structured chain-of-thought (CoT) mechanism via supervised fine-tuning to explicitly localize goal-relevant instances, accumulate grounding evidence, and support more reliable decision-making. Specifically, the structured CoT is designed with two key stages: (i) Observation-to-Evidence, which reasons over all intermediate frames between adjacent decision steps to retain useful goal-related evidence; and (ii) Evidence-to-Decision, which integrates the current observation with accumulated evidence to ground the target and support the generation of navigation actions or clarification questions. Extensive experiments show that GG-Nav outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in terms of success rates on the HM3D ObjectNav, VL-LN, and CoIN benchmarks by 3.1%, 16.6%, and 5.3%, respectively. Ablation studies further show that accumulating evidence from intermediate observations reduces evidence-missing failures by 11.6%, while explicit grounding reduces stopping errors by 49.4%. We also deploy GG-Nav on a Unitree Go2 robot, demonstrating its zero-shot generalization capability in unseen real-world environments.

</details>


### [240] [Who to Trust in Multi-Robot Systems: Joint Robot and Object Hypothesis Trust Inference via Neural-Symbolic Cross-Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14530)
*Ruiyang Wang,Hao-Lun Hsu,Haocheng Meng,Songqiao Xie,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 NeST-Bayes 神经符号框架，仅利用机器人间观测一致性联合推断机器人信任与假设信任，并通过 Beta 信任信念的贝叶斯更新实现时间一致估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在复杂环境中依赖信息共享与协调，但可靠合作需要推断信息来源及其假设的可信度，尤其是在部分可观测和潜在对抗干扰下。现有方法常依赖预置信任关系或侧信道信息，在现实部署中可能不可用或不可靠。

Method: NeST-Bayes 不依赖预分配机器人信任、声誉信息或侧信道身份信号，仅基于跨机器人观测一致性。它从以自我为中心的关系图推理机器人与物体假设的瞬时信任证据分数，并通过 Beta 分布信任信念的贝叶斯更新随时间累积，以维持时间一致的信任估计。

Result: 大量评估表明，NeST-Bayes 在所有基准设置中取得最高平均物体精确率和机器人召回率，在多数设置中取得最高机器人精确率和物体召回率。在强优化攻击下，达到 98.8% 机器人精确率与 93.8% 召回率，以及 98.3% 物体精确率与 95.4% 召回率。在遮挡丰富的 Webots 仓库环境中，保持 92.5% 机器人精确率与 92.0% 召回率，以及 97.3% 物体精确率与 85.0% 召回率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，NeST-Bayes 在保留信息的可靠性与覆盖范围上实现了联合提升。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems (MRS) offer significant advantages over single-robot platforms by enabling information sharing and coordination for different tasks in complex environments. However, reliable cooperation critically depends on inferring trust over both information sources and their reported hypotheses, particularly under partial observability and potential adversarial interference. Existing approaches frequently depend on pre-established trust relationships or side-channel information, which may not be available or reliable in realistic MRS deployments. This work introduces NeST-Bayes, a neural-symbolic framework that jointly infers robot and hypothesis trust using only inter-robot observation consistency, without requiring preassigned robot trust, reputation information, or side-channel identity signals. NeST-Bayes infers instantaneous trust evidence scores for robots and object hypotheses by reasoning over ego-centric relational graphs constructed from cross-robot observation consistency. These evidence scores are accumulated over time through Bayesian updates of Beta-distributed trust beliefs to maintain temporally consistent trust estimates. Extensive evaluations show that NeST-Bayes achieves the highest mean object precision and robot recall across all benchmark settings, and the highest robot precision and object recall in most settings, compared to trust-based methods. Under aggressive optimized attacks, it achieves 98.8\% robot precision with 93.8\% recall and 98.3\% object precision with 95.4\% recall. In an occlusion-rich Webots warehouse, it maintains 92.5\% robot precision with 92.0\% recall and 97.3\% object precision with 85.0\% recall. These results demonstrate joint improvements in the reliability and coverage of retained information.

</details>


### [241] [Safety-Critical Quadrotor Navigation Using Fourth-Order Control Barrier Functions with Full Nonlinear Quadrotor Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14522)
*Yanze Liu,Yaşar İdikut,Dhruv Madhusudan Agrawal,Wei Xiao,Guanrui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于四阶控制屏障函数（CBF）的安全控制器，直接在全非线性四旋翼动力学（含姿态与力矩级执行）上施加避障约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分层架构中，高层安全滤波器只修改位置参考，再由独立姿态控制器跟踪，导致平移安全目标与姿态演化不匹配，存在安全层与姿态控制脱节的问题。

Method: 构建显式包含完整非线性四旋翼动力学（包括姿态动力学和力矩级执行）的四阶CBF安全控制器；针对约束中的混合相对阶问题，采用积分高阶CBF技术；在优化控制力矩的同时施加避障约束，使安全层直接控制姿态行为。

Result: 在高保真仿真和真实飞行实验中验证，实现了可靠的约束满足与成功的障碍物规避。

Conclusion: 力矩级高阶CBF为完整刚体动力学下的四旋翼导航提供了一条可证明更安全的实用路径。

Abstract: Safety-critical quadrotor navigation in cluttered environments typically relies on hierarchical architectures in which a high-level safety filter modifies position references that are then tracked by a separate attitude controller. However, this separation can create a mismatch between translational safety objectives and attitude evolution. To address this gap, we propose a safety controller based on fourth-order control barrier functions (CBFs) that explicitly incorporates the full nonlinear quadrotor dynamics, including attitude dynamics and torque-level actuation. Furthermore, we apply the integral high-order CBF technique to resolve the mixed-relative degree issue in the derived constraint formulation. The resulting formulation enforces obstacle-avoidance constraints while optimizing control torques, granting the safety layer direct authority over attitude behavior and eliminating the hierarchical disconnect inherent in reference-tracking safety filters. We validate the approaches in both high-fidelity simulation and real-world flight experiments, demonstrating reliable constraint satisfaction and successful obstacle avoidance. These results suggest that torque-level high-order CBFs provide a practical pathway to provably safer quadrotor navigation under full rigid-body dynamics.

</details>


### [242] [Multi-modal Representation Fusion for Long-term Microsurgical Anastomosis Trajectory Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14492)
*Yuxuan Liu,Yating Luo,Yunfei Luan,Xinyao Zhou,Yuyang Zhuge,Yao Guo,Guang-Zhong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨模态特征融合框架，用于长期2D和3D显微外科吻合轨迹预测，通过融合语义识别与连续运动估计等多模态视觉线索，提升手术场景理解与机器人操作精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微外科吻合对手术机器人极具挑战，现有轨迹预测方法主要依赖手术视频特征和历史轨迹，忽略了复杂器械-组织交互中丰富的语义与运动信息所构成的多模态判别性表征。

Method: 构建由多模态表征提取器和跨模态特征融合网络组成的框架；将手术对象语义识别与连续运动估计作为多模态视觉线索，提供互补的时空引导；采用由粗到细的渐进融合策略并结合长短期记忆机制逐步捕获时序依赖；在自建机器人平台及包含体模与离体场景的综合数据集上训练与评估。

Result: 大量定量与定性实验表明，该框架持续优于现有方法，并在不同手术场景中表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作突出了多模态跨模态融合在显微外科吻合轨迹预测中的有效性，对智能机器人辅助手术与自主手术系统具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Microsurgical anastomosis is one of the most challenging and sophisticated procedures for surgical robotics, requiring accurate manipulation of surgical instruments within a limited space to achieve precise operations. Accurate surgical trajectory anticipation is essential for surgical scene understanding, intraoperative planning, robotic manipulation, and reliable behaviour analysis. However, existing approaches mainly focus on features extracted from surgical videos and historical trajectories, while overlooking rich semantic and motion information that can provide more discriminative multi-modal representations for complex surgical instrument-tissue interactions. In this work, we propose a cross-modal feature fusion framework for long-term 2D and 3D microsurgical anastomosis trajectory anticipation. The proposed framework consists of a multi-modal representation extractor and a cross-modal feature fusion network. Semantic recognition of surgical objects and consecutive motion estimation are incorporated as multi-modal visual cues, providing complementary spatial and temporal guidance for instrument-tissue interaction characterization. A coarse-to-fine progressive fusion strategy with a long-short-term memory mechanism is introduced to capture temporal dependencies step by step. The training and evaluation are conducted on our established robotic platform and a comprehensive dataset containing both phantom and ex-vivo surgical scenarios is constructed. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms existing methods and exhibits improved robustness across different surgical scenes, highlighting its potential for both intelligent robot-assisted and autonomous surgical systems.

</details>


### [243] [From Field to Planning: Learning Temporal Polar Gradient Fields for Vision-based AAV Trajectory Planning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14482)
*Haowen Gao,Zhan Chen,Yinuo Xu,Yifeng Niu,Mengyun Wang,Chao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段 Field-to-Planning (F2P) 学习框架，通过 Ego-centric Polar Occupancy (EPO) 表示与 Temporal Polar Gradient Field (TPGF) 为动态环境下 AAV 轨迹规划提供实时、准确的安全梯度指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AAV 轨迹规划方法通常依赖静态地图和动态障碍物跟踪来构建梯度场，计算负担重且生成轨迹偏保守，难以满足动态环境中的实时性与安全性需求。

Method: 第一阶段引入 EPO 表示，匹配机载相机视锥几何并支持非结构化场景建模；随后通过多核可分离高斯卷积构建 TPGF，统一提供安全梯度，无需显式区分静态与动态障碍物。为缓解极坐标网格卷积带来的空间歧义，提出 ESDF 引导的离线学习策略，将几何先验融入 TPGF。第二阶段使用校准后的 TPGF，以自监督学习方式指导轨迹规划策略。

Result: 与其他方法对比结果表明该方法具有优越性；在多种杂乱动态环境中的大量真实实验验证了其在资源受限机载系统上的实时性能与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: F2P 框架能够为动态环境中的 AAV 轨迹规划提供准确且高效的安全梯度，并具备在资源受限平台上实时、鲁棒部署的能力。

Abstract: Autonomous Aerial Vehicle (AAV) trajectory planning requires real-time and accurate safety gradient guidance, especially in dynamic environments. Existing methods typically rely on static maps and dynamic obstacle tracking to construct gradient fields, imposing heavy computational burdens and yielding conservative trajectories. To address these limitations, we propose a two-stage Field-to-Planning (F2P) learning framework to provide accurate and efficient safety gradients for trajectory planning in dynamic environments. In the first stage, we introduce an Ego-centric Polar Occupancy (EPO) representation that naturally matches the geometry of the onboard camera frustum, while supporting unstructured scene modeling. Subsequently, we formulate a Temporal Polar Gradient Field (TPGF) via multi-kernel separable Gaussian convolution to provide unified safety gradients, without explicitly distinguishing between static and dynamic obstacles. To mitigate the inherent spatial ambiguity introduced by applying convolutions within the polar grid, we propose an ESDF-guided offline learning strategy that incorporates geometric priors into TPGF. In the second stage, we employ the calibrated TPGF to guide the trajectory planning policy in a self-supervised learning manner. Comparative results with other approaches demonstrate the superiority of our method. Extensive real-world experiments within various cluttered dynamic environments validate the real-time performance and robustness on resource-constrained onboard systems.

</details>


### [244] [Shared-Control Teleoperation of Omnidirectional Aerial Robots for Continuous Valve Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14478)
*Jinjie Li,Yicheng Chen,Haokun Liu,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于前臂角度阈值的共享控制方法，当操作者前臂角度超过阈值时切换控制权，使全向空中机器人启动连续旋转，从而克服人体前臂运动范围限制，实现阀门连续转动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全向空中机器人具备6自由度独立位姿控制，适用于空中操作；但复杂空中环境中仍需人类操作。此前遥操作系统虽将人手旋转灵活性引入空中工作空间，并通过垂直安装阀门旋转任务验证，但受限于人类前臂运动范围，机器人无法连续旋转阀门，阻碍工业应用。

Method: 设置前臂角度阈值；当角度超过阈值时触发连续旋转，切换控制权，由机器人接管旋转控制。该机制属于人机交互中的共享控制。

Result: 提出一种在超过阈值时启动连续旋转的方法，实现控制权切换；摘要未给出定量实验数据，但表明其可扩展空中遥操作能力。

Conclusion: 该共享控制方法可弥补人体前臂运动范围不足，实现阀门连续旋转，推动空中遥操作在工业场景中的应用。

Abstract: Omnidirectional aerial robots offer full 6-DoF independent control over position and orientation, making them popular for aerial manipulation. Although there have been advancements in robotic autonomy, human operation remains essential in complex aerial environments. Previously, we proposed an aerial teleoperation system that brings the rotational flexibility of human hands into the unbounded aerial workspace, which was evaluated through a vertically mounted valve-turning task. However, due to the limited range of human forearm's motion, the robot could not continuously rotate the valve, hindering its application in industry. In this work, we propose an approach to initiate continuous rotation when the forearm angle exceeds a threshold. This switch of control authority exemplifies the concept shared control from human-robot interaction, marking a further step in aerial teleoperation.

</details>


### [245] [Red-Teaming of Vision-Language-Action Models via Quality Diversity Environment Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14415)
*Shihan Zhao,Henry Chen,Ya-Chuan Hsu,Minjune Hwang,Siddharth Srikanth,Freddie Liang,Akanksha Saran,Aaquib Tabrez,Stefanos Nikolaidis,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Q-DEG，通过质量多样性优化生成对抗环境，并利用VLA自身成功轨迹进行自模仿微调，以提升VLA对分布外扰动的鲁棒性并促进仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人泛化任务上很有前景，但分布内表现好，面对物体位置、光照、纹理、相机位姿等测试时轻微偏移时往往失败，因此需要提升其鲁棒性。

Method: Q-DEG使用质量多样性（QD）优化寻找一组高失败率环境，并利用从VLA提取的策略嵌入保证环境多样性；这些环境暴露脆弱性后，再用VLA自身成功rollout进行自模仿微调，从而缓解脆弱性并提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在仿真中对三种代表性VLA模型实验，Q-DEG相比基础VLA以及用两种红队基线采集数据微调的VLA，提升了对分布外对抗环境的鲁棒性；并证明该鲁棒性提升有助于sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: Q-DEG是一种通过对抗环境生成与自模仿微调提升VLA鲁棒性的有效方法，并能促进仿真到现实迁移。

Abstract: A long-standing goal in robotics is to develop generalist robots that can perform tasks across a wide range of settings, flexibly specified with images and text. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising path toward this goal by training on large collections of robot demonstrations. However, while VLAs perform well in-distribution, they often fail under minor test-time shifts, such as changes in object positions, lighting, textures, and camera poses. To address this, we propose Quality Diversity for Diverse Environment Generation (Q-DEG), a method for improving VLA robustness by generating adversarial environments and fine-tuning the VLA on its own successful rollouts. Q-DEG uses Quality Diversity (QD) optimization to find a set of high-failure environments, while promoting diversity using policy embeddings extracted from the VLA. These environments expose vulnerabilities that can be mitigated through self-imitation fine-tuning, thereby improving the VLA's robustness. In simulation, we conduct experiments across three representative VLA models, and our results indicate that Q-DEG improves robustness to out-of-distribution (OOD) adversarial environments compared to the base VLA and VLAs fine-tuned using data collected from two red-teaming baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate that improving VLA robustness through Q-DEG facilitates sim-to-real transfer. Our anonymous project website is at: this https URL .

</details>


### [246] [Memory Without Curation: Learning Multi-Stream Latent Memory for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14406)
*Yilin Wu,Rutav Shah,Shuaiyi Huang,Sumedh A. Sontakke,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Andrea Bajcsy,Max Simchowitz,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMPRINT 是一种策略无关的潜在记忆框架，通过多流潜在记忆增强 VLA 模型，无需显式约束记忆内容，在记忆密集型任务中显著提升成功率并保持低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略执行复杂任务需要长期记忆，但直接以全部历史观测为条件会导致策略学习到虚假捷径，并在部署时引入高延迟。现有记忆增强策略通常只保留近期帧、关键帧、文本摘要或仅学习视觉流的潜在表示，即人为筛选记忆内容。

Method: IMPRINT 将观测、动作、本体感觉等多流信息压缩为潜在记忆，并在推理时增量更新以实现低延迟。为避免捷径学习，采用两阶段训练：第一阶段监督记忆重建过去多流信息并估计当前任务进度；第二阶段与策略联合进行端到端微调，优化动作预测。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界记忆密集型任务中，IMPRINT 相比基础 VLA 平均成功率至少提升 26%，优于流行替代方法，并在记忆增强 VLA 中保持最低推理延迟。消融实验证明引入多样信息流对机器人记忆有效。

Conclusion: 结果表明，放弃对历史进行人工筛选、采用多流潜在记忆是有效的，IMPRINT 能提升记忆增强 VLA 的性能与部署效率。

Abstract: It is widely appreciated that robotic policies require long-term memory to perform complex tasks. However, directly conditioning policies on all past observations leaves policy learning susceptible to learning spurious shortcuts, and introduces large latency at policy deployment. As such, today's memory-augmented policies curate what to remember, either by retaining only recent frames, selected keyframes, or textual summaries, or by learning latent representations of the visual stream alone. In this work, we propose an alternative approach IMPRINT, a policy-agnostic latent memory framework that augments vision-language-action (VLA) models with multi-stream memory without explicitly constraining what the policy should remember. IMPRINT compresses different streams into a latent memory that is updated incrementally at inference, enabling low latency. To avoid shortcut learning, IMPRINT adopts a two-stage training procedure, first supervising memory to reconstruct multiple streams (observations, actions, proprioception) of past information, as well as estimate current task progress, followed by end-to-end finetuning, jointly with the policy, on action prediction. Across both simulation and real-world tasks demanding memory, IMPRINT improves average success over the base VLA by at least 26%, outperforming popular alternatives, and maintaining lowest inference latency among memory-augmented VLAs. Through careful ablations, we demonstrate the utility of incorporating diverse information streams into robotic memory, validating the advantages of foregoing history curation. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [247] [OATH: Object-Agnostic Tracking for Dexterous Hands, Enabling Rapid Physics-based Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14379)
*Solvin Sigurdson,Prasanna Sriganesh,Juan Borbon,Joel Burdick,Dingqi Zhang,Jiuguang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将基于物理的灵巧手重定向重构为残差学习问题，利用包含局部接触误差信号的结构化状态表示，训练统一残差策略，从而在未见物体上实现零样本泛化，并构建物理重定向器 OATH。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重定向方法要么忽略接触物理约束，要么依赖计算昂贵的仿真采样优化；强化学习虽可用于接触动力学规划与控制，但标准策略通常需针对特定物体训练，难以泛化到任意几何。

Method: 将物理重定向视为残差学习问题；在观测空间中加入局部接触误差信号，引导每个机器人连杆到达物体表面的目标坐标；训练单一统一的残差策略；在仿真中对运动学参考轨迹滚动执行该策略，得到基于物理的重定向器 OATH。

Result: OATH 每 1 秒参考轨迹仅需 0.4 秒计算，达到 2.4 倍实时性能，相比采样方法计算成本降低 60–350 倍；在 DexYCB 上恢复 58% 的留出轨迹和 54% 的未见物体轨迹，跟踪误差低于 2 cm。

Conclusion: 结构化状态表示与残差学习可实现跨物体零样本泛化的物理灵巧手重定向，并在效率与精度上显著优于采样优化方法。

Abstract: Retargeting human demonstrations into dynamically feasible dexterous hand trajectories requires resolving complex contact physics, yet current methods either omit physical constraints or rely on computationally expensive sampling-based optimization in simulation. Reinforcement learning has previously been used to achieve planning and control through contact dynamics; however, standard policies require object-specific training and fail to generalize across arbitrary geometries. This work reframes physics-based retargeting as a residual learning problem, where we demonstrate that zero-shot generalization across objects relies on structured state representations. Specifically, formulating the observation space to include a localized contact-error signal guiding each robot link to its target coordinate on the object surface enables a single, unified residual policy to successfully grasp novel, unseen object geometries. Rolling out this policy in simulation on a kinematic reference trajectory yields a physics-based retargeter, OATH. Operating at 0.4 seconds of compute per second of reference, OATH achieves 2.4x faster-than-real-time performance and a 60-350x reduction in computational cost compared to sampling-based methods. The system recovers 58% of held-out and 54% of unseen-object DexYCB trajectories while maintaining a sub-2 cm tracking error.

</details>


### [248] [DexFLEX: Contact-Aware Foundation Controller for Command-Guided Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14372)
*Weikang Wan,Zeyu Lai,Jianyu Hou,Yuchen Zhou,Jiawei Fu,Henrik I Christensen,Hao Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexFLEX 是一种接触感知的基础控制器，将上游指尖运动草案转化为接触一致的关节指令，并在仿真、真实共享控制和视触觉策略学习中显著提升鲁棒性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧机械手虽可从遥操作或学习策略获得运动意图，但成功执行还取决于这些指令很少指定的接触决策；因此需要一种能根据当前触觉-本体状态处理接触并稳定未来接触的控制器。

Method: DexFLEX 不将草案视为待复制轨迹，而视为意图证据。它从当前触觉-本体状态出发，提出可行的短时域运动块，预测其接触后果，并选择最符合指令且能保持未来接触稳定的候选。推理时采用 Draft-Dream-Select 流程，结合纯先验提议用于恢复和草案种子提议用于响应性，再将所选运动解码为可执行关节空间目标。

Result: 在退化指令仿真、真实世界共享控制和视运动策略学习中，同一训练好的控制器无需重训练即可提升鲁棒性；真实遥操作成功率从 29.2% 提升至 78.3%，策略学习成功率达 46.7%，是直接关节动作预测的 3.5 倍，是仅先验校正的 1.56 倍。

Conclusion: DexFLEX 通过接触感知的草案-预测-选择机制，将不完善的指尖运动指令转化为接触一致的关节命令，有效提升灵巧操作在真实场景中的鲁棒性和成功率，且无需额外重训练。

Abstract: Dexterous robot hands can receive useful motion intent from teleoperation or learned policies, but successful execution also depends on contact decisions that these commands rarely specify. We introduce DexFLEX, a contact-aware foundation controller that turns upstream fingertip-motion drafts into contact-consistent joint commands. Instead of treating a draft as a trajectory to copy, DexFLEX treats it as evidence about intent: from the current tactile-proprioceptive state, it proposes feasible short-horizon motion chunks, predicts their contact consequences, and selects the candidate that best follows the command while preserving future contact stability. At inference time, a simple Draft-Dream-Select procedure combines pure-prior proposals for recovery with draft-seeded proposals for responsiveness, then decodes the selected motion into executable joint-space targets. Across degraded-command simulation, real-world shared control, and visuomotor policy learning, the same trained controller improves robustness without retraining, increasing real-world teleoperation success from 29.2% to 78.3% and reaching 46.7% policy-learning success, 3.5 times direct joint-action prediction, and 1.56 times prior-only correction. Videos are available at this http URL .

</details>


### [249] [CoPlanVLM: Coordinated Path Planning for a Robot Team using Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14341)
*David R. Martin,Jared Paquet,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Pramod Khargonekar,Kyle Volle,J. Humberto Ramos,Scott Nivison*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CoPlanVLM，利用 VLM 将单条自然语言指令与图像转化为双机器人协同路径；通过图像叠加、任务分类和任务特定思维链增强空间推理，在仿真与 Turtlebot 4 上平均成功率达 84.4%，优于最接近基线的 50.0%，且成功路径平均缩短 27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 提供灵活的自然语言机器人团队控制接口，但其空间推理能力较弱，严重限制了基于 VLM 的路径规划，尤其是多机器人协同场景。

Method: CoPlanVLM 包含三个组件：(1) 图像叠加，标记自由空间与障碍物；(2) 任务分类，为每条指令选择最合适的底层规划器和推理提示；(3) 任务特定思维链推理，提示 VLM 识别关键物体、分配角色并验证路径无碰撞后再规划。该方法支持复杂协同机动、任务分工、特定路线、环路、大范围覆盖及方向推理。

Result: 在 Gazebo 仿真和物理 Turtlebot 4 硬件上验证。CoPlanVLM 平均成功率为 84.4%，最接近基线为 50.0%；在两者均成功的试验中，CoPlanVLM 路径平均缩短 27%。

Conclusion: CoPlanVLM 有效提升了 VLM 的空间推理与双机器人协同路径规划能力，可处理多种复杂协同任务，并已开源项目代码。

Abstract: We present CoPlanVLM, which uses a Vision Language Model (VLM) to translate a single natural-language instruction and image into coordinated paths for a team of two robots. While VLMs offer a flexible, natural-language interface for commanding robot teams, their weak spatial reasoning has greatly limited VLM-based path planning. CoPlanVLM improves the VLM's spatial reasoning and path planning abilities through three components: (1) an image overlay marking both free space and obstacles, (2) task classification that selects the most appropriate low-level planner and reasoning prompt for each instruction, and (3) task-specific chain-of-thought reasoning that prompts the VLM to identify key objects, allocate roles, and verify routes are collision-free before committing to a plan. Together, these components enable the team to complete complex coordinated maneuvers requiring both robots and divide large tasks between robots to increase efficiency. CoPlanVLM also supports detailed maneuvers involving specific routes, loops, and coverage of large areas, as well as tasks requiring directional reasoning such as north/south or left/right. We validate CoPlanVLM in both Gazebo simulation and on physical Turtlebot 4 hardware. CoPlanVLM attains an average success rate of 84.4% compared to 50.0% for the closest baseline. On trials where both methods succeed, CoPlanVLM's paths are 27% shorter on average. Project code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [250] [PILOT: Guiding Long-Horizon World-Model Planning for Robot Manipulation with Visual Subgoals and Policy Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14335)
*Yang Liu,Hanchen Cui,Karthik Desingh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PILOT 通过视频模型生成中间视觉子目标，并用学习策略初始化 CEM，结合动作条件世界模型进行滚动时域局部优化，从而提升长时程机器人操作的成功率与规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型规划在目标条件机器人操作中虽有效，但长时程规划面临两个问题：遥远视觉目标难以为准备性动作提供指导，且无信息动作采样使局部优化效率低下。

Method: 提出 PILOT（Policy-Initialized Local Optimization with Temporal subgoals）：先用视频模型生成中间视觉子目标；再用学习策略初始化交叉熵方法（CEM）；随后在滚动时域控制循环中，以动作条件世界模型为引导，将候选动作序列逐步优化至各子目标。

Result: 在四个 UR5e 真实机器人操作任务中，PILOT 成功率达 68.8%，高于无策略初始化的 55.0%、仅策略执行的 13.8% 和直接最终目标规划的 0.0%。仿真中，长时程任务增益持续：Push-T 上比直接执行同一策略高 30 个百分点，LIBERO-10 上高 22 个百分点。策略初始化还加速局部优化：Push-T 上 CEM 仅需 4 次迭代即达到通用初始化 10 次迭代的成本，并在相同采样预算下将最终规划成本降低 24.1%。

Conclusion: 将时间子目标与策略引导的动作搜索相结合，可同时改善长时程任务执行效果和局部规划效率。

Abstract: Latent world-model planning has shown promising results in goal-conditioned robotic manipulation, but remains challenging for long-horizon planning due to limited temporal guidance and inefficient action search. A distant visual goal provides little guidance for preparatory actions, while uninformed action sampling makes local optimization inefficient. We propose PILOT (Policy-Initialized Local Optimization with Temporal subgoals), which uses a video model to generate intermediate visual subgoals and a learned policy to initialize the cross-entropy method (CEM). CEM refines candidate action sequences toward each subgoal using an action-conditioned world model in a receding-horizon control loop. In our primary real-robot evaluation across four UR5e manipulation tasks, PILOT achieves 68.8% success, compared with 55.0% without policy initialization, 13.8% for policy-only execution, and 0.0% for direct final-goal planning. Simulation results show that these gains persist over longer horizons: PILOT exceeds direct execution of the same policy by 30 percentage points on long Push-T tasks and by 22 percentage points on LIBERO-10. Policy initialization also accelerates local optimization: on Push-T, CEM matches the cost reached by generic initialization after 10 iterations in only 4 iterations and reduces the final planning cost by 24.1% under the same sampling budget. These results show that combining temporal subgoals with policy-guided action search improves both long-horizon task execution and local planning efficiency. Videos are available on the webpage: this https URL .

</details>


### [251] [Akule: Fast and Scalable Multi-Robot Motion Planning via Sparse Interaction Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14248)
*Manav Vora,Gokul Puthumanaillam,Aditya Penumarti,Jane Shin,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Akule 提出稀疏交互扩散框架，在冻结的单机器人扩散先验上加入可学习的成对修正、上下文选择器和组合器，实现可扩展的多机器人运动规划，并交由下游搜索规划器验证与修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型能为多机器人运动规划（MRMP）提供表达力强的轨迹先验，但扩展到大规模机器人团队时，需要协调各机器人运动，同时避免昂贵的联合计算。

Method: 在冻结的单机器人先验上增加三个可学习模块：可复用成对修正模型 R_phi、决定评估哪些修正的上下文选择器 U_psi，以及通过上下文相关带符号权重组合效果的组合器 G_omega。该分解将“哪些交互值得计算”与“如何组合其效果”分离。先用匹配的成对轨迹优化训练 R_phi，再从物理优化的联合轨迹中学习 G_omega 和 U_psi，无需预设真实交互图；生成的联合提议随后交给下游基于搜索的规划器进行验证和修复。

Result: 在多种 MRMP 基准上，Akule 取得有竞争力的规划成功率，完整规划器加速最高达 25 倍，具备零样本群体规模迁移能力，并可成功规划多达 256 个机器人。消融实验表明，学习到的选择与组合相比启发式选择和均匀求和，能减少修复前冲突和下游修复开销。

Conclusion: 将交互选择与效果组合解耦，可有效利用单机器人扩散先验和稀疏成对交互，实现大规模多机器人协调规划，并与搜索规划器结合提升效率与可扩展性。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide expressive trajectory priors for multi-robot motion planning (MRMP), but scaling them to large teams requires coordinating individual motions without expensive joint computation. We introduce Akule, a sparse interaction diffusion framework that augments a frozen single-robot prior with three learned modules: a reusable pairwise correction model $R_{\phi}$, a contextual selector $U_{\psi}$ that determines which corrections to evaluate, and a composer $G_{\omega}$ that combines their effects through context-dependent signed weights. This decomposition separates which interactions merit computation from how their effects should be combined into coordinated denoising updates. We train $R_{\phi}$ using matched pairwise trajectory optimizations, then learn $G_{\omega}$ and $U_{\psi}$ from physically optimized joint trajectories without requiring a prescribed ground-truth interaction graph. The resulting joint proposals are passed to a downstream search-based planner for validation and repair. Across diverse MRMP benchmarks, Akule achieves competitive planning success, complete-planner speedups of up to $25\times$, zero-shot population transfer, and successful planning with up to $256$ robots. Ablations further demonstrate that learned selection and composition reduce pre-repair conflicts and downstream repair effort relative to heuristic selection and uniform summation.

</details>


### [252] [From LLM Plans to Authorized Motion: Execution-Bound Authorization for Excavators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14245)
*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Seihun Kim,Soyi Jung,Soohyun Park,Jouni Mattila,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出执行绑定授权，将审批—派发—执行区间内的动作、生成运动、任务契约与准入容差绑定到一次性租约；通过原子派发复查当前权限和关节漂移，并由周期监督触发五次多项式停车，防止LLM控制的挖掘机执行未经授权的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM控制的挖掘机可能执行机械上可行、但目的地或权限已不再符合操作者审批的运动，因此需要在审批、派发与执行之间建立与具体运动绑定的授权、完整性校验和中断机制。

Method: 提出执行绑定授权：一次性租约绑定动作、生成运动、任务契约和准入容差；原子派发重新检查当前权限与实测关节漂移；周期监督检测撤销，并通过采样关节、行程、流量和参考加速度检查选择带载俯仰终点的五次多项式停止。框架将精确运动身份、派发完整性与负载感知中断相连。评估包括672例接口测试套件、同步仿真中的四个带载挖掘案例、无载MuJoCo/Pinocchio迁移以及两个命令级LLM规划器。

Result: 网关接纳全部120个良性接口案例，在攻击套件中产生零未授权派发；带载派发窗口测试产生零未授权卸料。仿真卸料阶段撤销时，最大封闭场地卸料量从1.23 m³降至0.33 m³；带载回转停止使用低于75%的额定回转扭矩。

Conclusion: 结果证明了运动绑定授权的有效性，并量化了受监督挖掘技能在撤销后的保持与停止行为。

Abstract: An LLM-directed excavator can execute mechanically feasible motion whose destination or authority no longer matches the operator's approval. We propose execution-bound authorization for the approval--dispatch--execution interval. A one-use lease binds the action, generated motion, mission contract and admission tolerance; atomic dispatch rechecks current authority and measured joint drift. Periodic supervision detects revocation and requests a quintic stop with a carry-pitch endpoint, selected through sampled joint, stroke, flow and reference-acceleration checks. The framework connects exact motion identity to dispatch integrity and load-aware interruption. Evaluation combines a 672-case interface suite, four loaded digging cases in synchronous simulation, unloaded MuJoCo/Pinocchio transfers and two command-level LLM planners. The gateway admitted all 120 benign interface cases and produced zero unauthorized dispatches in the attack suite. Loaded dispatch-window tests produced zero unauthorized deposition. During simulated dump-phase revocation, maximum closed-site deposition decreased from 1.23 to 0.33\,m$^3$; loaded-slew stops used less than 75\% of nominal swing torque. These results demonstrate motion-bound authorization and quantify the retention and stopping behavior of supervised excavation skills.

</details>


### [253] [Composable Dynamics for Cooperative Humanoids: A RoboCompiler Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14237)
*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作扩展 RoboCompiler，以成员级动力学汇编实现物理连接人形机器人的一致动力学，支持执行器闭环、机器人间力与变支撑，达到1e-10量级精度并完成MuJoCo仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理连接的人形机器人需要在内部执行器闭环、机器人间相互作用力以及不断变化的地面支撑下保持动力学一致，现有方法难以统一处理这些耦合。

Method: 扩展 RoboCompiler：采用成员级动力学汇编，从刚体、运动执行器部件与反射转子惯量编译降阶动力学；由编译体雅可比和解析约束曲率得到显式科里奥利因子，满足浮基准速度功率恒等式；两级降阶保留可复用机器人模型并组合支撑与手部连接；局部惯性更新秩至多6，给定加速度约束力旋量对执行器出力仿射。

Result: 在8种人形配置、264个可行状态中，归一化加速度误差相对硬约束参考不超过1.6e-10，相对编译器独立参考不超过1.4e-10；MuJoCo仿真中环形舞骨盆RMS误差为0.73 mm，并完成经历20次支撑模式切换的侧步。

Conclusion: 该框架为五台手拉手人形机器人提供一致、可复用且高精度的动力学编译接口，支持执行器感知跟踪、耦合力分配、负载更新与支撑模式切换。

Abstract: Physically connected humanoids need consistent dynamics for internal actuator loops, inter-robot forces, and changing ground supports. We extend RoboCompiler with member-wise dynamics assembly, compiling reduced dynamics from rigid bodies, moving actuator parts, and reflected rotor inertias. Compiled body Jacobians and analytic constraint curvature yield an explicit Coriolis factor satisfying the power identity for floating-base quasi-velocities. A two-level reduction retains reusable robot models while composing their supports and hand connections; local inertial updates have rank at most six, and prescribed-acceleration constraint wrenches are affine in actuator efforts. These interfaces support actuator-aware tracking, coupled force allocation, payload updates, and support-mode switching for five hand-linked humanoids. Across 264 feasible states in eight humanoid configurations, normalised acceleration discrepancies remain within $1.6\!\times\!10^{-10}$ against a hard-constraint reference and $1.4\!\times\!10^{-10}$ against a compiler-independent reference; MuJoCo simulations achieve 0.73~mm root-mean-square (RMS) pelvis error in the ring dance and complete side steps through 20 support-mode changes.

</details>


### [254] [Mapping Tire-Road Friction Coefficients Along Driven Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14235)
*Risto Ojala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离线后处理方法FrictionMap，利用车载传感器数据沿行驶轨迹映射轮胎-路面摩擦系数，并在仿真中优于常用在线估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩擦系数决定车辆操控极限，对智能车辆系统至关重要；现有研究多关注在线摩擦估计，尚无基于记录传感器数据进行后处理并沿轨迹映射摩擦系数的研究，而此类映射信息可供网联车共享以在低摩擦区域采取主动措施。

Method: FrictionMap是一种离线后处理方法，仅需常用车载传感器信息、基本车辆参数集和滑移阈值。它采用扩展卡尔曼滤波与Rauch-Tung-Striebel平滑器估计车辆动力学，通过基于轮胎滑移的过滤识别摩擦极限可观测的数据点，并对这些点拟合高斯过程以获得沿轨迹的连续摩擦系数估计。

Result: 通过仿真研究验证，FrictionMap表现优于常用的在线摩擦估计技术。

Conclusion: 该方法为基于记录数据的摩擦系数映射提供了有效途径，代码与仿真基准将在论文录用后开源，以促进后续研究，并有望支持网联车共享摩擦信息。

Abstract: Information of the tire-road friction coefficient is critical for intelligent vehicle systems, as friction establishes the limits at which the vehicle can be manoeuvred without loss of control. While there is extensive literature on online friction estimation, no previous studies have explored post-processing and mapping friction coefficients along a driven trajectory based on recorded sensor data. Mapped friction information could be shared by connected vehicles, enabling proactive measures in low-friction areas. This paper presents an offline post-processing method, FrictionMap, for mapping friction coefficients from recorded driving data. The method operates on commonly available on-board vehicle sensor information, and requires only a basic vehicle parameter set and a slip threshold as prior knowledge. FrictionMap runs an extended Kalman filter combined with a Rauch-Tung-Striebel smoother to estimate vehicle dynamics. Data points where the friction limit is observable are identified by filtering points based on tire slip. A Gaussian process is fitted to these points to acquire a continuous estimate of the friction coefficient along the trajectory. FrictionMap is validated via a simulation study, and shown to outperform commonly used online friction estimation techniques. The code implementation and the simulation benchmark are released as open-source after paper acceptance to enable further research.

</details>


### [255] [ClothCode: Generative Modeling for Garment State Estimation and Dynamics Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14200)
*Yingdong Ru,Lipeng Zhuang,Shiyu Fan,Zhuo He,Florent P. Audonnet,Gerardo Aragon Camarasa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ClothCode 是一个统一的生成式框架，用于服装状态估计与动作条件动力学预测，在共享离散配置空间中通过残差向量量化与掩码生成 Transformer 实现高效推理，并显著提升真实机器人折叠/铺平任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服装状态估计与动作条件动力学预测对机器人布料操作至关重要，但现有方法通常分离、精度有限且推理慢，尤其扩散模型计算开销大，难以用于实时模型预测控制。

Method: 提出 ClothCode：共享离散配置空间；材料对齐 tokenizer 将服装配置编码为残差向量量化 patch token；任务特定的 Masked Generative Transformers 通过并行置信度引导细化预测这些 token，统一支持状态估计和动作条件动力学预测。

Result: 在三种服装模板上，状态估计平均位移误差降低 26.6%，动力学预测降低 37.0%（相对最强基线）。动力学预测每窗口仅 16.7 ms，而扩散模型为 556.2 ms。集成到同一模型预测控制器后，真实世界折叠与铺平成功率从 56.7% 提升至 78.9%。

Conclusion: ClothCode 以统一离散生成框架同时实现高精度服装状态估计与快速动作条件动力学预测，显著优于基线并提升真实机器人布料操作成功率，适合实时模型预测控制。

Abstract: We introduce ClothCode, a unified generative framework for garment state estimation and action-conditioned dynamics prediction in a shared discrete configuration space. A material-aligned tokenizer encodes garment configuration as residual vector-quantized patch tokens, while task-specific Masked Generative Transformers predict these tokens through parallel confidence-guided refinement. Across three garment templates, ClothCode reduces mean displacement error by 26.6% for state estimation and 37.0% for dynamics prediction relative to the strongest baselines. Dynamics prediction requires only 16.7 ms per window, compared with 556.2 ms for diffusion. Integrated into the same model-predictive controller, ClothCode improves real-world folding and flattening success from 56.7% to 78.9%. Further results and videos are available at: this https URL

</details>


### [256] [Observation-Referenced Tissue Deformation Forecasting with Tool-Driven and Residual Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14191)
*Tom Olesch,Nan Xiao,Xiongye Xiao,Jonathan C. DeLong,Fei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种观测参考的可微组织形变预测框架，利用已知未来器械轨迹预测组织形变，并在手术操作片段上验证其优于绝对仿真预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测未来器械运动引起的组织形变可支持预测性规划与日益自主的手术系统；但绝对仿真位置预测易受重建、初始化及持续仿真偏移影响。

Method: 使用场景特定的可微表面模型拟合观测到的器械-组织交互窗口；提出观测参考公式，将每个组织点锚定到最后有效观测，仅叠加由已知预规划未来器械轨迹引起的形变；物理模型采用可微、无网格的位置基础动力学，包含场景特定刚度、附着、区域尺度和抓取耦合；可选观测参考残差模型结合图谱空间模态与周期谐波动力学，建模工具输入未解释的结构化运动。

Result: 在三个数据集的12个手术操作片段上回顾性评估；观测参考带来最大增益，相对于对应绝对仿真预测的中位片段内误差比为0.598；当剩余运动呈现周期结构时，可选周期残差进一步改善预测。

Conclusion: 观测参考形变预测可作为未来手术世界模型、数字孪生和生成式模拟器的实用构建模块，用于预测器械操作下组织如何演化。

Abstract: Forecasting tissue deformation from future instrument motion could support predictive planning and increasingly autonomous surgical systems. We study this problem using a scene-specific differentiable surface model fitted to an observed tool-tissue interaction window. Rather than forecasting absolute simulated positions, we introduce an \textit{observation-referenced} formulation that anchors each tissue point to its last valid observation and adds only the deformation induced by the known, preplanned future tool trajectory. This reduces sensitivity to reconstruction, initialization, and persistent simulation offsets while preserving the predicted deformation response. The physical model uses differentiable, mesh-free position-based dynamics with scene-specific rigidity, attachment, region scale, and grasp coupling. We further introduce an optional \textit{observation-referenced residual model} combining graph-spectral spatial modes with periodic harmonic dynamics for structured motion not explained by the tool input. We evaluate the framework retrospectively on 12 surgical manipulation clips from three datasets. Observation referencing provides the largest gain, with a median within-clip error ratio of $0.598$ relative to the corresponding absolute simulation forecast. The optional periodic residual further improves forecasting when the remaining motion exhibits periodic structure. These results support observation-referenced deformation prediction as a practical building block for future surgical world models, digital twins, and generative simulators that must predict how tissue evolves under instrument actions.

</details>


### [257] [Disentangling Task-Relevant Latent Dynamics for Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14159)
*Dayou Li,Jionghao Wang,Peihao Wang,Jiuzhou Lei,Hao Wang,Jichen Chai,Hezhen Hu,Minghui Zheng,Zhangyang Wang,Xin Li,Wenping Wang,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiTaR 提出一种从异构视频中学习稠密奖励的框架：先通过非对称两阶段过程从多帧视频隐变量中学习任务相关连续表征，抑制任务无关视觉变化；再在该任务相关隐空间中用历史与语言条件化的 rectified-flow 模型建模任务动态，并由动态兼容性的时序变化导出方向性稠密奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中的强化学习长期受奖励设计瓶颈限制：人工稠密奖励需要大量任务特定工程，稀疏奖励指导有限；从视频学习奖励虽可扩展，但现有方法常在纠缠的视觉空间中计算奖励，使任务相关状态变化与无关视觉变化混杂，导致奖励易受视觉干扰项影响而非响应真实任务执行。

Method: DiTaR 从多帧视频隐变量中学习任务相关连续表征，采用非对称两阶段程序抑制任务无关视觉变化；在任务相关隐空间中训练历史与语言条件化的 rectified-flow 模型以捕捉任务动态，并基于动态兼容性的时间变化推导方向性稠密奖励，从而直接在任务相关动态而非通用视觉变化上进行奖励建模。

Result: 在 MetaWorld-v3 和 ManiSkill 上，DiTaR 相比基于视频的奖励基线显著提升策略学习效果，并通过奖励重标注迁移到真实世界离线强化学习；奖励分析进一步表明 DiTaR 提供的奖励更可靠。

Conclusion: DiTaR 通过解耦任务相关潜在动态来建模视频奖励，缓解视觉干扰导致的奖励不可靠问题，为异构视频下的可扩展稠密奖励学习提供了有效方案，并在仿真与真实场景中验证了其有效性和迁移能力。

Abstract: Reward design remains a major bottleneck for reinforcement learning in robotic manipulation. Handcrafted dense rewards can compensate for the limited guidance provided by sparse rewards, but require substantial task-specific reward engineering. Learning rewards from videos provides a scalable, data-driven alternative to manual reward design. However, existing video-based rewards are often computed in entangled visual spaces where task-relevant state changes are confounded with extraneous visual variation. As a result, the learned reward may respond to visual distractors instead of actual task execution. To address this limitation, we propose DiTaR (Disentangling Task-Relevant Latent Dynamics for Reward Modeling), a framework for learning dense rewards from heterogeneous videos. DiTaR learns a continuous task-relevant representation from multi-frame video latents through an asymmetric two-stage procedure that suppresses task-irrelevant visual variation. In the task-relevant latent space, we train a history- and language-conditioned rectified-flow model to capture task dynamics and derive a directional dense reward from temporal changes in dynamics compatibility. Together, these components enable reward modeling directly over task-relevant dynamics rather than generic visual variation. DiTaR substantially improves policy learning on MetaWorld-v3 and ManiSkill over video-based reward baselines, and transfers to real-world offline RL through reward relabeling. Reward analyses further show that DiTaR provides more reliable rewards.

</details>


### [258] [Axiom-0: Hierarchical Self-Refinement for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14155)
*Renbin Shu,Jing Yang,Xiangyuan You,Guolin Qiu,Yufei Xia,Yiming Zhu,Nanjun Dai,Fei Zhang,Feng Wu,Keke Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Axiom-0 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，通过分层自细化提升 VLA 表征质量与动作生成效果，在 RoboCasa365 上取得 62.32% 总体成功率，仅带来 1.7% 平均动作块生成延迟增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠机器人操作需要策略能泛化到新任务、布局和物体交互；现有 VLA 模型虽结合预训练多模态理解与学习控制，但在陌生任务组合上表现仍有限。

Method: Axiom-0 采用分层自细化：IVRR 在训练中通过一致性监督与学习到的特征投影增强选定的 VLM 中间特征；VAIR 在动作生成前递归更新条件表征；并对监督 VLM 层、VAIR 和动作生成器进行联合优化，连接两个组件。

Result: 在 RoboCasa365 上总体成功率为 62.32%，比已公布最高榜单分数 Paimon-0（58.1%）高 4.22 个百分点，比 Xiaomi-Robotics-1 参考高 4.92 个百分点；在该评测配置下，平均动作块生成延迟比 Xiaomi-Robotics-1 增加 1.7%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，Axiom-0 在仅带来适度推理开销的情况下提升了任务完成率，说明分层自细化可有效改善 VLA 策略在陌生任务组合上的表现。

Abstract: Reliable manipulation requires policies that generalize across tasks, layouts, and object interactions. Vision-language-action (VLA) models combine pretrained multimodal understanding with learned control, but their performance remains limited on unfamiliar task compositions. We present Axiom-0, a VLA policy that addresses both the quality of its representations and their use in action generation through hierarchical self-refinement. Intra-VLM Representation Refinement (IVRR) strengthens selected intermediate features through consistency supervision with learned feature projections during training. VLM-Action Interface Refinement (VAIR) recursively updates the resulting conditioning before action generation. Joint optimization of the supervised VLM layers, VAIR, and the action generator connects the two components. On RoboCasa365, Axiom-0 achieves 62.32% overall success in our evaluation, exceeding the highest published leaderboard score (Paimon-0, 58.1%) by 4.22 percentage points and the Xiaomi-Robotics-1 reference by 4.92 points. Under the evaluated profiling setup, mean action-chunk generation latency increases by 1.7% over Xiaomi-Robotics-1. These results demonstrate improved task completion with modest inference overhead.

</details>


### [259] [Refine Where It Matters: Contact-Adaptive Meshing for Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14142)
*Gabriel Johnson,Tom Olesch,Fei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向可变形物体操作的接触自适应网格细化框架，仅在接触相关区域局部提高四面体网格分辨率，并通过GPU并行冲突消解在仿真循环内同步更新拓扑与物理状态，从而在交互速度下提升接触仿真精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体操作需要精确建模高度局部化的接触，同时保持可用于交互的仿真速度。均匀细密四面体网格能解析接触但会引入大量冗余自由度；粗网格效率高却可能低估接触引起的变形。因此需要仅在最有用的位置引入空间分辨率。

Method: 利用可变形体求解器已有的几何、弹性与接触量对候选边进行评分；随后采用完全GPU并行的过程选择无冲突边子集，并直接在仿真循环内更新网格拓扑与物理状态。

Result: 在真实可变形物体操作序列上评估：自适应细化降低了仿真到真实的误差，提高了与细网格参考结果的一致性，并且比均匀细网格仿真显著更高效。

Conclusion: 局部细化能够在不承担全局细分离散化代价的情况下提升物理保真度，为交互式操作中的接触丰富仿真、sim-to-real建模以及在线物理数字孪生提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Deformable-object manipulation requires accurate modeling of highly localized contact while maintaining simulation speeds suitable for interactive use. Uniformly fine tetrahedral meshes can resolve these interactions, but they introduce unnecessary degrees of freedom throughout the object. Coarse meshes are more efficient, yet they may under-resolve contact-induced deformation. We present a contact-adaptive mesh refinement framework that introduces spatial resolution only where it is most useful. The method uses geometric, elastic, and contact quantities already available from the deformable-body solver to score candidate edges. A fully GPU-parallel procedure then selects a conflict-free subset and updates both mesh topology and physical state directly within the simulation loop. We evaluate the approach on real deformable-object manipulation sequences. Adaptive refinement reduces simulation-to-real error, improves agreement with a fine-mesh reference, and remains substantially more efficient than uniformly fine simulation. These results show that local refinement can improve physical fidelity without incurring the cost of a globally fine discretization. The framework provides a practical basis for contact-rich simulation in interactive manipulation, sim-to-real modeling, and online physical digital twins.

</details>


### [260] [Make π0.5 Learn to Call Astra for Reliable Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14117)
*Jianli Sun,Chengxiang Li,Yonglin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种选择性调用机制：为冻结的π0.5策略配备轻量CALL头，仅在必要时激活GPT-6 Astra，从而降低外部查询开销并保持快速执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPT-6 Astra具备强具身推理能力，但频繁查询外部模型会增加推理时间与token成本。

Method: 在冻结的π0.5策略上添加轻量CALL头，复用标准策略前向传播特征，在本地预测是否需要外部协助；仅在关键恢复事件中激活Astra，稳定后交还控制权给π0.5。

Result: 在40个LIBERO任务、200个episode上达到97.5%成功率，优于纯π0.5的95.5%；在Object、Goal和Spatial套件中，Astra仅执行5.49%的环境步，每个测试episode约消耗5,027 tokens。

Conclusion: 选择性干预保留了π0.5的快速常规执行，同时按需无缝集成Astra的推理能力；项目页面已提供。

Abstract: GPT-6 Astra provides robust embodied reasoning, but frequent external queries increase inference time and token costs. We equip a frozen $\pi_{0.5}$ policy with a lightweight CALL head that learns to predict when assistance is necessary. CALL reuses features from the standard policy forward pass and operates locally without consulting external models. Astra is activated solely for critical recovery events and returns control to $\pi_{0.5}$ upon stabilization. Evaluated across 40 LIBERO tasks (200 episodes), our system achieves a 97.5% success rate, outperforming the 95.5% baseline of pure $\pi_{0.5}$. In the Object, Goal, and Spatial suites, Astra executes only 5.49% of environment steps, consuming approximately 5,027 tokens per test episode. This selective intervention preserves the fast ordinary execution of $\pi_{0.5}$ while seamlessly integrating Astra's reasoning on demand. Our project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [261] [Embodied Zero-Shot Task Planning via Hardware-Aware LLM-VLM Integration for Grounded Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14106)
*Lonnie Tankersley,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Soroush Korivand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向仿真Franka Research 3机械臂的硬件感知LLM-VLM操作框架，将符号推理与度量执行分离，并在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中通过数字孪生试验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽能将自然语言指令转化为看似合理的操作计划，但机器人执行需要物理落地的决策，必须考虑机器人本体、工作空间几何、可达性、碰撞约束与抓取可行性。

Method: 采用云端LLM规划器接收结构化场景摘要，仅生成符号任务信息（操作物体、目标关系、接近偏好、避让物体）；本地Qwen3-VL 8B VLM验证器用于歧义物体与开放容器替代物的语义确认。两者均禁止输出机器人坐标、抓取位姿、禁入尺寸、夹爪命令、轨迹点与成功标签。这些决策改由确定性接地与执行模块完成，包括机器人模型、场景几何、MoveIt 2碰撞检测、逆运动学、抓取验证、释放验证与最终视觉评估；执行反馈仅以符号上下文返回用于重规划。

Result: 在仿真数字孪生试验中评估，任务涵盖拾取、遮挡源访问、禁入区推理、关系放置以及顺序操作；摘要未报告量化性能指标。

Conclusion: 该框架通过符号推理与度量执行的解耦，将LLM/VLM限制在符号层面，并由确定性模块保障物理可行性，为具身操作提供了一种可验证的集成范式。

Abstract: Large language models can translate natural-language instructions into plausible manipulation plans, but robotic execution requires physically grounded decisions that respect robot embodiment, workspace geometry, reachability, collision constraints, and grasp feasibility. This paper presents a hardware-aware large language model-vision-language model (LLM-VLM) manipulation framework for a simulated Franka Research 3 robot in NVIDIA Isaac Sim. The framework separates symbolic reasoning from metric execution: an API-hosted LLM planner receives a structured scene summary and generates only symbolic task information, including the object to manipulate, target relation, approach preference, and objects to avoid. A local Qwen3-VL 8B VLM verifier supports semantic confirmation of ambiguous objects and open-container surrogates. Both the LLM planner and the VLM verifier are prohibited from generating robot coordinates, grasp poses, keepout dimensions, gripper commands, trajectory waypoints, or success labels. Instead, these decisions are handled by deterministic grounding and execution modules that use the robot model, scene geometry, MoveIt 2 collision checking, inverse kinematics, grasp validation, release verification, and final visual assessment. Execution feedback is returned only as symbolic context for replanning. The framework is evaluated through simulated digital-twin trials involving pickup, occluded-source access, keepout reasoning, relation placement, and sequential manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [262] [When pose graphs are not enough: a failure analysis of pose-graph-based camera estimation for Gaussian splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14090)
*Senthil Palanisamy,Azwad Sabik,Alan Dolhasz,Mira Slavcheva*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文针对房地产外景高斯泼溅采集的固定无人机航线，尝试仅用位姿图优化（PGO）恢复相机位姿以替代BA；系统速度超COLMAP但精度始终未达BA，本文对此进行坦率复盘并提出改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该采集模式密集、顺序、带GPS和时间标签，且每帧与多帧形成回环，使仅优化位姿的PGO成为诱人方案：未知量远少于联合位姿与点的BA。核心问题是：仅位姿优化能否达到BA精度？

Method: 作者构建了专用于该采集结构而非通用SfM的系统，以PGO为核心测试上述假设；但系统采用手工设定权重、贪心合并和噪声GPS尺度，并受限于两个固有缺陷：SE(3)边无尺度自由度，独立尺度子图合并会漂移；两视图边无法表达单个3D点对多个观测帧的耦合约束。

Result: 系统在速度上优于COLMAP，但在质量上从未超越COLMAP。失败源于手工权重、贪心合并、噪声GPS以及SE(3)无尺度与两视图边建模不足等相互作用的因素。

Conclusion: 纯位姿PGO不足以达到BA精度。可行设计必须重新引入Sim(3)约束、绝对GPS先验、保留结构，以及不确定性感知加权。

Abstract: Exterior Gaussian-splat twins for real-estate listings are captured to a fixed recipe: a consumer drone orbits a single property in several overlapping passes at stepped altitudes, with occasional vertical ascents, so the imagery is dense, sequential, GPS- and time-tagged, and revisits each facade from many angles. This structure is tantalizing: it suggests camera poses could be recovered by a dense pose-graph optimization (PGO) whose only unknowns are the poses, far fewer than the joint pose-and-point problem bundle adjustment (BA) solves. PGO is mature in robotics, but almost always as post-processing: it redistributes the drift of an already-decent trajectory once loop closures tie it together. Splatting captures push that regime to its limit, every frame closing loops with dozens of others, raising a clean question: does pose-only optimization reach BA accuracy? We built a system specialized to this capture structure, not a general-purpose SfM tool, to test it. It beat COLMAP on speed but never on quality; this paper is the candid post-mortem. The shortfall arises from several interacting causes, hand-set weights, greedy merging and a noisy GPS ruler, together with two limits inherent to the formulation: SE(3) edges carry no scale degree of freedom, so independently scaled sub-maps drift when merged, and a two-view edge stops short of the coupling one 3D point imposes on every frame that sees it. A viable design must add back Sim(3) constraints, absolute GPS priors, retained structure, and uncertainty-aware weighting

</details>


### [263] [SenseWeather: Multimodal Sensing for Robust Robotic Perception under Extreme Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14082)
*Fucai Ke,Sukai Huang,Junyi Hao,Kal Backman,Naim Rastgoo,Gholamreza Haffari,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 SenseWeather 多模态基准与 Fusion-to-RGB 方法，将深度和红外等互补传感用于上游融合，恢复任务兼容的 RGB 观测，从而在不修改或重训练下游感知模型的情况下提升极端天气下的机器人感知鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人平台常配备深度、红外等互补传感器，但现代感知栈仍以 RGB 为中心，导致多模态机载传感与预训练下游模型之间存在不匹配。研究目标是：在严重雨、雪、尘、雾和黑暗等条件下，利用互补传感恢复可复用的 RGB 输入，同时保留现有下游感知能力。

Method: 构建 SenseWeather 受控多模态基准，包含配准的 RGB、深度和红外观测、干净参考以及有监督的高层感知任务。提出 Fusion-to-RGB，包含两种参数高效传感器融合实现：面向扩散 Transformer 的 token 空间融合，以及面向修复 UNet 的残差潜变量条件化。

Result: 在冻结的开放词汇定位和视觉语言模型上，RGB+D+IR 融合在所有配对下游比较中均优于仅 RGB 恢复器，十项检测器比较均取得 AP50 提升。感知增益并不总与常规图像保真度指标一致，且天气严重程度增加并未导致下游性能单调退化。

Conclusion: 互补传感可用于在极端条件下保持可复用的机器人感知，同时表明任务级感知鲁棒性是一个独立于视觉恢复质量的目标。

Abstract: Robotic perception must remain reliable when extreme environmental conditions severely degrade the primary RGB observation. Although robotic platforms commonly carry complementary sensors such as depth and infrared, much of the modern perception stack remains RGB-centric, creating a mismatch between multimodal onboard sensing and pretrained downstream models. We study whether complementary sensing can be used upstream to recover a task-compatible RGB observation that preserves existing perception capabilities without modifying or retraining the downstream stack. To this end, we introduce SenseWeather, a controlled multimodal benchmark for robotic perception under severe rain, snow, dust, fog, and darkness, with registered RGB, depth, and infrared observations, clean references, and grounded high-level perception tasks. We further develop Fusion-to-RGB, with two parameter-efficient sensor-fusion realizations based on token-space fusion for a diffusion transformer and residual latent conditioning for an inpainting UNet. Across frozen open-vocabulary grounding and vision-language models, RGB+D+IR fusion improves all paired downstream comparisons over the corresponding RGB-only restorers, including AP50 gains across all ten detector comparisons. Importantly, these perception gains do not consistently follow conventional image-fidelity metrics, and increasing weather severity does not induce monotonic downstream degradation. These results show that complementary sensing can preserve reusable robotic perception under extreme conditions, while highlighting task-level perception robustness as an objective distinct from visual restoration quality.

</details>


### [264] [Assessing the Marginal Utility of Exocentric Head Views in UMI-Style Large-FoV Wrist-Camera Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14075)
*Yuhui Duan,Shi Jin,Yuntian Wang,Jin Wang,Siao Liu,Wenchang Gao,Zhaxizhuoma,Yan Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视“多相机视角一定更好”的假设，在真实双臂桌面操作平台上比较仅用两个大视场腕部相机与额外加入头部视图的策略，发现额外视角并非稳定增益，增益取决于任务相关性与信息密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角感知常被视为机器人操作的稳妥设计：一个相机有用，增加相机应更好。作者希望在双臂桌面操作中验证该假设，明确额外视角在什么条件下才真正有价值。

Method: 在真实双臂平台上进行控制变量实验，比较两类策略：仅使用两个大视场（FoV）腕部相机，以及在此基础上额外接收头部视图。涵盖三个桌面任务，并进一步操纵腕部相机FoV、对头部视图进行ROI裁剪，以及考察示范数量与FoV的数据缩放关系。

Result: 加入头部视图并未稳定提升任务完成率或执行质量；当腕部相机已能观察到物体、夹爪和目标区域时，仅腕部策略是强基线。限制腕部相机FoV会提高头部视图的潜在价值，但性能提升最可靠的情况是新增视图提供集中且任务相关的信息：ROI裁剪比未裁剪图像带来更一致的头部视图增益，后者受背景和任务无关区域主导。数据缩放结果表明FoV与示范数量存在交互：宽观测提升覆盖，但可能需要更多数据才能稳定，并在示范充足时达到更高性能上限。

Conclusion: 相机设计不应被简化为输入视角数量的堆叠，而应被表述为任务相关性与信息密度问题；新增视角只有在提供集中且任务相关的信息时才更可能带来稳定收益。

Abstract: Multi-view perception is often treated as a safe design choice for robot manipulation: if one camera is useful, more cameras should be better. We revisit that assumption in tabletop bimanual manipulation by comparing policies trained with two large-field-of-view (FoV) wrist cameras against policies that additionally receive a head view, using controlled-variable experiments on a real bimanual platform. Across three tabletop tasks, adding a head view does not consistently improve task completion or execution quality: when the wrist cameras already observe the objects, grippers, and goal regions, wrist-only policies form a strong baseline. Restricting the wrist-camera FoV increases the potential value of a head view, but performance improves most reliably when the added view supplies concentrated, task-relevant information: region-of-interest (ROI) cropping yields more consistent head-view gains than the uncropped image, which is dominated by background and task-irrelevant regions. The data-scaling results further suggest an interaction between FoV and demonstration count: wide observations improve coverage but may require more data to stabilize, and they may reach a higher performance ceiling once enough demonstrations are available. These results suggest that camera design should be framed as a task-relevance and information-density problem rather than as a simple count of input views.

</details>


### [265] [From Hitter to Player: Separating Motor Execution and Strategy in Competitive Humanoid Table Tennis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14067)
*Fanrong Dong,Ziyi Sun,Ziqi Han,Junhan Sun,Lei Ye,Zetong Jing,Hao Zhao,Shaoxun Liu,Rongrong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO
