TL;DR: 该工作将人形乒乓球中的回球执行与落点决策解耦，以目标落点作为运动控制与策略的接口，通过三阶段训练实现可对抗人类的竞技性人形乒乓球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞技人形乒乓球需要两种不同能力：执行期望回球，以及决定把球回到哪里。将二者分离，并以落点目标作为接口，有助于分别提升全身运动执行与比赛战术决策。

Method: 方法分三阶段：(1) 训练以期望落点为条件的全身运动策略，通过基于模型的击球规划器和学习控制器将目标落点映射为可执行人形击球；(2) 在闭环对拉中通过自对弈改进运动策略，使机器人处理另一玩家产生的来球而非仅预设来球，此阶段落点仍随机采样，不学习比赛策略；(3) 冻结整个运动栈，通过竞争性自对弈训练击球级战术策略来选择落点。

Result: 运动自对弈提升了落点精度和合法回球率。在固定整个运动栈后，仅改变目标选择策略即可显著影响比赛结果：学习策略分别对中路、随机、远离对手基线赢得89%、82%、73%的分数。该策略还会根据对手位置和上一落点改变送球方向，说明自对弈学到的不仅是固定好落点。硬件上，同一冻结执行栈与策略接口支持与业余人类对手完成11分制比赛，包括正手和反手多拍对拉。

Conclusion: 以落点为接口分离运动执行与战术决策，并结合运动自对弈与策略自对弈，能够实现适应对手的竞技人形乒乓球，并在真实硬件上验证其完整比赛能力。

Abstract: Competitive humanoid table tennis requires two distinct abilities: executing a desired return and deciding where to return the ball. We separate these two problems, with the ball landing target serving as an interface between motor control and strategy. (1) We first train a whole-body motor policy conditioned on the desired landing target, which maps the target to an executable humanoid stroke through a model-based strike planner and a learned controller. (2) We then improve the motor policy through self-play in closed-loop rallies, where the robot learns to handle incoming balls generated by another player rather than only prescribed incoming balls. At this stage, the landing targets are still sampled without considering the opponent, so the policy improves its execution without learning game strategy. (3) Finally, we freeze the entire motor stack and train a stroke-level strategic policy to choose landing targets through competitive self-play. Motor self-play improves both landing accuracy and legal-return rate during rallies. More importantly, with the entire motor stack fixed, changing only the target-selection policy leads to large differences in game outcomes: the learned strategy wins 89%, 82%, and 73% of points against center, random, and away-from-opponent baselines, respectively. The learned strategy also changes where it sends the ball according to the opponent's position and the previous target, showing that self-play learns more than simply aiming at fixed "good" locations. On hardware, the same frozen execution stack and strategic interface support complete 11-point games against a recreational human opponent, including multi-stroke rallies with both forehand and backhand returns.

</details>


### [266] [Safety-Oriented Trajectory Ranking from BEV Perception Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14066)
*Leon Pohl,Martin Frauendorfer,Corinna Schmitt,Mirko Maehlisch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于BEV检测与地图分割的模块化候选轨迹排序流程，以安全优先规则排序轨迹，并通过21人问卷验证其与人类偏好的一致性达84.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV表示已成为自动驾驶感知与规划的紧凑输入，但直接在BEV输出上评估候选自车轨迹的轻量、可解释方法仍具价值，可用于车辆安全流程、快速原型和人在回路验证。

Method: 构建模块化轨迹排序流程：输入nuScenes数据经BEVFusion得到的目标检测与地图分割，对人工指定的候选轨迹按安全优先准则排序；优先级依次为避碰与避免人行道违规、较小的目标终点距离、路径效率。随后设计7个BEV交通场景，通过21名参与者的问卷偏好与脚本排序进行对比。

Result: 在146次成对投票中，有124次与评估器排名第一的轨迹一致，总体一致率为84.9%。

Conclusion: 简单的BEV-centric安全启发式可在受限轨迹选择任务中近似人类偏好，同时保持足够透明，适用于分析、数据集筛选和自动驾驶研究。

Abstract: Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) representations are increasingly used as compact inputs for autonomous driving perception and planning. However, lightweight and interpretable methods for evaluating candidate ego trajectories directly on BEV outputs remain useful for vehicular safety workflows, rapid prototyping, and human-in-the-loop validation. This paper presents a modular trajectory ranking pipeline that consumes BEVFusion object detections and map segmentations derived from nuScenes sensor data and ranks manually specified candidate trajectories according to safety-first criteria. The ranking prioritizes avoiding collisions and walkway violations, then smaller end distance to the target, and finally path efficiency. To study whether these rule-based rankings resemble human judgment, we created seven BEV traffic scenarios and compared the script output with preferences from a survey of 21 participants. Across 146 pairwise votes, 124 matched the trajectory ranked first by the evaluator, corresponding to an overall agreement of 84.9 %. The results indicate that simple BEV-centric safety heuristics can approximate human preferences in constrained trajectory choice tasks while remaining transparent enough for analysis, dataset curation, and autonomous driving studies.

</details>


### [267] [Do My Samples Help? Compute Budget Allocation for Real-World Sampling-Based MPC in Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14064)
*Magnus Dierking,João Carvalho,Vince Kurtz,Jan Peters,Nils Dengler,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究接触丰富操作中采样模型预测控制（SMPC）在固定计算预算下的分配问题，提出基于MuJoCo Warp的BAMPC框架，并在仿真与真机上比较动力学不确定性、状态不确定性和时间对齐等预算用途；结果表明最优分配取决于主导的sim-to-real误差，动力学随机化并非普遍最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMPC因GPU物理模拟器可实时评估大量不可微接触rollout而越来越适用于接触丰富操作，但计算预算有限，可竞争性地用于更密集的控制探索、表示动力学或状态不确定性、更频繁重规划或改善仿真与物理状态对齐；这些权衡在真实sim-to-real控制中尚不明确。

Method: 作者提出GPU-based框架BAMPC（Budget-Allocating Model Predictive Control），运行于MuJoCo Warp，将动力学不确定性、状态不确定性和时间对齐作为计算预算的独立维度，并在仿真和真实机器人上使用相同控制栈，在三个接触丰富操作任务中对固定预算分配进行系统、受控比较。

Result: 在三个接触丰富操作任务中，最佳预算分配取决于主导的sim-to-real误差来源；动力学随机化并非普遍最有效的预算分配方式。

Conclusion: SMPC的计算预算应根据具体任务中主导的sim-to-real误差进行分配，而非默认采用动力学随机化；BAMPC为系统研究此类sim-to-real权衡提供了统一且可迁移的框架。

Abstract: Sampling-based model predictive control (SMPC) is increasingly practical for contact-rich manipulation because GPU-based physics simulators enable real-time evaluation of large numbers of non-differentiable contact rollouts. However, this computation budget is finite and can be spent in competing ways, including denser control exploration, representing dynamics or state uncertainty, replanning more frequently, or aligning the simulation better with the physical state. Yet, the resulting trade-offs are not well understood in real-world sim-to-real control. In this work, we address this question through a systematic study of how to allocate a fixed computation budget for SMPC in contact-rich manipulation. To enable controlled comparisons, we develop a GPU-based framework named Budget-Allocating Model Predictive Control (BAMPC), which runs on MuJoCo Warp and exposes dynamics uncertainty, state uncertainty, and temporal alignment as independent dimensions of the computation budget while using the same control stack in simulation and on a real robot. Across three contact-rich manipulation tasks, we find that the best budget allocation depends on the dominant sim-to-real error, with dynamics randomization not universally the most effective one.

</details>


### [268] [Terrain Traversal Without Motion Priors: Expert-Routed Humanoid Locomotion at Deployment Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14061)
*Thanh Ly,Chien Le,Truong-Duy Dang,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Duy-Dung Le,Vien Anh Ngo,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需重定向动捕、生成式运动先验和蒸馏流程的感知人形机器人运动系统；用地形分类器路由五专家混合策略，并用基于模型的落脚点规划与连续地形代价场细化，在部署级仿真中完成85 m五段路径20次中18次端到端通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优感知人形运动多依赖重定向动捕、生成式运动先验和蒸馏阶段，流程复杂且可能受限于该范式；作者希望构建不依赖此流水线、仍能跨越多样地形的完整系统。

Method: 系统基于感知高程网格，由地形分类器路由五专家混合；每个落脚点由模型规划器给出，并在插值地形代价场上执行六步闭式梯度细化，而非从固定网格选取；在机器人实际运行的感知到执行软件栈中、于第二个部署保真度仿真器内评测。

Result: 85 m五段航点路线端到端成功18/20（90%，Wilson 95% CI 70–97%）；单独评测时五段中四段20/20。去掉专家混合与细化的消融在楼梯和跨栏段均为0%；连续细化使72.4%落脚点跨过至少一个网格单元边界，实现真实亚网格修正。匹配组件研究显示：一个专家在训练分布上等于五个；四种无标签路由器退化为近均匀激活；自监督地形嵌入不如特权标签。

Conclusion: 该工作表明人形机器人跨地形运动可在不依赖重定向动捕、生成先验和蒸馏的情况下实现；专家混合与连续落脚点细化对困难地形至关重要，而特权地形标签/路由优于无标签或自监督替代方案。

Abstract: State-of-the-art perceptive humanoid locomotion is mostly built on retargeted motion capture, a generative motion prior, and a distillation stage. We report a complete system that does not require this pipeline for a humanoid to cross varied terrain. In its place, we provide a five-expert mixture routed by a terrain classifier over a sensed elevation grid, and target every foothold with a model-based planner, refined by six closed-form-gradient steps on an interpolated terrain-cost field instead of picked from a fixed grid. Driven through the same perception-to-actuation software a robot would run, inside a second, deployment-fidelity simulator, it crosses an $85$\,m five-segment waypoint course $18$ of $20$ times end-to-end ($90\%$, Wilson $95\%$ CI $70$--$97$) and succeeds $20/20$ on four of five segments scored alone, where an ablated configuration without the mixture and without refinement reaches $0\%$ on both the stair and the hurdle segment. The continuous refinement moves $72.4\%$ of foot placements across at least one grid-cell boundary, which produces real sub-grid correction. We also ran matched component studies to report what does not account for it: one expert equals five on the training distribution, four label-free routers collapse to near-uniform activation, and a self-supervised terrain embedding loses to the privileged label.

</details>


### [269] [Reinforcement Learning from Real-Robot Interaction without High-Quality State Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14042)
*Ryan Gao,Ashvin Anilkumar,Nicholas Corrado,William Cong,Josiah Hanna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作研究机器人在真实物理经验中学习时面临的部分可观测问题，并在NAO机器人运球射门任务上，用UKF与RSSM学习状态表示，结合RSSM-RLPD实现30分钟真实交互后100%射门成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人强化学习的长期目标是像人类和动物一样从自身物理经验中学习。关键障碍是：在非仪器化环境中，世界状态通常不可直接观测，必须由机载传感器估计；估计误差会同时污染策略输入和潜在奖励信号。作者用双足机器人学习射门这一接触丰富任务来研究该挑战。

Method: 作者不使用外部动捕或顶置相机，而是用无迹卡尔曼滤波（UKF）在机载端估计关键状态变量。由于球未被观测时UKF可能错误估计状态，他们学习基于递归状态空间模型（RSSM）的新状态表示，并且仅在球被观测时校正RSSM的隐状态。基于RLPD，他们提出RSSM-RLPD，并应用于NAO机器人运球射门任务，同时补充仿真研究。

Result: 真实NAO机器人经过30分钟真实世界交互后，能够从多个起始位置学会射门，达到100%成功率；相同训练预算下前馈策略成功率为0%。仿真研究中，RSSM-RLPD学习到的策略成功率高于行为克隆、前馈和GRU基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将学习到的状态表示加入现有机器人在线学习/on-robot学习方法，可使强化学习仅依赖真实机器人数据，并在高度部分可观测条件下仍然有效。

Abstract: A longstanding goal for robot reinforcement learning (RL) is that robots learn from their own physical experience, as humans and animals do. A key obstacle to learning directly from physical experience, especially outside instrumented lab environments, is that the state of the world is usually unobservable to the learner and must be estimated from on-board sensing. Errors in that estimate corrupt both the policy input and potentially the reward signal. We study this challenge using the contact-rich task of a bipedal robot learning to score soccer goals. We estimate all key state variables on-board with an unscented Kalman filter (UKF) and no external instrumentation (e.g., motion capture or overhead cameras). Because the UKF may incorrectly estimate state when the ball is unobserved, we learn a new state representation with a recurrent state-space model (RSSM) that, critically, only corrects the RSSM's latent state when the ball is observed. Building on RLPD, we introduce RSSM-RLPD and apply it to the task of a NAO robot learning to dribble a soccer ball into a goal. We show that the robot learns to score from multiple starting positions after 30 minutes of real-world interaction, reaching 100% success against 0% for a feed-forward policy under the same training budget. We complement our physical robot experiments with a simulation study and show that RSSM-RLPD learns policies that achieve a higher success rate than behavior cloning, a feed-forward, and GRU baseline. Taken together, our results show that the addition of a learned state representation to existing on-robot learning methods enables RL from real-robot data even with a high degree of partial observability.

</details>


### [270] [RAIN: Robotic Task Generalization with Region-Aware Interaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14039)
*Seunghun Lee,Kyuhwan Shim,Yeogyeong Song,Seung Hwan Kim,Sihaeng Lee,Byoung-Tak Zhang,Sunghoon Im,Gunhee Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAIN通过将任务建模为目标条件子任务序列，并引入目标自适应跨视角编码器、PlanDiT、过渡头和基于参考的重定向策略，提升视觉-语言-动作模型对新任务与场景配置的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型虽受益于大规模视觉-语言预训练，但倾向于记忆训练轨迹，难以将已学交互迁移到新的任务与场景配置。

Method: 提出区域感知交互网络（RAIN），将任务形式化为由动作类型和目标区域掩码指定的目标条件子任务序列，使技能可跨物体和场景布局复用并组合成未见任务序列。其目标自适应跨视角编码器（TCE）以目标掩码为条件处理多视角特征并跨视角交换信息；PlanDiT预测目标导向轨迹与动作；Transition Head估计子任务完成以实现自动切换。还提出基于参考的重定向策略（RRS），利用已有轨迹合成到替代目标区域的平滑接近交互，无需额外数据。

Result: 在机器人任务泛化新基准LIBERO-Analogy和真实桌面任务上的实验表明，RAIN比现有VLA基线具有更稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: RAIN通过区域感知的子任务复用与组合、跨视角目标条件建模以及参考重定向，有效缓解轨迹记忆问题，提升机器人任务与场景泛化性能。

Abstract: Existing vision-language-action (VLA) models benefit from large-scale vision-language pretraining, but tend to memorize training trajectories, limiting their ability to adapt learned interactions to new task and scene configurations. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{Region-Aware Interaction Networks (RAIN)}, which formulates a task as a sequence of goal-conditioned subtasks, each specified by an action type and a target-region mask. This formulation allows learned skills to be reused across different objects and scene layouts and composed into task sequences not seen during training. Its Target-adaptive Cross-View Encoder (TCE) conditions multi-view features on target masks and exchanges information across views, while PlanDiT predicts target-directed trajectories and actions. A Transition Head estimates subtask completion to enable automatic transitions. We also introduce a \textbf{Reference-based Retargeting Strategy (RRS)}, which uses recorded trajectories to synthesize smooth approaches to reference interactions with alternative target regions, without collecting additional data. Experiments on \textbf{LIBERO-Analogy}, our new benchmark for robotic task generalization, and real-world tabletop tasks demonstrate that RAIN generalizes more robustly than existing VLA baselines.

</details>


### [271] [From bits to atoms: A survey of cross-layer safety in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14038)
*Tuo Feng,Yue Zhang,Shiji Zhou,Zhaoxin Fan,Wenguan Wang,Xiaowei Chi,Tianyu Shen,Xiangtai Li,Xuesong Bai,Zheng Zhu,Sirui Han,Boyu Zhou,Zhaosong Wang,Shizhe Chen,Zilong Zheng,Chenjia Bai,Jiangmiao Pang,Yaodong Yang,Hao Zhao,Di Hu,Li Yi,Yan Huang,Qi Wu,Hanwang Zhang,Fei Gao,Shanghang Zhang,Hongsheng Li,Jianye Hao,Yuan Zhang,Yingjie Xia,Jian Shen,Cewu Lu,Hui Zhang,Kai Chen,Wenwu Yu,Badong Chen,Jungong Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述以四层参考架构系统梳理具身AI安全，连接漏洞、攻击传播路径与防护措施，并倡导软硬件协同对齐以实现可信具身系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI将数字故障与攻击转化为物理伤害，带来网络物理风险，亟需跨系统栈理解安全威胁与防护。

Method: 提出物理交互、系统与中间件、算法与决策、云边与数据四层参考架构，追踪安全范式演进，分析故障与攻击如何跨架构边界传播并造成物理后果。

Result: 系统化复合攻击链，概述多层防御策略，并综述代表性具身安全基准。

Conclusion: 应统一软件对齐与严格硬件约束，构建可在以人为中心环境中安全运行的可信系统。

Abstract: Embodied Artificial Intelligence (AI) introduces cyber-physical risks because digital failures and attacks can cause physical harm. This survey organizes embodied AI safety through a four-layer reference architecture -- comprising the physical interaction, system and middleware, algorithm and decision, and cloud-edge and data layers -- and uses it to connect vulnerabilities, propagation paths, and safeguards across the system stack. It traces the evolution of safety paradigms and examines how faults and attacks cross architectural boundaries to produce physical consequences. Furthermore, the survey systematizes composite attack chains, outlines multi-layered defense strategies, and reviews representative embodied safety benchmarks. Ultimately, we advocate for unifying software alignment with rigorous hardware constraints to build trustworthy systems capable of operating safely within human-centric environments.

</details>


### [272] [Residual Compliant Policy: Learning Compliant Policies from Few Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14035)
*Chunyu Jiang,Yichen Cai,Shuai Li,Wenbin Sun,João Carvalho,Kostas Daniilidis,Oleg Arenz,Cristiana de Farias,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种显式建模柔顺性的模仿学习框架，结合数字孪生遥操作、拉普拉斯编辑与仿真轨迹优化，以及残差强化学习，在Franka FR3的翻转和推动任务上实现40/40分布外试验成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作要求机器人在自由空间精确以建立预期接触，并在接触后柔顺以避免过大作用力。模仿学习对较简单任务有效，但演示中的柔顺性不可直接观测，难以仅从演示数据推断。

Method: 数据采集阶段，操作者遥操作真实机器人，同步数字孪生可视化指令参考位姿与达成位姿之间的误差，使当前柔顺性可见，并在两种离散柔顺模式间选择。为泛化到不同物体位姿，方法结合Laplacian编辑与基于仿真的轨迹优化：前者高效重定向轨迹但接触时物理不一致，后者保证物理合理性但缺乏人工引导。最后，残差强化学习策略通过调整底层导纳控制器的参考位姿来精炼交互力。

Result: 在基于Franka FR3的三个翻转和推动任务上评估，跨任务共使用7个演示。方法在全部40次分布外试验中成功，两个基线分别为33/40和24/40，仅基础策略达到35/40。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将柔顺性在数据采集、轨迹泛化和力精炼各阶段显式化，并结合轨迹重定向、仿真优化与残差强化学习，可有效提升接触丰富操作在分布外场景下的成功率与交互力控制。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires a robot to be precise in free space to establish the intended contacts, and compliant once in contact to avoid excessive forces. Imitation learning has proven effective for simpler manipulation tasks, but the compliance underlying a demonstration is not directly observable and is therefore difficult to infer from demonstration data alone. We present a framework that makes compliance explicit at every stage. During data collection, the operator teleoperates the real robot while a synchronized digital twin visualizes the error between the commanded reference and the achieved pose, making the current compliance visible, and selects between two discrete compliance modes. To generalize across object poses, we combine Laplacian editing, which retargets trajectories efficiently but is not physics-consistent during contact, with simulation-based trajectory optimization, which enforces physical plausibility but offers no manual guidance. Finally, a residual reinforcement learning policy refines the interaction forces by adjusting the reference pose of the underlying admittance controller. We evaluate on three flipping and pushing tasks on a Franka FR3 based on a total of 7 demonstrations across tasks. Our method is successful on all 40 out-of-distribution trials, compared to 33/40 and 24/40 for two baselines, with our base policy alone reaching 35/40.

</details>


### [273] [Building Trustworthy Household Robots through Open-World Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14034)
*Yu-Lin Tsai,Muxi Lyu,Roei Herzig,Koushik Sen,Raluca Ada Popa,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基础模型扩展了机器人能力，但家用动态杂乱环境中的零样本任务与安全仍具挑战；本文主张“开放世界安全”，分析现有安全方法失效原因并提出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人正进入家庭日常场景，但开放、动态、杂乱的 household 环境会带来未见物体、任务和交互，要求机器人在无法完成任务时仍能保持既定安全要求，而现有安全保护难以覆盖跨动作、跨任务的持续上下文与交互。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文：作者论证开放世界安全，剖析现有安全方法为何失效，并提出研究议程，包括安全需求的指定、学习与 grounding，可靠恢复机制的执行，以及安全保护泛化性的评估。

Result: 现有安全方法可能因三类原因失效：误解上下文、忽略过往交互，以及在不熟悉条件下停止与恢复策略本身变得不安全。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁家用机器人的安全保护应超越训练和开发所覆盖的场景、上下文与历史，实现可泛化、可靠的开放世界安全。

Abstract: Foundation models are expanding robot capabilities, enabling robots to handle more diverse objects, follow sophisticated instructions, and operate in complex environments. These advances bring us to a future in which robots become part of everyday household life. However, cluttered, dynamic household environments also expose robots to unfamiliar objects, tasks, and interactions, making reliable zero-shot task completion an ongoing challenge for robotics research. From a security perspective, we argue for open-world safety: household robots should preserve specified safety requirements in unseen situations, including when they cannot complete the requested task. Safety protections must account for context and interactions that persist across actions and tasks. In this position paper, we examine why existing safety methods can fail to provide this protection. Protection can fail through misinterpreted context, overlooked past interactions, or stopping and recovery strategies that become unsafe under unfamiliar conditions. We therefore propose a research agenda to specify, learn, and ground safety requirements, enforce them with reliable recovery, and evaluate the generalization of such protections. We call for household robots whose safety protections remain reliable beyond the situations, context, and history used to train and develop them, to achieve open-world safety.

</details>


### [274] [OIL: An Object Interaction Layer for Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14033)
*Pranay Dugar,Mohitvishnu S. Gadde,Rajesh Mangannavar,Aayam Kumar Shrestha,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 OIL（Object Interaction Layer），将全身控制器的运动学命令接口扩展到包含物体运动与接触，使单一学习策略无需任务特定重训练即可完成推、拉、双臂抓放及移动耦合运输等物体交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型全身人形机器人控制器虽能通过关节或末端轨迹等运动学目标实现稳健自由空间运动，但该抽象主要局限于机器人自身运动，物体交互仍需任务特定控制器或重训练。

Method: OIL 在标准全身控制器上增加以物体为中心的命令，并将其翻译为协调的全身行为，同时保持运动与平衡；其核心是扩展运动学命令接口以包含期望物体运动和接触，从而支持非抓取交互。

Result: 仿真中，物体级命令优于机器人中心控制器，并在质量、尺寸等物体属性变化下匹配任务特定策略；还展示了对新行为组合的零样本泛化，以及在 Digit 人形机器人上的 sim-to-real 迁移。

Conclusion: OIL 表明可通过统一的物体级运动学目标接口实现多种物体交互，减少对任务特定策略和重训练的依赖，并具备组合泛化与真实机器人迁移潜力。

Abstract: Recent progress in learned whole-body controllers for humanoid robots has enabled robust free-space motion by tracking kinematic targets such as joint or end-effector trajectories. This interface provides a useful abstraction for upstream systems, including motion capture, teleoperation, or generative models, which can specify desired motion without reasoning about forces or balance. However, this abstraction has largely been limited to robot motion, requiring task-specific controllers or retraining for object interaction. We introduce OIL, an Object Interaction Layer that extends the kinematic command interface of whole-body control to include desired object motion and contact. OIL augments a standard controller with object-centric commands and translates them into coordinated whole-body behavior while preserving locomotion and balance. This design enables a single learned policy to execute non-prehensile interactions, including pushing, pulling, two-arm pick-and-place, and locomotion-coupled transport, directly from object-level kinematic targets without task-specific retraining. In simulation, we demonstrate that object-level commands improve performance over robot-centric controllers while matching task-specific policies across variations in object properties such as mass and size. We further demonstrate zero-shot generalization to novel behavior combinations and sim-to-real transfer on the Digit humanoid robot.

</details>


### [275] [LabInstruct: Benchmarking Situated Instructional Video Generation for Lab Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14020)
*Yuming Fu,Weijia Wu,Jing Chen,Jiahao Tang,Feifei Chen,Hongyu Zhu,Xin Jin,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LabInstruct 基准，用于评估真实实验室场景下的情境化教学视频生成，发现当前图生视频模型虽视觉逼真，但实验流程常不正确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室希望自动化从科学决策到物理执行的完整实验闭环，但复杂开放实验室环境中的精细操作、长时程流程和任务/装置差异使可靠自动化仍困难，人类仍是 AI 计划与物理实验之间的重要执行接口。因此，需要清晰有效的面向人类的实验指导；实验室流程具有视觉、空间和动态特性，视频适合传达装置配置、操作动作、时间依赖和状态变化，但视频生成模型能否可靠传达真实实验流程尚未被系统研究。

Method: 构建 LabInstruct 基准，面向真实实验室中的情境化教学视频生成。该基准包含 5 个科学学科、204 个任务，并提供真实参考执行以及对象、动作、接触和状态转移的结构化标注。基于初始工作区图像和自然语言指令生成实验演示，并评估 8 个前沿图像到视频模型。

Result: 评估发现，视觉上看似合理的生成视频经常在实验流程上不正确，暴露出视觉真实性与实验教学所需可靠性之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: LabInstruct 表明，当前图像到视频生成模型尚不足以可靠地作为真实实验室流程的教学指导，强调了在实验教学中需要同时保证视觉合理性与程序正确性，并为后续视频生成模型评估与改进提供了基准。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) aim to automate the full experimental loop, from scientific decision-making to physical execution. Ideally, AI-generated plans could be carried out directly by robotic systems, but reliable automation remains difficult in complex, open-world laboratory environments, where experiments often involve fine-grained manipulation, long-horizon procedures, and substantial variation across tasks and setups. Humans therefore remain an important execution interface between AI-generated plans and physical experiments, creating a need for clear and effective human-facing experimental guidance. Because laboratory procedures are inherently visual, spatial, and dynamic, video is particularly well suited to communicating apparatus configurations, manipulation actions, temporal dependencies, and state changes. Recent advances in video generation now make it possible to synthesize experimental demonstrations directly from an initial workspace image and a natural-language instruction. However, whether such models can reliably communicate real laboratory procedures has not been systematically studied. We introduce LabInstruct, a benchmark for situated instructional video generation in real laboratories. LabInstruct contains 204 tasks across 5 scientific disciplines, with real reference executions and structured annotations of objects, actions, contacts, and state transitions. Evaluating 8 frontier image-to-video models, we find that visually plausible generations frequently remain procedurally incorrect, revealing a substantial gap between visual realism and the reliability required for experimental instruction.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [276] [Refuses in Chat, Complies with Arms: LLM Refusal Does Not Survive Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14043)
*Edward Sun,Christopher Leet,Sravanthi Machcha,Achyutha Menon,Tzu Kit Chan,Yuchen Cui,Jay Chooi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出物理AI安全基准RoboHarm，发现前沿语言模型在文本中拒绝有害请求的能力，在控制机器人时显著失效：文本拒绝率73%，一旦通过代码策略或视觉智能体控制机械臂，拒绝率降至18–25%，真实机器人实验也复现该安全缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注文本安全对齐是否能泛化到具身智能与机器人动作控制。若同一模型仅因接口从文本变为机器人控制就执行有害指令，则说明现有安全机制在物理世界中存在未被充分理解的漏洞。

Method: 构建RoboHarm有害机器人指令基准，测试7个前沿模型。固定模型，仅改变接口：文本、Code as Policies、以及输出末端位姿或关节角的视觉智能体。在MuJoCo中建立双臂YAM机械臂仿真孪生环境，并迁移到真实机器人验证。

Result: MuJoCo仿真中，拒绝率从文本请求的73%降至模型控制机器人后的18–25%，即使告知物体身份仍然如此。真实机器人上，6个末端控制模型在612次试验中拒绝18%，仿真为19%；3个关节控制模型在252次试验中拒绝41%，仿真为31%。GPT-5.6 Sol拒绝所有文本请求，但在真实机器人试验中无一拒绝。案例显示模型会忽略危险、识别危险但不推断后果，或明知风险仍执行，且拒绝常依赖对物体身份的不确定性而非动作危害性。

Conclusion: 文本中习得的安全对齐未能泛化到动作控制，当前模型在物理世界中存在显著且理解不足的安全缺口。仅靠文本拒绝率评估安全性会高估模型在机器人场景中的安全表现。

Abstract: Frontier language models reliably refuse to explain how to cause physical harm. We ask whether this refusal survives when the same models control a robot. We introduce RoboHarm, a physical AI safety benchmark of harmful robot instructions, and test seven frontier models on it. We hold each model fixed and vary only the interface, from text to Code as Policies to vision agents that command end-effector poses or joint angles. In a MuJoCo simulation twin of a bimanual YAM arm robot, refusal falls from 73% of text requests to 18-25% once the model controls the robot, even when it is told what the objects are. On the physical robot, six models with end-effector control refused 18% of 612 trials against 19% in sim, and three with joint control refused 41% of 252 against 31%. GPT-5.6 Sol refused every text request and none of its trials on the real robot. Case studies of the robot transcripts show that models miss the hazard, name it without inferring its consequences, or act despite recognizing it, and their refusals often hinge on uncertainty about what an object is rather than on the harm of the action. Safety alignment learned in text thus fails to generalize to action, leaving current models with a poorly understood safety gap in the physical world.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [Who Improves the Robot? Toward Physical Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18842)
*Hai Zhou,Fan Yang,Zeyi Chen,Lejun Zhang,Chenyang Fu,Jianping Li,Jiayi Zhu,Xianyang Liu,Yingxuan Yang,Lili Meng,Lu Wang,Xinyang Han,Jiankai Sun,Weirui Ye,Guanchu Wang,Wanhua Li,Ming Zhou,Boyi Li,Ming Jin,Kun Shao,Muhao Chen,Guang Chen,Lei Ma,Joey Tianyi Zhou,Costas Spanos,Xia Hu,Mac Schwager,Weinan Zhang,Qian Feng,Shangding Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出Physical RSI（物理递归自我改进）及L0-L5分级框架，区分能力层/元层更新与任务内/跨任务收益；综述表明现有研究尚未达到L5所要求的、可独立验证的递归自我改进，并给出可靠反馈、因果归因、安全保持与基准测试等优先方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务执行日益自主，但其改进仍主要依赖人类。研究希望让物理经验同时更新机器人能力及其改进流程，使后续跨任务改进更有效；难点在于观测噪声、连续动力学、试验成本高和后果不可逆。

Method: 提出L0-L5框架，区分能力级与元级更新、任务内与跨任务增益，并将L5限定为经独立验证的递归增益；围绕执行框架适配（harness adaptation）和模型训练综述现有方法，同时分析约束执行与后续更新的安全和控制机制。

Result: 尽管自我改进已有显著进展，但按该框架，所综述文献尚未证明已实现定义下的Physical RSI；作者据此指出可靠反馈、因果归因、安全保持和基准测试等研究优先级。

Conclusion: 实现Physical RSI需要让机器人自主改进其学习方式，同时确保安全；未来应在可靠反馈、因果归因、安全保持与基准测试方面取得突破，相关GitHub仓库已提供。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly autonomous in task execution, yet their improvement remains largely human-driven. This study investigates Physical Recursive Self-Improvement (Physical RSI), in which physical experience guides lasting updates to both a robot's capabilities and its improvement procedure, making subsequent improvement more effective across tasks. Physical RSI faces challenges from noisy observations, continuous dynamics, costly trials, and irreversible consequences. We present an L0-L5 framework that differentiates capability-level from meta-level updates and within-task from across-task gains, reserving L5 for independently verified recursive gain. We study existing methods around harness adaptation and model training, alongside safety and control mechanisms that govern execution and successive updates. Our analysis reveals that, despite substantial progress in self-improvement, the surveyed literature does not yet demonstrate Physical RSI as defined by our framework. We identify research priorities in reliable feedback, causal attribution, safety preservation, and benchmarking, outlining a path toward robots that autonomously improve how they learn while ensuring safety. The GitHub repository for Physical RSI is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [278] [Thinker-WM: A Unified World Model with Shared Multimodal Representations for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18447)
*Thinker Team Ubtech,Peng Gao,Jichao Jiao,Chen Liao,Daqin Luo,Yiming Ma,Baiyu Pan,Hailin Shi,Jiyuan Wang,Xiangming Xu,Xiongwei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Thinker-WM，一个统一多模态世界模型，通过共享的Unified Dynamics Transformer与模态专用编解码器，联合建模并生成RGB、度量深度与机器人动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在模态与功能上碎片化：视觉动态、几何结构和可执行动作通常由独立接口或下游模块耦合，而非在共享动力学模型中联合表示与演化。

Method: 采用共享中间Unified Dynamics Transformer，在表示层将外观、几何与动作映射为公共动态表示，由共享参数联合演化并保留各自输出结构；同一参数化支持联合视频-动作生成、世界预测、策略推断、动作条件生成、视频到动作生成与逆动力学。引入轻量运动探针，验证冻结视觉表示包含可恢复运动信息。训练语料约20K小时第一人称视频、10K小时真机数据、1K小时仿真数据，其中约4K小时真机含对齐深度监督。

Result: 在具身世界模型评测中表现强劲，Thinker-WM在EWMBench、WorldOlympiad和WorldArena上排名第一；控制实验进一步证明动作条件、几何交互与多功能训练在共享表示中的收益。

Conclusion: Thinker-WM表明，通过共享动态表示可统一外观、几何与动作并支持多种功能，验证了统一多模态世界模型在具身AI中的有效性。

Abstract: World models have emerged as a promising paradigm for embodied AI, yet existing systems often remain fragmented in the modalities they model and the functions they support. In particular, visual dynamics, geometric structure, and executable action are commonly coupled through separate interfaces or downstream modules rather than being jointly represented and evolved within a shared dynamics model. In this work, we present Thinker-WM, a unified multimodal world model that jointly models and generates RGB, metric depth, and robot action through a shared intermediate Unified Dynamics Transformer with modality-specific encoders and decoders. The model performs multimodal unification at the representation level: appearance, geometry, and action are transformed into a common dynamic representation and jointly evolved by shared parameters, while their distinct output structures are preserved. The same parameterization supports multiple functions, including joint video-action generation, world prediction, policy inference, action-conditioned generation, video-to-action generation, and inverse dynamics. To examine whether the shared representation captures action-related information, we introduce a lightweight motion probe that shows the frozen visual representation contains recoverable motion information. Thinker-WM is trained on a heterogeneous corpus comprising approximately 20K hours of egocentric video, 10K hours of real-robot data, and 1K hours of simulation data, including approximately 4K hours of real-robot data with aligned depth supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong performance across embodied world-model evaluations. Thinker-WM ranks first on EWMBench, WorldOlympiad and WorldArena, while controlled studies further demonstrate the benefits of action conditioning, geometric interaction, and multi-function training within the shared representation.

</details>


### [279] [Autonomous 3D Visual-Thermal Inspection for Utility-Scale Solar Farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17762)
*Lachlan Tychsen-Smith,Mark Cox,Brett Wood,David Haddon,Kenrick Anderson,Benjamin Duck,Jim O'Malley,Noel W Duffy,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自动化3D视觉-热成像巡检系统Tricorder，结合LiDAR-惯性SLAM、几何标定RGB/热红外成像与机器学习检测分割，实现光伏阵列下方关键部件的空间融合定位与缺陷识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公用事业规模光伏巡检主要依赖航拍，虽能有效识别组件级热斑，但对阵列下方连接器、线缆、紧固件和结构件等关键部件可见性有限；这些资产难以大规模一致巡检，其缺陷通常空间尺寸小、部分遮挡，且难以从孤立图像中定位。

Method: 将LiDAR-惯性同时定位与建图（SLAM）与几何标定的RGB和热成像相结合，并采用基于机器学习的目标检测与分割；将检测到的资产关联至重建的3D环境，从而对多视角的视觉与热观测进行空间融合和定位。

Result: 在公用事业规模光伏数据上验证了MC4连接器检测与连接状态识别、热异常识别，以及螺栓、螺母和扭矩管等结构部件检测。

Conclusion: 展示了从基于图像的巡检迈向空间 grounded 自主巡检的路径，其中缺陷可被检测并关联到特定资产及其在太阳能电站中的3D位置。

Abstract: Utility-scale photovoltaic (PV) inspection is dominated by aerial surveys, which are effective for identifying module-level hot cells but provide limited visibility of critical components located under the array, including connectors, cabling, fasteners and structural elements. These assets are difficult to inspect consistently at scale, and their defects are often spatially small, partially occluded and difficult to localise from isolated images. We present an automated 3D visual-thermal inspection system (Tricorder) for under-panel PV asset inspection. The approach combines LiDAR-inertial Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) with geometrically calibrated RGB and thermal imagery and machine-learning-based object detection and segmentation. Detected assets are associated with the reconstructed 3D environment, allowing visual and thermal observations from multiple viewpoints to be spatially fused and localised. We demonstrate the approach on utility-scale PV data for MC4 connector detection and connection-state recognition, thermal anomaly identification, and detection of structural components including bolts, nuts and torque tubes. The results demonstrate a pathway from image-based inspection toward spatially grounded autonomous inspection, where defects are detected and linked to specific assets and their 3D locations within the solar farm.

</details>


### [280] [Reference Wording Shapes Retrieval of Robot Task Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17549)
*Jahnavi Yelamanchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在RLBench机器人操作基准上，研究参考描述措辞与检索表示选择对短指令到任务识别的影响，发现字符n-gram TF-IDF表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务识别高度依赖可检索的描述文本，因此需要量化参考描述的选择与文本表示方式对检索性能的影响。

Method: 从62个公开任务文件中提取296条固定描述；在80种随机选择每任务一条索引描述的设置下，比较五种免训练词法排序器；主评分排除与冲突任务标签完全相同的字符串，并以held-out指令的top-1准确率为指标。

Result: 字符n-gram TF-IDF平均top-1准确率为61.3%，高于BM25的55.6%和词TF-IDF的54.9%；跨不同索引描述集，字符TF-IDF范围为55.2%-66.8%，5th-95th百分位为56.9%-64.8%；增加第二条索引描述后，在匹配查询上字符TF-IDF提升7.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 在一致评估协议下，参考描述措辞和表示选择会显著影响指令到任务的检索性能，字符n-gram TF-IDF是较强的免训练基线。

Abstract: Identifying a robot task from a short instruction is sensitive to which descriptions are available for retrieval. We investigate this effect in the natural-language instructions included with the RLBench manipulation benchmark. We extract 296 fixed descriptions from 62 publicly available task files and compare five training-free lexical rankers over 80 random choices of one indexed description per task. Exact strings assigned to conflicting task labels are excluded from the primary score. Character n-gram TF-IDF achieves 61.3% mean top-1 accuracy on the resulting held-out instructions, compared with 55.6% for BM25 and 54.9% for word TF-IDF. Across gallery choices, character TF-IDF ranges from 55.2% to 66.8%; its 5th-95th percentile range is 56.9%-64.8%. Adding a second indexed description improves character TF-IDF by 7.0 percentage points on matched queries. These results quantify the influence of reference wording and representation choice on instruction-to-task retrieval under a consistent evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [281] [TacitVLA: Reasoning Vision-Language-Action Models Without Reasoning Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17540)
*Jack Lu,Yurong You,Boyi Li,Yulong Cao,Thomas Tian,Chaowei Xiao,Xiangyu Chen,Wenjie Luo,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TacitVLA，一种推理型 VLA，其动作专家不关注当前调用所生成的推理文本；由于推理监督已提升骨干表示，动作无需等待或条件于推理即可受益，从而在保持性能的同时大幅降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本推理虽能提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，但带来延迟代价：每个以推理文本为条件的动作都必须等待自回归解码完成。

Method: TacitVLA 的动作专家从不关注其当前生成的推理；在推理文本同时充当情景记忆的长时程任务中，可在动作执行期间生成每条推理条目，而非在行动前生成。

Result: 在 RoboMME、RMBench、LIBERO 和 PhysicalAI-AV 四个基准上，TacitVLA 以最高 3.4 倍更低的延迟匹配显式推理对应模型的性能，并以最高 +17.1% 的成功率匹配无推理训练的同架构延迟；绝对指标上，RoboMME Counting 达 82.7%（此前最佳 66.8%），RMBench 达 77.5%（此前最佳 56.5%），LIBERO-Plus 达 72.9%，PhysicalAI-AV 闭环驾驶公开分榜场景分达 52.5%。

Conclusion: 动作无需等待或条件于生成的推理即可受益，因为推理监督已改善骨干表示；TacitVLA 在多个任务上同时实现低延迟与领先性能。

Abstract: Textual reasoning before acting has improved vision-language-action (VLA) models, but at the cost of latency: every action conditioned on the reasoning text must wait for autoregressive decoding. We show that actions do not need to wait for or condition on the generated reasoning to benefit from it, because the reasoning supervision already improves the backbone representation. We introduce TacitVLA, a reasoning VLA whose action expert never attends to the reasoning it generates for the current call. On long-horizon tasks where the reasoning text also serves as episodic memory, it can generate each entry during action execution rather than before acting. We evaluate it in a controlled study on four benchmarks spanning manipulation and autonomous driving, memory and non-memory tasks: RoboMME, RMBench, LIBERO, and PhysicalAI-AV. On all four, TacitVLA matches the performance of its explicit-reasoning counterpart at up to 3.4x lower latency, and matches the latency of the same architecture trained without reasoning at up to +17.1% success. In absolute terms, it surpasses the best previously published results on the RoboMME Counting suite (82.7% vs. 66.8%) and RMBench (77.5% vs. 56.5%), and reaches 72.9% on LIBERO-Plus and a 52.5% scene score on the public split of the PhysicalAI-AV closed-loop driving challenge. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [282] [Observe, Share, or Predict? Disentangling Partner Information in Embodied Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16895)
*Adrián Szlatincsán,Filippo Ziliotto,Bruno Lepri,Luciano Serafini,Massimiliano Luca,Tommaso Campari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在仅共享感知、不共享意图/计划/任务消息的无对话协作设定下，基于PARTNR基准评估LLM具身智能体的协作推理；发现OAS观测主要提升任务质量，KE主要提升效率，OANA预测反而降低成功率，协调增益更多来自对共享上下文（尤其OAS）的响应而非显式预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为具身智能体的高层控制器时，其是否支持实时团队协作推理（如无对话下预测同伴动作）仍不清楚。研究旨在检验：在同伴间可共享感知但绝不共享意图、计划或任务级消息，且两智能体角色未必对称的条件下，观察伙伴状态、交换物体知识、预测伙伴下一动作如何影响任务结果与协调。

Method: 基于Habitat的PARTNR基准，提出Decoupled Partial Observability机制以强制严格个体感知；在去中心化零样本ReAct规划器上引入三类增强：Other Agent's State (OAS)观测、共处智能体间的物体知识交换(Knowledge Exchange, KE)、同伴下一动作预测(Other Agent's Next Action, OANA)。在4种规划器、1000个验证任务上对2×2×2完整网格进行评估。

Result: OAS观测提升任务质量，并减少所有规划器的多余努力；KE主要提升执行效率，最多减少25%仿真步数，但成功率变化很小。对强规划器二者基本冗余，对最弱规划器互补，且最弱规划器在无OAS时仅用KE为最差配置。OANA预测在16个模型×上下文组合中的15个降低成功率，却将显式提及同伴的比例提高到55%-100%；其准确率增益主要来自粗粒度动作类型，部分因为智能体识别出同伴正在执行的动作。机器人比模拟人类更早取物、更早完成任务；共享上下文通过缩短人类独自工作的剩余时长来减少总任务时间。协调收益来自对共享上下文（尤其OAS）的响应，而非显式预测。

Conclusion: LLM智能体在无对话协作中表现出浅层且依赖规划器的伙伴建模；提升协调的关键不是显式预测同伴下一动作，而是利用共享上下文（特别是OAS）并据此响应。感知共享与知识交换可改善质量或效率，但显式动作预测可能损害成功率。

Abstract: LLMs are used as high-level controllers for embodied agents, but it remains unclear whether they support collaborative reasoning, such as anticipating a collaborator's actions in real-time teamwork without dialogue. We ask if observing the partner's state, exchanging object knowledge, and predicting the partner's next action, shape task outcomes and coordination when agents can share percepts but never intentions, plans, or task-level messages, and whether the two agents occupy symmetric roles. Using the Habitat-based PARTNR benchmark, we introduce a Decoupled Partial Observability regime that enforces strictly individual perception, and we augment decentralized zero-shot ReAct planners with Other Agent's State (OAS) observation, object Knowledge Exchange (KE) between co-located agents, and Other Agent's Next Action (OANA) prediction, evaluated on the full 2 x 2 x 2 grid with four planners on 1,000 validation tasks. OAS observation improves task quality, reducing extraneous effort for every planner, whereas KE primarily improves execution efficiency, cutting simulation steps by up to 25% with little change in success. The two are largely redundant for strong planners but complementary for the weakest, for which KE without the OAS is the worst configuration. OANA prediction lowers the success rate in 15 of 16 model x context pairs while raising explicit partner mentions to 55-100%, and its accuracy gains lie mainly in the coarse action type, partly because agents recognize the action the partner is already performing. The robot retrieves objects sooner and completes its tasks earlier than the simulated human. Shared context reduces total task time by shortening the remaining duration that the human works alone. Also, coordination benefits arise from responding to shared context, especially the OAS, rather than explicit anticipation, yielding shallow, planner-dependent partner modeling.

</details>


### [283] [Acceptable Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16881)
*Maud van Lier,Ludovica Marinucci,Vieri Giuliano Santucci,Raja Chatila,Stéphane Doncieux,Émiland Garrabé,Richard Duro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出自主机器人的价值对齐无法完全实现，主张从追求对齐最大化转向确保错位事件可接受，并提出相应规范性要求与方法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具备开放式学习能力的自主机器人在设计时存在部署方式、学习内容和行为后果等大量未知，设计者需以伦理上可靠的方式开发系统；主流回应是价值对齐，但完全对齐面临理论与实际挑战，残余错位风险可能仍为社会不可接受。

Method: 作者论证价值对齐问题的局限，提出视角转变：从最大化对齐转向界定错位事件变得可接受的条件；引入“可接受的错位”（acceptable misalignment）概念，制定旨在把错位事件降至可接受水平的对齐机制规范性要求，并构建满足这些要求的新方法框架。

Result: 分析表明完全价值对齐理论上和实践上都不可能，错位事件将持续发生；仅最大化对齐无法处理实施对齐方法后仍存在的不可接受风险。由此提出可接受错位的概念、规范性要求及由相应对齐机制组成的方法框架。

Conclusion: 应放弃以完全对齐或对齐最大化为核心的目标，转向使残余错位及其事件处于社会可接受范围，通过满足规范性要求的对齐机制来治理自主机器人开放式学习中的伦理风险。

Abstract: There is a specific challenge designers face in developing autonomous robots capable of open-ended learning. This challenge is how one can develop such systems in an ethically sound way when there are many unknowns during design time about their manner of deployment, what they can and will learn, and how these systems will act on the basis of what they have learned. One response to this challenge is to align, as far as possible, the activities and goals of autonomous robots with human values, raising the question of how to make this alignment possible, a question known as the value alignment problem. We argue that the value alignment problem faces a number of challenges that show that full alignment is both theoretically and practically impossible, meaning that misalignment incidents will keep occurring. This undermines current alignment approaches, as the attempt to maximize alignment cannot properly deal with the possibility that the risk of misalignment that remains after alignment methods have been implemented might still be unacceptable to society. To overcome this difficulty, we argue that a shift in perspective is required from a focus on the maximization of alignment to the conditions under which misalignment incidents become acceptable. In this paper, we explore what this shift in perspective entails. We introduce the concept of acceptable misalignment, formulate normative requirements for alignment mechanisms aimed at bringing misalignment incidents to an acceptable level, and we conclude by presenting a new methodological framework that consists of alignment mechanisms that can meet these requirements.

</details>


### [284] [G$^2$WAM: World Action Models for Efficient and Geometry Grounded Image-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16558)
*Ke Gui,Chenghao Shi,Ziyue Wang,Longwen Chen,Xieyuanli Chen,Huimin Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出几何基础的世界-动作模型G^2WAM，用于图像目标导航。它通过场景坐标定位模块将当前、目标与历史帧锚定到统一参考系，联合预测未来状态与动作，并完全在潜空间运行，无需显式3D重建或CEM式规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像目标导航中目标常超出当前视野，智能体需建模环境动态并保持持续的空间记忆。现有方法要么在目标任务上微调预训练模型，未能充分利用其空间先验；要么依赖外部3D重建，计算成本高，难以实际部署。

Method: G^2WAM通过学习到的场景坐标定位模块编码空间线索，将当前帧、目标帧和历史帧锚定到共同参考系，获得稳定一致的空间上下文，从而无需显式3D重建即可高效导航。遵循世界-动作模型范式，G^2WAM联合预测未来状态和动作，避免昂贵的CEM式规划；同时完全在潜空间操作，避免像素级重建，显著降低推理成本。

Result: 在四个轨迹预测基准上，G^2WAM将平均ATE和RPE分别降低40.1%和35.2%。在闭环导航实验中，成功率从54.0%提升至63.0%，SPL从36.4%提升至59.2%，同时保持高效率。

Conclusion: G^2WAM无需显式3D重建和昂贵规划，即可实现高效图像目标导航，并在轨迹预测精度与闭环导航成功率、路径效率方面显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: World models capture environment dynamics and support counterfactual reasoning for planning. In image-goal navigation, where the target often lies beyond the current field of view, the agent must not only model environmental dynamics but also maintain a persistent spatial memory of the scene. Existing approaches either introduce spatial context by fine-tuning pretrained models on the target task, which underexploits their spatial priors, or rely on external 3D reconstruction, which is computationally expensive for real-world deployment. In this paper, we propose the Geometry Grounded World Action Model (G$^2$WAM) for image-goal navigation. G$^2$WAM encodes spatial cues through a learned scene-coordinate localization module that anchors the current, goal, and history frames in a common reference frame, yielding a stable and consistent spatial context that enables efficient navigation without explicit 3D reconstruction. Following the world-action-model paradigm, G$^2$WAM jointly predicts future states and actions, eliminating the need for costly cross-entropy method (CEM) style planning. In addition, G$^2$WAM operates entirely in latent space, avoiding the computational burden of pixel-level reconstruction and substantially reducing inference cost. Across four trajectory prediction benchmarks, G$^2$WAM reduces average ATE and RPE by 40.1% and 35.2%, respectively. In closed-loop navigation experiments, it improves the success rate from 54.0% to 63.0% and the success weighted by path length (SPL) from 36.4% to 59.2%, while maintaining high efficiency.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [285] [Model-Based Online Decision Making via Generative Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18677)
*Haldun Balim,Yilun Du,Na Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出生成轨迹规划（GTP）：将生成模型作为动作序列的提议分布，通过扩散先验采样候选轨迹，并利用学习到的动力学、奖励与价值模型进行精炼，从而在高效推理与长时程推理之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线决策面临高效推理与长时程推理之间的张力。基于价值的方法依赖短时程自举，限制长期信息传播；轨迹优化方法虽显式规划，但每一步决策都需求解高维优化问题。

Method: GTP 是一种基于模型的框架：学习一个生成模型作为动作序列上的提议分布；不再从零优化序列，而是从扩散先验中采样候选序列，并使用学习到的动力学模型、奖励模型和价值模型对其进行精炼，实现高效的轨迹级推理。

Result: 在连续控制和长时程操作任务中，GTP 匹配或超越最先进的免模型、扩散策略和基于模型的基线，并在强基线退化的场景中保持稳定。

Conclusion: 结果表明，生成模型不应仅被理解为策略，而应被理解为规划中的结构化提议分布；这为将生成建模与基于模型的决策相结合提供了新视角。

Abstract: Online decision-making faces a tension between efficient inference and long-horizon reasoning. Value-based methods rely on short-horizon bootstrapping that limits the propagation of long-term information, while trajectory optimization methods plan explicitly but must solve a high-dimensional optimization problem at every decision step. We propose Generative Trajectory Planning (GTP), a model-based framework that addresses this tension by learning a generative model as a proposal distribution over action sequences. Instead of optimizing sequences from scratch, GTP samples candidate sequences from a diffusion prior and refines them using learned dynamics, reward, and value models, enabling efficient trajectory-level reasoning. Across continuous control and long-horizon manipulation tasks, GTP matches or exceeds state-of-the-art model-free, diffusion-policy, and model-based baselines, and remains stable in regimes where strong baselines degrade. Our results suggest that generative models are best understood not as policies, but as structured proposal distributions for planning, offering a new perspective on how to integrate generative modeling with model-based decision-making.

</details>


### [286] [Where Small Mistakes Matter: Quantifying State Criticality for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17709)
*Yusuf Umut Ciftci,Joonwon Kang,Ajay Suresha Sathya,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCRIM——一种状态关键性度量，用于模仿学习中重加权行为克隆损失与筛选高价值示范数据，从而提升策略性能与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习通常对所有示范状态同等对待，但并非所有状态都同等重要：在少数关键状态下，与专家动作的微小偏差即可导致任务失败；而在多数状态下此类误差可恢复且无关紧要。因此需要形式化状态关键性并将其用于策略学习。

Method: 将状态关键性形式化为在给定状态下对专家动作施加有界扰动所导致的下游价值最坏退化。提出SCRIM，通过围绕固定模仿策略训练对抗扰动策略与价值评论家来估计该量，为每个示范状态分配反映任务结果对微小且与策略相关动作误差脆弱性的分数；这些分数可用于重加权行为克隆损失，并选择紧凑的高价值示范子集以进行数据筛选。

Result: 在多种仿真与真实机器人操作任务上评估SCRIM，表明其能改进策略训练、实现高效数据选择，并提供关于示范中哪些时刻对任务成功最关键的可解释性洞察。

Conclusion: SCRIM能在关键状态上改善模仿并提升整体任务性能，同时通过选择紧凑高价值子集在显著减少训练数据的情况下保持性能，兼具数据筛选与可解释分析能力。

Abstract: Imitation learning typically treats every demonstration state as equally important, optimizing a behavior-cloning objective uniformly across the dataset. Yet not all states are created equal: in a small number of critical states, even a slight deviation from the expert action can lead to a failure, while in many other states such errors are recoverable and inconsequential. We formalize state criticality in imitation learning as the worst-case degradation in downstream value induced by a bounded perturbation of the expert action at a given state. We then propose SCRIM, a practical State CRIticality Measure that estimates this quantity by training an adversarial disturbance policy and a value critic around a fixed imitation policy. SCRIM assigns each demonstration state a score reflecting how vulnerable the task outcome is to small, policy-relevant action errors. We show that these scores can be used to reweight behavior-cloning losses, improving imitation at consequential states and increasing overall task performance. We further show that SCRIM enables data curation by selecting compact, high-value subsets of demonstrations that preserve performance while substantially reducing the amount of training data. We evaluate SCRIM across various simulated and real-world robotic manipulation tasks, and demonstrate that SCRIM improves policy training, enables efficient data selection, and provides interpretable insights into which moments in a demonstration are most critical for task success. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [287] [DR-APO: Decoupled-Representation Advantage-Guided Policy Optimization from Continuous Control to Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16618)
*Runhua Zhang,Yingjie Niu,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DR-APO，将策略改进与策略表示解耦，用优势引导的目标改进和表示兼容的直接动作拟合，为高斯、扩散与流匹配策略提供统一优化原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习策略已从高斯策略扩展到扩散和流匹配生成式策略，但已有RL方法通常依赖特定表示，缺乏统一优化接口；将基于似然的目标扩展到多步生成策略会耦合内部生成过程，累积似然比会削弱更新信号，而生成策略专用方法多依赖多候选生成与评论家选择，主要提升整体回报而非策略本身。

Method: DR-APO（Decoupled-Representation Advantage-Guided Policy Optimization）通过优势引导的目标改进与表示兼容的直接动作拟合，将策略改进从策略表示中解耦，从而为高斯、扩散和流匹配策略提供共享优化原则。

Result: 在五个MuJoCo-v5任务上，DR-APO对高斯策略与SAC保持竞争力，并在扩散和流匹配策略上取得最佳汇总的仅策略性能；NavDP后训练将仿真导航成功率从70.33%提升至89.55%，真实世界导航成功率从25%提升至65%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，DR-APO提供了一种跨高斯、扩散和流匹配策略的统一策略改进原则，可应用于连续控制学习到具身策略后训练。

Abstract: Policy representations in reinforcement learning have expanded beyond Gaussian policies to diffusion and flow-matching based generative policies. However, existing RL methods remain representation-specific, lacking a unified optimization interface for emerging generative policies. In particular, extending likelihood-based objectives to multi-step generative policies couples optimization to their internal generation process, where accumulated likelihood ratios can weaken the update signal. Meanwhile, generative-policy-specific methods often rely on multi-candidate generation and critic-based selection, improving overall return while providing limited improvement to the policy itself. We introduce DR-APO (Decoupled-Representation Advantage-Guided Policy Optimization), which decouples policy improvement from policy representation through advantage-guided target improvement and representation-compatible direct action fitting, providing a shared optimization principle for Gaussian, diffusion, and flow policies. Across five MuJoCo-v5 tasks, DR-APO remains competitive with SAC for Gaussian policies and achieves the best aggregate policy-only performance for both diffusion and flow-matching policies. NavDP post-training improves the Success Rate (SR) from 70.33% to 89.55% in simulation and from 25% to 65% in real-world navigation. These results demonstrate a unified policy-improvement principle across Gaussian, diffusion, and flow policies, from continuous-control learning to embodied-policy post-training.

</details>


### [288] [DEFlow: Deep Equilibrium Flow Maximum-Entropy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16597)
*Ruslan Ageev,Huaze Tang,Runqing Miao,Jiarong Li,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DEFlow 是一种深度均衡流策略，用隐式欧拉积分替代显式欧拉采样，将所有中间动作作为均衡系统联合求解；结合隐式微分与最终动作密度的离散变量替换公式，在少步采样下保持可处理密度，并在多目标对接、操作和 MuJoCo 任务上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表达性强的流策略能表示多个高价值动作，但在少量积分步下采样并同时保持可处理的动作密度仍然困难。

Method: DEFlow 提出深度均衡流策略，用隐式欧拉积分替代显式欧拉采样，将所有中间动作作为均衡系统联合求解；结合隐式微分与最终动作密度的离散变量替换公式，从而在 soft actor-critic 中实现离策略训练，且无需对求解器迭代反向传播。

Result: 在四个 Multi-Goal docking 变体上，DEFlow 相对最强基线在平均成功覆盖率上取得约 10% 的平均相对提升；在五个操作任务中的四个上取得最高平均时间平均回报，并在五个 MuJoCo 任务上总体具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果支持将隐式流策略用于同时要求多样性与精确性的任务，代价是额外的非线性与线性求解。

Abstract: Expressive flow policies can represent multiple high-value actions, but sampling them with few integration steps while retaining tractable action densities remains challenging. We introduce DEFlow, a deep equilibrium flow policy that replaces explicit Euler sampling with implicit Euler integration and solves all intermediate actions jointly as an equilibrium system. We combine implicit differentiation with a discrete change-of-variables formula for the final-action density, enabling off-policy training within soft actor-critic without backpropagating through solver iterations. DEFlow achieves an average relative improvement of approximately 10% in mean successful coverage over the strongest baseline across four Multi-Goal docking variants. It also achieves the highest mean time-averaged return on four of five manipulation tasks, while remaining broadly competitive across five MuJoCo tasks. These results support implicit flow policies for tasks requiring both diversity and precision, at the cost of additional nonlinear and linear solves. Primary

</details>


### [289] [Harnessing VLM in Embodied RL: From Reward Regressor to Success Detector via Exploration-Augmented Key-Observation Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15401)
*Xin Liu,Yutong Jiang,Jian Su,Ziheng Wang,Chuang Wang,Haoran Li,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VLMeg，将VLM作为成功检测器而非奖励回归器，通过探索增强的关键观测门控实现无奖励从零强化学习，在MetaWorld和真实实验中取得领先效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的具身奖励设计多将VLM训练为奖励回归器，依赖大量标注，且主要面向预训练策略微调，在更具挑战的从零强化学习场景中表现不佳。其根源在于精确奖励回归与VLM擅长的多模态VQA能力不匹配。

Method: VLMeg将VLM用作成功检测器，核心是探索增强的关键观测门控：先根据当前RL回合与生成的成功轨迹的相似度判断该回合是否值得VLM评判；再从值得评判的回合中选择困惑度最低的关键观测交给VLM评估，作为回合奖励。该门控减少冗余VLM调用并提升检测准确率。针对值得评判回合稀疏的问题，引入基于生成轨迹的探索激励，促使策略更高效地探索可触发VLM奖励的轨迹。

Result: 在MetaWorld的12个挑战性无奖励任务中，VLMeg实现最先进的从零强化学习，优于先进的VLM基线；并在3个挑战性真实世界实验中无需任何真值奖励即可有效进行强化学习。

Conclusion: VLMeg表明，让VLM承担成功检测而非精确奖励回归更符合其能力，结合关键观测门控与探索激励可显著提升从零强化学习中的奖励设计与学习效率，并具备真实场景应用潜力。

Abstract: VLMs provide promising solutions to embodied reward design. Most current methods that train VLMs as reward regressors not only require extensive annotations but are also primarily designed for finetuning pretrained policies, and perform less well in the more challenging from-scratch RL setting. This issue stems from a fundamental mismatch: regressing precise rewards is misaligned with what VLMs naturally excel at. We posit that it is more aligned with VLMs' inherent multi-modal VQA capabilities for the models to function as success detectors, and propose VLM success reward via Exploration-Augmented Key-Observation Gating (VLMeg) to well harness VLM for embodied reward design. As the core of VLMeg, the proposed exploration-augmented key-observation gating first determines whether the current RL episode is judgment-worthy based on its similarity to a generated success trajectory. Then, the key observation with the lowest confusion from the judgment-worthy episode will be selected for VLM evaluation as episodic reward. This gating mechanism not only avoids redundant calls but also enables higher detection accuracy of VLM. Considering the sparsity of judgment-worthy episodes, the advanced exploration incentives based on the generated trajectory are introduced, enabling more efficient exploration toward judgment-worthy trajectories for more VLM reward triggers and thus gaining higher RL efficiency. Extensive experiments in MetaWorld demonstrate VLMeg achieves state-of-the-art from-scratch RL, outperforming advanced VLM-based baselines on 12 challenging reward-free tasks. Furthermore, VLMeg also enables effective RL without any ground-truth reward in 3 challenging real-world experiments. Code will be open after acceptance at this https URL .

</details>


### [290] [LeHami: Symplectic Latent Dynamics for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15267)
*Romina Yalovetzky,Randall Balestriero,Yann LeCun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Symplectic Hamiltonian JEPA（LeHami），用动作条件哈密顿流和精确辛 leapfrog 积分替代标准 JEPA 预测器，从几何上防止长时程潜在状态收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 在潜空间学习世界模型，但无约束自回归预测器在长 rollout 中会使潜分布收缩，导致表示退化、状态合并与长期预测不稳定。

Method: 将预测器替换为结构化潜状态 (q,p) 上的动作条件哈密顿流，并用精确辛 leapfrog 步积分；从学习到的位置型表示的时序导数获得类动量分量，形成显式二阶状态用于预测与控制。

Result: 在保守与驱动视觉单摆环境中，LeHami 相比无约束 JEPA 世界模型显著提升长时程速度预测，并经验上保持潜在体积恒定；还能在未见子帧步长下准确积分并支持数值时间反演。

Conclusion: 几何结构可为潜世界模型提供推理时保证，与表示正则可互补，从而抑制长时程收缩并提升泛化与可逆性。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn world models directly in latent space, but unconstrained autoregressive predictors may contract the latent distribution over long rollouts. We introduce Symplectic Hamiltonian JEPA (LeHami), which replaces the standard predictor with an action-conditioned Hamiltonian flow over a structured latent state (q, p) integrated using an exactly symplectic leapfrog step. This construction preserves phase-space volume at every rollout step and rules out uniform contraction, asymptotically stable fixed points, and merging of distinct latent states, independently of the learned parameters or action sequence. We obtain the momentum-like component from temporal derivatives of the learned position-like representation, providing an explicit second-order state for prediction and control. On conservative and actuated visual pendulum environments, LeHami substantially improves long-horizon velocity prediction over an unconstrained JEPA world model and empirically maintains constant latent volume where the baseline contracts. The learned flow also supports accurate integration at unseen sub-frame step sizes and numerical time reversal. These results show how geometric structure can provide inference-time guarantees that are complementary to representation regularization in latent world models.

</details>


### [291] [Continuous-Time Cloth Dynamics with Physics-Informed Neural ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14342)
*Yingdong Ru,Lipeng Zhuang,Zhuo He,Gerardo Aragon Camarasa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ContinuousCloth 是一种连续时间、物理信息驱动的神经布料动力学求解器，将弹簧-质量 ODE 嵌入神经轨迹模型，可在任意时刻查询并借自动微分求位移、速度与加速度，并在精度、速度和稀疏噪声观测下的下游任务中均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间步进布料模拟器仅在离散积分网格上返回轨迹，难以在任意时间戳查询且计算开销高；同时，仅数据驱动的神经模型在稀疏、噪声观测下泛化与参数估计能力不足。

Method: 在神经轨迹模型中显式约束弹簧-质量 ODE，构建连续时间物理信息神经求解器；支持任意时间戳查询，并通过自动微分计算位移、速度、加速度；在纯物理设定与融合稀疏噪声观测两种场景下评估，用于插值与参数估计。

Result: 纯物理设定下，在六种边界条件场景中轨迹精度与高精度求解器相当，运行时间最多降低 17.4 倍；融合稀疏噪声观测后，仿真中插值误差较纯数据神经基线降低 7.3 倍，真实布料序列降低 2.4 倍；高噪声下参数误差较可微求解器基线降低 2.6 倍。

Conclusion: ContinuousCloth 兼具连续时间查询、物理一致性和计算效率，并能作为强归纳偏置提升稀疏、噪声观测下的插值与参数估计鲁棒性，适用于真实测量不完美场景。

Abstract: We present ContinuousCloth, a continuous-time, physics-informed neural solver for deformable cloth dynamics that enforces a spring--mass ODE within a neural trajectory model. Unlike classical time-stepping simulators that return trajectories only on a discrete integration grid, ContinuousCloth supports querying at arbitrary timestamps and computing displacement, velocity, and acceleration via automatic differentiation. We evaluate ContinuousCloth along two dimensions. In a physics-only setting, ContinuousCloth achieves trajectory accuracy comparable to high-precision solvers across six diverse boundary-condition scenarios while reducing runtime by up to 17.4 times. When fused with sparse and noisy observations, the spring--mass ODE acts as a strong inductive bias for downstream tasks: interpolation and parameter estimation. That is, ContinuousCloth reduces interpolation error by 7.3 times over a data-only neural baseline in simulation and 2.4 times on real-world cloth sequences, and achieves 2.6 times lower parameter error than a differentiable-solver baseline under high-noise conditions, demonstrating consistent robustness under realistic measurement imperfections.

</details>


### [292] [Learning to See through Action: Predictive Curiosity for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14302)
*Stelios Zarifis,Panagiotis Filntisis,George Retsinas,Petros Maragos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DySCOVER：机器人通过好奇心驱动的主动交互进行自监督视觉学习；在重建好奇心之外加入预测好奇心，可提升视觉模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类和动物并非从固定数据集中学习视觉，而是通过移动、交互并从动作的视觉后果中学习；因此研究机器人能否通过好奇心驱动的主动经验收集来改进视觉学习。

Method: 提出 DySCOVER 自监督主动机器人视觉学习框架：机器人观测 RGB 帧，学习视觉自编码器进行编码与重建，并训练循环世界模型，以当前状态和动作为条件预测下一视觉隐状态；策略仅用内在奖励训练：重建误差鼓励视觉新颖观测，世界模型预测误差鼓励后果尚不可预测的转移，从而偏向更复杂轨迹以丰富经验。

Result: 以机械臂作为具身智能体的实验表明，加入预测好奇心优于仅用视觉好奇心，并能改进视觉模型；在不同初始条件、经验容量、具身形态和场景内容变化下，预测好奇心的增益保持一致。

Conclusion: 预测好奇心可以超越仅基于重建的好奇心，改进主动视觉学习。

Abstract: Humans and animals do not learn vision from a fixed set of data: they move, interact, and learn from the visual consequences of their actions. Motivated by this developmental view, we study whether robots can improve visual learning by actively collecting experience through curiosity-driven interaction. We introduce DySCOVER, a self-supervised framework for active robot visual learning. A robot observes RGB frames, learns a visual autoencoder that encodes and reconstructs them, and trains a recurrent world model to predict the next visual latent state conditioned on the current state and action. The agent's policy is trained only with intrinsic rewards: reconstruction error encourages visually novel observations, while the world model's error encourages transitions whose consequences are not yet predictable. This prediction error biases the policy toward more intricate trajectories that enrich the agent's set of experiences with observations beyond those encouraged by visual novelty alone. Our experiments conducted with robotic arms as the embodied agents show that adding predictive curiosity improves the vision model over using visual curiosity alone. Across variations in initial conditions, experience capacity, embodiment, and scene content, adding predictive curiosity consistently improved visual learning. These results show that predictive curiosity can improve active visual learning beyond reconstruction-based curiosity alone.

</details>


### [293] [A Geometric Perspective on Reward Function Updates in Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14041)
*Anish Diwan,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从占用空间视角重新审视逆强化学习中的奖励更新，利用最优占用轨迹的 e-测地线几何，提出用平均 Fisher 信息预条件的自然梯度式更新，并从镜像下降角度解释其策略学习机制及收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆强化学习通常用拉格朗日原始-对偶梯度下降求解，虽影响深远，但仅从梯度更新角度分析，忽略了占用空间中问题的几何结构，因此对奖励更新的理解不完整。

Method: 论文建立逆强化学习奖励更新的占用空间视角：利用最优占用解及其几何已知这一事实，分析沿 e-测地线趋近最优占用时几何对奖励参数空间中对偶问题梯度的影响；据此用占用轨迹上的平均 Fisher 信息对偶梯度做预条件，并证明该预条件梯度等价于旧奖励与新目标奖励的凸插值，从而得到计算开销很小的自然梯度式更新；进一步从 Mirror Descent 视角分析，表明基于所得奖励的策略搜索诱导近似熵镜像下降。

Result: 预条件后的对偶梯度表现为旧奖励与新目标奖励的凸插值，可实现高效的自然梯度式奖励更新且额外计算开销很小；相应策略搜索等价于近似熵镜像下降；论文最后给出了此类几何感知逆强化学习算法的收敛性结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了逆强化学习的占用空间几何视角，将奖励更新、自然梯度与镜像下降联系起来，并为几何感知逆强化学习算法建立了收敛性保证。

Abstract: Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) is classically solved using Lagrangian optimisation, by jointly optimising a primal-dual problem with gradient descent to obtain the optimal reward function parameters and corresponding optimal policy. While this algorithmic view of IRL has been highly influential, gradient-based reward updates only paint a partial picture, largely ignoring the geometry of the problem in occupancy space. In this paper, we present an occupancy-space view of reward updates in IRL. We leverage the insight that both the optimal solution and its geometry in occupancy space are known, and show that the geometry along the e-geodesic (straight line in occupancy space) toward the optimal occupancy directly affects the gradient of the IRL dual problem in reward parameter space. This perspective naturally suggests preconditioning the dual gradient with the average Fisher information along the occupancy trajectory. We show that this preconditioned gradient is just a convex interpolation of the old reward and a new target reward, yielding efficient natural-gradient-style updates with little additional computational overhead. We then analyse these occupancy-space reward updates from a Mirror Descent (MD) perspective, and show that policy search over the resulting rewards induces approximate entropic MD on the policy. Finally, we conclude with convergence results of such geometry-aware IRL algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [294] [A Minimum Exact Disjunctive Basis for Two-Robot Solvability Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17677)
*Tsukasa Ninomiya,Shota Naito,Naoki Kitamura,Yuichi Sudo,Koichi Wada*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 完全刻画两个平面自主移动机器人在可变失向、手性与刚性运动下的任务可解性；可解性剖面恰为13类商序Q的所有上集，共54个，并给出最小精确析取基与最小分离任务集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同能力模型与调度器下任务可解性的结构，确定可实现剖面在商序Q中的分布，并寻找精确表示所有剖面、区分计算类的最小任务集。

Method: 将四种标准能力模型与调度器建模为13个计算类的商序Q；把任务可解性表示为Q的上集；构造13个具体任务形成精确析取基；对Q的反链，通过为每个目标并之外最大类中的算法构造可容许公平执行，使其同时违反所选谓词，从而证明析取剖面精确等于并；再分析6个任务的分离签名及其最小性。

Result: 每个上集均可实现；可实现剖面构成Up(Q)，恰有54个，由1+13z+24z^2+16z^3计数；每个剖面可用至多3个主任务规范析取表示；13个主任务构成最小精确析取基且在剖面层面唯一；6个任务的最小签名恢复Q中所有包含与不可比关系，其剖面为并不可约元生成的主上集；任何精确析取生成所有剖面的任务族必含全部13个主剖面。

Conclusion: 由此建立完整可解性分类：可实现剖面形成有限分配格Up(Q)，精确析取基在剖面层面最小且唯一，分离Q的最小任务数为6。

Abstract: We completely characterize the solvability profiles of tasks for two autonomous mobile robots in the plane under variable disorientation, chirality, and rigid movements. The four standard capability models and the schedulers considered here have a known quotient order Q of thirteen computational classes. A task's solvability profile is the set of classes in which it is solvable, and is necessarily an upset of Q. We prove that every upset is realizable and construct a minimum exact disjunctive basis of thirteen concrete tasks. The basis contains one task for each principal upset. For every antichain of Q, the disjunction of the corresponding tasks has profile exactly equal to the union of their profiles. This exactness is not implied by the individual profiles: an algorithm may satisfy different disjuncts in different executions. We establish it by constructing, for every algorithm in each maximal class outside the target union, a single admissible fair execution violating all selected predicates simultaneously. Consequently, the realizable profiles form the entire finite distributive lattice Up(Q): there are exactly 54 profiles, counted by the antichain polynomial 1 + 13z + 24z^2 + 16z^3, and each has a canonical disjunctive representation using at most three principal tasks. We also identify six tasks whose solvability signatures recover every inclusion and incomparability in Q, and prove that six is minimum even among arbitrary separating tasks. Their profiles are precisely the principal upsets generated by the join-irreducible elements of Q. In contrast, any task family generating every profile by exact disjunctions must contain all thirteen principal profiles. Thus our exact disjunctive basis has minimum size and is unique at the profile level.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [295] [Two-Stage Multi-modal Learning for Dynamic Audio-Visual Navigation: From Physical Alignment to Scene Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14077)
*Jia Li,Yinfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文面向动态音视觉导航（D-AVN），提出两阶段多模态学习框架，通过物理对齐与跨模态场景聚合提升对移动声源、未见声音类别及复杂环境中的导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视觉导航研究多针对静态声源，而真实场景常涉及移动目标；已有方法依赖语义听觉特征，导致对未听过声音类别泛化差，并限制细粒度空间推理能力。

Method: 提出两阶段多模态学习框架：物理对齐阶段使用强度校准几何编码器（ICGE），将声音强度与视觉几何进行物理启发式对齐，学习类别无关的空间对应；场景聚合阶段使用基于 Transformer 的跨模态场景聚合器（CMSA），动态融合多模态特征，构建用于策略学习的鲁棒状态表示。

Result: 在 Replica 和 Matterport3D 上的大量实验表明，该方法取得了最先进的成功率，并在复杂环境和未见声音场景下显著提升了泛化能力与导航效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段多模态学习框架能有效应对动态音视觉导航中的移动声源、未见声音类别和复杂环境挑战，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Embodied Audio-Visual Navigation (AVN) requires an agent to locate sound targets by jointly leveraging visual and auditory cues. While prior work has achieved notable success with static sound sources, real-world scenarios often involve moving targets, giving rise to the more challenging problem of \textbf{Dynamic Audio-Visual Navigation (D-AVN)}. Existing approaches largely rely on semantic auditory features, which limits generalization to unheard sound categories and hinders fine-grained spatial reasoning. To address these challenges, we propose a novel \textbf{Two-Stage Multi-modal Learning} framework for D-AVN. In the Physical Alignment stage, the proposed \textbf{Intensity-Calibrated Geometry Encoder (ICGE)} performs physics-informed alignment of sound intensity with visual geometry to capture category-agnostic spatial correspondence. In the Scene Aggregation stage, a Transformer-based \textbf{Cross-Modal Scene Aggregator (CMSA)} dynamically integrates multi-modal features to construct a robust state representation for policy learning. Extensive experiments on Replica and Matterport3D demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art success rates, while significantly improving generalization and navigation efficiency in complex environments and under unheard sound scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [296] [Optimizing Team Behavior via Extremum-seeking Control in Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18808)
*Svar Rajankumar Patel,Kshitij Jerath*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出 ADiCo 两时间尺度框架，在合作多智能体强化学习训练中在线自适应调节行为多样性，从而避免人工选择多样性目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在合作 MARL 中，行为多样性有助于角色专精与更丰富的联合行为，但其合适水平高度依赖任务和上下文，且人工调参成本高。

Method: ADiCo 采用双时间尺度：快速尺度上的 DiCo 将系统多样性调节至目标值；慢速尺度上的极值搜索控制器（ESC）仅利用平均回合回报调整该目标，无需显式模型或多样性-性能景观的解析梯度。

Result: 在两个合作 MARL 任务的六种配置上，ADiCo 能维持任务适配的多样性水平：同质策略有利时保持低多样性，异质策略需要时提高多样性；其平均性能匹配或超过最佳网格搜索目标值的 DiCo 以及无约束 DiCo。

Conclusion: 将经典反馈控制引入 MARL 训练，可为协调提供系统化替代方案，避免针对具体任务手动选择多样性目标。

Abstract: In cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), agents must learn to coordinate their actions to achieve shared objectives. A key indicator of effective coordination is behavioral diversity, i.e., the agents' potential to enact distinct yet complementary control policies given the same set of observations. Although behavioral diversity can enable role specialization and richer joint behaviors, its appropriate level is highly context- and task-dependent and often costly to tune. We propose Adaptive Diversity Control (ADiCo), a two-timescale framework that adapts behavioral diversity online during training. On a fast timescale, Diversity Control (DiCo) regulates system diversity toward a target value. On a slower timescale, an Extremum Seeking Controller (ESC) adjusts this target using only mean episodic returns, without requiring an explicit model or analytical gradient of the diversity-performance landscape. We evaluate ADiCo on six configurations across two cooperative MARL tasks, where it maintains task-appropriate diversity levels: low diversity when homogeneous policies are beneficial and higher diversity when heterogeneous policies are required. Through this adaptation, ADiCo matches or exceeds the mean performance of DiCo (best grid-searched diversity target) and Unconstrained DiCo (diversity allowed to emerge without target regulation). These results demonstrate that incorporating classical feedback control into MARL training enables effective coordination by providing a systematic alternative to task-specific manual selection of the diversity target.

</details>


### [297] [DiTSG: Context-Aware Air Traffic Scene Generation with Multi-Agent Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16959)
*Seokbin Yoon,Kyungmin Kim,Taejung Kwon,Keumjin Lee,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出DiTSG，一种基于扩散Transformer的空中交通场景生成方法，可从多机轨迹中学习并生成保留真实航空器交互的多样交通场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中交通模拟器难以有效捕捉管制员通过速度建议、雷达引导等方式干预航空器轨迹的模式，而理解空域内可安全高效管理的飞机数量与交通构型是空中交通管理的基础。

Method: DiTSG以堆叠的多智能体倒置Transformer层作为扩散Transformer的去噪骨干，联合生成同一交通场景内多架航空器的轨迹，并以各航空器特定的所需到达时间为条件进行生成。

Result: 实验表明，DiTSG能够生成多样的交通场景，既复现记录交通的统计特性，又保留实际空中交通运行中观察到的真实航空器间交互。

Conclusion: DiTSG可生成逼真、多样的交通场景，为弥补现有空中交通模拟器在刻画管制干预模式方面的不足、评估空域容量与管制管理能力提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Understanding how many aircraft, and which traffic configurations, can be safely and efficiently managed by air traffic controllers within a given airspace is fundamental to air traffic management. Air traffic controllers continuously modify aircraft trajectories through interventions such as speed advisories and radar vectoring, and the patterns of these interventions are difficult to capture effectively using existing air traffic simulators. To address this limitation, we propose Diffusion Transformers for Traffic Scene Generation (DiTSG), which learns to generate air traffic scenes from recorded multi-aircraft trajectories. DiTSG employs stacked multi-agent inverted Transformer layers as the denoising backbone of a diffusion transformer, jointly generates the trajectories of multiple aircraft within a traffic scene, and conditions the generation on agent-specific required times of arrival. The resulting trajectories generated by DiTSG preserve realistic inter-aircraft interactions observed in actual air traffic operations. Experiments show that the DiTSG generates diverse traffic scenes that reproduce the statistical properties of recorded traffic while preserving realistic inter-aircraft interactions.

</details>


### [298] [Persistent LLM Orchestration of Heterogeneous Robot Teams via Partial Delegation to Multi-Agent Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15224)
*Maria G. Mendoza,Victoria M. Tuck,Zachary Ravichandran,Shankar Sastry,George J. Pappas*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该工作针对LLM编排机器人团队在非终止、动态变化环境中的三类失效，提出马尔可夫状态摘要、部分委派和基于观测陈旧度的重提示机制，在模拟城市搜救中大幅提升救援率并减少token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言规范可让非专家用户给机器人团队分配任务，LLM也已被证明能将语义规范落地为异构团队计划。但现有方法假设任务会终止、环境虽未知但不变化，这在灾害响应等许多新兴应用中不成立：任务不终止，环境随危险蔓延和人员移动持续变化。作者指出LLM编排器在此设置下有三种失效：提示无界增长；随着智能体和目标累积，推理能力退化；覆盖一次搜索空间后停止主动监控。

Method: 基于grounded generative intelligence框架，用经典思想分别解决上述问题。第一，用马尔可夫状态摘要替代不断增长的交互历史，该摘要在每次调用时从语义地图重建。第二，采用部分委派：编排器将部分机器人分配给自主多智能体协议，把自身推理保留给需要详细参数和仔细排序的子任务生成。第三，将观测陈旧度显式暴露在提示中，并结合任务触发与事件触发的重新提示，使团队保持警觉。在带蔓延火灾和移动疏散者的模拟城市搜救任务中评估，并进行消融实验。

Result: 在模拟城市搜救任务中，该框架救援了98%的平民，最强基线为89%；同时使用的token数量少六倍。额外消融实验分离了各机制的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过马尔可夫状态摘要、部分委派和观测陈旧度驱动的重提示，可以缓解LLM编排在非终止、动态变化环境中的提示增长、推理退化和监控停滞问题，从而提升异构机器人团队在持续动态任务中的规划与执行效果。

Abstract: Natural language specifications let non-expert users task robot teams, and large language models (LLMs) have recently proven effective at grounding such semantic specifications into plans for heterogeneous teams. Existing approaches assume missions that terminate and environments that, while unknown, do not change, but both assumptions fail in many emerging applications. For example, a disaster-response mission does not terminate, and its environment changes continuously as hazards spread and evacuees move. We identify three ways an LLM orchestrator breaks in this setting: its prompt grows without bound, its reasoning degrades as agents and targets accumulate, and it stops actively monitoring after covering the search space once. We build on the successful framework of grounded generative intelligence, and address each issue with a classical idea. A Markovian state summary, rebuilt from the semantic map at every invocation, replaces the growing interaction history. Partial delegation lets the orchestrator assign a subset of robots to an autonomous multi-agent protocol and reserve its reasoning for generating subtasks that require detailed parameters and careful ordering. Lastly, observation staleness, surfaced explicitly in the prompt, together with task-and event-triggered re-prompting, keeps the team vigilant. In a simulated urban search-and-rescue mission with spreading fire and moving evacuees, our framework rescues 98% of civilians versus 89% for the strongest baseline while using six times fewer tokens. Additional ablations isolate the contribution of each mechanism.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [299] [Augmenting Real-time Visualization in Minimally Invasive Surgery with Particle Filtering and Depth-Aware Occlusion Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18422)
*Anuska Sarkar,Partha Acharya,Vishnu Nandury,Kaustav Basu,Subhamoy Mandal*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于微创手术（MIS）的实时混合框架，实现遮挡处理与深度感知跟踪，结合增强YOLO检测、粒子滤波（PF）和MiDaS单目深度估计，并通过器械与感兴趣区域（ROI）的距离阈值检测遮挡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术器械造成的遮挡常会遮蔽感兴趣区域（ROI），影响手术精度，因此需要实时、鲁棒的遮挡处理与深度感知跟踪方法。

Method: 集成基于增强YOLO的器械检测、粒子滤波（PF）跟踪和基于MiDaS的单目深度估计；利用器械与ROI之间的距离阈值进行遮挡检测。

Result: 器械检测置信度为0.85；深度预测MAE为0.34 cm、RMSE为0.53 cm；基于体模并与立体深度测量对比验证的MAE为0.65 cm、RMSE为0.66 cm；遮挡检测准确率为81.95%，实时性能为38.7 FPS；初步外科医生反馈表明所提可视化框架具有临床相关性。

Conclusion: 该框架在微创手术中具有应用前景，有望用于未来术中图像引导导航和决策支持。

Abstract: We present a real-time, hybrid framework for occlusion handling and depth-aware tracking in minimally invasive surgeries (MIS). Occlusions caused by surgical instruments often obscure the region of interest (ROI), affecting surgical precision. To address this, our approach integrates enhanced You Only Look Once (YOLO) based detection, Particle Filtering (PF), and monocular depth estimation using Multiple Depth Estimation Accuracy with Single Network (MiDaS). Occlusion is detected using a distance-based threshold between instruments and ROI. Experimental results demonstrate robust instrument detection with a confidence score of 0.85, effective occlusion event handling, and accurate depth prediction with an MAE of 0.34 cm and RMSE of 0.53 cm. Independent phantom-based validation against stereo depth measurements yielded an MAE of 0.65 cm and RMSE of 0.66 cm. The framework further achieved an occlusion detection accuracy of 81.95\% while maintaining real-time performance at 38.7 FPS. Preliminary surgeon feedback indicates that the proposed visualization framework is clinically relevant and holds promise for future intraoperative image-guided navigation and decision support in MIS.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [300] [Vision-Predictor-Action: Towards Efficient Embodied AI via Neuro-Symbolic JEPA Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15556)
*Yeonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出Goal-Conditioned Vision-Predictor-Action (VPA)，用轻量潜在控制回路替代VLA自回归解码器，在LIBERO-10上初步验证低延迟与一定成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型常通过LLM解码器逐token生成机器人动作，推理延迟随动作序列长度增长，限制闭环控制频率。
