Method: VPA用共享视觉编码器与冻结文本编码器嵌入观测、目标图像和指令；神经符号选择器选离散技能；JEPA预测器预报下一潜在状态；流匹配求解器在少数积分步内生成整个动作块。作者分析推理复杂度、表示坍塌正则化，以及潜在子目标阈值限制跨任务阶段误差累积的条件。

Result: 可训练模型<20M参数（checkpoint<75MB，不含冻结63.4M参数CLIP文本编码器）；单相机参考配置决策步在Apple M4上10-14 ms；仅用LIBERO-10演示、单GPU<3小时训练，双相机策略在200个评估回合中成功率为40.0%。

Conclusion: 单次训练初步结果表明方法可行；代码与权重已公开，但结果仍属概念验证。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models commonly generate robot actions token by token through Large Language Model (LLM) decoders, causing inference latency to grow with action sequence length and limiting closed-loop control frequency. We present Goal-Conditioned Vision-Predictor-Action (VPA), which replaces autoregressive decoders with a lightweight latent control loop. A shared vision encoder and a frozen text encoder embed observations, goal images, and instructions; a neuro-symbolic selector chooses a discrete skill; a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) predictor forecasts the next latent state; and a flow-matching solver generates an entire action chunk in a few integration steps, so that sequential network evaluations per decision step do not depend on the action horizon. We analyze inference complexity, regularization against representation collapse, and conditions under which latent sub-goal thresholding limits error accumulation across task phases. As an initial proof of concept, we evaluated VPA on the LIBERO-10 suite. The trainable model has under 20M parameters (checkpoint under 75 MB, excluding a frozen 63.4M-parameter CLIP text encoder); a decision step of a single-camera reference configuration takes 10-14 ms on an Apple M4; and trained only on LIBERO-10 demonstrations in under three hours on a single GPU, a two-camera policy succeeded in 40.0% of 200 evaluation episodes. These preliminary results from a single training run suggest the approach is workable. Code and weights are available at this https URL and this https URL , and archived at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [301] [Filtering for Liveness: Recurrence via Backup Reachability Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17337)
*David E. J. van Wijk,Pio Ong,Aaron D. Ames,Joel W. Burdick*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种基于控制屏障函数（CBF）的控制器滤波框架，用于保证递现（recurrence）活性：允许闭环轨迹临时离开指定集合，但确保在规定时间内返回，并尽可能保留标称控制器行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CBF安全滤波器通常强制集合前向不变，难以处理需要临时离开指定集合的任务，例如在不相交区域间穿越或对信标可见性施加周期性约束。因此需要一种既保证按时返回、又尽量不改变标称行为的活性控制方法。

Method: 受CBF安全滤波范式启发，设计控制器滤波框架，仅在必要时调整标称控制器。框架包含两个核心组件：一是备份可达性CBF，用于始终保持系统在允许时间内返回指定集合的能力；二是基于时序的触发机制，仅当标称行为将无法满足返回时间要求时，才调用到达控制器。

Result: 该框架在可能时保持标称行为，同时保证递现约束；允许轨迹离开指定集合并按时返回，从而支持需要临时偏离集合的控制目标，如不相交区域间遍历。方法成功应用于无人机竞速示例，其中对信标可见性施加了递现约束。

Conclusion: 该方法将CBF安全滤波扩展到递现型活性约束，通过备份可达性与时序触发实现必要时的最小干预，为需要周期返回与临时离开集合的安全关键控制提供有效方案。

Abstract: Liveness requires a dynamical system to repeatedly accomplish a desired objective over time. In this work, we consider a particular form of liveness known as recurrence, which requires the closed-loop trajectory to regularly revisit a prescribed set, with no more than a specified amount of time between visits. Inspired by the control barrier function (CBF) safety-filter paradigm, we develop a controller-filtering framework that makes adjustments to a nominal controller only when necessary to guarantee recurrence. Unlike conventional CBF methods, which enforce set forward invariance, our framework allows the trajectory to leave the set while guaranteeing its timely return. To achieve this, our framework combines two components: a backup reachability CBF that preserves the ability to return to the prescribed set within the allowable time, and a timing-based mechanism that invokes a reaching controller only when the nominal behavior would otherwise fail to meet the return-time requirement. The resulting framework guarantees recurrence while preserving the nominal behavior when possible, enabling control objectives that require temporary excursions from the set, such as traversal between disjoint regions. Our method is successfully utilized in a drone racing example with a recurrence constraint on beacon visibility.

</details>


### [302] [Reach-Avoid Certificates for Contact-based Stochastic Hybrid Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17308)
*Tianyi Xia,Ryan K. Cosner*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究随机混合系统的 certified reach-avoid 控制：连续动态由随机微分方程描述，guard 触发跳变由受控随机核描述，且控制输入仅出现在 reset kernel 中。作者在无限时域内以规定概率保证先到达目标、后进入不安全集，提出接触证书、安全滤波器、非芝诺性及期望稳定时间/跳变次数界，并用线性规划搜索证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机混合系统证书方法通常假设跳变时刻自发或由控制器选择、仅考虑有限时域，或局限于离散时间动态；而本文关注的是输入仅影响跳变后分布的随机混合系统，并需要无限时域的概率性 reach-avoid 保证，同时从验证扩展到实际安全控制。

Method: 针对由 SDE 连续流和 guard-triggered 受控随机核组成的随机混合系统，提出 reach-avoid contact certificates，其 flow 与 jump 条件仅需在 sublevel corridor 上成立；证明任何满足兼容 reset policy 的此类证书均满足规范。进一步构造逐点安全滤波器，通过约束任意标称控制器将验证保证扩展为控制保证。作者还证明 executions 非芝诺，并给出期望 settling time 与 reset count 的界。证书搜索被表述为基于任意二次可微字典的线性规划，随后进行独立验证。

Result: 在对比实验中，认证区域是 ground truth 的子集，覆盖 93.6%。在 dribbling ball 问题的 200 个 episodes 中，基于证书的安全滤波器能够到达目标，并将碰撞次数从 68 降至 0。

Conclusion: 该工作为控制输入仅出现在 reset kernel 的随机混合系统提供了无限时域 certified reach-avoid 控制框架，结合接触证书、安全滤波和线性规划搜索，在保证非芝诺性与收敛性能的同时，实现了从形式化验证到在线安全控制的扩展。

Abstract: This work studies certified reach-avoid control of stochastic hybrid systems in which a stochastic differential equation governs continuous flow and a controlled stochastic kernel governs guard-triggered resets. The input appears only in the reset kernel, where it determines the post-jump distribution. Certificates for such systems have so far assumed spontaneous or controller-chosen jump times, a finite horizon, or discrete-time dynamics; we certify, over the infinite horizon, that the target is reached before the unsafe set with at least a prescribed probability. First, we introduce reach-avoid contact certificates, whose flow and jump conditions are imposed only on a sublevel corridor, and prove that any such certificate with a compatible reset policy meets the specification; a pointwise safety filter extends this guarantee from verification to control by constraining arbitrary nominal controllers. Next, we prove that executions are non-Zeno and bound the expected settling time and reset count. We frame certificate search as a linear program over any twice-differentiable dictionary followed by independent verification. We illustrate our approach via comparisons where the certified region is a subset covering 93.6\% of the ground truth and where our certificate-based safety filter reaches the goal while reducing collisions from 68 to zero across 200 episodes of a dribbling ball problem.

</details>


### [303] [Multi-Agent Deferred-Decision Trajectory Optimization with Anytime-Valid Target Retention for Moving Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17003)
*Farbod Siahkali,Mengxue Hou,Vijay Gupta*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出DDTO控制器，用于多智能体终端拦截中目标身份与候选终端位置不确定的问题，融合共形预测、候选剔除、容量约束分配与候选条件规划，并给出覆盖、保留及失败概率界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体终端拦截中，多个移动候选之一才是未知目标，且目标身份与候选终端位置均不确定，需要在滚动时域内延迟决策并保证拦截性能与安全性。

Method: 提出滚动时域延迟决策轨迹优化（DDTO），结合共形终端位置预测、序贯候选剔除、容量约束分配与候选条件规划；在终端和身份残差可交换且查询可预测条件下，证明固定时间终端位置覆盖与任意时间有效目标保留；最终重规划时，对收紧集DDTO计划通过非线性滚动验证但捕获失败的联合概率给出上界；闭环中对无验证计划的智能体应用回退制导。

Result: 在100个成对场景中，严格控制努力预算下捕获79%目标，放宽预算下捕获85%。

Conclusion: DDTO在目标身份与终端位置不确定下提供理论保证和较高捕获率，回退制导增强闭环鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study multi-agent terminal interception when one of several moving candidates is the unknown target and both target identity and candidate terminal locations are uncertain. We develop a receding-horizon deferred-decision trajectory optimization (DDTO) controller that combines conformal terminal-location prediction, sequential candidate rejection, capacity-constrained assignment, and candidate-contingent planning. Under exchangeability conditions for terminal and identity residuals, with predictable querying, we establish fixed-time terminal-location coverage and anytime-valid target retention. At the final replan, when one control step remains, we bound the joint probability that a tightened-set DDTO plan passes nonlinear rollout verification but capture fails. The closed-loop controller applies fallback guidance to any agent without a verified DDTO plan and, across 100 paired scenarios, captures 79% of targets under the strict control-effort budget and 85% when that budget is relaxed.

</details>


### [304] [Aerodynamic Promptness of Tilting-Propeller Multirotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16532)
*Antonio Franchi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出多旋翼气动 promptness 的几何理论：以带符号螺旋桨转速与朝向机构构型组成执行器乘积流形，在黎曼度量下构造任务速率权限映射，其矩阵行列式平方根定义气动 promptness，并统一分析方向权限、条件数、内部运动及 Holocopter/Voliro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对桨向可变的倾斜多旋翼，需要统一刻画执行器构型与转速如何影响力旋量变化率，超越固定桨或传统分配矩阵，并解释机械耦合倾斜的权限、奇异性与内部运动。

Method: 取执行器状态为带符号螺旋桨转速和机构构型，保留带符号二次推力律与构型相关推力方向；常值方向映射表示固定桨，机构坐标的共享依赖表示机械耦合倾斜；在执行器乘积流形上引入物理可解释黎曼度量；复合执行器映射微分、度量诱导音乐同构与对偶微分，得到由力旋量余向量到力旋量变化率向量的任务速率权限映射。在固定任务坐标下取该矩阵行列式平方根定义 aerodynamic promptness；分析其谱、度量水平逆、到力旋量纤维方向的正交投影及保任务内部运动；与动态反馈线性化建立内在联系，并特化到独立倾斜、径向布置的 Holocopter 与 Voliro。

Result: 正 promptness 等价于局部力旋量变化率满射；谱给出方向权限与条件数；同一构造给出度量水平逆、力旋量纤维正交投影，以及不改变瞬时力旋量而调节 promptness 的保任务内部运动；对 Holocopter 和 Voliro，澄清其现有分配与秩构造可纳入同一执行器流形几何。

Conclusion: 该几何框架统一了固定与倾斜多旋翼的分配、秩和敏捷度分析，为可变构型多旋翼的权限评估、奇异性理解及内部运动设计提供理论基础，并将动态反馈线性化与具体独立倾斜架构联系起来。

Abstract: This work develops a geometric theory of aerodynamic promptness for multirotor platforms whose propeller directions depend on a smooth orientation-mechanism configuration. The actuator state consists of the actual signed propeller speeds and the mechanism configuration, and the nonlinear actuator map retains both the signed-quadratic propeller law and the configuration-dependent thrust directions. Constant direction maps represent fixed propellers, while shared dependence on mechanism coordinates represents mechanically coupled tilts. A Riemannian metric on the product actuator manifold compares propeller accelerations and orientation-mechanism rates through physically interpretable scales. Composing the actuator-map differential, the musical isomorphism induced by this metric, and the dual differential defines a task-rate authority map from wrench covectors to wrench-rate vectors. In fixed task coordinates, the square root of the determinant of its matrix defines aerodynamic promptness. Positive promptness is equivalent to local wrench-rate surjectivity, while the associated spectrum resolves directional authority and conditioning. The same construction yields the metric horizontal inverse, the orthogonal projector onto wrench-fiber directions, and task-preserving internal motions that regulate promptness without changing the instantaneous wrench. The construction is related intrinsically to dynamic feedback linearization and specialized to the independently tilting, radially arranged Holocopter and Voliro architectures, clarifying how their established allocation and rank constructions fit within the same actuator-manifold geometry.
